Conclusion: 开放量子系统为扩散语言模型提供了一个统一且可操作的建模范式，既支持叠加提示/思维的生成，又衍生出置信度驱动的重掩码方法，二者在实验中均验证了有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion language models generate text by reversing a classical diffusion process over token sequences. We show that their continuous-time forward and reverse dynamics can be embedded in the framework of open quantum systems, which describes quantum systems interacting with their environment. This connection provides a basis for extending diffusion sampling to superpositions of sentence states and for modifying the underlying generation dynamics. We construct a $τ$-leaping unraveling scheme that simulates stochastic trajectories of these states while updating multiple token positions at each step. This enables generation conditioned on superposed prompts or thoughts, which we call Superposition of Prompts (SoP) and Superposition of Thoughts (SoT). The same formulation yields Quantum-Inspired Remasking (QIR): perturbing the masked-diffusion dynamics produces a confidence-based rule for revising previously generated tokens. Experiments with pretrained diffusion language models show that superposition-based generation can retain information from conditioning components not selected at the final measurement, while QIR improves LLM-assessed text quality when applied at an appropriate stage of generation.

</details>


### [104] [Classical methods for modern applications: synthetic tabular data generation using lightweight discrete response continuous latent variable model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21674)
*Bartosz Barzdajn,Mateusz Krzysztoń*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级表格数据建模算法：用经验边缘分布与分位数函数将连续/离散混合变量映射到连续隐空间，再用MCEM处理离散变量带来的区间不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型虽灵活但依赖多、训练复杂、采样昂贵；作者希望在当代计算生态下提供一种实现简单、训练稳健、采样高效的表格数据（含连续与离散混合变量）生成建模方法。

Method: 基于经验边缘分布和选定分位数函数做变换，将数据映射到连续隐空间，并在其中估计多元高斯等选定分布。关键点是离散变量被表示为连续形式，单个观测对应隐空间中的一个区域而非点，由此导出蒙特卡洛期望最大化（MCEM）框架，其辅助目标可写成加权隐空间似然，从而复用常规最大似然估计器。

Result: 变换保留了复杂的边缘分布与相关性，可实现高保真建模，尤其适用于单峰分布的数据；离散变量的连续表示带来的歧义被MCEM自然吸收。

Conclusion: 该方法与经典机器学习方法密切相关，但适配现代计算生态：实现简单、依赖少、估计稳健、采样高效，是深度架构之外的一种实用表格数据建模方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a lightweight algorithm for modelling tabular data containing both continuous and discrete variables. The method uses empirical marginal distributions and a selected quantile function to transform the data into a continuous latent space, where a distribution of choice, e.g. a multivariate Gaussian, can be efficiently estimated and applied. While this approach is closely related to classical well-established machine learning methods, we present a novel adaptation that fits well within the contemporary computational ecosystem. Unlike in the case of deep architectures, it results in a simple implementation with few dependencies, robust training/estimation, as well as efficient sampling. The transformation preserves complex marginal distributions and correlations, providing high-fidelity models, especially for data with unimodal distributions. One of the key characteristics of the proposed approach is its continuous representation of discrete variables. While highly desirable, this introduces a significant challenge, since a single observation corresponds to a region rather than a point in the latent space. We show that this ambiguity naturally leads to a Monte Carlo expectation-maximisation (MCEM) formulation, whose auxiliary objective can be expressed as a weighted latent-space likelihood. This, in turn, allows conventional maximum-likelihood estimators to be used within an efficient and robust optimisation procedure.

</details>


### [105] [Router Drift Control: Mitigating Off-Policy Degradation in Mixture-of-Experts Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21682)
*Liangyao Jin,Zhixin Wang,Liming Liu,Mingjun Zhang,Zhengzhou Zhu,Fangcheng Fu,Jinlong Hou,Yuan Cheng,Xiaohe Hu,Ping Zhang,Siyuan Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Router Drift Control (RDC)，以路由分歧作为off-policy陈旧度信号并做序列级过滤，缓解MoE强化学习中的训练-推理路由漂移，且不引入replay带来的梯度偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习因训练-推理不匹配、mini-batch更新与异步训练而变为off-policy；在MoE模型中该失配会改变路由决策造成router drift。已有R3（Rollout Routing Replay）复用rollout阶段的专家选择来缓解，但作者分析发现routing replay会丢弃“训练路由器选中而rollout路由器未选中”专家的贡献，从而引入梯度偏差。

Method: RDC以rollout与训练路由器之间的路由分歧（routing disagreement）作为off-policy陈旧度的度量，对高漂移的response进行序列级拒绝（sequence-level filtering）；对保留的response则保持原生专家选择及其学习信号，从而在无replay梯度偏差的前提下控制路由漂移。可与Truncated Importance Sampling (TIS) 结合用于异步RL。

Result: 随策略陈旧度增大，RDC收益更显著，并在异步RL中保持有效；在异步训练下，RDC+TIS在AIME 2024与AIME 2025上的平均峰值验证准确率较TIS提升5.5个百分点，较R3提升4.5个百分点。

Conclusion: RDC提供了一种无偏的MoE RL训练–推理失配缓解方案：以路由分歧为筛选准则替代routing replay，兼顾漂移控制与学习信号完整性，在异步训练与高陈旧度场景中优于TIS与R3。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can become off-policy due to training-inference mismatch, mini-batch updates, and asynchronous training.The resulting mismatch is especially harmful in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models, where rollout--training discrepancies can change routing decisions and lead to router drift. Rollout Routing Replay (R3) mitigates this mismatch by reusing expert selections from the rollout stage during training. Our analysis shows that routing replay introduces gradient bias by losing contributions from experts selected by the training router but not the rollout router. To address this, we propose Router Drift Control (RDC), which uses routing disagreement between rollout and training as a signal of off-policy staleness and selectively rejects high-drift responses. The sequence-level filtering avoids gradient bias introduced by replay while preserving native expert selection and the associated learning signals for retained responses. Experiments show that RDC achieves larger gains as policy staleness increases and remains effective in asynchronous RL. In asynchronous training, RDC combined with Truncated Importance Sampling (TIS) improves the average peak validation accuracy across AIME 2024 and AIME 2025 by 5.5 points over TIS and 4.5 points over R3.

</details>


### [106] [ShiftQAT: Quantization-Aware Training for the Numerical Contract of a Fixed-Function Edge CNN Accelerator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21695)
*Oguzhan Buyuksolak,Osman Erman Okman,Mehmet Gorkem Ulkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ShiftQAT 是面向 MAX7800x 固定功能 CNN 加速器的量化感知训练流程，通过构造式满足电源次方、逐张量对称缩放、8 位激活/偏置、整数运算与单次移位等硬件契约，在 ImageNet 上达到与宽松约束 QAT 相当的精度，并在 MAX78002 上实现毫秒级、毫焦级推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定功能 CNN 加速器虽快速节能，但只执行满足特定数值契约的网络（MAX7800x 要求 2 的幂、逐张量对称缩放、8 位激活与偏置、纯整数运算、每层一个静态移位）。现有量化方法难以直接满足全部约束，尤其在有残差、拼接与权重共享的结构中缩放难以静态传播。

Method: 提出 ShiftQAT 量化感知训练流程（ai8x-training 默认训练路径）：解析残差、拼接与权重共享的依赖关系，使激活缩放静态传播，将每层重量化归约为一个离线配置的移位；在联合的 2 的幂网格上训练权重与偏置，以适配 8 位偏置预算；满足包括有符号 8 位激活存储在内的全部缩放约束。

Result: 在同类方法对比中，仅 ShiftQAT 满足契约的全部缩放约束；在 ImageNet 上其 W8A8 精度下降与约束更宽松的 QAT 方法相当，W4A8 下与 FP32 差距在 2.9 个百分点以内；在 MAX78002 芯片上，MobileNetV2 与 ResNet-18 单张推理分别为 14.6 ms / 5.1 mJ 和 8.4 ms / 2.8 mJ。

Conclusion: 通过将硬件数值契约内嵌于训练过程，ShiftQAT 在不牺牲精度的前提下实现了可直接部署于固定功能加速器的高效量化网络，验证了“契约驱动”的 QAT 流程在边缘推理中的可行性与实用性。

Abstract: Fixed-function CNN accelerators make edge inference fast and energy-efficient, but they execute only networks that obey a precise numerical contract. On the MAX7800x family, this contract requires power-of-two, per-tensor, symmetric scales, 8-bit activations and biases, integer-only arithmetic, and one statically configured shift per layer. We present ShiftQAT, the quantization-aware training (QAT) flow we developed for this family, which produces networks satisfying the contract by construction. It is the default training path of ai8x-training, the family's open-source training toolchain. ShiftQAT resolves residual, concatenation, and weight-sharing dependencies so that activation scales propagate statically and each layer's requantization reduces to one offline-configured shift. It also trains weights and biases on a joint power-of-two grid that fits the 8-bit bias budget. Among the compared methods, only ShiftQAT satisfies all of the contract's scaling constraints, including signed 8-bit activation storage. Even so, on ImageNet it matches the W8A8 accuracy drop of less constrained QAT methods and stays within 2.9 percentage points of FP32 at W4A8. On MAX78002 silicon, ShiftQAT-trained MobileNetV2 and ResNet-18 classify ImageNet images in 14.6 and 8.4 ms at 5.1 and 2.8 mJ per inference.

</details>


### [107] [Human Interventions as Labels: Localizing, Steering, and (Sometimes) Editing the Computations Behind LLM Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21708)
*Cesare Zavattari,Alessandro Tommasi,Giuseppe Prencipe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将人类对LLM智能体不当行为的干预视为“内部计算失败”的测量标签，在受控环境中定位“过度提问”失败的机制；发现决策可在残差流中被因果定位与翻转，存在两种机制签名，探针门控可减少监督负担，但将修复固化到权重后呈现“地图而非规则”的异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类介入智能体错误时产生的干预信号通常被丢弃或当作训练数据回放，作者主张将其重新理解为对内部失败计算的“标签”，从而实现对失败机制的定位与修正。

Method: 受控智能体环境，聚焦单一失败（请求已明确时仍追问澄清）；标签来自规则型监督者并用盲评人类判断验证。13个合格模型中5个入选（Qwen2.5-3B、gemma-2-2b、OLMo-3-7B、Mistral-Nemo-12B、Phi-4）。用干预标签学习方向，在训练数据上选定残差流层位与干预剂量，做激活转向；随机方向作对照；探针门控转向；LoRA将修复固化到权重并与行为克隆对比。

Result: （1）ask-vs-act决策在所有合格家族中可因果定位：习得方向在确定残差流深度上翻转72–100%的留出过度提问，随机方向至多翻转一个样本。（2）两种机制签名：四个家族中模型可解码地“知道”请求明确（信念可解码、因果惰性、与决策正交）却仍然追问；第五个家族中罕见过度提问对应不同的世界表征。（3）基于探针读数的门控转向消除大部分模拟监督负担且合法提问保留（bootstrap 95%区间不含零）；提示指令反而使其恶化。（4）将修复固化入权重得到“地图而非规则”：某模型上限因果带的LoRA通过全部预设标准而相同数据的行为克隆破坏保留；三个家族相反；第五个跨随机种子跨越边界。预先写下的几何规则在两项家族外测试中失败，作者如实报告失败而不拟合替代规则。

Conclusion: 人类干预可作为失败内部计算的有效测量；过度提问在多数模型中表现为“明知明确仍追问”的决策层面失败，而非知识缺失，因而可通过残差流方向转向与探针门控修复。但权重级固化效果强烈依赖模型家族与条件，尚无可推广的预测规则。

Abstract: When an LLM agent misbehaves, a human steps in, and that intervention is usually discarded or replayed as training data. We treat it instead as a measurement: a label pointing at the internal computation that failed. We study it in a controlled agent environment, on one failure (asking a clarifying question when the request was already unambiguous), with labels from a rule-based overseer validated against blind human judgment. Claims cover eligible models, which both over-ask and sometimes act on matched unambiguous requests: five of thirteen qualified (Qwen2.5-3B, gemma-2-2b, OLMo-3-7B, Mistral-Nemo-12B, Phi-4). Four findings. First, the ask-vs-act decision is causally localizable in every eligible family: a direction learned from intervention labels flips 72-100% of held-out over-asks at a definite residual-stream depth, with site and dose chosen on training data, and random directions under the same procedure flip at most one episode. Second, over-asking has two mechanistic signatures: in four families the model demonstrably knows the request is unambiguous (the belief is decodable, causally inert and orthogonal to the decision) and asks anyway; in the fifth, the rare over-asks track different world-representations. Third, steering gated on a probe readout removes most of the simulated oversight burden while legitimate questions survive (bootstrap 95% intervals exclude zero); a prompt instruction makes it worse. Fourth, consolidating the fix into the weights yields a map, not a rule: on one model a LoRA confined to the causal band passes every criterion fixed in advance while behavior cloning on identical data breaks retention; on three families the reverse holds; on the fifth the outcome crosses the boundary across seeds. A geometric rule written down in advance to predict the regime failed on two out-of-family tests; we report the failure and stop rather than fit a replacement.

</details>


### [108] [FIRM: Fisher-Informed Retention Merging for Federated LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21720)
*Reeyansh Shah,Maram Hasan,Subhasis Chaudhuri,Biplab Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FIRM（Fisher-Informed Retention Merging），一种单轮的服务端聚合框架，用于联邦大模型遗忘（federated LLM unlearning）。它利用客户端本地 Fisher 统计量作为统一敏感度信号，构建 Fisher 归一化基础合并并显式约束保留敏感参数，缓解幅度爆炸问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦 LLM 遗忘中，遗忘数据与保留数据分散于私有客户端、服务器不可见，本地训练的 adapter 编码了相互冲突的目标。未归一化的跨客户端聚合会使合并更新幅度膨胀（magnitude explosion），在更强遗忘下损害保留效用，因此传统联邦优化聚合方式不适用。

Method: FIRM 为单轮服务端聚合框架：(1) 以客户端本地 Fisher 统计量作为统一敏感度信号，构建 Fisher 归一化的基础合并；(2) 对保留敏感参数施加显式控制的二阶保留投影，该投影具有唯一的最小距离解，其对偶规模随保留客户端数量而非模型维度增长。

Result: 在 Zephyr-7B 与 Llama-3-8B 上，于 WMDP、TOFU、MUSE-HP 数据集实验中：在 WMDP 与 MUSE-HP 上于可比遗忘水平取得效用提升，TOFU 上则呈现依赖指标的权衡。受控对比支持基础合并与额外保留校正组合在特定工作点上的收益。

Conclusion: Fisher 信息可作为统一敏感度信号有效指导联邦遗忘的稳定聚合，单轮聚合即可缓解幅度爆炸并兼顾保留效用；方法在不同基准上表现依指标而异，组合设计的收益具有操作点依赖性。

Abstract: Federated LLM unlearning addresses targeted knowledge removal when forget and retain data remain distributed across private clients and are inaccessible to the server. The resulting locally trained adapters encode conflicting objectives, making their aggregation fundamentally different from conventional federated optimization. A key failure mode is magnitude explosion, whereby unnormalized cross-client aggregation increases the merged update magnitude and degrades retained utility under stronger unlearning. We propose FIRM (Fisher-Informed Retention Merging), a single-round framework for stable and retention-aware server-side aggregation. FIRM uses client-local Fisher statistics as a unified sensitivity signal to construct a Fisher-normalized base merge and impose explicit control over retain-sensitive parameters. Its second-order retention projection admits a unique minimum-distance solution, while the corresponding dual scales with the number of retain clients rather than the model dimension. Experiments across WMDP, TOFU, and MUSE-HP using Zephyr-7B and Llama-3-8B show utility gains at comparable forgetting on WMDP and MUSE-HP, with metric-dependent trade-offs on TOFU. Controlled comparisons support benefits of the combined base merge and additional retention correction at selected operating points.

</details>


### [109] [Sparse Federated Echo State Networks for Time-Series Classification via Smooth-L1 Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21742)
*Gengcheng Lyu,Manuel Röder,Frank-Michael Schleif*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Smooth-L1正则化回声状态网络（SL1ESN），在可微框架下获得稀疏读出层，实现集中式与联邦式训练下的模型压缩与推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ESN虽高效，但稠密读出层使存储模型和单样本推理成本偏高，不利于资源受限的边缘/IoT场景。

Method: 对读出层施加二次可微的Smooth-L1正则化以诱导稀疏且保持可导性，兼容牛顿法；用于集中式训练，并构造二阶（牛顿步）联邦聚合方案；所有数据集与两种设置共用同一固定收缩阈值。

Result: 在七个时间序列分类基准上，读出层稀疏度集中式77%–95%、联邦式75%–95%；集中式下七个基准中五个与最强稠密基线差距≤0.7个准确率点，联邦式下同样五个≤1.6点；二阶联邦更新仅需数轮收敛。

Conclusion: 稀疏读出可同比例压缩存储模型、单样本推理成本与服务器到客户端的广播量，验证了其在边缘计算与IoT部署中的实用价值。

Abstract: Echo State Networks (ESNs), as lightweight implementations of reservoir computing, provide efficient solutions for temporal data processing in resource-constrained environments. While standard ESNs offer computational efficiency, their dense readout layers inflate the stored model and the per-sample inference cost. This paper introduces a Smooth-L1 regularized ESN (SL1ESN) that promotes sparsity in the readout layer while preserving differentiability for gradient-based optimization. The method employs a twice-differentiable Smooth-L1 approximation compatible with Newton's method, which we exploit both for centralized training and for a second-order (Newton-step) federated aggregation scheme. Under a single fixed shrinkage threshold shared by every dataset and both settings, experiments on seven time-series classification benchmarks show that SL1ESN learns readouts that are $77$--$95\%$ sparse in the centralized setting and $75$--$95\%$ sparse in the federated one, while staying within $0.7$ accuracy points of the strongest dense baseline on five of the seven benchmarks in the centralized setting and within $1.6$ points on the same five in the federated one, and the second-order federated update converges in only a few rounds. The resulting sparsity compresses the stored model, the per-sample inference cost, and the server-to-client broadcast by a comparable factor. These results demonstrate the practical value of sparse readouts for edge computing and IoT deployments.

</details>


### [110] [Pairwise AUC Optimization Needs Corrective Power: A Unified View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21743)
*Jia Chen,Zhiyong Yang,Shilong Bao,Qianqian Xu,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"Corrective Power"视角，指出成对AUC代理训练中度量对齐不等于随机训练始终具有纠错性：当U统计量退化、严重排序误差常见但不再产生有效纠正动量、小批量噪声减弱时，训练会陷入纠错失效区域。论文从成对梯度的U统计量退化性质给出理论解释，并据此预测平方合页损失的有效性、交叉熵预热的作用以及参数高效微调从预训练起点减少预热需求的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对AUC代理训练因与测试期排序指标直接对齐而在类别不平衡下具有吸引力，但这种度量对齐并不能保证随机训练过程始终保持"纠错性"——即对更严重的正负排序误差产生更强、更可靠的改善。作者希望解释"度量对齐"与"训练过程纠错动力"之间的鸿沟，并说明为何某些实践经验（损失选择、预热、预训练起点）有效。

Method: 以"Corrective Power"为核心概念，考察成对小批量更新对严重正负排序误差的纠正强度与可靠性；主要观察是直接成对训练可能进入严重误差常见但不再产生有用纠正运动的区域，且此区域内有助于沿最陡严重误差纠正方向前进的小批量噪声显著减弱。理论上通过分析该区域成对梯度所构成U统计量的退化性质给出机理解释。

Result: 理论结果给出若干经验规律的机制解释：(a)平方合页损失常为有效AUC代理；(b)交叉熵预热可帮助从较差起始区域进入AUC阶段的模型；(c)从预训练模型出发的参数高效微调因起点更接近有利排序几何而降低预热需求。实验覆盖8个CV任务与8个NLP任务，使用ResNet50、DenseNet121、DistilBERT-base与Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct，结果支持这些预测。

Conclusion: 度量对齐本身不足以保证随机训练持续纠错，成对梯度U统计量的退化会导致纠错动量与小批量噪声同时减弱；Corrective Power提供了统一机制解释损失选择、预热策略与预训练起点/参数高效微调的作用，并在多任务多模型实验中获支持。

Abstract: Direct pairwise AUC-surrogate training is attractive because it trains toward the ranking metric used at test time, especially under class imbalance. This creates a direct alignment between the training objective and the evaluation metric, but such metric alignment alone does not guarantee that stochastic training remains corrective. We study this gap through Corrective Power: whether pairwise mini-batch updates produce stronger and more reliable improvements for more severe positive-negative ranking errors. Our main observation is that direct pairwise training can enter regions where severe errors remain common but no longer produce useful corrective motion. In such regions, even the mini-batch noise that would normally help SGD move along the steepest severe-error correction direction can become much weaker. In this paper, we seek a theoretical explanation for this by exploring the degenerate property of the U-statistics formed from the pairwise gradients therein. Our results provide a mechanistic explanation for several empirical patterns: (a) squared-hinge loss is often an effective AUC surrogate; (b) cross-entropy warm-up can help models that enter the AUC phase from a poor starting region; and (c) parameter-efficient fine-tuning from a pretrained model can reduce that need by starting closer to a favorable ranking geometry. Experiments on 8 CV tasks and 8 NLP tasks, using ResNet50, DenseNet121, DistilBERT-base, and Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, support these predictions.

</details>


### [111] [Harmful Partial Interventions and Benign Leakage in Concept-based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21750)
*Gregory Verghese,Christopher J. Soelistyo,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Alessandro Barp,Christopher R. S. Banerji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文形式化提出「干预危害」（intervention harm），证明充分性与局部化不足以保证概念干预改善下游性能，指出概念监督不完整与危害相关；提出单调干预目标惩罚部分干预轨迹上任务损失的上升，与端点干预训练结合后显著降低部分干预危害的频率与幅度，同时保持概念与任务预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险场景中AI系统需要有意义的人类监督，概念模型（CM）允许用户检查和干预中间的人类可理解概念；但概念干预反而可能恶化下游预测，而现有关于泄漏无害性的「充分性+局部化」论证并不足以排除这种危害，且最具实践意义的部分干预场景缺乏保障。

Method: 区分「全概念干预后的端点危害」与「部分干预危害」并形式化干预危害；从理论上证明充分性与局部化条件不充分；在受控与真实设置中分析概念监督不完整性与干预危害的关联；提出单调干预目标，直接惩罚沿部分干预轨迹的任务损失增量，并与已有的端点干预（泄漏缓解）目标联合训练。

Result: 概念监督不完整性与干预危害增加相关；仅优化全概念干预端点可降低端点危害，但不能可靠防止有害的部分纠正；联合端点与单调干预训练显著降低部分干预危害的发生率与幅度，同时保持概念预测与任务预测性能。

Conclusion: 充分性与局部化不足以保障概念干预的有效性，需针对部分干预轨迹显式施加约束；单调干预目标与端点目标互补，是提升概念模型人类监督可靠性的实用方案。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems deployed in high-risk settings increasingly require models that support meaningful human oversight. Concept-based models (CMs) offer a promising framework by enabling users to inspect and intervene on intermediate, human-understandable concepts. However, concept interventions can counterintuitively worsen downstream predictions, a failure mode we formalise as intervention harm. We distinguish harm after all concepts have been intervened on from harm under partial interventions, the more likely practical use case. Modern CMs often achieve high accuracy by encoding, or leaking, additional task-relevant information in their intermediate concept representations. Recent work argues that such leakage can be benign with respect to intervention harm when concept representations satisfy two conditions: sufficiency and localisation. We prove that these criteria are not sufficient to guarantee that concept interventions improve downstream performance. Across controlled and real-world settings, we find that concept supervision incompleteness is associated with increased intervention harm. Importantly, optimising the model when all concepts are intervened on, a previously proposed leakage mitigation objective, reduces endpoint harm but does not reliably prevent harmful partial corrections. Hence, we introduce a complementary monotonic intervention objective that directly penalises increases in task loss along partial intervention trajectories. Combining endpoint and monotonic intervention training substantially reduces the prevalence and magnitude of partial intervention harm while preserving concept and task predictive performance.

</details>


### [112] [Q($γ_H$): Entropy-Guided Horizon Mixing in Multi-Discount Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21764)
*Arwa Albelaihi,Abdulrahman Altahhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Q(γ_H)，一种多视界Q学习框架：并行维护多个不同折扣因子对应的价值头，并用熵导出的置信度信号在控制时自适应地混合各头，实现随策略置信度提升而由短视界向长视界的平滑切换，无需修改贝尔曼更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定折扣因子在训练全程强加单一规划视界，无法兼顾早期探索（需短视界）与长期信用分配（需长视界）的差异化需求；同时手动选择折扣因子成本高。

Method: 维护多个折扣特异的价值头（多视界Q学习），各头保持标准贝尔曼更新不变；在控制阶段利用熵导出的置信度信号计算状态相关的混合权重，按策略置信度将控制从短视界头逐渐转向长视界头。该方法与标准值学习方法兼容，属轻量级插件式框架。

Result: 在经典控制与深度强化学习基准上，Q(γ_H)相对强固定折扣基线在延迟信用分配任务上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 将视界自适应与底层学习更新解耦，可在避免人工选择折扣因子的同时实现自适应规划视界控制，为强化学习提供轻量且通用的视界调控方案。

Abstract: A fixed discount factor in Q-learning imposes a single planning horizon throughout training, despite the differing demands of early exploration and long-term credit assignment. We propose Q($γ_H$), a multi-horizon Q-learning framework that maintains multiple discount-specific heads and adaptively combines them at control time using an entropy-derived confidence signal. The resulting state-dependent mixing weights shift control from shorter- to longer-horizon heads as policy confidence increases, enabling adaptive horizon mixing without modifying individual Bellman updates. Across classical control and deep reinforcement-learning benchmarks, Q($γ_H$) shows its strongest improvements on delayed-credit tasks relative to strong fixed-discount baselines. By decoupling horizon adaptation from the underlying learning updates, Q($γ_H$) provides a lightweight framework for adaptive planning-horizon control in reinforcement learning, avoiding the need for manual discount-factor selection while remaining compatible with standard value-learning methods.

</details>


### [113] [N-Gram Memories at Two Bits: Quantizing Lookup Tables in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21810)
*Donghoon Han,Minseop Jung,Dong Woo Kwak,SeungJae Lee,Junhoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出条件程序（CP）表示，用共享重构程序为每个表行生成短码与局部尺度，实现按需重建，将嵌入表压缩至约1/4存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件记忆（如条件嵌入表）可扩展语言模型容量，但完整嵌入表必须对所有可能输入常驻可用，存储开销巨大，需要压缩表示。

Method: CP 为每行分配短码与局部尺度，并共享一个跨全表的重构程序；以高斯格（Gaussian trellis）、范数恢复与可独立解码的行记录实例化该表示。

Result: 两比特码相对2-bit round-to-nearest将输出KL降低41–63%；三个已训练的n-gram记忆族分析显示高斯型坐标统计与行间学习关系共存；同等存储预算下共享格优于拟合码本与低秩残差变体；DeepSeek上表存储由203GB降至51GB。

Conclusion: 共享重构程序+短码的CP表示在存储紧缩下保持输出质量，并为GPU常驻更大规模表留出空间。

Abstract: Conditional memory expands language-model capacity through selective lookup, but the full embedding table must remain available for every possible input. We introduce Conditional Program (CP), a compressed representation that reconstructs only the rows an input requests. CP represents each row by a short code and a local scale, with a reconstruction program shared across the table. We instantiate this representation with a Gaussian trellis, norm restoration and independently decodable row records. CP's two-bit code lowers output KL by 41--63\% relative to 2-bit round-to-nearest. Analysis across three trained n-gram memory families shows that Gaussian-like coordinate statistics coexist with learned relationships among rows; at matched storage budgets, the shared trellis outperforms the fitted codebooks and low-rank residual variants we test. For DeepSeek, it reduces table storage from 203GB to 51GB, leaving room for larger workloads when the table is kept on GPU.

</details>


### [114] [Measuring the local shape of learning-rate transfer curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21814)
*Satoki Ishikawa,Rio Yokota,Ryo Karakida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用两个关系式（Edge of Stability 下的理论公式、以及将 LR 曲线梯度视为超参数梯度的近似式）定量刻画学习率-损失曲线的梯度，给出其下界与归一化层下零梯度的条件，并可逐层分解 LR 梯度以量化特定层的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习率（LR）对训练后模型性能的影响至关重要，但最优 LR 依赖于学习动力学、架构及其隐含偏置，其复杂依赖关系尚不清楚。近期跨设置的 LR 迁移实验揭示了损失随 LR 变化的曲线，因此亟需对 LR 曲线形状做定量刻画的方法论。

Method: 给出两个经验上能很好解释 LR 迁移曲线梯度的关系式：一是 Edge of Stability 下成立的理论公式；二是将 LR 梯度朴素地视为超参数梯度得到的近似表达式。二者互补使用进行理论推导与实验分析。

Result: 理论上给出曲线梯度的下界，并证明归一化层在特定条件下梯度为零；近似表达式适用于广泛实验设置，可将 LR 梯度分解为逐层分量，量化特定层对曲线形状的强贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解 LR 依赖性的内部机制提供了定量基础与分析工具，有助于更好地刻画与迁移学习率。

Abstract: Understanding the learning-rate (LR) dependence of trained model performance is essential for better optimization of neural networks, but the appropriate size of the LR depends on learning dynamics, architecture, and their hidden biases, and thus our understanding of this complex dependence remains limited. The behavior of the loss curve as a function of the LR has recently been observed in extensive experiments on LR transfer across different settings, such as model sizes, and it is becoming more important to explore methodologies for quantitatively characterizing the shape of this LR curve. In this work, we provide two relational equations that give a good empirical explanation of the gradient of the LR transfer curve, i.e., the LR gradient. One is a theoretical formula that holds at the edge of stability, and the other is an approximate expression obtained by naively treating this gradient as a hyper-parameter gradient. Using these two relational equations in a complementary manner, we show a lower bound on the gradient of the curve and a zero gradient under specific conditions for normalization layers in theory. The approximate expression also applies to a wide range of experimental settings and allows us to decompose the LR gradient into layer-wise components and quantify the strong contribution of specific layers to the curve shape. Thus, this serves as a foundation for better understanding the internal mechanisms of LR dependence.

</details>


### [115] [A Closed Form Depth-Width Law for Neural Collapse in Deep Linear Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21820)
*Pankhil Gawade,Kirsten Fischer,Guy Wolf,Ewa Szczurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文在可解的特征学习设定中给出闭式解，将神经坍缩指标（NC1–NC3）直接表示为学到的特征核，并由此导出深度-宽度的表示级几何定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度与宽度如何塑造表示几何通常只在训练后经验性地观察，缺乏理论刻画；作者希望用可解析处理的特征学习框架，先把表示几何（以神经坍缩为探针）与特征核建立精确联系，从而在理论上预测结构依赖。

Method: 将 NC1–NC3 表达为学到的特征核的函数（把 NC1 的已知恒等式推广到 NC2 的角度部分，以及岭读出下的 NC3）；对具有可交换类别结构的深度线性网络，求解所得核方程，得到类内变异逐层演化的精确几何定律。

Result: 在大宽度下，累积压缩仅依赖深度-宽度比 L/N，其速率由数据、先验与架构自洽决定；数值实验验证了其随深度、宽度与信噪比的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 得到一条源自特征学习理论而非训练后拟合的表示级标度律，说明表示几何的深度/宽度依赖可被闭式预测。

Abstract: How depth and width of neural networks shape learned representations is usually studied only after training. We show that, in a tractable feature-learning setting, this dependence can instead be obtained in closed form. Using neural collapse as a probe of representation geometry, we express NC1--NC3 directly in terms of the learned feature kernel, extending an identity known for NC1 to the angular part of NC2 and to NC3 under a ridge readout. For deep linear networks with exchangeable class structure, the resulting kernel equations yield a geometric law, exact within the theory, for the layerwise evolution of within-class variability. At large width, the accumulated compression becomes a function of the depth-to-width ratio $L/N$, with its rate determined self-consistently by the data, prior and architecture. Numerical experiments support the derived dependence on depth, width and signal-to-noise ratio. The result is a representation-level scaling law derived from feature-learning theory rather than fitted after training.

</details>


### [116] [Local Epochs as Optimal Stopping: Theory and Evidence in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21832)
*Ke Xiao,Qiyuan Wang,Christos Anagnostopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将联邦学习的本地训练轮数建模为有限期最优停止问题，提出按“下一轮损失下降是否超过其定价的漂移平方”决定是否继续训练的阈值规则FedStop，并证明该一阶前瞻阈值在停止决策嵌套时最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习通常使用固定、人工调参的本地epoch数；但额外epoch只有在消除的损失大于其引入的客户端漂移时才有益，现有做法缺乏原则性的自适应本地训练预算。

Method: 把本地训练形式化为有限期最优停止：当下一epoch的期望损失下降超过按价格折算的平方漂移时继续训练；证明在停止决策嵌套条件下单步前瞻阈值最优，给出几何与幂律收益下的闭式解，并分析估计规则仅因错误符号决策而损失效用。基于epoch级证据（几何递减收益、加性漂移）发现“训练阶段而非客户端决定预算”的规律，据此提出FedStop。

Result: FedStop平均准确率至多比调优FedAvg低1个点；在预算变化处，最差十分位准确率比调优FedAvg和自适应间隔基线高4–8个点，且经Holm校正后仍显著；相对ALT与FLASH，在每个随机种子上CIFAR-10最差十分位最高，其余持平；在逐客户端早停失效的场景中保持稳定，每次停止决策为常数时间。

Conclusion: 以最优停止视角自适应分配本地训练预算既理论最优又可实用，FedStop在保持平均精度的同时显著改善最差十分位鲁棒性、决策开销低且优于现有客户端侧停止规则。

Abstract: How long should each client train before communicating? Federated learning usually answers with a fixed, hand-tuned number of local epochs, yet an extra epoch helps only while the loss it removes outweighs the client drift it adds. We cast local training as finite-horizon optimal stopping: a client trains while the next epoch's expected loss reduction exceeds its priced squared drift. We prove that this one-stage look-ahead threshold is optimal whenever stopping decisions are nested, derive closed forms for geometric and power-law returns, and show that an estimated rule loses utility only through wrong-signed decisions. Epoch-level evidence supports the model's structure (geometric diminishing returns and additive drift) and reveals a simple regularity: the stage of training, not the client, sets the budget. The resulting rule, FedStop, is never more than one point below tuned FedAvg in mean accuracy. Where its budgets vary, it raises worst-decile accuracy by 4-8 points over tuned FedAvg and an adaptive-interval baseline, and every one of these gains remains significant after a Holm correction. Against the client-side stopping rules ALT and FLASH, it has the highest CIFAR-10 worst decile on every seed and is level elsewhere. It stays stable where per-client early stopping collapses, and each stopping decision takes constant time.

</details>


### [117] [Physics-Aware Distillation of a Grid Foundation Model for Real-Time Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21833)
*Vlad Alexandru Sănduleac,Shahariya Rasheed Rayamarakkarveettil Puthanpurayil,Noman Khan,Remus Teodorescu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将20.1M参数、12层的GridFM教师模型蒸馏为0.62M参数、6层的学生模型（参数降低32.5×），在含可再生能源的IEEE 118节点场景上验证；蒸馏使有功调度误差最多降低48%，所有蒸馏学生在无功调度上均优于教师，但物理残差迁移项有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GridFM虽比迭代求解器快数个数量级，但其规模面向数据中心推理；低惯量电网运行要求在边缘侧、测量点旁、批大小为1、且在一个PMU上报帧内完成推理，因此需要大幅压缩模型。

Method: 采用多目标蒸馏：在掩码潮流损失基础上结合输出匹配、物理残差迁移、隐状态迁移和注意力迁移四项迁移项；在IEEE 118节点基准的可再生变体（26台同步机组被逆变器型新能源替代，配电池灵活性与IEEE 1547标准无功限值，共97,529个AC-OPF可行场景）上，对八种迁移项组合做消融，分别评估对真值的精度与对教师的保真度。

Result: 蒸馏使学生在全部四个预测量上误差均下降，有功调度最多降低48%；每个蒸馏学生在无功调度上均比教师更精确（增益主要由输出匹配项贡献）。层聚合形式的物理残差项因对母线级残差场做空间平均而持续产生负面影响。

Conclusion: 学生模型参数量缩小32.5×、消息传递深度减半，可进入后续边缘设备基准测试；物理残差迁移项需重新设计（避免空间平均）才能发挥作用。

Abstract: Grid foundation models (GridFMs) are graph transformers pretrained on a masked power-flow objective, and evaluate power flow orders of magnitude faster than an iterative solver. Their size, however, targets data-centre inference, whereas low-inertia operation increasingly asks for inference at the edge, next to the measurement, at batch size one and within a single synchrophasor (PMU) reporting frame. We distil a 20.1M-parameter, 12-layer GridFM teacher into a 0.62M-parameter, 6-layer student, a $32.5\times$ parameter reduction at half the message-passing depth, using an objective that combines the masked power-flow loss with output, physics-residual, latent-state and attention transfer. The physics-residual term, which matches the teacher's power-residual profile, has not previously been applied to typed power-grid graphs. We evaluate on a renewable variant of the IEEE 118-bus benchmark grid in which 26 of the conventional (synchronous) generators are replaced by inverter-based renewables with battery flexibility and standardised reactive-power limits (IEEE 1547), giving $97\,529$ physically valid (AC-OPF-feasible) operating scenarios, and we ablate all eight combinations of the transfer terms, reporting accuracy against ground truth and fidelity to the teacher separately. Distillation lowers student error on all four predicted quantities, by up to $48\%$ on active-power dispatch, and every distilled student is more accurate than its teacher on reactive-power dispatch. The gain is largely carried by the output matching term. The physics-residual term, in the layer-aggregated form used here, is consistently harmful, which we attribute to the spatial averaging it applies to a bus-level residual field. The resulting student is $32.5\times$ smaller in parameter count and has half the message-passing depth, making it a candidate for subsequent edge-device benchmarking.

</details>


### [118] [Lethe: Link Inference Attacks For Evaluation of Edge Unlearning Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21869)
*Andrea D'Angelo,Francesco Gullo,Davide Mottin,Sayan Ranu,Giovanni Stilo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Lethe：首个面向边遗忘（Edge Unlearning）的综合基准，含15种方法与8个数据集，强调大图、多难度评估任务及基于链接推断攻击的度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图遗忘研究多聚焦节点与特征删除；边遗忘因彻底消除某条边影响而困难、已受独立关注，但缺乏专门基准。已有评估多基于Cora、Citeseer等特征丰富数据集，GNN依赖节点特征而非结构，导致两类问题：专用边遗忘方法可能比重新训练更慢，且边删除影响极小使准确率指标无信息量。

Method: 提出Lethe基准：覆盖15种方法与8个数据集，侧重大规模图，设置不同难度的评估任务，并提出基于链接推断攻击（link inference attacks）的经验性度量，替代间接准确率指标。

Result: 系统化实证分析揭示了现有边遗忘评估的缺陷：专用方法效率可能不及从头重训，准确率指标无法反映遗忘效果。

Conclusion: Lethe以可复现、可扩展形式发布，为边遗忘提供标准化评估平台，弥补该方向基准空白。

Abstract: Graph Unlearning (GU) aims to remove the influence of specific training data from a Graph Neural Network without retraining anew. While most methods address removal of nodes and features, Edge Unlearning (EU), aiming at forgetting edges, has recently attracted dedicated attention as a standalone problem due to the hardness of completely removing the impact of an edge in the model. Despite this, no dedicated benchmark for EU exists besides isolated evaluations on feature-rich datasets such as Cora and Citeseer where GNNs exploit node features rather than graph structure. As such, we show two systematic limitations in existing evaluations: dedicated EU methods can be slower than retraining anew, and accuracy metrics are uninformative due to the negligible impact of edge removal. To address this gap, we introduce Lethe, the first comprehensive benchmark for Edge Unlearning, featuring 15 methods and 8 datasets. Our benchmark emphasizes large graphs, introduces evaluation tasks of varying difficulty, and proposes empirical measures based on link inference attacks rather than proxy accuracy metrics. We provide a comprehensive empirical analysis, demonstrate the shortcomings of current evaluations, and release Lethe as a reproducible, extensible benchmark.

</details>


### [119] [Provably Efficient Risk-Sensitive Offline Reinforcement Learning via Optimized Certainty Equivalents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21870)
*Hanzhen Wang,Boxiang Lyu,Zhongxiang Dai,Siyang Gao,Dake Zhang,Shuang Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究基于优化确定性等价（OCE）的离线风险敏感强化学习，针对动态OCE与静态OCE两种形式，在表格设定下提出悲观值迭代算法并给出紧的次线性次优界与极小极大下界，在函数逼近下设出悲观最小二乘值迭代算法并给出依赖效用函数与特征维度d的次线性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线风险敏感RL在交互代价高昂时很重要，但现有工作多关注在线设定或特定风险度量，缺乏对一般风险度量下离线风险敏感RL的理论理解，因此需要建立通用的理论框架与算法保证。

Method: 采用优化确定性等价（OCE）这一通用风险度量类，考虑两种形式：动态OCE（逐步递归应用OCE）与静态OCE（对累积回报应用一次OCE）。在表格设定下分别设计悲观值迭代算法；在大状态空间下将OCE风险准则嵌入线性函数逼近，设计悲观最小二乘值迭代算法。

Result: 表格设定下证明了两类算法的紧次线性次优界，显式刻画效用函数所编码的风险敏感性对学习难度的影响，并建立极小极大次优下界以刻画问题的根本极限；线性函数逼近下给出依赖效用且显式依赖特征维度d的次线性保证。

Conclusion: 本文给出了动态OCE与静态OCE离线风险敏感RL的首批算法及首套上下界理论结果，填补了通用风险度量下离线风险敏感RL理论空白。

Abstract: Risk-sensitive reinforcement learning (RL) addresses decision-making under uncertainty and adverse outcomes. Learning risk-sensitive policies from fixed datasets is important when additional interaction with the environment is costly. Existing work on risk-sensitive RL focuses on online settings or specific risk measures, leaving a general theoretical understanding of its offline setting underdeveloped. In this work, we investigate efficient offline risk-sensitive RL using the optimized certainty equivalent (OCE), a general class of risk measures. We consider two formulations: dynamic-OCE RL, which applies OCE recursively at each step, and static-OCE RL, which applies a single OCE to the cumulative rewards. Under the tabular setting, we develop pessimistic value-iteration algorithms for both formulations and establish tight sublinear suboptimality bounds, explicitly capturing how risk sensitivity, encoded by utility functions, affects learning difficulty. Moreover, we establish minimax suboptimality lower bounds to characterize the problem's fundamental limits. To handle large state spaces, we further develop pessimistic least-squares value-iteration algorithms that incorporate the general OCE risk criterion into linear function approximation. We establish utility-dependent sublinear guarantees with explicit dependence on the feature dimension $d$. To our knowledge, we provide the first offline algorithms along with the first upper and lower suboptimality bounds for both dynamic- and static-OCE RL.

</details>


### [120] [Learning Lean Feedback for Efficient Proof-Candidate Evaluation on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21881)
*Lazar Milikic,Etienne Bamas,Emmanuel Abbe,Viktor Kunčak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 训练神经网络评估器在GPU上预测Lean 4的判定结果与编译器反馈，实现比Lean基线快4.2倍的验证吞吐，并构建混合奖励系统缩短GRPO训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化定理证明的语言模型训练依赖大量验证器调用，而Lean检查缓慢且易超时，导致GPU空等并使潜在正确的证明无法及时获得奖励。

Method: 基于4.84M标注程序训练神经评估器，在GPU上预测Lean 4判定与编译器反馈；对比两种有效性分类器与一个反馈模型（为证明尝试标注预测的目标状态和错误信息）；提出两阶段评估器：先由分类器筛选候选，再用反馈模型对正例打分。

Result: 在68,932个程序基准上预测Lean是否接受候选证明的准确率达87.8%；单张H100上每秒处理3.5个候选，是64-worker Lean基线（含环境复用）吞吐的4.2倍；混合奖励将GRPO训练步缩短约31%，一个epoch后miniF2F pass@1为62.2%（Lean-only 62.4%），pass@32为72.4%（对比71.3%）。

Conclusion: 神经评估器可在保持证明性能基本不变的前提下显著提升验证吞吐并加速强化学习训练，可替代或补充Lean验证用于奖励塑形。

Abstract: Training language models for formal theorem proving requires large numbers of verifier calls. Slow checks and timeouts often leave GPUs waiting and withhold rewards from potentially correct proofs. We train neural evaluators to predict Lean 4 verdicts and compiler feedback on GPUs using 4.84M labelled programs. We compare two validity classifiers with a feedback model that annotates proof attempts with predicted goal states and error messages. A two-stage evaluator that screens candidates with a classifier and scores its positive predictions with the feedback model reaches 87.8% accuracy in predicting whether Lean accepts a candidate proof, evaluated on a 68,932-program benchmark collected from public provers. The combined evaluator processes 3.5 candidates per second on one H100, 4.2x the throughput of our 64-worker Lean baseline with environment reuse. Finally, we combine our evaluator with Lean verdicts to obtain a hybrid reward system that shortens GRPO training steps by approximately 31%. After one GRPO epoch with hybrid rewards, the prover achieves nearly the same miniF2F pass@1 as with Lean-only rewards (62.2% versus 62.4%) and a pass@32 of 72.4% versus 71.3%.

</details>


### [121] [Graph Dirichlet Energy for Fitting-Free Bivariate Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21892)
*Povilas Daniušis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GDECI（图狄利克雷能量因果推断）准则，以核相似图上的狄利克雷能量区分双变量因果方向，无需回归拟合与独立性检验，具备单调变换不变性，时间复杂度O(n log n)、空间O(n)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双变量因果发现方法（如RECI、IGCI、RESIT等）通常依赖回归拟合或独立性检验，计算代价高且对变量单调变换敏感，需要一个更简洁、快速且具不变性的判别准则。

Method: 对候选方向X→Y，由X构造核相似图，将Y视为图信号，以非归一化拉普拉斯算子诱导的狄利克雷能量作为得分；交换变量得反向得分，取能量较低方向为因果方向。经经验概率积分变换后决策对任一变量的严格单调变换不变；图运算化为卷积，实现O(n log n)时间与O(n)内存的精确计算。总体层面，GDECI比较度加权贝叶斯平方预测误差加回归函数的非负光滑项；带宽趋零时权重均匀、光滑项消失，退化为copula空间中期望条件方差的RECI型比较。

Result: 在1899个因果对及推理速度上与QCCD、GRCI、CAM、IGCI（两变体）、RESIT、LOCI、QPE-k、RECI（两变体）共11种方法比较：12个合成数据集的宏平均准确率排名第五（0.776）；真实Tuebingen数据上未加权准确率第三（0.684）、加权准确率第五（0.678）；在n≲10^4范围内为所有对比方法中最快。

Conclusion: GDECI以图信号能量为准则，在保持理论可解释性（与RECI的极限关系）和变换不变性的同时实现高效计算，整体准确率处于中上水平但速度为最优，是双变量因果方向判别的实用且可扩展方案。

Abstract: We propose the Graph Dirichlet Energy Causal Inference (GDECI) criterion for distinguishing cause from effect in bivariate settings. For a candidate direction $X\to Y$, we construct a kernel similarity graph from $X$ and treat $Y$ as a graph signal. The score is the Dirichlet energy of this signal, induced by the unnormalized graph Laplacian. The reverse score swaps the variables, and the lower-energy direction is inferred as causal. The method requires neither regression fitting nor independence testing. After the empirical probability integral transform, its decisions are invariant under strictly monotone transformations of either variable, and the graph operations reduce to convolutions, allowing exact evaluation in $O(n\log n)$ time and $O(n)$ memory. At the population level, GDECI compares degree-weighted Bayes squared prediction errors plus a non-negative smoothness term of the regression function. As the bandwidth tends to zero, the weights become uniform and the smoothness term vanishes, reducing the criterion to a RECI-type comparison of expected conditional variances in copula space. Empirically, we compare GDECI with QCCD, GRCI, CAM, IGCI (two variants), RESIT, LOCI, QPE-k, and RECI (two variants), in accuracy on 1899 cause--effect pairs and in inference speed. GDECI ranked fifth of eleven methods in macro-averaged accuracy on 12 synthetic suites ($0.776$). On the real-world Tuebingen pairs, it ranked third in unweighted accuracy ($0.684$) and fifth in weighted accuracy ($0.678$). It was also the fastest of the compared methods up to $n\approx10^{4}$. The accompanying code repository: https://github.com/povidanius/gdeci.

</details>


### [122] [Self-Calibrated Subspace Robust PCA for Parameter-Free Removal of Mixed Noise and Line Stripes in Hyperspectral Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21896)
*Wenzhe Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自校准RCCTV（SC-RCCTV）模型，迭代中自适应估计各波段噪声参数，无需预先指定噪声水平与条纹预算，ADMM各子问题均有闭式解，去噪性能与参数无关且显著优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像常受强度随波段变化的高斯噪声、脉冲噪声与条纹污染；RCCTV模型虽高效但缺少高斯项与条纹项，其噪声感知版本需预先给定噪声水平和条纹预算，依赖人工调参。

Method: 在RCCTV中引入按波段的稠密噪声项与行/列线常值条纹项；各波段噪声水平由条纹不变的中位数绝对偏差（MAD）估计器初始化，并基于残差在线精化，使稀疏与稠密噪声分离转化为波段自适应Huber损失；条纹阈值由显著性检验确定且虚警率可解析预测；采用ADMM求解，所有子问题闭式求解。

Result: 线状条纹下，原生尺寸立方体上比无参数FastHyMix高8.17 dB；在无任何噪声信息时，比给定真实噪声参数的S3TTV高1.52 dB且快1900倍以上；平均而言与给定真实噪声水平的同模型差距在0.01 dB以内；在线校准优于两阶段“先估计后去噪”方案；真实Indian Pines与Urban数据实验界定其对非整行条纹的适用范围。

Conclusion: SC-RCCTV实现了无需图像特定调参的自适应高光谱去噪，在线噪声校准有效且计算高效，对线状条纹尤为有效；对不贯穿整行的条纹适用性有限。

Abstract: Hyperspectral images (HSIs) are often corrupted by Gaussian noise, impulses and stripes whose intensities vary across bands. The representative coefficient correlated total variation (RCCTV) model restores HSIs efficiently, but it lacks Gaussian and stripe terms, and its noise-aware extensions require the noise level and a stripe budget to be specified in advance. This article proposes a self-calibrated RCCTV (SC-RCCTV) model, in which the noise parameters are estimated adaptively during the iterations. A band-wise dense term and line-constant column and row stripe terms are introduced into RCCTV. The noise level of each band is initialized by a stripe-invariant median absolute deviation estimator and refined online from the residual, which turns the separation of sparse and dense noise into a band-adaptive Huber loss, and the stripe thresholds follow from a significance test whose false-alarm rate can be predicted analytically. No parameter is tuned for a specific image, and every subproblem of the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) solver is solved in closed form. Under line stripes, SC-RCCTV outperforms the parameter-free FastHyMix by 8.17 dB on native-size cubes, and, without any noise information, it exceeds S3TTV, which is given the true noise parameters, by 1.52 dB while running more than 1,900 times faster. On average, it stays within 0.01 dB of the same model given the true noise levels, and its online calibration improves on a two-stage estimate-then-denoise scheme. Experiments on real Indian Pines and Urban data further delineate its scope for stripes that do not span entire lines.

</details>


### [123] [In-Context Learning Before Specialisation: Theory of Learning in a Solvable Topic Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21901)
*Francesco Cagnetta,Casper L. Christensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在一个可解的主题模型（有限主题混合生成的文档）中，为序列模型的“专门化预测器”与“上下文学习预测器”之争建立了学习理论，预测二者在不同样本复杂度上依次涌现，并被Transformer实验定量验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列模型存在两种本质不同的泛化方式：外推训练中见过的结构（专门化），或利用提示适应新任务（上下文学习）。已有工作分别从理论上刻画策略、从经验上描述随预训练数据质量与数量变化的策略转变，但缺乏关于“为何某种解会被偏好”的学习理论，因而缺少根本理解。

Method: 构建一个可解的主题模型：文档来自有限潜在主题的混合，以模拟现代预训练数据的异质性。该数据分布自带两个不同解——存储有限主题混合的专门化预测器，以及对训练中从未出现的主题最优的上下文预测器。在该设定下分析两种解的样本复杂度与涌现阈值。

Result: 理论预测二者在显著不同的样本复杂度上涌现：上下文预测器较早可用，而专门化预测器仅在训练token数超过数据生成过程的参数量之后才出现。训练的Transformer在无任何拟合参数的情况下复现了这两个尺度及二者之间的分离交叉点，与理论高度一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为预训练中不同泛化策略的竞争提供了首个学习理论基础，解释了策略偏好的来源，并将理论预测的样本复杂度阈值与Transformer的实际涌现行为定量对应。

Abstract: Sequence models can generalise in fundamentally distinct ways: extrapolating structures seen in training, or using their prompt to adapt to novel tasks. The prevailing strategy determines how the model behaves beyond its training distribution, controlling potential capabilities and risks. Recent work has characterised many different strategies theoretically, and empirically described the transition between them as the quality and quantity of pretraining data vary. However, there is no learning theory for this competition, hence no fundamental understanding of \textit{why} one solution might be favoured over another. Here we provide such a theory in a solvable topic model---documents drawn from a finite mixture of latent topics, mirroring the heterogeneity of modern pretraining data. The dataset comes with two distinct solutions: a specialised predictor, that stores the finite topic mixture, and an in-context one, optimal for topics never seen during training. Our theory predicts their emergence at distinct sample complexities: the in-context predictor is available early, while the specialised one emerges only after the training tokens outnumber the parameters of the data-generating process. Trained transformers reproduce both scales and the separating crossover in remarkable agreement with the theory, with no fitted parameters.

</details>


### [124] [The Tilted Sampling Problem: Optimism, Posterior Sampling, and Logarithmic Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21908)
*Mohammad Pedramfar,Siamak Ravanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"倾斜采样"问题——从由未知奖励函数诱导的Gibbs分布中采样，定义采样遗憾为训练者分布与Gibbs目标之间的温度归一化累积KL散度，证明采样遗憾仅呈对数级增长并给出统一分解框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代学习系统常需采样多样化的高质量输出（如熵正则化RL、RLHF、奖励引导生成），而非仅选择单一最优动作；而已有的基于采样的bandit与KL正则化遗憾框架无法刻画"软最优分布采样"特有的不确定性结构，需要专门的理论框架。

Method: 提出倾斜采样的在线学习问题与采样遗憾（温度归一化累积KL散度）；建立通用遗憾框架，刻画采样遗憾由置信宽度平方而非一阶不确定性驱动；设计UCB与Thompson采样的采样版本（UCS与TS²），并给出统一遗憾分解，含首个基于后验随机Gibbs分布新界的贝叶斯采样遗憾分解。

Result: 标准遗憾为Õ(√T)，而固定温度下采样遗憾受平方置信宽度支配，呈现对数级T遗憾；统一分解解释并扩展了近期若干对数保证结果。

Conclusion: 从软最优分布中学习采样在统计上与识别硬最优有本质区别；采样目标的困难度显著低于经典遗憾最小化，对数级遗憾可实现。

Abstract: Many modern learning systems are used not to select a single best action, but to sample diverse high-quality outputs. Examples include entropy-regularized reinforcement learning, RLHF, and reward-guided generative modeling. Motivated by these settings, we study tilted sampling: an online learning problem in which the goal is to sample from the Gibbs distribution induced by an unknown reward function. We define sampling regret as the temperature-normalized cumulative KL divergence between the learner's distribution and the Gibbs target, corresponding to the suboptimality gap in entropy-regularized reward maximization. Our framework generalizes recent sampling-based and KL-regularized formulations in bandits and reinforcement learning. Our main contribution is a general regret framework that captures the different uncertainty structure of tilted sampling and classical regret minimization. While standard regret scales as $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ due to first-order uncertainty, sampling regret is governed by squared confidence widths, yielding logarithmic-in-$T$ regret in standard settings at fixed temperature. We develop sampling analogues of UCB and Thompson sampling (UCS and TS$^2$) and provide unified regret decompositions that explain and extend recent logarithmic guarantees, including the first Bayesian sampling-regret decomposition based on new bounds for posterior-random Gibbs distributions. Our results show that learning to sample from a soft-optimal distribution is statistically distinct from learning to identify a hard optimum.

</details>


### [125] [The Lagrangian Structure of Cost-Penalized Active Feature Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21919)
*Joseph Bingham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为主动特征获取(AFA)中的贪心启发式 EIG-Cost 建立事后理论证书：将硬预算最优策略的差距分解为预算松弛项与贪心次优项 Del_lam，并在小规模异构成本实例上精确验证 Del_lam 近似为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EIG(j|x_S)-lam*c_j 这一"期望信息增益减成本惩罚"的贪心策略虽自然，但其相对预算约束最优策略的理论保证不清晰，作者希望给出可计算或可诊断的误差界定。

Method: 事后证书分解（预算松弛项 + Del_lam）；证明 EIG-Cost 是期望成本 AFA 拉格朗日松弛的一步贪心优化、lam 为对偶乘子；等成本下证明 Del_lam <= exp(-gam*I(Y;X_[d]))，gam 为子模比率；建立自适应链式法则恒等式；反例证明 A→I(Y;X_A) 一般非子模（即使联合高斯），朴素贝叶斯可恢复子模性；另给出随机化间隙界、对偶乘子误差敏感性分析及条件 CVAE 生成器偏移界（以前向预测 KL 为诊断量）。

Result: 4,000 个已解的小规模异构成本实例中 Del_lam 近似为零；等成本近子模情形获得结构性保证；证明非子模性排除直接 N-W-F 界；MIMIC-IV 上 EIG-Cost 在 $30 预算下取得 macro-F1 0.180±0.012，仅用全面板成本的 6.6%。

Conclusion: 该分解提供了诊断性框架，在大规模临床任务上 Del_lam 不可计算，故证书为诊断而非数值保证；结果界定了 EIG-Cost 的适用条件与局限，并给出生成模型偏移与对偶误差的理论刻画。

Abstract: Active Feature Acquisition (AFA) sequentially selects features to maximize predictive accuracy under an acquisition budget. A natural heuristic greedily maximizes Expected Information Gain minus a cost penalty, EIG(j|x_S)-lam*c_j (EIG-Cost), but its theoretical guarantees are unclear. We provide an a-posteriori certificate decomposing the gap to the optimal hard-budget policy into a budget-slack term and a greedy-suboptimality term Del_lam. The certificate is computable when the penalized optimum is tractable; on small heterogeneous-cost instances, we verify it exactly, finding Del_lam near zero across 4,000 solved cases. On the large clinical task, Del_lam is not computable, so the decomposition is diagnostic rather than a numerical guarantee. Our analysis identifies EIG-Cost as one-step greedy optimization of the Lagrangian relaxation of expected-cost AFA, with lam as its dual multiplier. For equal costs, we prove Del_lam <= exp(-gam*I(Y;X_[d])), where gam is the submodularity ratio, yielding a structural guarantee in the near-submodular regime. Supporting results establish an adaptive chain-rule identity showing that cumulative stepwise EIG optimizes transcript information, and prove that A -> I(Y;X_A) need not be submodular, even for jointly Gaussian variables. This rules out a direct Nemhauser-Wolsey-Fisher bound; naive Bayes restores submodularity. We also bound the randomization gap between hard-budget and expected-cost optima, analyze sensitivity to dual-multiplier error under local smoothness, and derive a conditional CVAE generator-shift bound given a bound on forward predictive KL. We treat this KL as a diagnostic quantity, not as a transform of training loss. Experiments cover synthetic Gaussian settings, AFA benchmarks, and clinical differential diagnosis on MIMIC-IV. On MIMIC-IV, EIG-Cost achieves macro-F1 0.180 +/- 0.012 at a $30 budget, using 6.6% of full-panel cost.

</details>


### [126] [ChronoSheaf: Joint Spatio-Temporal Spectral Filtering on Cellular Sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21958)
*Abeer Mostafa,Zahra Ahmadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有sheaf网络将sheaf Laplacian定义为瞬时空间算子，无法建模信号在时间上的演化；本文提出时空联合的spectral sheaf Laplacian及ChronoSheaf网络，通过TRM与DSF在不扩大sheaf结构的前提下扩展时空感受野，在时空预测基准上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sheaf神经网络通过为每条边学习线性限制映射来刻画相邻节点局部表示关系，从而缓解过平滑。但现有sheaf架构将sheaf Laplacian视为瞬时空间算子，只做空间聚合，无法显式建模图信号随时间演化，难以直接用于时空图问题。

Method: 1) 提出在空间与时间上联合定义的spectral sheaf Laplacian，用单一算子同时调控空间图与时间域的信号传播；2) 不通过扩大sheaf结构来扩展感受野，而设计spectral filter在时空两维共同扩展有效感受野；3) 基于该算子构建ChronoSheaf，含两个组件：Transitional Restriction Maps (TRM) 将每个节点的限制映射分解为空间项与两个时间帧，分别控制其与前一/后一时刻邻居的关系，实现非对称时间影响；Dual Spectral Filter (DSF) 对联合时空sheaf算子施加单一有界阶多项式滤波器，谱半径自适应估计。

Result: 在多种时空预测基准上验证，ChronoSheaf优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 将sheaf Laplacian从瞬时空间算子推广为时空联合谱算子，并配合TRM与DSF，可在不增加sheaf复杂度的情况下获得时空感受野，为时空图学习提供了新的sheaf建模范式。

Abstract: Sheaf neural networks generalize graph neural networks by assigning each edge a learned linear restriction map that specifies how the local representations of adjacent nodes relate to one another, thereby mitigating oversmoothing. Existing sheaf architectures, however, define the sheaf Laplacian as an instantaneous spatial operator and therefore cannot explicitly model how signals evolve over time on a graph. We first introduce a spectral sheaf Laplacian, defined jointly over space and time, such that a single operator regulates signal propagation across both the spatial graph and the temporal domain. Directly extending this operator's receptive field in space or time would require increasingly complex sheaf structures. Instead, we propose a spectral filter that expands the effective receptive field along both dimensions without enlarging the underlying sheaf. Building on this operator, we introduce ChronoSheaf, a spatio-temporal sheaf network with two key components. First, Transitional Restriction Maps (TRM) decompose each node's restriction map into a spatial term and two temporal frames that independently control its relation to neighboring nodes at the preceding and following timestep, enabling asymmetric temporal influence. Second, the Dual Spectral Filter (DSF) applies a single bounded-degree polynomial filter with an adaptively estimated spectral radius to the joint spatio-temporal sheaf operator. We validate ChronoSheaf on a diverse set of spatio-temporal forecasting benchmarks, where it outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. The code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ChronoSheaf-1D7D/.

</details>


### [127] [Why Shallow Neural Networks Learn Sinusoidal Components First: An Orthogonal Polynomial Dynamics Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21964)
*Yanyang Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从理论上解释神经网络谱偏置（F-principle）：用广义傅里叶级数（GFS）在正交多项式基下展开网络输出，将梯度下降动力学写为ODE，通过分析动力学矩阵H的特征值衰减与特征向量形状，给出谱偏置的渐近解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谱偏置理论存在五点不足：(1)缺乏渐近解释及其来源、偏好成分为正弦型的成因；(2)难以推广到sigmoid、ReLU等一般激活函数；(3)对数据分布假设过强；(4)有限域傅里叶分析带来混叠效应；(5)缺乏适用于任意网络的统一框架。论文旨在同时解决这五点。

Method: 将网络输出在正交多项式基下展开为广义傅里叶级数，把GFS系数上的梯度下降动力学表述为常微分方程组，得到动力学矩阵H；分析H的特征值衰减速率与特征向量形状，并借助正交多项式理论刻画学习偏置对激活函数及网络结构的依赖。

Result: H呈现分级的块结构，其分级结构可直观解释谱偏置；特征值衰减给出谱偏置的渐近刻画；正交多项式理论支持对激活函数（如sigmoid、ReLU）及潜在网络架构的分析，且对数据分布假设较弱、避免有限域混叠。

Conclusion: 以GFS加正交多项式为基础的统一理论框架，可从动力矩阵H的特征结构出发渐近地解释谱偏置，并具备推广到一般激活函数与任意网络结构的潜力。

Abstract: Neural networks often learn low-frequency components before high-frequency ones --- a phenomenon called spectral bias or termed F-principle. Many numerical studies have demonstrated this phenomenon, and theoretical studies have sought to explain it. However, several goals remain insufficiently addressed: (1) explaining spectral bias asymptotically, including its origin and why the preferred components are generally sinusoidal; (2) extending the explanation to general activation functions, such as sigmoid and ReLU; (3) making weak assumptions about the data distribution; (4) avoiding aliasing effects, such as those arising in finite-domain Fourier analysis; and (5) developing a theoretical framework applicable to arbitrary networks. We address these goals theoretically by analyzing the eigenvalue decay and eigenvector shapes of the dynamics matrix H, which arises when we expand the network output as a general Fourier series (GFS) in an orthogonal polynomial basis and express the gradient-descent dynamics of the GFS coefficients as ordinary differential equations. The graded structure of H enables an intuitive explanation of spectral bias, while orthogonal polynomial theory helps us analyze how learning bias depends on the activation function and, potentially, the network architecture.

</details>


### [128] [Time to Fake It: Continual Machine Learning Intensifies Unfairness in Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21974)
*Kajaree Das,Oliver Böse,Subarnaduti Paul,Gabriel Storch,Martin Mundt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在11种真实深度伪造生成方法构成的时间序列上渐进训练检测器，系统评估持续学习（CL）方法对公平性的动态影响，揭示性能退化通常加剧不公平、公平性改善稀缺，并指出不公平遗忘不能仅由代表性不足解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造检测器的失效兼具社会与技术风险；生成器不断演化导致静态数据集训练的检测器遗漏新威胁，而渐进训练会灾难性干扰。既有CL研究只关注准确率意义上的遗忘，不足以反映真实世界影响，因此需从随时间演化的公平性视角审视CL方法。

Method: 按11种真实深度伪造生成方法的时间顺序渐进训练检测器；在不同人口群体上沿四个公平性维度跟踪指标随时间的变化；对比剖析rehearsal、prompt、regularization三类CL方法族；并分析不公平遗忘与代表性不足及表征学习之间的关系。

Result: 性能与公平性之间存在非平凡的相互作用，且依赖具体CL算法与人口交叉属性；准确率下降通常加剧不公平性，而公平性改善随时间较为罕见；不公平遗忘无法仅用训练数据代表性不足解释，可能与表征学习纠缠。

Conclusion: 呼吁CL方法在权衡稳定性—可塑性时纳入社会结果维度，将公平性作为持续学习深度伪造检测的核心评价指标。

Abstract: Deepfake detectors safeguard against misinformation, rendering their failures as much a societal as a technical concern. As generators constantly evolve, detectors trained on static datasets miss newly arising threats, while progressive training interferes catastrophically. Yet, mitigating forgetfulness in terms of accuracy, the prevalent focus of continual learning (CL), is insufficient to reflect real-world impact. We therefore scrutinize the effects of CL methods with respect to evolving fairness. To this end, we analyze four dimensions of fairness across diverse demographics for detectors trained progressively on a chronological sequence of 11 real-world deepfake generation methods. Tracking fairness metrics over time, we expose non-trivial interplays between performance and fairness that depend on both the choice of CL algorithm and demographic intersections. We find accuracy degradation to typically exacerbate unfairness and, conversely, fairness improvements to remain scarce over time. Dissecting the CL method families (rehearsal, prompts, regularization), we observe that inequitable forgetting is not explained by underrepresentation alone, but likely entangled with representation learning. We thus issue a call to action for CL methods to take social outcomes into account when balancing the stability-plasticity tradeoff.

</details>


### [129] [Learn Only Where You Can't Compute: Structured Corrections to Gaussian Process Posteriors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21977)
*Tobias Weber,Carla Sagebiel,Philipp Hennig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构化后验修正框架：以可解的高斯过程为参考分布，结合均值修正、协方差校准，并用扩散模型在低维子空间内学习针对性修正，其余方向保留高斯律，从而在小数据科学推断任务中兼顾物理约束与生成建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息高斯过程能以概率方式融合观测、边界条件与支配方程，但难以扩展到大规模数据和强非线性信息；扩散模型擅长大数据下的复杂函数分布学习，但在训练数据稀缺、需融入物理或观测信息的科学场景中表现受限。作者希望结合两者优势。

Method: 构建结构化后验修正框架：先以可处理的高斯参考分布为基础，进行均值修正与协方差校准；再选取能刻画与参考分布偏差的低维子空间，在该子空间内用扩散模型学习目标修正，而正交补空间维持高斯分布。

Result: 在三个科学推断任务上，修正能够恢复被高斯参考掩盖的隐藏模态、改善样本结构，并相对高斯参考降低预测误差；与全域生成模型相比，效果呈任务依赖性，在预测精度与计算成本之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: 将可解高斯参考与生成式后验修正结合，可在数据稀缺的科学推断中有效提升表达能力，同时保持结构化与计算可控性；是否为最优选择取决于具体任务对精度与成本的偏好。

Abstract: Physics-informed Gaussian processes (GPs) provide a principled framework for scientific inference by expressing uncertainty over empirical measurements, boundary conditions, and governing equations. However, they struggle to scale to larger datasets and nonlinear information. Conversely, diffusion models excel at learning complex distributions over functions from massive datasets, but struggle in scientific contexts where training data are scarce and physical or observational information must be incorporated. We propose a structured posterior-correction framework that combines a tractable Gaussian reference with data-driven corrections via generative learning. After a mean correction and covariance calibration, a diffusion model learns a targeted correction in a low-dimensional subspace selected to capture discrepancies from the reference, while the complement retains its Gaussian law. Across three scientific inference tasks, these corrections recover hidden modes, improve sample structure, and reduce prediction error relative to the Gaussian reference. Comparisons with full-field generative models reveal task-dependent benefits and tradeoffs between predictive accuracy and computational cost.

</details>


### [130] [P2TPO: Structure-Aligned Page-to-Token Credit Assignment for Full-Page Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21979)
*Yong Zu,Ninghua Yang,Zezhong Tan,Xinhong Ma,Ziqiang Dong,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Page-to-Token Policy Optimization (P2TPO)，一种面向整页文档解析轨迹的结构对齐信用分配框架，在六个基准上优于所有对比的策略优化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整页端到端文档解析需同时保留异构元素的内容与结构，但即使整体指标较高的输出也存在局部错误与遗漏；仅靠页面级优势无法识别哪些元素质量不同、错误发生在何处，因此需要更细粒度的信用分配。

Method: P2TPO 含三个组件：(1) 参考元素对齐，将块级质量对比关联到生成片段；(2) Unified Anchor Pricing (UAP)，纳入匹配与缺失元素结果，并在同一参考元素下各采样输出得分相同但不完美时，以固定质量上限为参照提供反馈；(3) Span Credit Redistribution (SCR)，在匹配块内依据对齐的正确/错误片段重分配块内信用，以边界代理处理块内遗漏，同时保持块级优势在 token 上的总量不变。理论上在固定权重加性页面效用下，证明元素索引对比可保留页面级聚合会相互抵消的局部质量差异。

Result: 主实验中，P2TPO 在六个文档解析基准的种子平均得分上均优于所有评估的策略优化基线；补充实验显示其在不同模型规模与初始化下均有增益。

Conclusion: 细粒度的元素-片段级信用分配比页面级奖励更有效，结构对齐的对比信号可显著提升整页文档解析的策略优化效果，并具备跨规模与初始化的泛化性。

Abstract: End-to-end full-page document parsing must preserve the content and structure of heterogeneous elements, yet otherwise accurate outputs can contain local errors and omissions. A single page-level advantage does not explicitly identify which elements differ in quality across sampled outputs or where their errors occur. We introduce Page-to-Token Policy Optimization (P2TPO), a structure-aligned credit-assignment framework that operates on full-page policy trajectories. Reference-element alignment first associates block-level quality contrasts with generated spans. Unified Anchor Pricing (UAP) incorporates matched and missing element outcomes and provides feedback relative to a fixed quality ceiling when sampled outputs receive identical but imperfect scores for a reference element. Within matched blocks, Span Credit Redistribution (SCR) redistributes existing block credit according to aligned correct and erroneous spans, using boundary proxies for within-block omissions while preserving the total block-derived advantage across its tokens. Under a fixed-weight additive page utility, we show that element-indexed contrasts retain local quality differences that page-level aggregation can cancel out. In the main experimental comparison, P2TPO outperforms all evaluated policy optimization baselines in seed-averaged scores on each of the six document parsing benchmarks. Additional experiments demonstrate gains across model scales and initializations.

</details>


### [131] [Interfaces and Modules: A Scalable Representation for Weight-Space Learning across Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21995)
*Michał Kozyra,Michael Bronstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出以"接口"节点和"模块"超边构成的类型化图表示权重空间，编辑器自动从PyTorch模型导出，配合通用权重编码器UWE实现跨架构、可扩展的等变权重学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨架构的权重空间学习需要尊重参数对称性且可扩展到大网络。现有等变张量编码器依赖架构特定对称性规范，而神经元图做全局注意力代价高昂。

Method: 构建围绕共享坐标置换动作的类型化图：节点（接口）表示必须联合置换所绑定张量轴的坐标空间，操作实例（模块）构成类型化超边；编译器从受支持的PyTorch模型导出该图，并精确恢复由约束定义的置换因子。UWE结合对参数特征的局部等变更新与对不变接口摘要的全局注意力。

Result: 在既有基准上与架构特定的SOTA方法相当；在含100种架构、1200个transformer的新数据集上，在未见架构内（含大于训练规模的架构）的网络排序任务上优于所评估基线，且内存占用显著更低。

Conclusion: 以接口为中心的稀疏类型化图消除了架构特定对称性规范，使单一编码器可处理多种架构，并通过缩减全局注意力的节点规模实现高效、可泛化的权重空间表示。

Abstract: Weight-space learning across architectures requires representations that respect parameter symmetries and scale to large networks. Existing equivariant tensor encoders require architecture-specific symmetry specifications, while neuron graphs make global attention costly. We introduce a typed graph organized around shared coordinate-permutation actions. Its nodes, called interfaces, represent coordinate spaces whose bound tensor axes must be permuted jointly; operation occurrences, called modules, form typed hyperedges. A compiler derives this graph from supported PyTorch models, with exact recovery of the permutation factors defined by its constraints. The Universal Weight Encoder (UWE) combines local equivariant updates to parameter features with global attention over invariant interface summaries. The small number of interfaces makes global attention practical, and a single encoder processes different supported architectures. UWE is competitive with architecture-specific state-of-the-art methods on established benchmarks. On a new zoo of 1,200 transformers spanning 100 architectures, it outperforms the evaluated baselines at ranking networks within unseen architectures, including architectures larger than those used for training, while using substantially less memory.

</details>


### [132] [GraphBEAT: Graph-based Bucketized Event Aggregation Over Time for Early Risk Stratification of Gastrointestinal Cancers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22014)
*Leo Y. Li-Han,Chris Varghese,Elizabeth B. Habermann,Mark J. Truty,William R. G. Perry,Cornelius A. Thiels,Hojjat Salehinejad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GraphBEAT框架，将纵向EHR事件按时间桶聚合并构建患者特异性时序有向图，实现胃肠道癌症早期风险分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向EHR同时包含诊断与检验等互补信息，但存在记录不规则、稀疏、异质与不完整等问题，给建模带来困难，需要一种能有效刻画时序结构且计算高效的方法。

Method: GraphBEAT（Graph-based Bucketized Event Aggregation over Time）：将临床事件分组到时间桶，把观测特征转化为患者特异性的时序有向图；每个节点编码特征身份、观测值和时间位置；采用上下文感知、时间衰减的消息传递机制建模相邻时间桶间的状态转移，并用多头注意力池化生成患者级表示。

Result: 在多中心纵向诊断编码与实验室检验数据上，针对胰腺癌、肝癌、肠癌的12、24、36个月诊断提前期进行训练与评估；所有实验设置下均优于GraphSAGE、GAT、GCN以及Transformer、LSTM；相比Transformer和LSTM，每患者浮点运算量减少250倍以上，参数量减少20倍以上。

Conclusion: GraphBEAT在稀疏、异质的纵向健康记录上兼顾准确性与计算效率，是癌症风险分层的有效方案。

Abstract: Longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs) contain complementary diagnostic and laboratory information about patient health. However, irregularity, sparsity, heterogeneity, and incompleteness in documentation complicate EHR modeling. To address the problem, we introduce GraphBEAT (Graph-based Bucketized Event Aggregation over Time), a temporal graph representation learning framework that groups clinical events into time buckets and transforms observed features into patient-specific, temporally directed graphs to enable early risk stratification for gastrointestinal cancers. Each node in an EHR graph encodes feature identity, observed value, and temporal position of clinical events. A context-aware, time-decay message-passing mechanism models transitions between successive buckets, followed by multi-head attention pooling to generate patient-level representations. GraphBEAT was trained and evaluated on multi-site longitudinal diagnosis codes and laboratory tests to predict pancreatic, liver, and intestinal cancer at 12-, 24-, and 36-month diagnostic lead times. In all experimental setups, GraphBEAT consistently outperformed baseline graph neural networks (GraphSAGE, GAT, and GCN) and sequence modeling approaches (Transformer and long short-term memory [LSTM]). GraphBEAT also required over 250x fewer floating-point operations per patient and over 20x fewer parameters than the Transformer and LSTM baselines. These results support GraphBEAT as an accurate and computationally efficient approach for cancer risk stratification from sparse, heterogeneous longitudinal health records.

</details>


### [133] [Close Enough is Nowhere Near: Fidelity-Consumability Decoupling in KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22016)
*Adam Deng,Shicheng Liu,Yada Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 冻结模型下直接替换读者的KV cache不可行，障碍在读者侧的“可容忍集”；有效方向是保留读者自身cache并把发送者cache作为额外上下文、仅适配query。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统以文本通信有损且浪费——发送方丢弃了自己已完成的“阅读”。KV cache是该阅读的自然载体，因此“双方冻结、不训练、直接把发送方cache替换给读者”的想法最具吸引力，值得检验其可行性。

Method: 提出“可容忍集”（tolerable set）概念：冻结的transformer只能读取与其本应写入的cache相近的cache。用竞争性注意力（competitive attention）解释该现象，并证明几何相似度不能预测读者能否使用该cache、且失败是突变的而非渐进的。实验上以无信息cache基线和读者自行阅读原文的准确率为两个锚点，测试八种不同的替换方法。

Result: 八种替换方法全部停留在无信息cache基线水平；更“忠实”的替代并非补救。相反，若读者保留自身cache并将发送者cache作为额外上下文做注意力、仅适配query，则可超越自身基线；八发送者汇总时同样超过基线，而无定向注意力在同一并集上低于基线。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是方向修正：追求忠实的替代cache是错误的靶标，把cache“加”到读者已有内容上才是有前景的路径。结论基于单次探针的性质刻画，而非排行榜式主张；并给出推翻该结论的明确条件。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems communicate in text, which is lossy and wasteful: the sender throws away the reading it already did. A growing literature instead transmits the KV cache, the natural carrier of that reading. We study the most appealing form of the idea: both models stay frozen and the sender's cache is substituted for the reader's with nothing trained. It does not work, and the obstruction belongs to the reader. A frozen transformer reads only caches close to the one it would have written itself, a region we call its tolerable set. We show that geometric similarity to that cache does not predict whether the reader can use it, and the failure is not gradual, so a more faithful substitute is not the remedy it appears to be. We explain this phenomenon with competitive attention. Experimentally, we test eight diverse substitution methods against two measured anchors: an uninformative cache baseline and the reader's own accuracy from having read the passage. All sit at the baseline. That turns a long tail of separate negative results into one outcome with a stated condition for overturning it. A different design does work: if the reader keeps its own cache and attends over the sender's as extra context, adapting only its queries, it beats its own baseline, and also clears the baseline with eight senders pooled where undirected attention over the same union falls below it. We offer this as a characterization on one probe, not a leaderboard claim. Our central contribution is a redirection: pursuing faithful substitute caches is the wrong target; adding a cache to what the reader already has is the promising one.

</details>


### [134] [RICE-Alpha: Reliability-Informed Correction with Event Graphs for LLM-Agent Stock Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34004)
*Tong Liu,Lanmiao Liu,Xiang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RICE-Alpha，一个点对时（point-in-time）股票打分框架：将历史感知的多视图 Base Alpha 与基于历史事件延续性、经可靠性校准的残差修正分离；在 Nasdaq-100 与恒生指数 2024–2026 日频数据上优于所评估的 LLM 智能体与动量基线，ICIR 超过最强基线两倍以上，净夏普分别为 1.656 与 1.725。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 股价相关新闻通过时间依赖的公司事件演化，历史信息只有在建模事件连续性、信息可用性与转移可靠性时才有用。现有 LLM 金融智能体虽引入历史证据，但在点对时约束下难以保持发行人特定的时间顺序，也难以判断历史转移是否提供超出当前预测的增量信息。

Method: RICE-Alpha 将打分拆分为两部分：(1) 历史感知的多视图 Base Alpha；(2) 由历史事件延续性导出的、经可靠性校准的残差修正。Multi-Tier Memory Layer 将新闻解读锚定在时间上合规的发行人特定历史中；Typed Event Agent 构建事件状态，后继关系仅在发行人体内形成，且仅在合法本地配对后才跨公司池化；成熟转移按其实证可靠性进行校准，所得图信号对 Base Alpha 与技术视图做残差化，得到 RICE Delta。

Result: 在 2024–2026 年 Nasdaq-100 与恒生指数日频面板上，RICE-Alpha 在四项预测指标与四项组合层面指标上均优于所评估的 LLM 智能体与动量方法；ICIR 超过最强基线两倍以上，美国和香港市场净夏普分别达 1.656 与 1.725。美国市场消融实验显示，移除主要组件后 IC 与 RankIC 在 Holm 校正下显著下降。

Conclusion: 历史事件延续性在同时满足时间合规、可靠性校准、并以残差修正形式叠加到多视图预测时，能提供增量信息。

Abstract: Equity-relevant news evolves through temporally dependent corporate events, making historical information useful only when event continuity, information availability, and transition reliability are modeled. Existing LLM-based financial agents incorporate historical evidence, yet they provide limited support for preserving issuer-specific chronology under point-in-time constraints and for identifying when historical transitions contribute information beyond the current forecast. We present RICE-Alpha (Reliability-Informed Correction with Event Graphs), a point-in-time stock-scoring framework that separates a history-aware multi-view Base Alpha from a reliability-calibrated residual correction derived from historical event continuation. A Multi-Tier Memory Layer grounds news interpretation in temporally eligible issuer-specific history, while a Typed Event Agent constructs event states whose successor relations are formed within issuers and pooled across firms only after valid local pairing. Matured transitions are calibrated by their empirical reliability, and the resulting graph signal is residualized against the Base Alpha and technical view to obtain the RICE Delta. On daily Nasdaq-100 and Hang Seng Index panels from 2024 to 2026, RICE-Alpha achieves the strongest results among the evaluated LLM-based agents and momentum across four predictive and four portfolio-level metrics. Its ICIR more than doubles that of the strongest baseline, while net Sharpe ratios reach 1.656 and 1.725 in the U.S. and Hong Kong, respectively. U.S. ablations further show significant reductions in IC and RankIC after Holm adjustment when major components are removed. These results indicate that historical event continuation adds incremental information when it is temporally grounded, reliability-calibrated, and introduced as a residual correction to a multi-view forecast.

</details>


### [135] [Not All Unlabeled Data Are Equal: Unlabeled Data Selection for Semi-Supervised QSAR Modeling with Large Chemical Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22025)
*Oliver Majer,Saso Dzeroski,Jurica Levatic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究QSAR建模中半监督学习（SSL）的无标注数据选择问题：在10个来自ChEMBL的QSAR数据集与超过40万无标注分子池上，评估自训练、协同训练与标签传播三种SSL算法，以及基于随机、最近邻、最远邻与混合抽样的四种选择策略。结果显示大型化学库的无标注数据可提升QSAR模型，10个数据集上均至少有一种SSL配置优于监督基线，且最稳健的改进通常来自选取少量化学上相距较远的分子，说明无标注集的构成比其规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QSAR建模中标签获取依赖昂贵湿实验，标注数据规模小，而ChEMBL等化学库含数百万无标注分子，SSL应用潜力大；但大型化学库通过SSL改进QSAR模型的潜力尚未充分探索，且在真实无标注数据场景下如何选择无标注数据亦不清晰。

Method: 从ChEMBL提取10个QSAR数据集，构建逾40万无标注分子池；评估三种成熟SSL算法（self-training、co-training、label propagation），并基于随机、最近邻、最远邻与混合抽样设计四种无标注数据选择策略，进行系统性对比实验。

Result: 大型化学库的无标注数据能够改进QSAR模型：10个数据集上每种均至少有一种SSL配置优于监督基线；最可靠的改进通常通过选取相对少量、化学上相距较远的分子获得。

Conclusion: 无标注集的构成可能比其规模更重要，这挑战了SSL必须有大量无标注数据这一常见前提。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning (SSL) is particularly attractive in domains where labels are expensive to obtain, while unlabeled examples are readily available. Quantitative Structure-Activity Relationship (QSAR) modeling, which predicts molecular biological activity from chemical structure, is one such domain. Obtaining activity measurements requires costly wet-lab experiments, resulting in small labeled datasets, while chemical repositories such as ChEMBL contain millions of unlabeled molecules. Despite this apparent suitability, the potential of large chemical databases to improve QSAR models via SSL remains largely unexplored. Furthermore, it is unclear how unlabeled data should be selected in realistic SSL settings with genuine unlabeled data. We conduct a systematic study of unlabeled-data selection using 10 QSAR datasets extracted from ChEMBL and a pool of more than 400,000 unlabeled molecules. To this end, we evaluate three established SSL algorithms (self-training, co-training, and label propagation) and formulate four unlabeled-data selection strategies based on random, nearest-neighbor, furthest-neighbor, and mixed sampling. Our results show that unlabeled data from large chemical repositories can improve QSAR models, with at least one SSL configuration outperforming the supervised baseline on each of the 10 datasets. Moreover, the most reliable improvements are generally achieved by selecting a relatively small number of chemically distant molecules. This suggests that the composition of the unlabeled set may be more important than its size, challenging the common premise that SSL necessitates an abundance of unlabeled data.

</details>


### [136] [Augment the Cache, Replace the Activation: A 2x2 Study of Internal-State Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22026)
*Adam Deng,Shicheng Liu,Yada Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将智能体间内部状态通信从"传输"重构为"共享"：一次发布、单机制读取，无需为每对智能体建桥；并发现载体类型与融合机制是耦合的——KV缓存需"增强"（接收方交叉注意同时保留自身缓存），激活需"替换"（直接写入接收方）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结语言模型构建的多智能体系统以文本通信，丢弃发送方已计算出的内部状态，并使接收方重复重建，代价高昂。已有内部通道工作把问题视为"A→B传输"，导致每对通信智能体都需独立的传输桥，每新增一个智能体都要与之前所有智能体逐一适配，扩展性差。

Method: 将问题重构为共享：智能体将内部状态一次发布到状态池，所有对等体通过同一不针对特定发送方拟合的机制读取，新增智能体只增加发布者、不增加桥接。采用共享控制阶梯（control ladder）的单一协议，对比载体（KV缓存 vs 激活）与融合方式的组合，并考察仅训练查询侧小适配器、无训练直接写入、以及训练一次的适配器跨未见发布者与多发布者池的泛化。

Result: 核心发现是载体选择与融合机制相互耦合，决定因素是载体进入接收方计算的位置，可消费性是接收计算的属性而非表征的属性。KV缓存必须增强而非替换：接收方对同伴缓存做交叉注意并保留自身缓存，仅训练小查询侧适配器即可达到阅读源文档的准确率。激活则相反：直接写入接收方最优，无需桥、适配器或训练，优于与自身状态并列暴露，但前提是知道状态应写入接收方的哪一位置。一次训练的适配器也能服务未见过的发布者及同时多个发布者，但仅限内部状态与接收方自身接近者。

Conclusion: 提出经验规则"增强KV缓存、替换激活"，在统一协议下确立；将多智能体内部通信从O(n²)桥接转为可扩展的共享池范式，并指出可消费性取决于接收计算的结构而非表征本身，跨发布者泛化受状态相似性约束。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems built from frozen language models communicate through text, which discards the internal state the sender already computed and makes the receiver pay to rebuild it. Work on internal channels avoids that cost, framing the problem as transfer: agent A sends information to agent B. Every agent pair that must communicate needs its own transfer bridge, and every agent added to a system needs a new bridge fitted against each previous agent. We reframe this problem as sharing: an agent publishes its internal state once into a pool, and every peer reads that pool through a single mechanism fitted to no sender in particular, so adding an agent adds a publisher and no new bridge. Whereas the carrier of the internal state and the fusion mechanism are usually chosen independently in the literature, our main finding is that these two choices are coupled; the deciding factor is where the carrier enters the receiver's computation. Consumability is a property of the receiving computation rather than of the representation. We find that the key-value (KV) cache must be augmented rather than swapped in: the receiver cross-attends over a peer's cache while keeping its own, and with only a small query-side adapter trained, matches the accuracy it gets from reading the source document. Activations invert this. They are best written straight into the receiver, with no bridge, no adapter and no training, which beats exposing them alongside the receiver's own, though that advantage rests on knowing where in the receiver the state belongs. The rule is augment the KV cache, replace the activation, established under one protocol with a shared control ladder. One adapter, trained once, also serves publishers it never trained on, and a pool of several at once, but only those whose internal state lies close to the receiver's own.

</details>


### [137] [Selection Is Not Learning: Choosing Model Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22029)
*Haoyi Yang,Kshitij Mishra,Nils Lukas,Salem Lahlou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 自进化语言模型每轮需决策"继承哪个更新后模型"。作者指出现有循环(R-Zero、Agent0、Skill Self-Play)以"任务看似可学习"作为选择依据并继承全部更新，存在选择—更新错配(selection-to-update gap)。提出HeirCommit：冻结更新模型并用新鲜评测题决定是否继承，或保留当前模型；EvoHeir进一步将参数继承与经验继承解耦。有害更新占比降低68–83%，数学平均提升5.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自演化训练循环把"任务可学习性"当作继承判据，并默认继承所有更新。但实验表明，在已验证任务上产生的多数更新并不会提高正确答案的概率，即"看起来可学习"不等于"产生有用更新"，由此形成选择—更新错配，被接受的有害更新不断积累。

Method: 1) 定义并刻画选择诱导的乐观偏差(selection-induced optimism)，将遗憾分解为选择误差、吸收误差与确认误差；2) HeirCommit：冻结候选更新模型，用新鲜(未参与训练)评测题做确认，通过则继承，否则保留当前模型；3) EvoHeir：把参数继承与经验继承分离，利用当前与归档经验先修复失败、保留成功，再做最终确认。
