Our work proposes R-DLD (Robotic-supported Data Loss Detection), a robot-assisted infrastructure for data loss detection in Android applications, offeringless invasive and more realistic tests by interacting directly with smartphone sensors. The robot is constructed using cost-effective materials, facilitating its adoption in testing environments. In our empirical evaluation R-DLD successfully identified 341 data loss issues in 77 randomly selected apps from an Android store. All reported bugs received responses from the developer, with 89.55% confirming the data loss problems, while 35.82% have being subsequently fixed.

</details>


### [172] [Skip the Compressor: Coding Agents Fix More Issues When They Pick Whole Files](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21073)
*Xiaocong Yang,Rishit Chatterjee,Himanshu Udupi,Vikram Ramavarapu,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在固定token预算下，将预算用于"逐字选择必须修改的文件"（CQ2-CARE）比用于压缩或检索更能提高编码agent的issue解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码agent面对远超上下文窗口的大型代码库。补丁任务同时需要两件事：必须修改的文件，以及其逐字原文（模型需复现所编辑的行）。但检索器按与issue的相似度排序而非"是否必须修改"，压缩器则通过删除文本以适应预算，二者均未同时满足这两项需求。

Method: 提出简单选择器CQ2-CARE，核心思想是在同等token预算下把预算分配给逐字（verbtim）展示哪些文件的问题。在300个近期SWE-rebench issue上为每个上下文设定相同的单issue预算，并依据基准测试对每个补丁进行评分；与三种压缩器、三种检索器以及两个专为issue解决训练的方法（SweRank、SWE-Pruner）对比，使用GPT-5.6与Claude Opus 5两个模型。

Result: 在两个模型下CQ2-CARE分别解决43.7%和46.7%的issue，比最佳压缩器高6.3个百分点，比最佳检索器高9.3与12.7个百分点；且优于专为issue解决训练的方法：较SweRank高0.7与1.7个百分点，较SWE-Pruner高2.0与7.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 基线方法的缺陷在于：检索器常遗漏需编辑文件，压缩器虽保留文件但改动了文本，导致更少补丁可成功应用。逐字整文件选择无需压缩模型，且完整呈现仓库原文，使模型能引用其要编辑的代码，因此是更有效的上下文分配策略。

Abstract: A coding agent works on repositories that can be far larger than its context window can hold. Prompt compressors and retrievers offer solutions to this problem, but patching an issue needs two things at once: the files that must change, and their code exactly as written, since the model must reproduce the lines it edits. Retrievers rank code by its similarity to the issue rather than by whether it must change, and compressors delete text to fit the budget. We show that at an equal token budget, an agent resolves more issues when the budget goes to choosing which files to show verbatim, as our simple selector CQ2-CARE does, than when it goes to pruning tokens or retrieving chunks. We fit every context to the same per-issue budget on 300 recent SWE-rebench issues and grade each patch with the benchmark's tests. With GPT-5.6 and Claude Opus 5, CQ2-CARE resolves 43.7% and 46.7% of the issues, 6.3 points more than the best of three compressors with either model, and 9.3 and 12.7 points more than the best of three retrievers. CQ2-CARE even resolves more issues than two methods trained for issue resolution, SweRank (by 0.7 and 1.7 points) and SWE-Pruner (by 2.0 and 7.3). Our analysis shows the defects of the baseline methods: retrievers often miss the files to edit, and compressors keep them but alter their text, so fewer patches apply. Exact-file selection needs no compression model and shows every line as the repository has it, so the model can quote the code it edits.

</details>


### [173] [Plausible Is Not Enough: Trust and Evidence in AI-Authored Pull Request Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21144)
*Md Shamimur Rahman,Khairul Alam,Banani Roy,Chanchal K. Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基于对31国239名实践者的配对问卷调查，发现评审者对AI撰写PR的初始信任更低、证据要求更高、更倾向要求部分重实现验证，其评审本质是"证据-归属"问题而非单纯正确性检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI撰写的Pull Request（AIPR）需在合入前获得评审者信任，但尚不清楚评审者是否沿用了对待人类撰写PR（HPR）的同一套标准。本文旨在同一受访者内部对比AIPR与HPR在信任、证据要求和验证实践上的差异，厘清AIPR评审的真正障碍。

Method: 对来自31个国家的239名实践者开展问卷调研，采用HPR与AIPR配对的同题设计以实现受试者内比较；统计初始信任、合入标准、证据要求、残余不确定性下的阻断意愿、验证手段选择与归属不清的影响，并结合开放式回答做定性分析。

Result: 64.9%受访者对AIPR初始信任更低；更少依赖作者履历与子系统熟悉度，转而看重人类提示者专业能力与变更规模受限。仅凭对关键用例的自行推演即认为足够合入的比例为55.2%（AIPR）对28.5%（HPR），对测试覆盖的依赖下降。61.1%报告AIPR证据要求更高；残余不确定性下阻断合入的比例由19.4%升至43.9%；归属不清致评审暂停由14.6%升至29.3%。优先采用测试检查、项目上下文与本地执行，更常用部分重实现验证非平凡逻辑。开放题指向：缺乏可靠上下文与人类问责时难以核验"看似合理"的代码。

Conclusion: AIPR评审应被界定为"证据-归属"问题（evidence-ownership），而非单纯的正确性检查。评审工具与智能体工作流需暴露变更来源（provenance）、区分生成式主张与人类已验证证据，并在合入前使不确定性显式可见，从而将生成变更关联到已验证行为与可问责的维护者。

Abstract: Code review is where AI-authored pull requests (AIPRs) must earn reviewer trust before integration, yet it is unclear whether reviewers apply the same criteria used for human-authored pull requests (HPRs). We surveyed 239 practitioners from 31 countries using matched HPR and AIPR questions to compare trust, evidence requirements, and verification practices within respondents. Most respondents (64.9%) reported lower initial trust in AIPRs. They relied less on author track record and subsystem familiarity, emphasizing instead human prompter expertise and bounded change size. For approval, respondents more often considered their own reasoning through critical cases sufficient for AIPRs than HPRs (55.2% versus 28.5%), while relying less on test coverage. Overall, 61.1% reported higher evidence requirements for AIPRs, and the share who would block approval under residual uncertainty increased from 19.4% for HPRs to 43.9% for AIPRs. Respondents prioritized test inspection, project context, and local execution, and more often selected partial reimplementation to verify non-trivial logic. Unclear ownership was also more likely to pause AIPR review (29.3% versus 14.6%). Open-ended responses pointed to the difficulty of validating plausible code without reliable context and human accountability. These findings frame AIPR review as an evidence-ownership problem, not merely a correctness check. Reviewers need to connect generated changes to validated behavior and responsible maintainers. Review tools and agentic workflows should expose provenance, distinguish generated claims from human-validated evidence, and make uncertainty visible before approval.

</details>


### [174] [Beyond Confidence: Rethinking Uncertainty Calibration in Deep Code Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21248)
*Ravishka Rathnasuriya,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了7个代码模型在缺陷预测与漏洞检测任务上的13种不确定性信号和3种事后校准方法，发现强不确定性信号仍校准不良、标准校准器难以转化为选择性预测收益、且校准指标与选择性预测表现常不一致，主张采用以决策为导向的可靠性评估视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在代码任务中表现优异，但实际部署中对准确率要求严格；现有置信度校准技术虽被提出以提升可靠性，其在真实高准确率需求任务中的有效性尚不明确，尤其是不确定性度量能否有效指导选择性预测（accept–defer）缺乏系统证据。

Method: 在缺陷预测和漏洞检测两个任务上，使用7个代码模型，评估13种不确定性信号与3种广泛使用的事后校准方法，围绕两个问题展开：现有置信度与不确定性度量能否指导选择性预测；事后校准能否改善这些度量以更准确区分预测成功/失败案例。

Result: 三点主要发现：(1) 即使强不确定性信号也可能校准不良，并在高置信端分离度有限，限制了选择性预测的实际效果；(2) 标准事后校准器常能提升校准分数，但不稳定地转化为更好的选择性预测行为；(3) 常用校准指标与选择性预测性能可能相互矛盾，需联合评估。

Conclusion: 提出面向决策的深度代码模型可靠性视角：校准分数必须与选择性预测质量及保留区域风险联合评估，方能支撑accept–defer式部署决策。

Abstract: Deep learning models have shown significant promise in code-related tasks. While confidence calibration techniques have been proposed to improve model reliability, their effectiveness in real-world tasks with strict accuracy requirements remains uncertain. In this paper, we investigate two key questions: first, whether existing measurement metrics, including confidence scores and uncertainty metrics, are effective for guiding selective prediction; and second, whether existing calibration techniques can improve these metrics to better identify cases where model predictions are likely to succeed or fail.
  We conduct a systematic study on defect prediction and vulnerability detection using seven code models, evaluating thirteen uncertainty signals and three widely used post-hoc calibration methods. Our analysis reveals three major findings: (1) even strong uncertainty signals can remain poorly calibrated and provide limited separation at the high-confidence end, constraining selective prediction in practice; (2) standard post-hoc calibrators often improve calibration scores but do not consistently translate into better selective prediction behavior; and (3) commonly used calibration metrics can disagree with selective prediction performance, underscoring the need to evaluate both jointly. Moreover, our findings motivate a decision-oriented view of reliability in deep code models, where calibrated scores must be evaluated together with selective-prediction quality and retained-region risk before they can support accept--defer deployment.

</details>


### [175] [When CONTRIBUTING Files Fall Short: Revising OSS Contribution Guidelines with Repository Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21316)
*Kai Zheng,XinGuo Zhou,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ContribAlign框架，通过类别引导的证据整合自动修订开源项目CONTRIBUTING文件；分类器加权F1达0.764（较基线提升0.156），冲突验证器精确率97.3%，人工评估显示其在有用性、新手友好性和完整性上均优于原始指南与三个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贡献指南对新手参与开源项目至关重要，但常存在信息不完整或过时的问题。修订这类指南需从分散来源中恢复并验证项目特定知识，现有研究仅刻画新手信息需求与文档缺口，缺乏自动化修订方法。

Method: 先通过定性分析构建涵盖六大贡献类别、30种细粒度指导类型的分类法；再用分类器诊断原始指南在六类上的覆盖情况；基于分类法收集并组织项目特定证据；过滤检测到的冲突，最后用大语言模型生成类别级修订内容。

Result: 分类器加权F1为0.764，较现有分类器提升0.156；验证器以97.3%精确率和91.5%估计召回率检测事实冲突；人工评估中ContribAlign在原始指南和三个基线中整体质量最高，有用性、新手友好性与完整性显著提升。

Conclusion: ContribAlign能够帮助维护更准确、更有用的贡献指南，验证了类别引导证据整合与LLM生成相结合的自动化修订路径的有效性。

Abstract: Contribution guidelines help newcomers participate in open-source software projects, yet often contain incomplete or outdated information. Existing studies characterize newcomers' information needs and documentation gaps, but revising these guidelines requires recovering and validating project-specific knowledge from scattered sources. We present ContribAlign, a framework for automatically revising CONTRIBUTING files through category-guided evidence integration. A qualitative analysis yields a taxonomy of 30 fine-grained guidance types across six contribution categories. ContribAlign uses a classifier to diagnose coverage of the six categories in the original guidance, then uses the taxonomy to collect and organize project-specific evidence. It filters detected conflicts and uses a large language model to generate category-level revisions. The classifier achieves a weighted F1 score of 0.764, improving on the existing classifier by 0.156, while the verifier detects factual conflicts with 97.3% precision and 91.5% estimated recall. Human evaluation shows that ContribAlign achieves the highest overall quality among the original guidelines and three baselines, with significant improvements in usefulness, beginner-friendliness, and completeness. These results suggest that ContribAlign can help maintain more accurate and useful contribution guidelines.

</details>


### [176] [SWE-at-Scale: Scalable Issue Synthesis from Historical Bug Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21359)
*Silin Chen,Zihan Zhang,Xiaodong Gu,Jiale Huang,Yuling Shi,Han Li,Zhao Shan,Chengcheng Wan,Zhensu Sun,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 SWE-at-Scale 框架，通过逆向工程真实修复自动合成多样化、逼真的 SWE 任务，实现可扩展的编码智能体评测数据生产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体能力提升，需要更广泛多样的可验证 SWE 任务；但真实任务受议题报告频率与人工标注成本限制，而现有合成方法仅依赖大模型预训练知识与启发式提示，难以还原真实软件故障机制。

Method: 核心洞见是历史修复蕴含 bug 形成的深层机制知识，反转修复可将"修复监督"转化为基于真实开发场景的"bug 注入监督"。框架从真实 fix 中学习 bug 模式，迁移到新代码上下文，反复采样多样 bug 类型以构建可执行任务。

Result: 相比现有合成基线，生成的任务更多样、更逼真，对 SOTA 编码智能体更具挑战性；可从单一代码上下文生成不同 bug 类型，bug 多样性稳健扩展至每文件 200 次生成。

Conclusion: SWE-at-Scale 将有限真实修复转化为可复用的监督信号，实现逼真、多样、有挑战性的 SWE 任务的可扩展合成。

Abstract: As coding agents become increasingly capable, robust evaluation requires a broader and more diverse supply of verifiable software engineering (SWE) tasks. However, real-world tasks are constrained by the natural frequency of issue reporting and the substantial cost of manual curation. Furthermore, existing synthetic approaches rely primarily on the pretrained knowledge of large models coupled with heuristic prompting, often failing to capture the authentic, complex mechanics of software failures. To exponentially scale SWE task production, we introduce SWE-at-Scale, a framework that automatically synthesizes realistic issues by reverse-engineering real-world software fixes. Our key insight is that historical fixes encapsulate deep mechanistic knowledge of bug formation: inverting a fix transforms repair supervision into bug-induction supervision grounded in actual development scenarios. SWE-at-Scale transfers these learned bug patterns to novel code contexts, repeatedly sampling diverse bug typologies to construct executable SWE tasks. Extensive experiments show that SWE-at-Scale generates more diverse and realistic SWE tasks than current synthesis baselines while posing greater challenges to state-of-the-art coding agents. Notably, SWE-at-Scale can generate distinct bug types from a single code context, with bug diversity scaling robustly even up to 200 generations per file. Overall, SWE-at-Scale turns finite real-world fixes into reusable supervision for scalable synthesis of realistic, diverse, and challenging SWE tasks.

</details>


### [177] [Agentic Dependency Transformations: Lessons from an Exploratory Case Study of Flask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21372)
*Kaya Kitada,Yoshiki Higo,Raula Gaikovina Kula*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该新兴结果论文以 Flask 为案例，探索 AI 编码代理如何改变仓库对第三方依赖的处理方式，总结出五条初步经验并提出五部分研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件高度依赖第三方库，维护者需在复用收益与依赖带来的维护负担和风险之间权衡；现有研究缺乏对"依赖转换"这一新兴做法的系统认识。

Method: 提出"依赖转换"（dependency transformation）概念，涵盖本地重实现等策略；通过设计实验，考察可用上下文如何影响代理的依赖发现与迭代转换过程，并基于 Flask 开展探索性案例研究。

Result: 案例得出五条初步经验，表明上下文可用性显著塑造代理的依赖发现与转换行为。

Conclusion: 依赖转换构成新兴研究方向，需明确转换契约、来源约束、保留要求与独立验证标准，并以五部分研究议程推进。

Abstract: Modern software relies heavily on third-party libraries, requiring maintainers to balance the benefits of reuse against dependency-related maintenance and risk. We define dependency transformation as an emerging approach in which an AI coding agent alters how a repository handles external dependencies. Local reimplementation is one transformation strategy that recreates required functionality as local code; dependency transformation is broader and may include other strategies. In this emerging-results paper, we report an exploratory case study of Flask to explore dependency transformation strategies. We design experiments that show how available context shapes an agent's dependency discovery and iterative transformation of dependencies. The case yields five preliminary lessons. These lessons and the five-part research agenda position dependency transformation as an emerging research direction and motivate explicit transformation contracts, provenance constraints, preservation requirements, and independent validation criteria.

</details>


### [178] [The Role of Agentic Instruction Categories for Code Generation: A Study of Five Python Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21421)
*Warren Gerald Polandra,Yoshiki Higo,Raula Gaikovina Kula*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估五类 AGENTS.md 指令（仓库概览、函数描述、实现细节、测试、重构步骤）的 31 种非空组合对 agent 文件生成与代码生成的影响，发现指令数量并非 token 消耗的决定因素，且不同指令组合对功能正确性与结构相似性的作用差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级 agent 文件（如 AGENTS.md）被用于向编码智能体传达项目知识与开发者意图，但哪类指令有助于高效生成 agent 文件并提升生成代码的正确性尚不明确，因此需要系统性的类别级评估。

Method: 在 5 个由人类编写的 Python 库上，使用 Gemma 4 与 GPT-5.4 两个 LLM，枚举五类指令（Repository Overview、Function Description、Implementation Detail、Testing、Reconstruction Steps）的全部 31 种非空组合；围绕 agent 文件生成与代码生成设置两个研究问题、五个子问题，考察 token 消耗、编译成功率、功能正确性（部分通过/全部通过）以及与人工代码的结构相似性（文件行数、总圈复杂度、文件数、函数数）。

Result: 指令数量本身不决定 agent 文件生成的 token 消耗，模型与库间差异显著；多数 AI 生成库可成功编译，但功能正确性有限——Gemma 4 无任何变体通过全部测试，GPT-5.4 有 12.99% 变体全部通过；Function Description 使 Gemma 4 部分通过率最高，Reconstruction Steps 使 GPT-5.4 全通过率最高；结构相似性方面，Reconstruction Steps 最常对应行数与总圈复杂度最接近原库的生成库，Testing 与 Reconstruction Steps 在文件数与函数数上并列最频繁。

Conclusion: 指令类别而非指令数量是影响 agent 文件生成效率与代码生成质量的关键因素；面向功能正确性应优先考虑 Reconstruction Steps（对强模型）与 Function Description（对弱模型），而结构贴近人类代码同样以 Reconstruction Steps 最为有效。可编译性并不等价于功能正确性，评估需同时关注多维度指标。

Abstract: Agentic coding systems increasingly rely on repository-level agent files, such as AGENTS.md, to communicate project knowledge and developer intent. However, it remains unclear which categories of agent instructions enable efficient agent-file generation and accurate code generation. We evaluate five instruction categories: Repository Overview, Function Description, Implementation Detail, Testing, and Reconstruction Steps, across all 31 non-empty combinations using two Large Language Models (LLMs), Gemma 4 and GPT-5.4, and five human-written Python libraries. Our study addresses two research questions, comprising five sub-questions, on agent file generation and code generation. Results show that the number of instructions alone does not determine token usage for agent-file generation, with substantial variation across models and libraries. Although most AI-generated libraries compiled successfully regardless of the instruction combination, functional correctness remained limited. No Gemma 4 variant passed all tests, whereas 12.99% of GPT-5.4 variants did. Function Description achieved Gemma 4's highest partial-pass rate, while Reconstruction Steps achieved GPT-5.4's highest full-pass rate. For structural similarity to human-written code, Reconstruction Steps was most frequently associated with libraries closest to the originals in Lines of File and total Cyclomatic Complexity, while Testing and Reconstruction Steps were jointly most frequent for File Count and Function Count.

</details>


### [179] [Poison Valleys: Diagnosing Path-Dependent Failure in LLM Code Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21514)
*Veljko Skarich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出Poison Valleys，通过在模型假设（hypothesis）与实现计划（plan）中注入"看似合理但错误"的软件机制，追踪上游误解如何传导至生成代码的错误机制与失败形态。在1,844次模型请求、两个合成Python案例上，将误解从假设移至计划使失败率分别上升32.2%和28.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码修复智能体失败时，最终测试只能表明补丁错误，却无法定位是哪一个更早的"承诺"（假设或计划）导致错误，也无法指出是哪一项软件义务被违反，缺乏从上游误解到代码失败的可测量因果链条。

Method: 定义若干"看似合理但错误"的软件机制（misconceptions），将其分别注入模型的假设、实现计划或两者之中；在PersistQueue（为磁盘队列加入原子批量认领）与Dijkstra（为最短路搜索加入源点敏感过滤）两个合成Python任务上运行1,844次模型请求，用Oracle和针对性诊断坐标（diagnostic coordinates）评估生成代码，并在匹配实验混合后比较不同注入位置的影响。

Result: 将误解从假设移至计划后，PersistQueue失败率增加32.2%，Dijkstra增加28.9%（匹配实验混合后）。不同误解产生彼此不同且依赖模型的义务违反模式，但多种误解可能收敛到相同的最终行为，形成从上游误解到代码机制与失败形态的可测量联系。

Conclusion: 上游误解的位置（假设vs计划）显著影响修复失败概率，且误解类型与模型共同决定被违反义务的模式；该框架建立了误解→代码机制→失败形态的可测量链路，并公开了代码、数据与演示。

Abstract: When code-repair agents fail, final tests reveal that a patch is wrong but not which earlier commitment caused it or which software obligation failed. Poison Valleys traces this path in 1,844 model requests on two synthetic Python cases: PersistQueue adds atomic batch claiming to a disk-backed queue, while Dijkstra adds source-sensitive filtering to shortest-path search. We define plausible but incorrect software mechanisms, place each in a model's hypothesis, implementation plan, or both, and evaluate the generated code with an Oracle and targeted diagnostic coordinates. Moving the misconception from hypothesis to plan increased failure by 32.2% in PersistQueue and 28.9% in Dijkstra after matching the experimental mix. Different misconceptions produced distinct, model-dependent patterns of violated obligations, although several could converge on the same behavior. This creates a measured connection from upstream misconception to code mechanism and failure form. Code, data, and demo: https://poisonvalleys.org.

</details>


### [180] [Realization Divergence in Multi-Provider Quantum Computing: A Pre-Registered Study of What Survives Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21714)
*Jouni Peltonen,Vlad Stirbu,Tommi Mikkonen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"实现分歧"(realization divergence)概念,即同一源电路在不同量子软件栈中实现与行为的可观测差异,并通过三厂商四设备的硬件实验结合预先注册预测与探索性分析加以测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子程序在编译、转译与厂商服务后才执行,但"所写"与"所运行"之间的距离很少被度量;同时跨厂商接口的可观测性受限,存在静默失效问题。

Method: 跨三厂商四设备的硬件研究;预注册预测加明确标注的探索性分析与修订;带指定布局策略的编译实验(嵌入半径、电路规模与双比特开销的相关性);固定最低索引策略下8比特与12比特Bernstein-Vazirani对比,并在两次重复中验证;以全连接IonQ作为对照,在Rigetti Cepheus上进行拓扑感知原生布局的后续实验;检查厂商接口(如Rigetti支持操作列表)在器件代际间的变化。

Result: 嵌入半径与双比特开销的Spearman相关为0.805,高于电路规模的0.492;在IQM Emerald上8比特BV电路执行分歧大于12比特电路,Garnet上顺序相反,两者均在两次重复中复现且置信区间不重叠;该顺序反转在全连接IonQ对照上不出现;Cepheus的拓扑感知原生布局在两条提交路径上均无一致的正确答案概率优势,但也未确立等价性;结构比较依赖指标加权、布局与器件代际;Rigetti支持操作列表在替换原生纠缠器的器件代际间未变。

Conclusion: 结果支持依赖布局的解释,但未隔离具体硬件噪声机制;组合工具链中存在静默接口失效,提出在工具链边界设置检查,将有效的跨厂商比较与具体软件质量实践联系起来。

Abstract: Quantum programs pass through compilers, transpilers and vendor services before execution. However, the distance between what was written and what runs is rarely measured. We call this distance realization divergence: the observable difference in how an identical source circuit is realized and behaves across quantum software stacks. We report a hardware study across three vendors and four devices, combining pre-registered predictions with explicitly identified exploratory analyses and amendments. In a compilation experiment with assigned placement policies, embedding radius correlates with two-qubit overhead at Spearman 0.805, against 0.492 for circuit size. Under a fixed lowest-index policy, an eight-qubit Bernstein-Vazirani circuit exhibits greater execution divergence than a twelve-qubit circuit on IQM Emerald. The opposite ordering occurs on IQM Garnet. Both comparisons replicate across two repetitions with disjoint confidence intervals. These results support a placement-dependent explanation, without isolating individual hardware noise mechanisms. The same ordering inversion is absent on the all-to-all IonQ control. On Rigetti Cepheus, a follow-up using topology-aware native placement found no consistent advantage in correct-answer probability for either submission track, but did not establish equivalence between the tracks. Structural comparisons depend on metric weighting, placement and device generation. Provider interfaces also limit observability: Rigetti's supported-operation list remained unchanged across a device generation that replaced the native entangler. We classify silent interface failures in composed toolchains and identify checks at toolchain boundaries, connecting valid cross-provider comparisons with concrete software-quality practices.

</details>


### [181] [CodeHealth Predicts the Token Cost of Agentic Code Review: A Pre-Registered Study of 465 Agent-Authored Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21914)
*Lo Gullstrand Heander,Markus Borg,Emma Söderberg,Christofer Rydenfält*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基线 CodeHealth 每提高 1 分，多智能体代码评审与修复的 token 成本降低 3.5%–6.1%，且该效应不由 PR 规模解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织越来越多地按 token 计费将代码评审外包给多智能体 LLM 流水线；低代码质量会增加人工维护成本，但其对智能体评审成本的影响尚未被测量。

Method: 预注册观察性研究：从 AIDev 的 246 个仓库中按 CodeHealth 与 PR footprint 联合分层（仓库层面设上限）抽取 465 个智能体撰写的 PR；使用冻结的五智能体评审-修复流水线，配三种不同 LLM 处理全部 PR；对每个 LLM 拟合以 log token 为因变量、CodeHealth 与 log PR footprint 为自变量、仓库为随机截距的混合模型。

Result: 三种 LLM 的 CodeHealth 系数均为负、95% CI 不含零，每分对应 3.5%–6.1% 的 token 成本下降；CodeHealth 与 PR footprint 无关；三个注册的坏味中两个可预测修复成本；批量修复后 CodeHealth 中位数不变；探索性分析显示关联主要位于评审阶段（对不健康代码迭代更多但未写更多评论），控制被评分文件大小后关联消失，提示由文件大小中介。

Conclusion: 与人类可维护性标定的质量指标也能预测多智能体评审与修复的推理成本；单点效应温和且不由 PR 规模承载，因此重构的价值延伸到智能体评审场景，前提是它改变结构与文件大小而非仅改变评分。

Abstract: Context: Organizations increasingly delegate code review to multi-agent large language model (LLM) pipelines billed per token. Low code quality raises human maintenance effort, but its effect on agent review cost has not been measured.
  Objective: We test whether baseline CodeHealth predicts the token cost of agentic review and fix beyond pull request (PR) footprint.
  Method: In a pre-registered observational study, we sampled 465 agent-authored PRs from 246 repositories in AIDev, jointly stratified by CodeHealth and footprint under repository caps. A frozen five-agent review-and-fix pipeline processed every PR with three different LLMs. Per LLM, we fitted a mixed model of log tokens on CodeHealth and log PR footprint, with a repository random intercept.
  Results: For all three LLMs the CodeHealth coefficient was negative, with 95% CI excluding zero; each CodeHealth point was associated with 3.5% to 6.1% lower token cost. CodeHealth was unrelated to PR footprint, so PR size does not explain the association. Two of three registered smells predicted fix cost; batch fixing left median CodeHealth unchanged. Exploratory analyses located the association in the review stage, where agents iterated more on unhealthy code without writing more comments. Adjusting for the size of the scored files absorbed the association, which we interpret as mediation by file size.
  Conclusions: A quality metric calibrated against human maintainability also predicts the inference cost of multi-agent review and fix. The effect is modest per point and not carried by PR size, so the refactoring case extends to agent-reviewed code when it changes structure and file size, not only the score.
  Data Availability: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22127920

</details>


### [182] [Benchmarking and Enhancing Design-Aware Code Review in the Linux Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21925)
*Zhenhao Zhou,Kai Yu,Ying Wang,Zhiqiang Yuan,Junhao Zeng,Yijian Wu,Chong Wang,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 DesignLore，一种知识增强方法，从历史评审讨论中抽取设计决策、组织为自适应设计分类体系并复用以指导代码评审（即"设计感知代码评审"），同时构建 DesignReviewBench 基准；实验显示最强基线在设计权衡检测上 F1 仅 0.213，DesignLore 达 0.259（相对提升 21.6%），并在不同智能体系统与主干模型上稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化代码评审主要聚焦缺陷与局部正确性，忽视了成熟项目中评审所需的更高层推理：识别变更带来的设计权衡、比较替代方案、评估与项目特定约束的兼容性。历史评审讨论中积累的设计知识难以复用，缺少系统性评测手段。

Method: 提出 DesignLore 知识增强流程：(1) 从历史评审讨论中抽取设计决策；(2) 将其组织为自适应设计分类体系（adaptive design taxonomy）；(3) 检索并复用相关历史知识以引导代码评审。同时构建 DesignReviewBench——基于真实补丁-评审历史的基准，用于系统评估设计感知代码评审。

Result: 设计感知评审对现有编码智能体仍具挑战：最强基线在设计权衡检测上 F1 仅 0.213；DesignLore 取得 F1 0.259，相对提升 21.6%，且在多种智能体系统与主干模型（backbone）上均保持一致性收益。

Conclusion: 结果凸显设计感知评审的重要性，并验证了恢复与复用历史设计知识以支撑该类评审的潜力。

Abstract: Code review often requires reasoning beyond local correctness. In mature software systems, reviewers must recognize design trade-offs introduced by a proposed change, compare alternative solutions, and reason about their compatibility with project-specific constraints. We refer to this problem as design-aware code review. However, existing automated review approaches primarily focus on defects and correctness, while the design knowledge accumulated in historical review discussions remains difficult to reuse.
  To address these challenges, we present DesignLore, a knowledge-augmented approach that extracts design decisions from historical review discussions, organizes them into an adaptive design taxonomy, and reuses relevant historical knowledge to guide code review. We further construct DesignReviewBench, a benchmark derived from real patch-review histories for systematically evaluating design-aware code review. Our evaluation shows that design-aware review remains challenging for current coding agents, with the strongest baseline achieving an F1 of only 0.213 on design trade-off detection. In comparison, DesignLore achieves an F1 of 0.259, a 21.6% relative improvement, and consistently delivers gains across different agent systems and backbone. These results highlight the importance of design-aware review and demonstrate the potential of recovering and reusing historical design knowledge to support it.

</details>


### [183] [Stateful Testing for Deep Learning Compilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22003)
*Jiatao Ge,Ruixiang Qian,Yibo Hu,Yanzhou Mu,Yinglong Zou,Jiawei Liu,Zhenyu Chen,Chunrong Fang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Rondo：面向深度学习编译器的有状态测试方法，通过“模型+调用序列”表示测试用例，结合序列变异与MCMC采样、三方差分判定，检出117个缺陷（含35个有状态缺陷）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习编译器采用惰性编译并跨调用缓存产物，保留的编译器状态可使单独运行正确的调用在后续调用中出错；对PYTORCH、JAX、TENSORFLOW中26个公开问题的分析表明，检测此类缺陷必须构造“先建立编译器状态、后暴露失败”的调用序列。现有测试方法难以生成这类有状态触发条件。

Method: 将测试用例表示为模型与调用序列的配对；对调用序列进行变异，并用由编译行为引导的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛采样器偏好罕见行为；采用三方差分预言机比较三种执行：不编译执行、重置编译器状态的编译执行、保留编译器状态的编译执行；确认缺陷时，在保持输入与程序状态不变的前提下，分别以保留与重置状态重放同一调用，随后在保持原缺陷的前提下缩短序列。

Result: 在4个深度学习框架、24种常用模型架构上评估：检出117个缺陷，其中35个为有状态缺陷；提交39份缺陷报告（18份为有状态缺陷），开发者确认35份（16份为有状态缺陷），修复18个缺陷（11个为有状态缺陷）。行为覆盖率与编译器PYTHON源码行覆盖率均为对比方法中最高，告警精度达99.26%；对117条调用序列中的44条完成缩短，平均缩短83.34%。

Conclusion: 有状态测试是发现深度学习编译器状态残留类缺陷的有效途径；Rondo在缺陷检出量、覆盖率与告警精度上表现优异，且序列缩短显著提升了缺陷报告的可用性。

Abstract: Modern deep learning compilers compile models lazily and cache artifacts across invocations. Retained compiler state can make a later invocation incorrect even when it is correct in isolation. An analysis of 26 publicly reported issues in PyTorch, JAX, and TensorFlow shows that detecting such bugs requires sequences that first establish compiler state and then expose a failure. We present Rondo, a stateful testing method that represents each test case as a model paired with an invocation sequence. Rondo mutates invocation sequences and uses a Markov chain Monte Carlo sampler guided by compilation behavior to favor rarely observed behaviors. Its three-way differential oracle compares execution without compilation, compiled execution with compiler state reset, and compiled execution with state retained. It confirms stateful bugs by replaying the same invocation with state retained and reset while preserving inputs and program state, then shortens sequences while preserving the original bug. We evaluate Rondo on four deep learning frameworks using 24 common model architectures. Rondo detects 117 bugs, including 35 stateful bugs. We submit 39 bug reports, including 18 for stateful bugs; developers confirm 35 reports, including 16 for stateful bugs, and fix 18 bugs, including 11 stateful bugs. Rondo achieves the highest behavior coverage and compiler Python source-line coverage among the compared methods, with 99.26% alarm precision. It shortens 44 of 117 invocation sequences, reducing the lengths of these 44 sequences by 83.34% on average.

</details>


### [184] [WEAVE: Weaving Real-World Code Evolution into Dependency-Structured Tasks for Benchmarking and Training Code Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22039)
*Weihao Li,Zihang Xu,Zhongqiu Wang,Songwei Yu,Wenqiang Zhu,Yuechen Luo,Jingxuan Xu,Yanan Wu,Haoyang Huang,Ken Deng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 WEAVE 框架，将真实仓库中相互依赖的多个 PR 历史转化为可执行、具备依赖结构（有向无环图 DAG）的任务，并引入 Graph Credit 指标衡量依赖一致的部分进展，用于长程代码智能体的评测与训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实软件开发中的特性、迁移和系统性修复往往跨多个 PR 协同完成，而现有可执行仓库级基准多以单一 issue/PR 为任务单元，已有跨 PR 设置又依赖粗粒度聚合与结构无关的评估，掩盖了变更间依赖关系，无法准确衡量进展并为长程代码智能体提供有效学习信号。

Method: WEAVE 利用仓库校准的类型化变更位置（typed change loci）将相关 PR 组织为有界 DAG，并将每个 DAG 实例化为统一的、可执行的任务；同时提出 Graph Credit，一种确定性的、以测试为依据的度量，刻画依赖一致的部分进展，可作为密集、可验证的强化学习奖励。

Result: 在任务构建、智能体评测、监督微调和强化学习四类实验中，WEAVE 能生成连贯的可执行任务；与扁平评分相比，Graph Credit 提供更符合实际依赖结构的进展度量；并能为代码智能体训练提供有效监督信号与可验证奖励。

Conclusion: 将多 PR 变更建模为依赖结构化的可执行任务，并配合细粒度的部分进展度量，是提升长程代码智能体评测真实性与训练效果的可行路径。

Abstract: Real-world software engineering rarely consists of isolated changes. Features, migrations, and systematic fixes often unfold across multiple pull requests (PRs), requiring coordinated modifications across a repository. Yet most executable repository-level benchmarks retain a single issue or PR as the task unit, while existing multi-PR settings rely on coarse aggregation and structure-agnostic evaluation. This mismatch obscures dependencies among changes and limits progress measurement and learning signals for long-horizon code agents. We introduce WEAVE, a framework that transforms verified PR histories into executable, dependency-structured tasks. WEAVE leverages repository-calibrated typed change loci to organize related PRs into bounded DAGs and instantiate each as a unified executable task. We further introduce Graph Credit, a deterministic, test-grounded metric that measures dependency-consistent partial progress and supports dense, verifiable rewards for reinforcement learning. Experiments on task construction, agent evaluation, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning show that WEAVE yields coherent executable tasks, enables more faithful progress measurement than flat scoring, and provides effective supervision and verifiable rewards for training code agents.

</details>


### [185] [Are We Testing the Wrong Quantum Bugs? A Study of Fault Representation Gaps in Quantum Software Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22089)
*Asif Mohammed Saad,Ahsanul Ameen Sabit,Jonah Brandenburgh,Jiyuan Wang,Qian Zhang,Soneya Binta Hossain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 审计4个量子变异测试工具和3个缺陷基准，并收集410个真实缺陷，归入56个bug家族的统一分类学：变异工具覆盖20个家族、基准再增15个，但仅13.7%的真实缺陷落在这些家族中，86.3%属于未被覆盖的家族，集中于编译变换、类型处理与输入验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件依赖编译器、中间表示与执行框架对程序进行变换，这些组件的缺陷会改变原本正确的量子程序行为。然而现有量子测试技术多以变异生成的缺陷和人工整理的缺陷基准作为评估依据，其缺陷与真实量子软件中遇到的缺陷是否一致缺乏实证检验，因此需要审计这些评估手段的代表性。

Method: 对4个量子变异测试工具与3个精心整理的缺陷基准进行审计，并从10个量子软件仓库中收集两年内410个经核实的真实缺陷；将三类来源的缺陷统一归入包含56个bug家族的共同分类学，比较变异算子、基准与真实缺陷在家族层面的覆盖分布。

Result: 变异工具覆盖20个bug家族，基准额外贡献15个；但410个真实缺陷中仅56个（13.7%）属于上述任一来源所代表的家族，其余354个（86.3%）属于两者均缺失的家族。最大的未覆盖家族为编译器变换（66个缺陷）、类型处理（53个）与输入验证（47个）。与局部的门级或语句级修改不同，这些缺陷往往依赖中间表示、编译流水线、输入类型或依赖版本。

Conclusion: 现有变异算子主要修改电路与程序构造，而许多真实缺陷产生于处理这些构造的编译器、表示与框架接口，二者存在系统性覆盖错位。弥合该差距需超越电路级缺陷，转向编译变换的语义保持、表示边界的正确性以及组件间交互的测试；本研究为构建能刻画这些机制并保留复现所需上下文的缺陷模型与基准提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Quantum software relies on compilers, intermediate representations, and execution frameworks that transform programs before execution. Faults in these components can change the behavior of otherwise correct quantum programs. Yet quantum testing techniques are often evaluated using mutation-generated faults and curated bug benchmarks. We investigate how well the faults represented by these artifacts reflect those encountered in modern quantum software. We audit four quantum mutation-testing tools and three curated bug benchmarks, and collect 410 verified bugs from ten quantum software repositories over two years. We classify faults from all three sources into a common taxonomy of 56 bug families and compare their representation across mutation operators, curated benchmarks, and recent real-world bugs. The mutation tools support 20 bug families, and curated benchmarks add 15 more. Yet only 56 of the 410 real-world bugs (13.7%) belong to families represented by either source; the remaining 354 (86.3%) belong to families absent from both. The largest unrepresented families concern compiler transformations (66 bugs), type handling (53), and input validation (47). Unlike local gate or statement changes, these faults often depend on intermediate representations, compiler pipelines, input types, or dependency versions. Existing mutation operators primarily modify circuits and program constructs, whereas many real-world faults arise in the compilers, representations, and framework interfaces that process them. Addressing this gap requires looking beyond circuit-level faults toward testing semantic preservation across compiler transformations, correctness at representation boundaries, and interactions between software components. Our study provides empirical evidence to guide fault models and benchmarks that capture these mechanisms and preserve the contexts needed to reproduce them.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [The World Won't Wait: Evaluating and Teaching Agents Under a Live Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20665)
*Ningxin Pan,Hanyu Li,Yeqing Shen,Ning Ding,Yehui Tang,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RealtimeBench（180 任务/6 类游戏，推理期间游戏持续运行）揭示慢而强的模型在实时环境中反而落后；提出 TORSI 双时间尺度离线递归自改进（慢教师离线修正并写指令、快学生在线不调用教师），两轮使 Jev 均分由 13.51 升至 24.19。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体可解复杂交互任务，但推理与工具调用耗时；在持续变化的环境中，即使正确的动作也可能因延迟而失效。现有评测多在暂停/回合制的静态设定下进行，掩盖了时延对有效性的影响，因此需要衡量并解决"实时性"问题。

Method: 1) 构建 RealtimeBench：6 类游戏、180 个任务，游戏在模型推理期间不暂停，采用配对评估比较不同速度—能力组合的智能体。2) 提出 TORSI（Two-Timescale Offline Recursive Self-Improvement）：慢教师在尝试之间于暂停游戏中分析失败、测试修正方案，并将其提炼为可复用的指令；快学生在对局中不调用教师、直接依指令行动；以验证分数决定哪些更新被采纳，形成递归的离线自改进循环。

Result: 配对评估显示：游戏暂停时 GPT-6.1-sol 优于更快的 Jev，但游戏持续运行时其表现落后，说明能力优势被时延侵蚀。TORSI 两轮训练后 Jev 平均测试分由 13.51 提升至 24.19（0–100 进度刻度），六类游戏全部提升；所生成指令无需再调参即可提升另外三个模型的平均分，显示跨模型可迁移性。

Conclusion: 实时环境下的智能体有效性不只取决于单步决策质量，还取决于推理时延与学习/行动的编排。通过跨时间尺度组织"深思—学习—行动"（慢教师离线改进、快学生在线执行），可在不增加在线计算的前提下显著提升实时表现，指向"实时智能"这一研究方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can solve complex interactive tasks, yet reasoning and tool calls take time. In continuously changing environments, an otherwise correct action may arrive too late. We introduce RealtimeBench, with 180 tasks across six game types, to evaluate agents while games continue running during inference. Paired evaluations show that GPT-6.1-sol outperforms the faster Jev when games are paused, but falls behind when they keep running. We propose Two-Timescale Offline Recursive Self-Improvement (TORSI): a slow teacher studies failures and tests corrections in paused games between attempts, then writes reusable instructions for a fast student. The student acts without teacher calls during gameplay, and validation scores determine which updates are adopted. Two rounds raise Jev's average test score from 13.51 to 24.19 on a 0--100 progress scale, improving all six game types. The instructions also improve average scores for three other models without retuning. These findings point toward real-time intelligence: enabling powerful LLM agents to remain effective in a continuously changing world by organizing deliberation, learning, and action across timescales.

</details>


### [187] [Evaluating Model Capabilities After Benchmark Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20667)
*Morio Hamada,Jehyeok Yeon,Matteo Migliarini,Justin Olive,Magda Dubois*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究基准饱和后其解题转录文本（transcript）是否仍保留能力信号，发现仅用饱和基准中正确解答的转录即可预测模型在未饱和留出任务上的能力，其中成对评判（pairwise judging）效果最佳，预测误差较仅用饱和分数的基线降低5.3倍，但跨分布偏移时泛化性不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI能力快速提升导致基准趋于饱和：当多个模型接近满分时，基准失去区分度，也无法支撑AI安全论证与前沿政策评估。然而基准条目产出的信息远多于分数本身——完整的解题过程转录蕴含更丰富信息，作者希望挖掘这部分被浪费的信号以延长基准寿命。

Method: 利用历史基准与模型发布进行回溯式饱和模拟；仅使用饱和基准中正确解答的转录作为输入，预测模型在留出、未饱和条目上的能力。评估三类代表性信息抽取协议：成对评判（pairwise judging）、单转录绝对打分，以及基于确定性转录特征或嵌入的预测；并分析转录各段落与信息通道（如推理、工具调用）的贡献及分布偏移下的泛化表现。

Result: 饱和转录保留了超出基准准确率的能力信息；在所评估协议中成对评判最优，相比仅用饱和基准分数的基线将预测误差降低约5.3倍。能力相关信息分布于转录各段落，但集中于推理与工具调用等特定通道；协议预测力在分布偏移下并不总是泛化，例如会低估2025年初首批推理模型的能力。

Conclusion: 饱和基准的任务转录中仍存在可预测能力的信号，有可能在分数饱和后继续用于能力评估；但要真正延长现有基准的寿命，需要专门设计能稳健抽取这些信号的方法。

Abstract: Rapid advances in AI capabilities are causing benchmarks to saturate. Once several models approach perfect scores, a benchmark loses its power to differentiate between them, and with it its utility for informing AI safety cases and frontier policies. Yet benchmark items produce far more information than the scores we typically extract from them: a full transcript of how each model solved the task. We investigate whether these transcripts can be used to assess model capabilities even after benchmark scores have saturated. We simulate saturation retrospectively using historical benchmark and model releases, and test whether protocols that access only correctly solved transcripts from saturated benchmarks can predict capabilities measured on held-out, unsaturated items. We evaluate representative contemporary approaches for extracting information from model transcripts: pairwise judging, single-transcript absolute scoring, and predictions from deterministic transcript features or embeddings. We find that saturated transcripts retain capability information beyond benchmark accuracy. Amongst our evaluated protocols, pairwise judging performs best, reducing prediction error by 5.3 times compared to a baseline using only saturated benchmark scores. This capability-relevant information is distributed across transcript sections, but concentrated in specific channels of information such as reasoning and tool-calls. However, the predictive power of protocols does not always generalise across distribution shifts -- for example by underestimating the capabilities of the first reasoning models introduced in early 2025. Saturated benchmarks thus retain capability-predicting signal in their task transcripts, but extending the lifetime of existing benchmarks requires methods designed to extract these signals robustly.

</details>


### [188] [Value-Informed Schedule Trimming for Accelerated Sequential Monte Carlo Guidance in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20669)
*Jaehyeon Kim,Ziseok Lee,Siyoung Choi,Kyungsu Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VISTA：一种免训练方法，在固定预算下自动选择离散扩散模型稀疏引导的时间步，通过将稀疏引导建模为对全引导概率路径的近似并优化可估计的ℓ1路径偏差上界，实现任务自适应的引导调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励引导的离散扩散可在推理时对齐下游目标，但在每个去噪步施加SMC引导计算代价高（奖励评估与值梯度计算）；稀疏引导可降本，但常用调度为固定启发式，跨任务效果差异大。

Method: 将稀疏引导表述为理想全引导概率路径的近似，推导可估计的ℓ1路径偏差上界；利用少量全引导预热生成收集的值统计量构造经验代理目标，并用动态规划精确求解最优引导时间步调度；预热生成可保留为模型输出，选定调度后每次生成的引导复杂度与同预算的其他稀疏调度相同。

Result: 在文本、生物序列和图像生成任务上，VISTA自动发现任务相关的调度，在多数测试设置下优于手工设计的稀疏调度，并在若干任务指标上接近或超过全引导性能；在有毒文本生成中，唯一毒性比例为0.99（全引导为1.00），而选择后每次生成延迟从204.23 s降至38.16 s，加速5.35倍。

Conclusion: VISTA以理论驱动的路径偏差上界与动态规划实现了免训练的稀疏引导调度自动选择，在保持低推理成本的同时显著提升相对手工稀疏调度的对齐效果。

Abstract: Reward-guided discrete diffusion enables test-time alignment with downstream objectives, but applying Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) guidance at every denoising step is computationally expensive. Active guidance steps require additional reward evaluations and, for gradient-based proposals, value-gradient computations. Sparse guidance reduces this cost by guiding only a subset of timesteps, but commonly used schedules are fixed heuristics whose effectiveness can vary substantially across tasks. We propose VISTA, a training-free method that automatically selects guided timesteps under a fixed budget. VISTA formulates sparse guidance as approximation of the ideal full-guidance probability path and derives an estimable upper bound on \(\ell_1\) path deviation. Using value statistics collected from a small number of full-guidance warmup generations, VISTA optimizes the resulting empirical surrogate exactly via dynamic programming. The warmup generations can be retained as model outputs, and after schedule selection VISTA has the same per-generation guidance complexity as other sparse schedules with the same budget. Across text, biological sequence, and image generation tasks, VISTA discovers task-dependent schedules and outperforms hand-designed sparse schedules in most tested settings, while approaching or exceeding full-guidance performance on several task metrics. In toxic-text generation, for example, VISTA attains a 0.99 unique-toxic ratio compared with 1.00 for full guidance while reducing post-selection per-generation latency from 204.23 s to 38.16 s, a 5.35$\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [189] [AlphaDiana: A Measurement System for Agents in Verifiable Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20675)
*Zhanke Zhou,Zongze Li,Weikai Huang,Xuan Li,Chentao Cao,Xiao Feng,Xiangyu Lu,Jinbo Hu,Yi Xie,Nico Pelleriti,Shiyang Liu,Menghan Lu,Max Zimmer,Brando Miranda,Jiangchao Yao,Bo Liu,Sanmi Koyejo,Sebastian Pokutta,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AlphaDiana是一个模块化测量系统，用于将智能体的执行条件（harness、工具、技能、记忆）与正确性、行为及资源消耗关联起来，发现harness与组件干预并不稳定提升推理准确率，且需结合正确性评估token使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体由推理模型与harness（组织模型调用、执行工具、加载技能、保留历史）组合而成，理解其性能需将执行条件与结果、行为、资源使用建立联系，但缺乏可分别控制各因素的测量手段。

Method: 提出AlphaDiana测量系统，提供数据集、智能体、环境、评分器的模块化接口；保留harness原生控制循环，同时独立控制文件复用与记忆保留；支持对完整智能体的宏观分析，以及在匹配条件下对工具、技能、记忆等组件的微观干预。实验覆盖3个开源模型、3个开源harness、5个答案型基准，其中2个模型另测2个交互式基准。

Result: harness并未稳定提升推理准确率：在模型单独无法解决的问题上的收益，可能被模型本可正确回答的问题上的损失抵消；技能引导与保留历史均不能可靠提升准确率；低token熵可能伴随错误答案，单次执行输出更少在准确率下降时可能意味着每个正确答案消耗更多输出token。

Conclusion: 行为与输出token使用必须与正确性联合评估；AlphaDiana将答案正确性与执行状态关联到轨迹和资源记录，可在共享任务与资源策略下比较智能体并诊断失败。

Abstract: Agents pair reasoning models with harnesses that organize model calls, execute tools, load skill instructions, and retain history in memory. Understanding agent performance requires linking execution conditions to outcomes, behavior, and resource use. We introduce AlphaDiana, a measurement system with modular interfaces for datasets, agents, environments, and scorers. It preserves harness-native control loops while controlling file reuse and memory retention separately. AlphaDiana supports macro analyses of complete agents and micro interventions on agent components such as tools, skills, and memory under matched conditions. We study three open-source models and three open-source harnesses on five answer-based benchmarks, with two models also tested on two interactive benchmarks. We find that harnesses do not consistently improve the accuracy of reasoning: gains on questions unsolved by the model alone can be outweighed by losses on questions it answers correctly without tools. This inconsistency extends to component interventions: neither skill guidance nor retained history reliably improves accuracy. Behavior and output-token use must be assessed with correctness: low token entropy can accompany wrong answers, and less output per execution can mean more output tokens per correct answer when accuracy falls. AlphaDiana links answer correctness and execution status to traces and resource records for comparing agents and diagnosing failures under shared task and resource policies.

</details>


### [190] [When Correctness Feedback Erases Evidence: Phase-Separated Completeness Auditing in Verus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20676)
*Adarsh Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示“正确性反馈”会抑制规格完整性审计本来要发现的证据（满足书面契约但违反意图的程序），在Verus/Rust任务上验证该目标反转并提出“先保留并认证失败、再修复”的分离式流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规格完整性审计旨在发现“满足书面契约但违反意图行为”的程序，而正确性反馈（corrective-feedback）在优化表面正确性的同时可能抑制这类证据的产生，导致两类目标相互冲突。作者希望实证这一目标反转并量化其对认证证据产出与规格修复的影响。

Method: 在12个Verus/Rust任务、4个模型族、匹配调用预算下对比：IntentShield的纠正反馈回路 vs 无反馈独立发现；加入格式匹配的迭代控制以区分反馈内容与独立重启的贡献；进行解析器修复检验；并采用阶段分离执行（Phase-Revised）与IS-Revised、Direct-Revised及弱化契约对比。

Result: 无反馈独立发现的认证见证产出为59.0%，显著高于IntentShield的27.1%；格式匹配迭代控制表明差距主要来自反馈内容而非独立重启，且在所有模型族中成立并通过解析器修复检验。阶段分离执行在不复用响应的情况下达到相同聚合产出，多数认证见证仍失败于留出用例。Phase-Revised规格修复的实现成功率为81.3%，IS-Revised为62.5%，Direct-Revised接近；两类无反馈见证来源均显著优于弱化契约。该效应在现有Dafny与Lean评估中未能复现。

Conclusion: 完整性审计与正确性修复的即时目标相互对立；有效性边界限于所研究的Verus/Rust工作流。实践上应先保留并认证被承认的失败，再进行修复。

Abstract: Correctness feedback suppresses the very evidence that specification-completeness auditing is designed to uncover: programs that satisfy a written contract but violate intended behavior. We demonstrate this objective reversal on 12 Verus/Rust tasks across four model families under matched call budgets. Independent no-feedback discovery more than doubles certified-witness yield relative to IntentShield's corrective-feedback loop (59.0% versus 27.1%). A format-matched iterative control places the larger share of this gap in feedback content, not independent restarts. The advantage holds in every model family and survives parser repair. A separate phase-separated execution matches the aggregate yield without reusing responses; most certified witnesses also fail held-out cases. Preserving these witnesses delivers stronger specification repair: Phase-Revised contracts achieve 81.3% implementation success, versus 62.5% for IS-Revised, with Direct-Revised close behind. Both no-feedback witness sources substantially outperform weakened contracts. The effect does not reproduce in the available Dafny and Lean evaluations, establishing an empirical boundary around the studied Verus/Rust workflow. Completeness auditing and correctness repair have opposing immediate objectives. Preserve and certify the admitted failure before repairing it.

</details>


### [191] [Counterfactual Neighborhood Risk Fields: Evidence-Grounded Selective Safety for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20679)
*Tianshi Wang,Jingsong Wang,Fengling Li,Jingjing Li,Xin Li,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CNRF以“证据构建”替代“边界学习”，将风险方向与邻域支持分离，实现VLM的选择性安全干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于表征的VLM安全防御只学“有害/无害”分离，未界定风险分数的语义与可信条件，易把“不熟悉”误判为“有害”，且倾向于一律拒答。

Method: 构造上下文匹配的良性—有害反事实对，以其表征差作为与能力相关的风险方向；将邻域支持度与风险分数分开估计；设计受害者自适应接口以适配不同VLM架构的安全观测，并将选中请求路由至同模型响应复核，或放行可接受候选，或生成有害能力受限的替代回复。

Result: 在3个VLM与5类多模态越狱族上取得86.06–89.30%宏平均防御成功率，并保留可观的良性任务效用；受控实验表明查询匹配参照与保持上下文—方向对应关系能显著改善低假阳性区间的风险排序。

Conclusion: CNRF把基于表征的VLM安全从“学习有害性边界”重构为“为选择性风险决策构造可用证据”，支持选择性干预而非全面拒答。

Abstract: Representation-based safety defenses for vision-language models (VLMs) often learn to separate harmful from benign inputs, but separation alone does not establish what a risk score means or when it should be trusted. We introduce Counterfactual Neighborhood Risk Fields (CNRF), an evidence-grounded framework for selective VLM safety. CNRF constructs context-matched benign-harmful counterfactual pairs, uses their representation differences as capability-related risk directions, and estimates neighborhood support separately from risk. This separation prevents unfamiliarity from being conflated with harmfulness and enables selective intervention rather than blanket rejection. A victim-adaptive interface accommodates different safety observations across VLM architectures and routes selected requests for same-victim response review, which either releases acceptable candidates or produces constrained alternatives with reduced harmful capability. Across three VLMs and five multimodal jailbreak families, CNRF achieves 86.06-89.30% macro defense success rate while retaining substantial benign-task utility. Controlled experiments further show that query-matched references and preserved context-direction correspondence materially improve risk ranking in the low-false-positive regime. CNRF thus reframes representation-based VLM safety from learning harmfulness boundaries to constructing applicable evidence for selective risk decisions.

</details>


### [192] [IneqAgent: Agentic Inequality Proving in Lean with Symbolic Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20681)
*Gaolei He,Jinhui Li,Ruobing Zuo,Xia Zeng,Zhengfeng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IneqAgent：结合LLM推理与符号计算、以引理级MCTS协调的Lean4不等式证明智能体框架，四基准上186题解出175题（94.09%），并给出Dittert猜想四、五维加强的Lean验证及Holens–Djokovic (4,4)不等式的认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化不等式证明需要数学推理与精确计算，二者常通过中间引理和目标变形衔接；现有方法缺乏对引理构造、目标重写与符号后端选择的系统协调，导致成功率与推理成本难以兼顾。

Method: 提出IneqAgent框架：在Lean4中将LLM推理与符号计算结合；采用引理级蒙特卡洛树搜索协调引理构造、目标重构与符号后端选择；配备平方和（SOS）、Bernstein、逐次差分代换三类互补后端，为局部Lean证明生成精确证书；由智能体将这些局部证明与LLM生成的步骤整合，并利用Lean反馈构建完整证明并对原定理陈述进行校验。

Result: 在四个基准上，IneqAgent（DeepSeek-V4-Flash）解出186题中的175题（94.09%），为所评估方法中总体成功率最高；在四个骨干模型上，于低LLM推理成本阈值处的成功率高于同骨干的Direct Agent，且各方法成功运行的平均推理成本更低。此外获得Dittert猜想在四维、五维的Lean验证加强，并认证Holens–Djokovic (4,4)不等式，从而蕴含四维的Merris猜想。

Conclusion: 引理级MCTS协调的LLM推理与多符号后端证书生成，可在可控推理成本下显著提升Lean4不等式形式化证明的成功率，并支撑对公开猜想的加强性形式化验证。

Abstract: Formal inequality proving requires mathematical reasoning and exact computation, often connected through intermediate lemmas and goal reformulations. We introduce IneqAgent, an agentic framework combining LLM reasoning with symbolic computation for inequality proving in Lean4. Lemma-level Monte Carlo Tree Search coordinates lemma construction, goal reformulation, and symbolic backend selection. Complementary sum-of-squares, Bernstein, and successive difference-substitution backends produce exact certificates for local Lean proof generation. The agent integrates these proofs with LLM-generated steps and uses Lean feedback to construct complete proofs checked against the original theorem statements. On four benchmarks, IneqAgent with DeepSeek-V4-Flash solves 175 of 186 problems (94.09%), achieving the highest overall success rate among the evaluated methods. Across four backbones, it achieves higher success rates at low LLM inference cost thresholds than Direct Agent using the same backbone, with lower mean inference costs over each method's successful runs. We also obtain Lean-verified strengthenings of Dittert's conjecture in dimensions four and five, and certify the Holens--Djokovic $(4,4)$ inequality, implying Merris's conjecture in dimension four.

</details>


### [193] [L2G-SR: Learning to Guide LLM-based Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20685)
*Letian Huang,Xiangyu Wang,Haowei Lin,Di He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: L2G-SR 提出用学习到的结构验证器引导 LLM 符号回归搜索，以树编辑距离构建结构监督信号，在 LLM-SRBench 上取得数值拟合、方程级精度与 TED 的最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归目标是寻找解释观测数据的方程，但仅以数值误差作为搜索引导，容易偏向精确的替代式（surrogate），而非具有正确结构的候选式。

Method: 构建基于树编辑距离（TED）的结构监督信号，并考虑交换律算子与等价表达形式；验证器先将数值观测与公式对齐，再学习为候选式与目标公式的结构相似度打分。搜索时目标公式被隐藏，验证器在父代选择中与数值误差互补；分析师依据验证器反馈与诊断工具提出修改，生成器将其转化为精炼表达式。

Result: 在 LLM-SRBench 上，跨所评估的 LLM 骨干模型与领域，L2G-SR 在数值拟合、方程级精度和 TED 三项指标上均达到当前最优（state-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: L2G-SR 证明了学习到的结构引导对 LLM 符号回归具有重要价值。

Abstract: Symbolic regression searches for an equation that explains observed data. Numerical error is commonly used to guide the search, but it alone can favor an accurate surrogate over a candidate with the right structure. We propose L2G-SR, a framework for LLM-based symbolic regression that guides search with a learned structural verifier. We construct structural supervision signal using tree edit distance (TED), accounting for commutative operators and alternative expression forms. A verifier first aligns numerical observations with the formulas, then learns to score a candidate's structural similarity to the target formula. At search time, the target formula is hidden and the verifier complements numerical error in parent selection. An analyst then uses verifier feedback and diagnostic tools to propose revisions, which a generator converts into refined expressions. On LLM-SRBench, L2G-SR achieves state-of-the-art numerical fit, equation level accuracy and TED across the evaluated LLM backbones and domains. L2G-SR demonstrates the value of learned structural guidance for LLM-based symbolic regression.

</details>


### [194] [CARA: Causally Guided Retrieval Agent for Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22017)
*Weicheng Liu,Xiangcheng Wu,Depeng Xu,Haopeng Zhang,Xi Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练的因果引导检索智能体 CARA，用"后果性"与"必要性"两项因果相关性标准评估证据，在三个 STaRK 数据集上优于现有检索器，且探索节点更少、检索操作更少，同时提升下游问答质量与忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本丰富知识图上的 RAG 需要识别"对回答问题有贡献"的证据，而非仅匹配文本内容；现有智能体检索器虽能自适应平衡全局检索与多跳探索，但主要依赖文本相似度引导检索，导致证据选取与真实答案需求脱节。

Method: CARA 为免训练（training-free）的因果引导检索智能体：以两条互补准则显式评估证据的因果相关性——后果性（加入证据是否提升可回答性）与必要性（移除证据是否削弱可回答性）；将二者与路径一致性结合以引导图探索，并采用倒数排名融合（RRF）聚合多智能体的证据。为支持下游问答评估，在三个 STaRK 数据集上补充了标准答案。

Result: 在三个 STaRK 数据集上一致优于现有检索器；同时提升答案质量与忠实度，且检查的图节点更少、所需检索操作更少。

Conclusion: 因果相关性评估能使智能体图检索更聚焦、更有效，同时提升检索性能与下游问答表现，说明以因果相关性替代纯文本相似度作为检索信号具有实际价值。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation over text-rich knowledge graphs requires identifying evidence that contributes to answering a question, rather than merely matching its textual content. Existing agentic retrievers adaptively balance global search and multi-hop exploration but primarily rely on textual similarity to guide retrieval. We propose CARA, a training-free, CAusally guided Retrieval Agent that explicitly evaluates the causal relevance of retrieved evidence through two complementary criteria: consequentiality, measuring whether adding evidence improves answerability, and essentiality, assessing whether removing evidence weakens answerability. CARA integrates these assessments with path consistency to guide graph exploration and employs reciprocal rank fusion to aggregate evidence from multiple agents. We evaluate CARA on three STaRK datasets and augment STaRK with ground-truth answers to enable downstream question answering evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that CARA consistently outperforms existing retrievers. CARA also improves answer quality and faithfulness while examining fewer graph nodes and requiring fewer retrieval operations. These findings demonstrate that causal relevance assessment enables more focused and effective agentic graph retrieval, improving both retrieval performance and downstream question answering.

</details>


### [195] [When Reality Outpaces Cognition: A New Scaling Principle for Reliable Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20764)
*Ahsan Adeel,Talha Bin Riaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"连贯认知有效速度"(\(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\))作为理论与模型无关的新基准,衡量智能系统在"观测-现实"差距不断增大时仍能可靠推理与行动的速度上限;实验表明,受细胞机制启发的\(Co^4\)架构显著优于Transformer和门控MLP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI范式与世界模型是否具备真正的"理解"能力尚存疑。作者提出一个根本性问题:现实变化快到何种程度,智能系统就无法可靠地感知、推理和行动?这一视角可跳出具体理论或模型,直接检验系统的认知可靠性边界。

Method: 将问题形式化为一个与理论、模型无关的挑战:系统需在逐渐扩大的观测-现实差距下,为越来越遥远的未来状态推断可靠动作。用"连贯认知有效速度"\(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\)进行量化,即系统从基线起持续满足预设行为可靠性标准时所通过的最高"现实速度"\(S_R\)。作为概念验证,强化学习智能体在基线\(S_R\)下训练,随后在不重新训练、计算资源固定的条件下,将\(S_R\)提升至最高50倍进行评估;比较Transformer、门控MLP基线与受细胞机制(与意识加工和清醒思维相关)启发的\(Co^4\)架构。

Result: 在所有测试场景中,\(Co^4\)的\(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\)均显著高于Transformer和门控MLP基线,即在现实变速更大时仍能保持行为可靠性。

Conclusion: \(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\)可作为衡量智能系统认知可靠性的新基准;"连贯认知的规模化"可作为实现可靠AI的设计原则。

Abstract: This paper challenges the understanding capabilities of existing AI paradigms and emerging world models by asking a fundamental question: How quickly can reality change before an intelligent system can no longer reliably perceive, reason, and act? We formulate this as a theory- and model-agnostic challenge in which systems must infer reliable actions for increasingly distant future states across a growing observation--reality gap. We operationalize this challenge through the effective Speed of Coherent Cognition (\(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\)), defined as the highest tested Speed of Reality (\(S_R\)) through which a system continuously satisfies a predefined behavioural reliability criterion from baseline. As a proof of concept, reinforcement-learning agents are trained at the baseline \(S_R\) and evaluated, without retraining, as \(S_R\) is increased by up to \(50\times\) while computational resources remain fixed. We compare Transformer and gated-MLP baselines with \(Co^4\), an architecture inspired by cellular mechanisms implicated in conscious processing and awake thought. Across the tested scenarios, \(Co^4\) achieves substantially higher \(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\) than the baselines, motivating \(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\) as a new benchmark and the scaling of coherent cognition as a principle for reliable AI.

</details>


### [196] [ARIA: Runtime Inspection and Amendment for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20780)
*Bolin Shen,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARIA通过将执行验证过的修复转化为可复用监督数据，训练小型模型在多智能体系统运行过程中实时判断何时干预及如何修复，实现跨框架、跨宿主模型的泛化修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统中局部错误会被其他智能体继承放大，使可纠正错误演变为任务失败；而现有事后诊断或反复重执行搜索的方式无法提供可学习的实时干预策略。

Method: 采集四类多智能体框架、六个基准、八种宿主模型上超过24,000条成功/失败轨迹，构建首个大规模“失败轨迹—执行验证修复”配对数据集（3,953对，含错误定位、干预位置与继续执行方式）；据此训练紧凑模型进行MAS无关的运行时检查与修正，把修复搜索移到数据构建与训练阶段。

Result: 训练后的ARIA模型在事后与运行时两种设定下均表现出强修复性能，并能泛化到未见过的框架与宿主模型。

Conclusion: 将执行验证修复转化为可复用监督是学习运行时干预策略的有效途径，ARIA为多智能体系统提供了可泛化、免重复试错的修复范式，并公开了代码。

Abstract: In LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS), a local mistake can propagate as other agents build on it, turning a correctable error into a failed task. Diagnosing completed runs or repeatedly re-executing them to search for corrections does not provide a learned policy for intervening while execution is still in progress. We present Agent Runtime Inspection and Amendment (ARIA), a framework for learning when and how to repair ongoing multi-agent executions. Our key intuition is to turn execution-verified repairs into reusable supervision, moving repair search into data construction and training so that a learned model can intervene at runtime without repeatedly testing alternatives. First, we collect over 24{,}000 successful and failed trajectories across four multi-agent frameworks, six benchmarks, and eight host models. From these trajectories, we construct the ARIA dataset, to our knowledge the first large-scale dataset pairing failed multi-agent trajectories with execution-verified repairs. It contains 3{,}953 verified repair pairs, each specifying what went wrong, where to intervene, and how execution should continue. Then, we develop the ARIA framework for MAS-agnostic runtime inspection and amendment, designed to integrate with any MAS that exposes execution trajectories and supports continuation replacement. Using verified repairs as training examples, we teach a compact model to recognize when intervention is needed and how to correct an ongoing run. The aim is to learn repair strategies that apply to related failures in new configurations, rather than merely reproduce individual corrections. Finally, the trained ARIA model demonstrates strong repair performance in both post-hoc and runtime settings, with generalization to unseen frameworks and host models. Our code is available at https://github.com/LabRAI/ARIA.

</details>


### [197] [Open-world AI: a Tale of Three Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20782)
*Jianyu Zhang,Léon Bottou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文区分"封闭世界"与"开放世界"两种机器学习设定，指出开放世界要求机器具备推理时学习、丰富表征、预测性解耦三项特性，并在至多8B的语言模型上做定性验证：满足要求的模型随相关任务数据积累而提升，不满足者反而退化，规模扩大与层类型交错都无法修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 封闭世界假设任务固定、数据iid、可用固定判据采集样本以覆盖任务；开放世界则要求通用性——从少量样本、少人工先验中接手非预设任务，且数据成组而非打乱、任务事先未知，因此需要新的理论刻画与判据。

Method: 将任务建模为共享潜在因子，对训练目标做分解，推导出三项要求：推理时学习（第一要求）、保留超出iid泛化所需的丰富表征、使输入输出对齐为匹配低维组的预测性解耦；分析还预测需一致的内层学习器，以及跨相关任务的长上下文带来收益。实验在至多8B参数的语言模型上做定性检验。

Result: 满足要求的模型在相关任务数据累积时性能提升；不满足要求的模型则退化，且模型规模扩大与层类型交错（interleaving）均无法修复该问题。

Conclusion: 开放世界通用性可由共享潜在因子的目标分解刻画为可检验的性质；推理时学习、丰富表征与预测性解耦是必要条件，而单纯扩大规模或调整层结构不足以替代这些性质。

Abstract: The closed world asks AI for specialized competence and lets it manufacture the iid data its theory assumes: a fixed criterion licenses collecting examples until the task is covered. The open world asks for versatility --- taking up tasks a machine was not built for, from few examples and with little prior from a human designer --- and offers data that is grouped, not shuffled. Its tasks are not known in advance, so a machine must do inference-time learning (1st requirement). Modeling tasks as sharing latent factors and decomposing the training objective, we derive two more: a *rich representation*, retaining more than iid generalization requires, and a predictive disentanglement that aligns inputs and outputs into matched low-dimensional groups. The analysis further predicts the need for a consistent inner learner, and the benefit of long contexts spanning related tasks. We test these claims on language models up to 8B, asking whether a model has a property (qualitative) rather than how much it wins (quantitative). Models that meet the requirements improve as data from related tasks accumulates; the rest degrade, and neither scale nor layer-type interleaving repairs this.

</details>


### [198] [EvolvingBrain: An Adaptive Skill System for Multi-modal Agents Self-Evolution in Open Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20786)
*Runxuan Liu,Jingqi Shi,Baoxin Wang,Honghong Zhao,Haoyuan Xu,Yueqing Sun,Jiafeng Liang,Tao He,Dayong Wu,Qi Gu,Shijin Wang,Ming Liu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 EvolvingBrain，用自适应技能体系作为多模态智能体自我进化的大脑，通过分层技能自进化与状态感知自适应执行提升开放世界任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态智能体在开放世界交互中进展显著，但自我进化能力有限：监督微调只能模仿、无法主动组织经验；基于结果的强化学习虽促进探索，却未充分利用中间轨迹信息。

Method: 两大模块：(1) 分层技能自进化——从模型特定轨迹中抽取过程模式（Procedural Patterns）与问题模式（Problematic Patterns），通过生成技能总结经验，并随开放世界多次交互迭代进化，最终形成由领域（Domain）、计划（Plan）、动作/陷阱（Action/Trap）技能构成的分层技能架构，提供多粒度知识库与新的进化方式；(2) 状态感知自适应执行——将长时程交互总结为结构化抽象记忆（Structured Abstractive Memory），据此引导状态感知技能融合策略，使智能体按当前状态调用知识。

Result: 在 VAB-Minecraft 与 DiscoveryWorld 上的实验表明，EvolvingBrain 提升了开放世界任务的成功率，并展现出泛化能力。

Conclusion: 以分层技能体系替代纯模仿或纯结果驱动范式，可有效利用轨迹中的中间信息实现智能体持续自我进化；代码将在正式版公开。

Abstract: Multi-modal agents have made significant progress in interacting with open worlds. However, their ability to self-evolve remains limited. Supervised fine-tuning enables imitation but not active experience organization, while outcome-based reinforcement learning promotes exploration yet underuses intermediate trajectory information. Therefore, we propose EvolvingBrain, which uses an adaptive skill system as the brain for agent self-evolution, consisting of two following components. Stratified Skill Self-Evolution: extracts Procedural Patterns and Problematic Patterns from model-specific trajectories, summarizes experience by generating skills, and evolves through multiple interactions with the open world, ultimately forming a Stratified Skill Architecture consisting of Domain, Plan, and Action/Trap Skills, which provides the agent with a multi-granularity knowledge repository and a novel evolution approach. State-Aware Adaptive Execution: summarizes long-horizon interactions into Structured Abstractive Memory and uses it to guide the State-Aware Skill Fusion Strategy, enabling the agent to utilize knowledge according to its current state. Experiments on VAB-Minecraft and DiscoveryWorld demonstrate that EvolvingBrain improves success rates on open-world tasks and exhibits generalization capabilities. Our code is provided in the supplementary materials and will be publicly released in the camera-ready version.

</details>


### [199] [When Reasoning Traces Conflict with Evidence: How Answer Configuration Shapes Evidence Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20805)
*Shaoang Li,Yanhang Shi,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文区分了推理轨迹（reasoning trace）在答案生成中的两种作用——提升准确率与在冲突时"压过"当前证据——并指出延续模型自身思考（continuation）与独立答案调用（separate call）是两种可分离的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保留到答题时的推理轨迹既能提高准确率，也可能在与新证据矛盾时与当前证据竞争。已有评估仅用"删除轨迹"无法揭示答案究竟依据哪个来源，因此需要拆解轨迹的这两重角色。

Method: 将经核验的证据版本与基于各版本生成的轨迹交叉组合，在同一轨迹与证据下用两种配置作答：延续模型自身思考，以及独立的答案调用。在 gpt-oss、Gemma4、Qwen3 三个模型家族上，于 HotpotQA 与 MuSiQue 上评测，并辅以消融分析（最终行价值、金证据时机、干扰项、重新推理 vs 验证指令）。

Result: 独立答案调用比延续配置在遵循证据上高出 29.9–60.6 个百分点；删除轨迹在两种配置下都使准确率下降相近幅度；延续配置在多数冲突中重复陈旧结论，而独立调用更常遵循当前证据。此外，轨迹最后一行承载其大部分价值，金证据在轨迹写入前帮助最大，干扰项削弱独立调用下的纠错，重新推理比验证指令更常遵循当前证据。

Conclusion: "受益于轨迹"与"服从轨迹"是两种不同性质；仅靠删除轨迹无法判断答案来源，评估应同时报告准确率获益与证据遵循性，并注明作答配置。

Abstract: A reasoning trace retained at answer time plays two roles: it can raise accuracy, and it can compete with the current evidence when the two disagree. We disentangle these roles by crossing verified evidence versions with the traces generated from each and answering from the same traces and evidence under two configurations: continuation of the model's own thinking and a separate answer call. Across three model families (gpt-oss, Gemma4, Qwen3) on HotpotQA and MuSiQue, the separate call raises evidence-following over continuation by 29.9 to 60.6 points. On the same pairs, removing the trace lowers accuracy by similar amounts under both configurations. Yet continuation repeats the stale conclusion in most conflicts, and the separate call follows the current evidence far more often. Benefiting from a trace and deferring to it are therefore distinct properties. Further analyses show that the final line of a trace carries most of its value, gold evidence helps most before the trace is written, distractors weaken correction under a separate call, and renewed reasoning, unlike a verification instruction, follows the current evidence in most conflicts. Because trace deletion alone cannot reveal which source an answer follows, evaluations should report both properties together with the answer configuration.

</details>


### [200] [A Self-Improving Agent that Grows its Own DSL: Oracle-Free Operator Induction for Exact Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20809)
*Yun-Shiuan Chuang,Yirou Ge,Yixi Li,Yiming Bian,Zijie Pan,Jenna L. Ballard,Uma Kona,Linsey Pang,Prakhar Mehrotra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种无需奖励、评判模型或答案键的自改进机制：单个冻结模型在精确DSL上运行双层循环，内层靠两个独立实现的表达式全记录一致来答题，外层自动发现能力缺口并自撰算子，仅当第二个独立实现一致且满足守恒律时被接纳。该准入规则在138个有bug算子变体中全部拒绝，而传统"能跑即收""自一致性""压缩"信号大多放任；机制在三个不相关领域把可答问题集扩大约3至20倍，并可跨模型家族迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进智能体以高奖励、说服评判模型或压缩技能库作为保留标准，即使技能本身错误也可能被保留；但在答案必须精确正确（如交易表总计非对即错）的场景中，这种信号不可靠。作者追问：能否在没有奖励、没有评判、没有答案键的条件下，仍让智能体精确正确地扩展其可计算能力。

Method: 单个冻结模型围绕一个小型精确领域特定语言（DSL）运行两个嵌套循环：内层循环仅当两个独立编写的表达式在所有记录上一致时才回答新问题；外层循环自行识别缺失能力并为其编写算子，仅在独立的第二份实现结果一致且输出满足守恒律时才允许该算子入库——全程不使用ground truth。并设置无归纳对照组，在不同模型家族与不同领域上做迁移验证。

Result: 内层循环使可答问题集约增至三倍；外层循环在不相交起始集上自扩展，发明出起始闭包之外的算子，而无归纳对照组对新问题一个都答不出。准入规则经验上可靠：在独立参考下拒绝138/138个有bug算子变体（包括由不同模型家族编写的），而"能跑即收"、自一致性、压缩等常用信号则接纳其中大多数。未经修改的同一机制在商业账本与临床就诊问答两个模式不相关的领域分别将可答集扩大十倍以上和近二十倍；跨模型家族迁移时门控仍保持可靠，仅归纳产出随各提议者的DSL熟练度变化。

Conclusion: 无需奖励、评判与答案键，仅凭双重独立实现一致性与守恒律约束，即可构建可靠且可迁移的精确自改进循环；该准入规则显著优于领域常用的伪验证信号，为答案必须精确正确的场景提供了可落地的自我扩展范式。

Abstract: A self-improving agent has to decide which new skills to keep, and in practice it keeps whatever earns a high reward, convinces a judge model, or compresses its library, even when the skill is wrong. That is fine until the answers have to be exactly right: over a table of transactions, a total is either right or wrong. We ask a harder question: can an agent grow what it can compute with no reward, no judge, and no answer key, and still be exactly right? A single frozen model runs two nested loops over a small, exact domain-specific language (DSL). The inner loop answers new questions only when two independently written expressions agree on every record. The outer loop discovers its own missing capability and authors an operator for it, admitted only when an independent second implementation agrees and the result obeys a conservation law, all without ground truth. The inner loop roughly triples the answerable-question set, and the outer loop self-extends on disjoint starting sets, inventing operators outside the starting closure; a no-induction control answers none of the new questions. The admission rule is empirically sound: it rejects 138 of 138 buggy operator variants under an independent reference, even one written by a different model family, where the admit-if-it-runs, self-consistency, and compression signals the field relies on admit most of them. Unmodified, the same mechanism self-extends on two further domains with unrelated schemas, business-ledger and clinical-encounter question answering, growing their answerable sets more than tenfold and nearly twentyfold. The loop transfers across model families too: run with further proposers, the gate stays sound and independent families self-extend comparably, with only the induction yield tracking each proposer's DSL fluency.

</details>


### [201] [Temporal Specification Guidance for Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20822)
*Avishree Khare,Hideki Okamoto,Bardh Hoxha,Georgios Fainekos,Rajeev Alur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将时序要求表示为形式化时序逻辑规范，并给出免训练推理期框架TSG，用可微定量语义的梯度引导采样，在5个模型上将时序约束满足准确率较CFG提升9.5–20个百分点且显存开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生视频模型常能生成提示中的视觉概念，但难以满足概念出现的顺序、时序与持续性要求；现有方法缺少可形式化表达并可执行控制的时序接口。

Method: 将时序需求写成形式化时序逻辑规范；TSG在推理采样阶段把规范转成可执行目标：用可微定量语义对视觉概念证据轨迹打分，用该分数梯度构造规范引导的去噪方向。具体实例化采用视频-文本注意力轨迹、Metric Temporal Logic的高效可微评分与梯度导出的注意力偏置，无需外部概念分类器，也不需对视频Transformer反向传播；其注意力引导前向传播产生去噪方向，用于增强CFG。

Result: 在涵盖11类时序约束（含有序物体进入与状态转换）的220条提示基准上，该实例化在五个模型上将规范满足准确率相对CFG提升9.5–20个百分点，显存开销极小。

Conclusion: 结果表明，时序规范与可微满足度目标可作为控制视频生成的灵活接口。

Abstract: Text-to-video models often generate the visual concepts requested in natural language prompts but fail to satisfy their intended ordering, timing, and persistence requirements. We propose representing these requirements as specifications in a formal Temporal Logic, and introduce Temporal Specification Guidance (TSG), a training-free inference-time framework that turns temporal specifications into executable objectives for guiding video generation during sampling. TSG scores traces of visual concept evidence using differentiable quantitative semantics and uses the score's gradients to construct a specification-guided denoising direction. We instantiate this framework with video-text attention traces, efficient differentiable scores for Metric Temporal Logic, and gradient-derived attention biases. This instantiation requires neither an external concept classifier nor backpropagation through the video transformer. Its attention-steered forward pass yields a denoising direction that augments classifier-free guidance (CFG). On a benchmark of 220 prompts spanning 11 categories of temporal constraints, including ordered object entry and state transitions, this instantiation improves specification satisfaction accuracy over CFG by 9.5-20 percentage points across five models, with minimal memory overhead. These results demonstrate the potential of temporal specifications and differentiable satisfaction objectives as a flexible interface for controlling video generation.

</details>


### [202] [Confidence-Calibrated Inference Expansion for Evaluator-Guided Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20836)
*Weida Liang,Kenji Kawaguchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将评估器引导推理形式化为算力感知的三动作控制器（信任当前答案/回退/继续扩展），基于动作间隙与期望后悔分析学习并校准动作置信度，在宽度扩展与修订任务上以更低在线算力取得更高效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估器引导推理在每一步都面临局部控制问题：直接信任当前答案、回退到备选，还是投入更多推理算力。现有方法缺乏对这种逐步骤资源分配的显式成本权衡与理论刻画。

Method: 构建算力感知的三动作控制器：终态动作按预测答案成功率打分，扩展动作按基于rollout的延续成功率替代信号打分，并显式计入计算成本；给出局部动作间隙分析（刻画评分误差何时可能改变决策）与考虑实际扩展路径误差的期望后悔界；据此学习并校准各动作的置信度估计，并对模糊决策施加局部不确定性惩罚。

Result: 在留出的宽度扩展与修订任务上，该控制器相比经校准选择的固定策略提升了观测效用，同时将归一化在线算力分别降低约30%和16%。在Qwen3-8B的四个随机种子上，两种设置的算力均低于拟合Q，但效用差异较小且方向不一。

Conclusion: 结果将评估器引导推理重新定位为不确定性下的资源分配问题，而非普遍更强的推理器；控制器的收益主要体现在算力节省，效用提升有限且不稳定。

Abstract: Evaluator-guided reasoning presents a local control problem at every step: trust the current answer, return a fallback, or spend more inference. We formulate this choice as a compute-aware three-action controller, scoring terminal actions by predicted answer success and expansion by a rollout-based continuation-success surrogate, with compute cost priced explicitly. A local action-gap analysis characterizes when score errors can change a decision, while an expected regret bound accounts for errors along the realized expansion path. Guided by this analysis, we learn and calibrate action-specific confidence estimates, with local uncertainty penalties for ambiguous decisions. On held-out width expansion and revision tasks, the controller improves observed utility over calibration-selected fixed policies while reducing normalized online compute by about 30% and 16%, respectively. Across four Qwen3-8B seeds, compute is lower than fitted Q in both settings, while utility differences are small and mixed. The results frame evaluator-guided reasoning as resource allocation under uncertainty, rather than a uniformly stronger reasoner.

</details>


### [203] [FDE-Gym: The Last Mile of AI Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22350)
*Dongxu Yang,Hongda Yan,Renxin Wang,Baoli Zhang,Haojia Zuo,Ying Zhao,Youmin Chen,Xiaojian Liao,Congming Gao,Tianyang Jiang,Yixiao Chen,Qing Wang,Zhiguo Yu,Xiaqing Li,Yuhao Zhang,Jiawen Sun,Renzhe Xu,De Mi,Tiancheng Wu,Xianya Fu,Xuanlu Zhao,Bo Feng,Meixuan Ren,Guanming Lin,Ziheng Zhou,Xinhua Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出FDE-Gym基准，包含18个行业20个客户场景的95个端到端交付任务，以客户业务指标提升（0到1归一化）并计入交互惩罚来评测智能体，揭示其在真实交付环境中的显著能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准多基于issue报告、规格说明或任务工单等"良定义"任务，而真实客户交付中前置部署工程师（FDE）需澄清模糊需求、在运行系统中工作并挖掘未明示约束，二者存在本质差异，缺乏对应评测手段。

Method: 构建FDE-Gym：95个来自真实项目的端到端交付任务，由行业专家改写；在含脚本化利益相关者与可修改客户系统的仿真环境中，智能体需与干系人交互并在类生产系统上作业；评分以客户自身成功指标提升为准，归一化至0（系统未变）到1（参考解），并对消息、试运行与等待时间施加交互惩罚；另设在线平台供人类以同一标准训练。

Result: 评测7家厂商13种模型配置：最佳配置平均得分0.56，95个任务中仅14个达标（得分≥0.8且无事故）；未明示约束的发现是主要瓶颈，缺乏显式引导时性能骤降；即便在最佳配置已从干系人处获取全部关键事实的23个任务上，也仅10个达到0.8以上的归一化业务提升。

Conclusion: 解决良定义任务与为真实客户交付有效AI系统之间存在实质性差距，信息发现不足并非唯一原因，端到端交付能力仍有待提升。

Abstract: Existing agent benchmarks typically evaluate agents on well-specified tasks defined by issue reports, specifications, or task tickets. Real-world customer delivery is different: forward-deployed engineers (FDEs) must clarify ambiguous requirements, work in live systems, and uncover unstated constraints. We introduce FDE-Gym, a benchmark of 95 end-to-end delivery tasks from 20 customer scenarios across 18 sectors, each adapted by industry experts from real-world delivery projects. In simulated customer environments with scripted stakeholders and modifiable customer systems, agents interact with stakeholders and operate on production-like systems to deliver working solutions. FDE-Gym scores a delivery by how much it improves the customer's own success metric, normalized between 0 (unchanged system) and 1 (reference solution), with penalties for stakeholder interactions (messages, trial runs, and waiting time). An online platform lets humans practice FDE skills under the same grading criteria. We evaluate 13 model configurations from seven providers. In the realistic setting, where unstated constraints must be discovered, the best configuration achieves a mean FDE-Gym score of 0.56 and resolves only 14 of the 95 tasks (a score of at least 0.8 with no incident). Discovering unstated constraints is the primary bottleneck: performance drops sharply when models must uncover them without explicit guidance. Yet information discovery alone does not account for the gap: even on the 23 tasks where the best configuration obtains every key fact from stakeholders, it reaches a normalized business uplift of 0.8 or above on only 10. These results reveal a substantial gap between solving well-specified tasks and delivering effective AI systems for real customers.

</details>


### [204] [Evolving Together, Failing Apart: The Skill-Policy Co-evolution Trap in Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20845)
*Yujie Feng,Xiaobin Wang,Jinluan Yang,Xiaonan Shi,Pengjun Xie,Philip S. Yu,Xiaoming Wu,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示技能-策略协同进化方法的评估盲区：策略在技能库漂移后变得脆弱，并提出面向部署的评估协议 CODA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同进化方法仅在训练所用的“交付技能库”上评估策略，而部署后技能被更频繁地重写/增删，导致策略面对漂移技能库时性能未知；该盲区带来的脆弱性此前未被刻画。

Method: 先通过理论分析与实证分析证明该脆弱性非偶然，指出各方法共用的“协作式接受”规则将策略能力绑定于协同进化的技能库；进而提出 CODA（协同进化漂移审计）评估协议，模拟技能漂移，并在最坏漂移下成功率之外引入两项新指标：语义保持编辑下的一致率、对损坏技能的抗性率。

Result: 在四种协同进化方法与五个基准（六种设置）上，最坏漂移下的成功率比各交付配对峰值低 5–13 个点，抵消了交付技能库相对无技能基线增益的 52–93%。

Conclusion: 协同进化的目标应从协同进化库上的峰值性能，转变为“无论技能如何演化都能利用技能”的能力；CODA 为后续方法提供统一审计标准。

Abstract: Skill-policy co-evolution, in which an agent's policy model and its skill bank improve each other during training, is a promising paradigm for self-improving agents. Current evaluations score the co-evolved policy on the final bank it trained with (the delivered bank). After deployment, however, skills may be rewritten, added or removed more frequently than the policy model is retrained, leaving the policy to operate on a drifted skill bank that neither the training objective nor existing evaluation protocols address. In this paper, we first establish, through theoretical and empirical analysis, that the fragility hidden by this blind spot is not incidental. The acceptance rule these methods share (cooperative acceptance) ties a policy's competence to the bank it co-evolved with, leaving it fragile once skills drift, regardless of the implementation; empirically, sensitivity to skill drift grows as co-evolution training proceeds. We therefore introduce the CO-evolution Drift Audit (CODA), a deployment-oriented evaluation protocol that simulates skill drift and, alongside success rate under worst-case drift, introduces two new metrics: a consistency rate under meaning-preserving edits and a resistance rate to corrupted skills. Across four co-evolution methods and five benchmarks (six settings), worst-case success is 5-13 points below the peak measured for each delivered pair, erasing 52-93% of the delivered bank's gain over the no-skill baseline. CODA thus reframes the target of co-evolution, from peak performance on the co-evolved bank to the ability to harness skills however they evolve, and provides a shared standard against which future methods can be audited.

</details>


### [205] [Anticipatory Commonsense with Moving Objects: An ASP-Based Framework for Predictive Reasoning with Interacting Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20867)
*Julius Monsen,Jakob Suchan,Mehul Bhatt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个混合、模块化、容忍细化的声明式框架，用于复杂动态环境中交互运动的预期推理，在语义层面构建可解释的时空心智模型，以启发式引导低层运动计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端随机预测缺乏显式、可检验的事件级假设，难以解释；需要在语义层面形成可解释的预期性时空心智模型，并以其启发式指导低层运动计算。
