Method: 三部分组成：1）刻画交互运动轨迹动力学的ASP（回答集编程）模型；2）语义引导的预测，用于推导“分支式轨迹未来”；3）基于能量的优化模型，对预期结果进行评估、排序与筛选。

Result: 在Argoverse 2基准上进行评估；每条预期轨迹均附带显式、可检视的事件级假设，区别于端到端随机预测。

Conclusion: 声明式与语义引导的混合框架能够在交互运动预期中提供可解释、模块化且容忍细化的推理，并以事件级假设支撑轨迹预测。

Abstract: We present a general declarative framework for anticipatory reasoning about interactive motion in complex dynamic environments. Our focus is on (explainable) anticipatory space-time mental model formation at a semantic level to heuristically guide low-level motion computation. Our method is hybrid, modular and elaboration tolerant integrating: 1) An ASP model characterising the dynamics of interactive motion trajectories; 2) Semantically-guided prediction for deriving `branching trajectory futures'; and 3) An energy-based {optimisation} model to evaluate, rank and filter anticipations. We evaluate with the Argoverse 2 benchmark, where each anticipated trajectory carries an explicit, inspectable event-level hypothesis, unlike end-to-end stochastic prediction.

</details>


### [206] [A Multi-Tiered AI Accessibility Model (MAIAM): Proportionate Access and Capability Limits for Comprehensive Frontier AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20921)
*Chai K. Toh,Vasu Raj Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出多层级AI可及性模型MAIAM，将AI访问权按目的、安全与能力分级，而非按模型发布形态（API或权重）二分，并与SAFE AI Operating Point结合为"能力边界+可及性边界"的双部框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于能力型AI发布的争论把选择视为二元：要么置于API之后，要么公开权重。这种框架混淆了两个独立决策——系统可以达到多强的能力，以及谁能以何种条件获得该能力。论文聚焦第二个决策，主张仅设能力上限不足以保障安全，还需分层的访问治理。

Method: 提出MAIAM，按目的、安全与能力划分三级访问：Tier 1"学习用AI"广泛可及但刻意设能力上限；Tier 2"探索用AI"面向研发，需注册、身份与使用控制，并随能力提升而收紧；Tier 3"全规模前沿AI"仅通过结构化、可记录、可问责的渠道提供。给出沿能力、自主性、领域后果、使用/安全四个轴的层级判定标准；论证发布前沿级权重会丧失发布后的一切安全杠杆，并引用"数百美元即可剥离开放权重的安全微调"的证据说明基座模型评测难以代表其衍生模型；最后将MAIAM与SAFE AI Operating Point整合为双部框架——固定不可逾越安全限的"能力边界"与固定其下谁可获取的"可及性边界"。

Result: 论文给出层级归属的四轴判定准则与双部框架的能力/可及性边界划分；并指出开放权重发布使安全杠杆失效、基座模型评测对衍生模型信息量有限。

Conclusion: 可及性应与能力、安全与风险成比例。能力限制本身不足以使AI安全，仍需由监管机构实施的分层访问制度。

Abstract: Most debate about releasing capable AI systems treats the choice as binary: keep the model behind an API, or publish the weights. That framing runs together two separate decisions. One is how capable a system may become. The other is who may obtain a given capability, and on what terms. This paper is about the second decision. We propose a Multi-Tiered AI Accessibility Model (MAIAM) that sorts access by purpose, safety, and capability instead of by release artifact. Tier 1, AI for Learning, is broadly accessible and deliberately capability-bounded. Tier 2, AI for Exploration, serves research and development under registration, identity, and usage controls that tighten as capability rises. Tier 3, Full-Scale Frontier AI, is available only through structured, recorded, and accountable access. The principle behind the tiers is that accessibility should be proportional to capability, safety, and risk. We show why publishing frontier-class weights forfeits every safety lever a developer has after release, and, using evidence that safety fine-tuning can be stripped from open weights for a few hundred dollars, why an evaluation of the base model says little about its derivatives. We give criteria for assigning a system to a tier along capability, autonomy, domain-consequence, and usage/safety axes, and combine MAIAM with the SAFE AI Operating Point into a two-part framework: a Capability Boundary that fixes the safety limits not to be crossed, and an Accessibility Boundary that fixes who may obtain a capability below that limit. Capability limits alone do not make AI safe; a tiered access regime administered by regulating bodies is also needed.

</details>


### [207] [Defining and Generating Compositional Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20922)
*Eric Elmoznino,Tejas Kasetty,Blaise Agüera y Arcas,Yoshua Bengio,Guillaume Lajoie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文用算法信息论形式化"组合结构"：当各部分被联合建模时显现出分别建模所无法捕获的共享结构，即存在组合性；并据此反向生成具有层级复用与部分-整体组织的对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合性（部件的复用与重组）是自然与人工系统复杂性的根本来源，人类识别它轻而易举，但刻画"组合结构"直觉含义的数学理论仍很有限，缺乏不预设结构形式的通用定义。

Method: 基于算法信息论（信息与压缩的数学），将组合结构定义为：部件联合考虑时呈现的共享结构，超出各部分单独建模所能捕获的部分；在多个尺度上应用该定义以定位组合结构出现的位置与方式；再用可计算的近似方法反转框架，在字符串与图像上直接优化，只指定期望的组合结构形式而不规定具体内容，从而生成组合性对象。

Result: 生成的字符串与图像表现出易于识别的层级复用与部分-整体组织，表明该定义刻画的是组合结构本身，而不仅仅是复杂性、规律性或共享信息。

Conclusion: 该框架为组合结构提供了原则性的信息论定义与可操作近似；并给出对机器学习的启示，包括关于神经架构"深度"的新视角，以及构建支持开放式学习的组合性数据课程原则。

Abstract: Compositional structure is pervasive in nature and across scales: atoms combine into molecules, molecules into cells, and cells into organisms, while further compositional hierarchies appear within each level. More broadly, compositionality, the reuse and recombination of parts into novel structures, is a fundamental source of interesting complexity in both natural and artificial systems. Yet despite its importance, and despite our apparent ease in recognizing it, mathematical accounts capturing what we intuitively mean by compositional structure remain limited. Here, we fill this gap by formalizing a simple idea: compositional structure emerges when parts, considered together, reveal shared structure that is not captured when those parts are modeled separately. We express this idea using algorithmic information theory, the mathematics of information and compression, which provides a natural way to identify structure in arbitrary data without specifying in advance what that structure must look like. Applied across scales, our definition informs where and how compositional structure emerges within an object. Using tractable approximations, we then invert the framework to generate compositional objects: we specify the desired form of their compositional structure without prescribing their particular content, and optimize directly over strings and images. The resulting objects exhibit readily recognizable hierarchical reuse and part-whole organization, suggesting that the definition captures compositional structure rather than merely complexity, regularity, or shared information. Finally, we discuss implications for machine learning, including a new perspective on depth in neural architectures and principles for constructing compositional data curricula capable of supporting open-ended learning.

</details>


### [208] [SkillNEO: Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization of Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20926)
*Enze Ma,Chunyu Miao,Yankai Chen,Jie Yang,Liancheng Fang,Kening Zheng,Hanrong Zhang,Muyao Xu,Xinle Yu,Chengze Li,Wei-Chieh Huang,Ziwei Ren,Yunhao Du,Zhixiang Shen,Xue Liu,Philip S. Yu,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillNEO：将"选用版本"与"继续改进版本"分离的非精英式技能进化，用相关版本执行证据指导保留与修复，使弱模型的部分进展沉淀为可复用的程序性知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent技能进化依赖执行反馈迭代改进技能，但改进常需多个相互依赖的编辑，低奖励中间版本仍有开发价值；强制即时奖励提升会过早丢弃有益的部分修复。因此需非精英进化，并需证据说明"保留什么、下一步修复什么"。

Method: 提出SkillNEO（面向Agent技能的非精英进化优化）：分离"选择使用版本"与"选择继续改进版本"；通过比较相关版本在同一任务上的表现，识别可保留的有效步骤与待修复的失败点；并可用后续证据回溯修订早期版本。由GPT-5.4 Mini执行任务、提出修订、编辑技能。

Result: 在8个领域86个SkillsBench任务上，GPT-5.4 Mini平均奖励由49.1%升至66.4%，进入GPT-5.5与GPT-5.6 Luna原始技能区间，增益覆盖全部8个领域。修改技能的41个任务上，复用技能使Luna提升10.5个百分点，使GPT-5.5高出其公开原始技能基线10.6个百分点；强模型无需再编辑即受益。搜索历史显示低分版本最终导向被采用的技能。

Conclusion: 技能进化可将较弱模型的部分进展转化为可复用的程序性知识，用于提升更强Agent的能力；非精英选择与证据导向的保留/修复策略是有效的技能进化范式。

Abstract: Agent skills turn procedural knowledge into reusable artifacts, allowing LLM agents to accumulate and refine expertise through experience. Building on this, skill evolution iteratively improves skills using execution feedback. However, improvement may require several interdependent edits, so low-reward versions can still be worth developing. Requiring immediate reward gains can discard useful partial repairs before their benefits emerge. Non-elitist evolution allows new versions to receive further edits despite scoring below earlier ones. For agent skills, this calls for evidence of what to preserve and repair next. We introduce SkillNEO (Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization for Agent Skills), which separately selects a version to use and versions to keep improving. Comparing how related versions perform the same task reveals working steps to preserve and remaining failures to fix. It can also revisit and revise earlier versions using later evidence. Across 86 SkillsBench tasks in eight domains, including software engineering and office work, SkillNEO raises GPT-5.4 Mini's average reward from 49.1% to 66.4%, within GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.6 Luna's original-skill range. Mini executes tasks, proposes revisions, and edits skills; final evaluation uses the same task instances. Gains span all eight domains. The same skills improve stronger models without further editing. On the 41 tasks with modified skills, reuse improves Luna by 10.5 percentage points and places GPT-5.5 10.6 points above its published original-skill reference. Execution records show these skills can benefit stronger models even when Mini still fails some task requirements. Search histories also show lower-scoring versions leading to skills ultimately selected for use. Together, these findings show that skill evolution can turn a weaker model's partial progress into reusable procedural knowledge for improving more capable agents.

</details>


### [209] [RAC: A Relational Agent Contract for Constraint-Governed Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20935)
*Kaiyuan Zheng,Fengqing Jiang,Yichen Feng,Zubin Aaron Assadian,Yuetai Li,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Luyao Niu,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAC：一种与harness无关的工程智能体动作与反馈层契约，将“语义决策”与“机械推导”分离，显著提升约束型工程任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程智能体需产出满足精确、耦合约束的工件，但现有方案要求智能体既做语义决策，又自行推导精确几何或时序等后果，导致约束主导型任务频繁失败。

Method: RAC规定权威观测、语义动作、基于工具的具化、独立验证与修复证据五要素：智能体只决定工件应满足的关系，由专用工具推导具体后果；并在PCB布局/布线/修复、作业车间调度、四类微控制器的141个嵌入式代码任务上评估。

Result: PCB任务中九个模型均取得最高干净通过率，平均90.9%（最强基线“仅需求委托”为74.0%）；调度任务中直接关系动作达96.3%质量通过率，对比同后端代码中介交付的59.6%。

Conclusion: 动作契约本身是智能体能力的来源：保留语义控制权同时委托可机械推导的输出，可提升约束型工件的可靠性。

Abstract: Engineering agents must produce artifacts that satisfy exact, coupled constraints. Existing solutions often require agents to make semantic decisions and also derive consequences such as precise geometry or timing from those decisions, leading to failures in constraint-governed engineering tasks. In this paper, we introduce RAC, a harness-agnostic contract for the action and feedback layer of engineering agents. It specifies authoritative observations, semantic actions, tool-based realization, independent verification, and repair evidence. The agent decides which relations an artifact should satisfy; specialized tools derive their concrete consequences. On PCB placement, routing, and repair tasks, RAC achieves the highest clean-pass rate for all nine evaluated models, averaging 90.9%, compared with 74.0% for the strongest baseline, requirements-only delegation. In job-shop scheduling, direct relational actions achieve 96.3% quality pass versus 59.6% for code-mediated delivery with access to the same scheduling backend. Our evaluations on 141 embedded-code tasks across four microcontroller identify the action contract as a source of agent capability: preserving semantic control while delegating mechanically derivable outputs improves the reliability of constrained artifacts.

</details>


### [210] [MAI-Gym: An Open All-in-One Platform for GUI Agent Evaluation and RL Training across Mobile and Computer Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20939)
*Panrong Tong,Quyu Kong,Pengxiang Li,Tianyu Xia,Hanzhang Zhou,Lei Wang,Chenglin Cai,Long Li,Long Chen,Jianan Zhang,Zhengwen Zeng,Xu Zhang,Gongjie Zhang,Yue Wang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAI-Gym：面向GUI智能体的统一开源平台，整合评估、数据采集与在线强化学习（覆盖移动端、PC与Web），通过共享rollout管线保证训练-评估一致性、支持10个基准与2万并行环境实例，并支持自动化合成可重置模拟应用及带执行校验的任务；据此训练的统一9B模型MAI-Gym-9B在10B以下开源GUI智能体中取得SOTA（MobileWorld 78.4%、OSWorld-Verified 76.2%），且模拟应用上训练的RL可迁移至真实手机场景（28.8%→48.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习配合可验证奖励已在数学、编程与工具调用上取得快速进展，但向GUI智能体扩展受限：交互环境成本高昂，且缺乏真实且具备挑战性的训练任务。现有平台在评估与训练管线割裂、环境规模与任务多样性不足方面存在瓶颈。

Method: 提出MAI-Gym一体化平台：(1) 共享rollout管线，使评估与训练中智能体行为保持一致；(2) 支持10个既有基准，并可扩展至20,000个并行环境实例；(3) 自动化合成多样GUI应用与高难度任务，包括可重置的模拟（mocked）应用与执行校验过的任务验证器，构建跨应用一致的世界；(4) 基于该平台训练统一的9B模型MAI-Gym-9B，同时覆盖移动端与计算机使用。

Result: MAI-Gym-9B在10B参数以下的开源GUI智能体中达到SOTA：MobileWorld 78.4%，OSWorld-Verified 76.2%。关键的是，在模拟购票应用中获得的RL能力可迁移到真实场景，物理手机上的任务成功率由28.8%提升至48.1%。

Conclusion: 可扩展的模拟环境结合可验证任务合成，能够有效支撑GUI智能体的在线强化学习，并向真实设备迁移。作者开源框架、合成技能、模拟应用、2,000条已验证任务及模型权重，以支持可复现研究与可扩展GUI智能体训练的后续工作。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards has driven rapid progress in mathematics, coding, and tool use, but scaling this success to GUI agents remains challenging due to the high cost of interactive environments and the scarcity of realistic training tasks. We introduce MAI-Gym, an open all-in-one platform that unifies GUI agent evaluation, data collection, and online RL training across mobile, computer, and web environments. Through a shared rollout pipeline, MAI-Gym ensures consistent agent behavior across evaluation and training, supports ten established benchmarks, and scales to 20,000 parallel environment instances. Beyond scalable infrastructure, MAI-Gym enables automated synthesis of diverse GUI applications and challenging tasks, including resettable mocked apps and coherent cross-app worlds with execution-validated task verifiers. Using MAI-Gym, we train MAI-Gym-9B, a unified 9B agent for mobile and computer use, achieving state-of-the-art performance among open GUI agents under 10B parameters: 78.4% on MobileWorld and 76.2% on OSWorld-Verified. Importantly, RL in a mocked train-ticketing app transfers to its real-world counterpart, improving task success on a phys- ical phone from 28.8% to 48.1%. We release the framework, synthesis skills, mocked apps, 2,000 verified tasks, and the model checkpoint to support repro- ducible research and further advances in scalable GUI agent training.

</details>


### [211] [LEV: Thinking Fast and Deep with Looped Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20951)
*Dingjie Song,Zhiling Yan,Yuxuan Zhang,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LEV决策模型：通过循环复用层而非生成token来"想得更深"，同一权重可自适应深度，在精度与计算间取得更好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型只在一次前向传播中对选项给出概率，深度在训练时固定，对易/难题花费相同计算；而文本推理模型可延长思考却要为每个解码token付出代价。

Method: LEV基于预训练为循环结构的语言模型，每步循环后读取选项并联合训练所有循环步，使单一checkpoint可在任意深度作答；并用小型stopper以无标注请求拟合阈值，实现按题分配深度。

Result: 三个循环骨干上精度随深度提升（同权重），主要增益在难题；2.6B LEV在JevBench上从深度1的63.2%升至深度4的76.2%；按需深度节省15%–24%计算，精度损失不足1点；锁定测试集上1.4B与3B在<50ms延迟下比同延迟dense模型及级联高5.4–8.8点。

Conclusion: 循环复用层的决策模型可在固定参数下实现可变计算深度，兼顾System 1式快速作答与更深推理，并在同等低延迟下优于dense模型与级联方案。

Abstract: Decision models answer typed questions about an input with one probability per option, computed in a single forward pass without generating text. Like the fast System 1 of dual-process theory, they answer at once, but their depth is fixed at training time, so they spend the same computation on easy and hard questions. Models that reason in generated text can think longer, but they pay for every extra step in decoded tokens. We present LEV, a decision model that thinks deeper by rerunning its layers in a loop instead of generating tokens. LEV builds on a language model pretrained as a loop, reads the options after every loop step and trains all steps jointly, so one checkpoint can answer at any depth. On three looped backbones, accuracy rises with depth on the same weights, mostly on hard questions, and a 2.6B LEV goes from 63.2% at depth one to 76.2% at depth four on JevBench. On-demand depth, set per question by a small stopper with a threshold fitted on unlabelled requests, saves 15% to 24% of the computation at a cost of under one accuracy point, level with or slightly above a fixed mix of depths. On a locked test set, the 1.4B and 3B models are 5.4 to 8.8 points more accurate than dense (non-looped) models and cascades of the same latency below 50 ms.

</details>


### [212] [StepSafe: Learning Timely Intervention with Context-Aware Safety Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20960)
*Siyi Gu,Kunhan Gao,Chao Peter Yang,Sukanta Ganguly,Rex Ying,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ASPO训练框架与StepSafe（4B）步骤级推理护栏，在五个智能体安全基准上取得开源护栏最优性能，Youden's J达53.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统安全具有时序性：某动作是否安全依赖先前动作、工具输出、信息披露与多智能体交互。现有工作虽已从轨迹级事后评估转向步骤级动作诊断与风险预测，但训练目标对"步骤证据如何随时间累积、何时足以支撑干预"缺乏监督信号。

Method: 1) Pre-fix级阶段对齐：在同一前缀下采样的多个响应间比较，奖励契合当前阶段的动作——风险未显时继续、澄清仍可避免危害时询问、危害不可逆前停止；2) 自适应反馈自蒸馏：以模型自身作教师，借助干预步骤上的步骤标注与从其自身误报中挖掘的规则，为决策背后的推理提供信用分配；3) 训练数据每步携带违规分类体系与干预窗口，并通过自演化多智能体红队扩展。

Result: StepSafe在五个智能体安全基准上，于安全-效用帕累托曲线上以Youden's J 53.0（4B）优于对比的46.2，达到开源护栏SOTA。

Conclusion: 将监督对齐到"步骤—阶段—干预时机"层面，配合自蒸馏推理信用与自演化红队数据，可有效实现步骤级及时干预；4B规模即可在安全-效用权衡上超越现有开源护栏。

Abstract: Safety in agentic systems is inherently sequential: whether an action is safe depends on prior actions, tool outputs, information disclosures, and interactions among agents. Recent work has shifted agent safety guardrails from post-hoc trajectory-level assessment toward step-level action diagnosis and proactive risk prediction, yet existing training objectives provide limited supervision for how step evidence accumulates over time and when it becomes sufficient to justify intervention. We propose \methodlong~(ASPO): a training framework for step-level timely intervention. Pre-fix level stage alignment allows crediting each decision at its own step: it compares each response with the other responses sampled at the same prefix and rewards the action that fits the stage, continuing before the risk is visible, asking while clarification can still avert harm, and stopping before harm is irreversible. Adaptive feedback self-distillation further credits the reasoning behind each decision, with the model itself as a teacher that sees the step annotations on intervention steps and rules mined from its own false alarms on safe steps. Its training data carry a violation taxonomy and an intervention window at every step and are extended through self-evolving multi-agent red-teaming. Finally we present \textsc{StepSafe}, a step-level reasoning guardrail with timely intervention, that achieves the state-of-the-art peformance among open-source guardrails across five agent-safety benchmarks on safety-utility pareto curve with Youden's $J$ of 53.0 (4B) against 46.2.

</details>


### [213] [MSP: Modality Self-Play for Training Multimodal LLMs Without Paired Cross-Modal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20965)
*Robert Moseley,Md Kaykobad Reza,Ameya Patil,Edward Ayrapetian,Salman Asif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Modality Self-Play (MSP)训练框架，用"代理文本描述"替代真实配对非文本数据，实现零样本模态组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模多模态模型依赖昂贵、难以跨模态组合扩展的配对多模态数据，成为进一步发展的根本瓶颈。

Method: 基于代理中介的模态组合：不使用配对跨模态数据，而以描述未见模态的"锚点支撑代理文本"作为语义桥梁；模型由观测模态生成互补未见模态的代理描述，经对比预训练编码器编码后，通过学习到的桥接与观测模态对齐，实现无需真实配对非文本监督的跨模态交互。

Result: 实现零样本模态组合：仅用单一真实锚点模态及代理描述训练的模型，推理时可整合并推理多模态，尽管训练中从未见过真实配对非文本数据；在多个多模态基准上取得SOTA或竞争性表现。消融表明学习桥接改善对齐，锚点支撑代理描述支持有效跨模态组合。

Conclusion: MSP免除了对真实配对非文本（如音视频）监督的需求，但仍依赖模态-文本对、预训练对比编码器与模态分布桥接校准；代理文本结合对比编码器可提供足以涌现多模态组合的语义结构。

Abstract: Large multimodal models have achieved remarkable progress by scaling model capacity and training on massive paired multimodal data. However, this paradigm introduces a fundamental bottleneck: aligned multimodal datasets are expensive to curate, difficult to scale across diverse modality combinations, and increasingly constrain further progress. We introduce Modality Self-Play (MSP), a training framework for multimodal foundation models based on proxy-mediated modality composition. Instead of relying on paired cross-modal data, MSP uses anchor-supporting proxy textual descriptions of unseen modalities as semantic bridges during training. Given an observed modality (e.g., image or audio), the model generates proxy descriptions for complementary unseen modalities, which are then encoded and aligned with the observed modality through learned bridges, enabling cross-modal interaction without real paired non-text supervision.
  Our central hypothesis is that proxy text, combined with contrastively trained pretrained encoders, provides sufficient semantic structure for multimodal composition to emerge. We show that MSP enables zero-shot modality composition: models trained only with a real anchor modality and accompanying proxy descriptions can integrate and reason over multiple modalities at inference time despite never observing real paired non-text data during training. Empirically, MSP achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance on multiple multimodal benchmarks. Ablation studies further demonstrate that learned bridges improve alignment, while anchor-supporting proxy descriptions enable effective cross-modal composition. MSP removes the need for real paired non-text (e.g., audio-vision) supervision, while continuing to rely on modality-text pairs, pretrained contrastive encoders, and modality-distribution bridge calibration.

</details>


### [214] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for HPC Job Scheduling: A Statistical Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21008)
*Justin M. Cheney,Clement N. Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文在统一非参数框架下，用带掩码随机对照检验DRL作业选择是否真正优于动作掩码本身，发现仅异构轨迹上的MaskablePPO相对对照有实质调度收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HPC作业选择研究多以启发式为唯一基线，无法区分性能提升来自DRL学习本身还是来自动作掩码（action masking）这一机制，因此需要引入与掩码处理种子配对的随机对照，以界定学习选择的真实增量价值。

Method: 比较6种DRL处理：PPO、A2C、DQN各含带掩码与不带掩码版本；基线为3种启发式及种子配对的带掩码均匀随机对照。在两条真实Slurm负载轨迹（同构与异构）上、每种10个随机种子、采用统一非参数检验框架开展评估。

Result: 所有带掩码处理均完成评估，未掩码处理被截断。同构轨迹上，无带掩码DRL处理在平均等待时间、平均与最大有界减速上显著优于对照。异构轨迹上MaskablePPO三项指标均优于对照：平均等待时间在10%误差内与三种启发式等效，最大有界减速比LCFS低22%。MaskablePPO在两条轨迹的完整处理中平均等待时间排名第一，但Nemenyi检验未检出与MaskableA2C的显著差异。带掩码变体中MaskablePPO平均训练时间最低，MaskableDQN平均决策延迟最低。

Conclusion: 研究区分了“完成评估”与“相对特定基线的调度增益”，表明DRL收益高度依赖轨迹特性与掩码机制；建议在评估学习型作业选择价值时纳入带掩码随机对照作为必要基线。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) offers learned job selection for High-Performance Computing (HPC), but performance against heuristics alone does not establish value beyond action masking. Six DRL treatments are compared: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), Advantage Actor-Critic (A2C), and Deep Q-Network (DQN), each with and without masking. Three heuristics and a seed-paired masked uniform random control provide baselines across ten seeds on two real Slurm (Simple Linux Utility for Resource Management) workload traces, one homogeneous and one heterogeneous, under a common non-parametric framework. All masked treatments complete evaluation, whereas unmasked treatments are truncated. On the homogeneous trace, no masked DRL treatment significantly improves average waiting time or average and maximum bounded slowdown over the control. On the heterogeneous trace, MaskablePPO improves all three metrics relative to the control. Its average waiting time is equivalent to that of the three heuristics within a 10% margin, and its maximum bounded slowdown is 22% lower than that of Last-Come, First-Served (LCFS). MaskablePPO has the leading average waiting-time rank among complete treatments on both traces, but Nemenyi comparisons detect no significant difference from MaskableA2C. Among masked variants, MaskablePPO has the lowest recorded mean training time, while MaskableDQN has the lowest mean decision latency. These findings distinguish completion from baseline-specific scheduling gains and support including a masked random control when assessing the value of learned selection.

</details>


### [215] [Failure Is the Mother of Success: Recovering Supervision with Grounding Hindsight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21018)
*Sheldon Yu,Tong Yu,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Zhendong Chu,Zhenwei Tang,Sungchul Kim,Lina Yao,Julian McAuley,Junda Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Replay-Selected Hindsight Distillation (RSHD)，将 GRPO 中失败的 rollout 转化为经过验证的额外监督信号，在全失败组与混合组上均可恢复学习信号，且不增加部署开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 依赖组内相对优势与成功样本，当某问题所有 rollout 均失败时，既无成功范例也无非零优势，导致模型恰好在对最需要学习的问题上无梯度信号；而失败推理本身仍可提示下一步尝试方向。

Method: 对每个失败尝试，策略在不见参考答案/解答的情况下写出纠正性笔记（corrective note），再将该笔记置于上下文中重新解题；仅当重放成功率高于原组、且经筛选排除直接给出答案的笔记时，才接纳该笔记，并将接纳的重放蒸馏进纯提示（plain-prompt）策略。接纳由新的成功而非失败 rollout 本身决定；只训练策略，唯一评判者为结果验证器（outcome verifier）。

Result: 在多个模型家族与规模、两个训练域上，RSHD 在域内基准上稳定优于 GRPO，且增益可迁移到域外评测集。

Conclusion: 基于重放成功率的事后笔记筛选可作为 GRPO 的有效补充监督来源，能从全失败组与混合组失败中回收信号，且部署时无需笔记或额外推理。

Abstract: When every rollout for a problem fails, GRPO receives neither a successful exemplar nor a nonzero group-relative advantage, so it learns nothing from exactly the problems it most needs to learn. The failed reasoning can nevertheless suggest what to try next. We present Replay-Selected Hindsight Distillation (RSHD), which turns failed attempts into an additional, tested source of supervision during GRPO training. The policy writes a corrective note about each failed attempt without seeing a reference answer or solution, then re-solves the problem with the note in context. A note is admitted only if its replays succeed more often than the original group did, after screening for notes that state the answer, and an admitted replay is distilled into the plain-prompt policy. Because admission is decided by fresh successes rather than by the failed rollout itself, the procedure can recover supervision from groups in which every attempt failed, and it applies equally to failures in mixed groups. Only the policy is trained, the only judge is the outcome verifier, and deployment requires no notes or extra inference. Across model families and sizes and two training domains, RSHD consistently improves over GRPO on in-domain benchmarks, and the gains carry over to out-of-domain evaluation sets.

</details>


### [216] [Recursive Reward Improvement via Adversarial Meta-Prompt Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21040)
*Wei Dai,Qianchu Liu,Guanghui Qin,Xing Han,Tristan Naumann,Paul Pu Liang,Sheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RRI（Recursive Reward Improvement），用“奖励黑客对抗者”监督模型自写的评分细则（rubric），修复开放式专家任务中自我改进循环的奖励作弊问题；在HealthBench Professional上9B提升10.9分、27B提升12.4分，且可跨任务泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有proposer-solver递归自我改进（RSI）循环在数学、编程等有标准答案的领域奏效，但临床报告生成等开放式专家任务没有答案键，无法简单生成问答对。改为让proposer同时写rubric后，核心难题是rubric的全面性：朴素生成的rubric易被solver利用——只罗列预期主题而缺乏实质内容，导致学习信号被腐蚀。

Method: RRI在一个生成任务上训练solver之前加入对抗校验：由对抗者（同一模型）在rubric下写出尽可能无用但能“钻空子”刷分的答案，同时在不看rubric的情况下写出诚实答案；随后最多两步修复（repair）改写rubric，使诚实答案胜出。被发现的作弊模式还会反馈到未来任务与rubric的生成过程中。

Result: HealthBench Professional上，调整后得分相对Generation Only自我改进基线提升10.9分（9B）和12.4分（27B）；完全不使用外部模型时提升10.3分；增益集中在困难任务与写作任务。仅用HealthBench Professional描述训练的27B模型在未见过的PRBench Hard上提升6.6分、ProfBench上提升8.0分。

Conclusion: 以对抗式奖励校验抑制rubric被利用，可显著提升开放式专家任务的自我改进效果并具备跨任务泛化能力；论文进一步分析了模型生成问答-rubric对的可利用点，为开放式self-play研究提供参考。

Abstract: Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI) has gained wide attention for its ability to work with limited data. In a proposer-solver loop, a proposer model writes training tasks and a solver model learns to solve them through reinforcement learning. Such loops have succeeded in mathematics and coding, where every answer can be checked automatically. Open-ended expert tasks, like clinical report generations, have no answer key, which makes simple question-answer generation not viable. Here the proposer must also write a rubric that lists the content an answer should include and the errors it should avoid. However, the unique challenge behind model-written rubric is the comprehensiveness of it. For naively generated rubric, solvers tend to exploit it, naming the expected topics without the substance behind them, which corrupts the learning process. To solve this problem, we introduce RRI, Recursive Reward Improvement, which uses a reward hacking adversary to supervise the reward. Before the solver trains on a generated task, an adversary writes an answer that games its rubric while being as useless as possible, the same model writes an honest answer without seeing the rubric, and up to two repair steps revise the rubric to make the honest answer win. The exploits found also rewrite how future tasks and rubrics are generated. On HealthBench Professional, RRI improves the adjusted score over the Generation Only self-improvement baseline by 10.9 points at 9B and 12.4 at 27B, and by 10.3 points without any external model. In particular, the gains are largest on difficult and writing tasks. We show that the 27B model trained on the HealthBench Professional description improves by 6.6 points on PRBench Hard and 8.0 on ProfBench without seeing a task from either. We further analyze the exploitable points of model-generated question-rubric pairs to facilitate future research on open-ended self-play.

</details>


### [217] [Learning When to Withdraw: Curriculum Coverage and Rule-Specific Failures in Fine-Tuned Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21045)
*Mohamed Abouzahra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究在NAQD-Env中通过监督微调提升语言智能体的"选择性撤回"能力，并指出聚合指标会掩盖规则级回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型语言智能体在证据变化或授权撤销时必须修正已准备的动作：暂停受影响动作、保留仍受支持的工作、仅在充分修复后恢复。现有评估多用聚合准确率，难以暴露规则层面的失效模式。

Method: 构建合成环境NAQD-Env（显式依赖+确定性参考策略）；基于Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct训练4个LoRA检查点，比较原始课程与富化课程（后者含三种系统提示框定，视图与目标相同）；在三种推理提示下，于新验证案例、留出依赖族与留出结构组合上评测，并对照3个指令微调基线；辅以规则级诊断（如错误撤回仍有替代支持的动作、受禁组合收窄、委托授权、事件报告）。

Result: naqd提示下SFT检查点决策一致率0.77–0.92、撤回召回0.46–0.86，基线为0.45–0.63与≤0.06；原始课程检查点在34个仍有替代支持的动作中错误撤回26个，富化课程为0–1；但所有SFT检查点在收窄受禁组合上不如7B/8B基线（该情形两个课程均未覆盖）；委托场景撤回更多但独立授权动作保留更差，且均遗漏有效事件报告；提示框定效应随指标与划分波动，无法确立可复现优势或等价性。

Conclusion: 贡献在于一套检测聚合准确率所掩盖的规则级回归的方法，而非证明微调本身即可保证智能体安全；每臂仅一次训练、课程属性联合变化、先前评估暴露与基线-SFT量化差异限制了因果归因。

Abstract: Tool-using language agents must revise prepared actions when evidence changes or authority is revoked. Selective withdrawal means suspending affected actions, preserving supported work, and resuming only after sufficient repair. We study supervised fine-tuning in NAQD-Env, a synthetic environment with explicit dependencies and a deterministic reference policy. Four LoRA checkpoints of Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct compare an original curriculum with an enriched one, the latter under three system-prompt framings with identical views and targets. Three instruction-tuned baselines and all four checkpoints are evaluated under three inference prompts on fresh validation cases, held-out dependency families, and held-out structural combinations. Under the naqd prompt, SFT checkpoints reach decision agreement of 0.77-0.92 and withdrawal recall of 0.46-0.86, versus 0.45-0.63 and at most 0.06 for the baselines. Rule-level diagnostics qualify these gains. The original-curriculum checkpoint wrongly withdraws 26 of 34 actions with surviving alternative support; enriched checkpoints withdraw 0-1. Conversely, all SFT checkpoints narrow prohibited combinations less often than the 7B and 8B baselines, a case absent from both curricula. Delegation results show more withdrawal but poorer preservation of independently authorized actions, and all four checkpoints omit valid event reports. Framing contrasts vary across metrics and splits and do not establish a reproducible advantage or equivalence. One training run per arm, jointly changed curriculum properties, prior evaluation exposure, and baseline-SFT quantization differences limit causal attribution. The contribution is a method for detecting rule-specific regressions that aggregate accuracy can conceal, not evidence that fine-tuning alone ensures agent safety.

</details>


### [218] [Athanor: An Agent-Native System for Autonomous Post-Training Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21055)
*Yufa Zhou,Yichen Wang,Jiakun Fan,Ziqi Shu,Zhichao Liu,Anru R. Zhang,Neiwen Ling,Zijun Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Athanor——一个面向自主后训练研究(AutoPostTrain)的开放式agent原生系统，让四个Claude Opus 5.5智能体在104小时内、无外部教师模型条件下，将Qwen3-4B-Base从15.9/9.2提升至67.3/48.2（开发/留出集），达到或超越官方instruct版本(66.5/47.9)，首次证明前沿agent团队可自主完成基座模型的后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（AI构建更强的AI）是通向超级智能的路径之一，但难以直接测量。作者研究其可测的弱化形式：以前沿agent对小型原始基座模型进行后训练，作为其研究潜力的检验。

Method: 构建Athanor系统：为agent提供完整研究自主权、低摩擦工程工具与高效starter code；agent自主读数据集与评测结果、构造训练数据、编写修改训练算法、调超参、分析结果决定下一步。配套服务包括冻结评测、托管作业、去污染数据、持久化研究记录，以及agent不知情的留出测试套件；运行于沙盒中且禁用外部教师模型。实验为四节点八卡、104小时的自主训练campaign。

Result: 四智能体团队在六任务开发集上将Qwen3-4B-Base从15.9提升到67.3，留出集从9.2提升到48.2；对照官方Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507为66.5与47.9，即在两套评测上均持平或超越。

Conclusion: 该工作首次证明前沿agent团队可自主把原始基座模型后训练为跨域通用模型并达到官方instruct水平。完整记录显示其路径为已知方法的重组：公开推理数据SFT、某agent引入的rollout改动下的RL、跨agent权重合并及多轮自蒸馏。作者认为在执行层面已被解决的情况下，当今agent已具备攻克该任务的工程与研究能力，Athanor可作为扩展自主AI研究的基础设施。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement, in which AI systems build stronger AI systems, is one promising route to superintelligence. We study a weaker form that can be measured today: frontier agents post-training a small raw base model with public data, as a test of their research potential. We build Athanor, an open agent-native system for autonomous post-training research (AutoPostTrain) on real GPUs. We give the agents full research autonomy, low-friction engineering tools, and efficient starter code. The agents carry out the research themselves: they read datasets and evaluation results, build their training data, write and modify training algorithms, tune hyperparameters, and analyze the results to decide what to try next. They work in a sandbox and cannot use external teacher models. Athanor's services supply frozen evaluation, managed jobs, decontaminated data, a persistent research record, and a held-out suite the agents are unaware of. In a 104-hour campaign, a team of four Claude Opus 5.5 agents, each on an eight-GPU node and given no research advice, improves Qwen3-4B-Base from 15.9 to 67.3 on a six-task development suite and from 9.2 to 48.2 on a held-out suite, against 66.5 and 47.9 for the official Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration that a team of frontier agents can autonomously post-train a raw base model into a cross-domain generalist model that matches or surpasses the official instruct version on average. The campaign's full record shows that the agents reach this level by recombining known methods and each other's models: SFT on public reasoning data, RL with a rollout change one agent introduced, weight merges across agents, and rounds of self-distillation. With execution handled, today's agents already have the engineering and research ability to conquer this task. We hope Athanor provides a foundation for scaling autonomous AI research.

</details>


### [219] [OmicsBench: a long-horizon multi-turn benchmark of AI agents on research-study-scale bioinformatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21074)
*Gideon Shaked,Siddharth Nagisetty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OmicsBench——面向AI智能体的长程、多轮生物信息学基准（97个任务、662个子任务，DAG依赖+确定性评分），12个模型跨3个框架均未饱和；并配套无监督失败模式发现流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体生信能力基准多为单轮短任务、单次提示，与真实场景中长程多轮、子任务相互依赖的分析委托方式不符，无法刻画依赖链上的表现与失败模式。

Method: 构建OmicsBench：97个任务由依赖子任务构成有向无环图，共662个子任务；每个子任务对应用户一轮提问，按答案键确定性判分，并可对错误答案提供可选的上下文反馈。评估12个模型×3个执行框架。另设计无监督失败模式发现流程：对失败尝试进行描述、嵌入与聚类。

Result: 最慢智能体平均每任务3.6小时；无反馈平均分0.43–0.57，有反馈0.54–0.67（满分1），均未接近饱和；依赖前序子任务的子任务得分更低，上下文反馈可部分弥补；聚类揭示定性缺陷，如以自设统计过滤替代指定过滤、凭记忆编写完整答案。

Conclusion: 多轮长程评估能暴露单轮提示无法发现的依赖敏感性与失败行为；OmicsBench结合确定性评分与无监督失败模式发现，为生信智能体能力评估提供更贴近真实工作流的诊断工具。

Abstract: Computational biologists increasingly delegate substantial analyses to AI agents in long-horizon, multi-turn sessions, yet most benchmarks of agents' bioinformatics capabilities pose a single short task with a single-shot prompt. We present OmicsBench, a long-horizon, multi-turn bioinformatics benchmark for AI agents. Each of its 97 tasks links dependent subtasks into a directed acyclic graph, for 662 underlying subtasks in all. Each subtask is posed as one user turn and graded deterministically against an answer key, with optional in-context feedback on incorrect answers. Across 12 models on three harnesses, the slowest agent averages 3.6 hours per task, and no combination comes near saturating the benchmark, with mean scores of 0.43 to 0.57 out of a possible 1 without in-context feedback and 0.54 to 0.67 with it. Multi-turn evaluation also uncovers behavior that single-shot prompts cannot, as agents score lower on subtasks that depend on earlier subtasks, and in-context feedback helps agents recover some of that loss. Because deterministic scoring gives no qualitative account of how an agent failed, we also introduce an unsupervised failure mode discovery pipeline that describes, embeds, and clusters agents' failed attempts to surface emergent failure modes. Applied to OmicsBench, this pipeline exposes qualitative shortcomings in how agents carry out bioinformatics analyses, ranging from agents applying their own statistical filters in place of the specified ones to agents writing whole answers from memory.

</details>


### [220] [Sci$Π$vot: Benchmarking Principal-Investigator Judgment at Pivotal Moments of Scientific AutoResearch Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21082)
*Youran Sun,Xingyu Ren,Chugang Yi,Kejia Zhang,Di Wu,Yingkang Cao,Allen Zhou,Jianping Ye,Suying Liu,Jiaxuan Guo,Jianda Du,Weicheng Ye,Kecheng Liu,Hefan Zhang,Haixin Wang,Jie Xiao,Eric Yang,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 构建基于人类修正记录的248例科学判断基准SciΠvot，发现前沿LLM智能体系统性地偏好自身原始计划而非人类修正方案，在提供项目记录时表现低于机会水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体已能长期自主运行科学实验，但可靠的自主研究还依赖"科学判断"能力；而研究决策开放且依赖项目语境，导致科学判断难以评估，缺乏系统化基准。

Method: 构建SciΠvot基准（248个案例），数据取自六个月真实科研过程：资深研究者带领LLM智能体在六大科学领域的26个项目中反复介入纠正其研究判断。每个案例要求智能体在"原始LLM计划"与"人类修正后的替代方案"之间二选一，且只能使用修正发生前的项目记录作为上下文。对比LLM与人类表现，并考察推理努力程度、项目记录可及性和重新考虑的影响。

Result: 中等推理努力且可访问项目记录时，模型系统性地偏好原始LLM计划；五个前沿模型中四个在有筛选的案例上低于机会水平。所有测试模型与努力设置下，带项目记录的最高准确率为63.5%，而新晋研究者为73.4%。中等努力下，所有模型带项目记录的准确率均低于不带项目记录；重新考虑未能一致提升总体准确率。

Conclusion: 即便存在人类修正的备选方案，前沿模型在所筛选的研究决策上仍表现不佳，揭示了LLM智能体科学判断能力的显著缺陷；SciΠvot为LLM智能体的科学判断提供了系统化评估手段。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can keep scientific experiments running for months with little human supervision, but reliable autonomous research also requires scientific judgment. Evaluating scientific judgment is difficult because research decisions are open-ended and depend on project context. We construct Sci$Π$vot, a 248-case benchmark of scientific judgment based on human corrections. The benchmark draws on records from six months of research in which senior researchers ran LLM agents on 26 projects across six scientific fields and repeatedly intervened to correct the agents' research judgments. In each case, an agent chooses between the original LLM plan and the human-corrected alternative, using only the project record available before the correction as context. We compare LLM and human performance and examine the effects of reasoning effort, access to the project record, and reconsideration. At medium reasoning effort with the project record, models systematically prefer the original LLM plan over the human-corrected alternative. Four of five frontier models perform below chance on the selected cases. The highest accuracy in the project condition across the tested models and reasoning effort settings is 63.5%, compared with 73.4% for fresh researchers. At medium effort, every model achieves lower accuracy with the project record than without it, and reconsideration does not consistently improve overall accuracy. Even with a human-corrected alternative available, frontier models struggle with the selected research decisions, while Sci$Π$vot supports systematic evaluation of scientific judgment for LLM agents.

</details>


### [221] [Probe-and-Resume: When and How to Evaluate Agent Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21088)
*Jiakai Xu,Tiancheng Ge,Tujie Guan,Yusheng Li,Rohan Timmaraju,Eugene Wu,Kostis Kaffes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在终端类计算机使用环境中，常规 MCTS 无法显著提升任务成功率，原因是 critic 缺少关键环境状态、且中间状态的可判别性有限；据此提出 Probe-and-Resume：仅在 agent 声称完成时调用具备读写执行权限的 critic 在状态 fork 上探查，Terminal-Bench 2.1 上成功率比常规 critic 的 MCTS 高 8.4 个百分点，接近 Codex CLI 且成本不到其一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时搜索（探索备选动作 + critic 选枝）本应提升 agent 表现，但在只暴露部分状态的终端式计算机使用环境中，实测的 MCTS 配置并未带来显著的任务成功率提升。作者希望定位 critic 失效的根源，并据此重新设计搜索与 critic 的调用方式。

Method: 归因分析发现两类问题：(1) agent 的观测遗漏了评估所需的环境状态；(2) 中间状态对未来轨迹是否成功的证据有限——critic 在中间状态对后续分支的排序仅略优于随机，即便给予环境访问权限改善也很小。基于此设计 Probe-and-Resume：只在 agent 自认为处于终态的节点调用 critic，而不在部分进展阶段调用；同时赋予 critic 对计算机当前状态与初始状态之 fork 的读、写、执行权限，使其可主动探查甚至执行破坏性动作，以揭示轨迹所遗漏的隐藏状态；若 critic 否决，agent 从停止处恢复继续。

Result: 在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上，相较使用常规 critic 的 MCTS，成功率提升 8.4 个百分点；在使用相同底层模型的情况下，成功率接近 Codex CLI agent，而成本不到其一半。

Conclusion: 在部分可观测的终端环境中，critic 的价值取决于调用时机与信息访问权限：把 critic 限制在终态判断、并赋予其在状态 fork 上的完整操作权限，比在中间状态做分支排序更有效，能以更低成本逼近强基线 agent。

Abstract: Test-time search can improve agent performance by exploring alternative actions and using a critic to select promising branches. We study this approach in terminal-based computer-use environments, which expose only a partial view of a large underlying state, and find that the Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) configuration we evaluate does not significantly improve task success. We identify two sources of poor critic performance: the agent's observations omit environment state needed for evaluation, and intermediate states offer limited evidence of whether a trajectory will succeed. At intermediate states, critics rank continuations only modestly better than chance, and environment access improves this little. We study when to invoke a critic and what access to give it to evaluate a node. Our findings motivate a policy that invokes the critic only at nodes the agent believes are final, rather than during partial progress. Further, the policy gives the critic read, write, and execute access to a fork of the computer's current and initial state, allowing it to probe and perform potentially destructive actions to surface any hidden state the trajectory omits. We call the resulting design Probe-and-Resume: when the agent claims to be done, the critic probes a fork of its state, and a rejected agent resumes where it stopped. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, this raises success by 8.4 percentage points over MCTS with conventional critics, and approaches the success rate of the Codex CLI agent with the same underlying model at less than half its cost.

</details>


### [222] [MemComposer: Learning to Assemble Memories for LLM Agents via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21091)
*Yixuan Xie,Xueqiang Xu,Xinyi Fan,Xinxi Lyu,Tao Feng,Xing Fan,Chenlei Guo,Jie Hao,Shaowen Wang,Jiashuo Sun,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MemComposer 记忆组合器，联合审视查询与候选记忆，决定选取哪些经验、选多少、如何排序；用基于 GRPO 的 Experience Policy Optimization（ExPO）以下游任务成功反馈优化装配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体可借助记忆复用过往经验而无需更新参数，但仅凭语义相似度无法保证记忆有用性；下游表现取决于记忆如何被选择与呈现。

Method: MemComposer 作为记忆组合器，同时建模当前查询与检索候选，输出记忆子集、数量与顺序；训练采用 ExPO——以 GRPO 为基础的框架，通过完整智能体交互评估装配后的记忆上下文，并用任务成功信号优化装配策略。

Result: 在 τ²-bench 三个领域、两种冻结执行器下均优于各自最强基线，平均记忆 token 用量分别降低 16.9% 与 4.9%；所学策略可迁移至未见领域、执行器与记忆库，并能直接受益于更强模型生成的记忆而无需再训练。

Conclusion: 以任务反馈学习查询特定的记忆装配是有价值的，优于纯相似度驱动的方式，且具备良好的泛化与复用性。

Abstract: Agent memory enables large language model (LLM) agents to reuse past experiences without updating their parameters. However, semantic similarity alone does not guarantee memory usefulness: downstream performance depends on how memories are selected and presented to the agent. We propose MemComposer, a memory composer that jointly examines the current query and retrieved candidates to decide which experiences to include, how many to select, and how to order them. We train the composer using Experience Policy Optimization (ExPO), a GRPO-based framework that evaluates assembled memory contexts through complete agent interactions and uses task-success feedback to optimize the assembly policy. Across three $τ^2$-bench domains with two frozen executors, MemComposer outperforms the strongest baseline for each executor while reducing average memory-token usage by 16.9% and 4.9%, respectively. The learned policy also transfers to unseen domains, executors, and memory banks, and benefits from memories generated by stronger models without further training. These results demonstrate the value of learning query-specific memory assembly using downstream task feedback.

</details>


### [223] [Planning Commitment of LLM under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21108)
*Cassie Huang,Zhanwei Cao,Yeawon Lee,Nathalia Gomez,Zhuoyi Yang,Ian G. Harris,Tiffany Do,Jiayuan Mao,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究对比了基于LLM的激进式与保守式规划在部分可观测环境中的安全性-效率权衡，并探索了可调超参数的混合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM规划代理在交互前需决定规划长度：激进派基于未观测状态假设承诺长计划，保守派仅规划已观测内容。两类行为在预训练模型中隐式涌现，分别带来失败代价与调用开销，其安全性-效率权衡未被系统刻画，也无法控制。

Method: 在两个部分可观测领域中，考察直接输出计划与生成PDDL交由经典求解器两种形式的激进/保守LLM规划器与形式化器；先验以显式统计或常识知识提供；测试三个LLM；并提出带可调超参数的混合策略。

Result: 两类策略精度相近；保守方法失败尝试更少（更安全）但API调用更多（效率更低）；激进方法失败更多，仅当先验信息充分时重规划才高效。分析表明LLM几乎把先验当作事实，先验不可靠时易致失败。

Conclusion: 激进与保守策略构成安全性与效率的权衡，可通过混合策略的超参数进行调节；LLM对先验的过度信任是潜在风险来源。

Abstract: LLM-based planning agents commit to plans of different lengths before interacting with a partially observable environment. Some aggressively commit to a long plan based on assumptions about unobserved states, while others conservatively plan only over what they have observed. Both behaviors emerge implicitly in pre-trained models, whether the LLM outputs a plan directly or generates a PDDL formulation for a classical solver. Aggressive commitment risks costly failures, while conservative commitment risks excessive LLM calls. This choice pits safety against efficiency, a trade-off that has been neither characterized systematically nor made controllable. We investigate aggressive and conservative LLM planners and formalizers on two partially observable domains, where priors are provided as either explicit statistics or common-sense knowledge. Across three LLMs, both strategies reach similar accuracy. Conservative methods achieve fewer failed attempts (safety) at the cost of more API calls (efficiency). Aggressive methods fail more often but replan efficiently only when priors are informative. Interestingly, analysis shows that LLMs treat the provided priors nearly as facts, which risks failure when priors are unreliable. We also explore hybrid strategies with tunable hyperparameters that adjust the balance between safety and efficiency.

</details>


### [224] [Kolab: Benchmarking Human-Agent Collaboration in Long-Horizon Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21109)
*Shirley Anugrah Hayati,Patricia Anugrah Setiani,Ruizi Wang,Zhouyi Li,Khanh Chi Le,Young-Jun Lee,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Kolab 数据集：115+ 小时、50 名来自 40 种职业的专业人士与 AI 智能体协作的屏幕录制及协作/产出质量评判，并据此构建评测预测专业人士如何使用智能体贡献、以及验证其协作满意度与产出质量判断的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务中人类更看重与智能体的持续交互（如修订计划）而非一次性完成能力，但现有 agentic 基准偏重自动化，其高分并不代表真实协作效果；专业工作流步骤相互依赖，是研究该问题的合适场景。

Method: 构建 Kolab 数据集，包含 115+ 小时屏幕录制，覆盖 50 名专业人士、40 种职业，与智能体协作解决真实长时程问题，并附参与者对协作过程与产出质量的判断；分析协作模式，并将基准操作化为两项任务：(1) 预测专业人士在不同工作流中对智能体贡献的采纳/修改/拒绝；(2) 验证其对协作满意度与产出质量的判断。

Result: 前沿模型（含 agentic 与非 agentic）均高估与专家的协作程度，且难以区分高质量与低质量协作；更长的工流上下文并未稳定提升预测表现。

Conclusion: 前沿智能体尚不能理解与专家的长时程协作，Kolab 可作为基于真实专业工作流研究人机协作的有价值数据集。

Abstract: When humans collaborate with AI agents on long-horizon tasks, what happens to the agents' contributions? For solving long-horizon tasks, humans place greater importance on ongoing interaction with AI agents (e.g., revising plans) than on an agent's ability to complete the task in a single attempt. However, most agentic benchmarks have focused more on automation. Strong performance on such benchmarks does not imply effective real-world collaboration. Professional workflows offer a useful setting, as they often involve interdependent steps. To address this gap, we introduce Kolab, a new dataset of 115+ hours of screen recordings of 50 professionals from 40 occupations collaborating with agents on realistic, long-horizon problems. Kolab also contains participants' collaboration and output quality judgments. Our analysis reveals diverse collaboration patterns, highlighting the importance of when and how professionals coordinate with agents. A collaborative agent must do two things that automation benchmarks never test: anticipate whether experts will adopt, modify, or reject its contribution once it leaves the chat window to guide its next steps, and assess collaboration quality from the unfolding workflow without constant expert feedback. Our benchmark operationalizes both: (1) forecasting how professionals use agent contributions at diverse workflows, and (2) verifying their judgments of collaboration satisfaction and output quality. Findings show that frontier models, both agentic and non-agentic, overestimate collaboration with experts and struggle to distinguish between high- and low-quality collaboration. Longer workflow context does not consistently improve these predictions. As frontier agents cannot yet understand long-horizon collaboration with experts, we position Kolab as a valuable dataset for studying human-agent collaboration grounded in real professional workflows.

</details>


### [225] [CleanRM: Reward Modeling from Noisy Feedback with a Few Clean Feedback for RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21138)
*Licheng Pan,Haocheng Yang,Yuzhou Chen,Yichen Sun,Yuan Lu,Yunfei Teng,Jiajun Fan,Guangyi Liu,Zhixuan Chu,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CleanRM利用少量干净反馈，通过最优传输校准样本级噪声率，构建无偏的奖励建模目标以应对偏好数据中的反馈噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好数据中的反馈噪声普遍存在，来源包括人类主观判断、标注错误和不完美的LLM评判者。在噪声反馈上训练标准奖励模型会得到对干净风险的有偏估计。经典去噪方法虽可直接用于噪声反馈学习，但普遍假设样本间噪声率均匀，而反馈数据的噪声本质上是样本级的，该假设不成立。

Method: 提出CleanRM：使用少量干净反馈，通过最优传输（optimal transport）校准样本级噪声率；具体将对噪声反馈还原出的偏好联合分布与干净反馈的联合分布对齐，随后采用噪声校正的代理损失（noise-corrected surrogate loss）训练奖励模型。

Result: 理论上证明所得目标相对于干净风险是无偏的；大量实验表明CleanRM优于当前先进的去噪基线，并在下游RLHF对齐中更有效。

Conclusion: 通过最优传输实现样本级噪声率校准，CleanRM有效解决了反馈噪声的异质性难题，获得无偏奖励建模并提升下游对齐效果。

Abstract: Feedback noise is pervasive in preference data, arising from subjective human judgments, annotation mistakes, and imperfect LLM judges. Standard reward modeling trained on such feedback yields a biased estimate of the clean risk. Although classical denoising methods are directly applicable to noisy feedback learning, they generally assume a uniform noise ratio across samples. However, this assumption fails for feedback data, where noise is intrinsically sample-wise. To address this challenge, we propose CleanRM, which uses a few clean feedback to calibrate sample-wise noise ratios via optimal transport. Specifically, CleanRM aligns the joint distribution of preferences reverted from noisy feedback with that of clean feedback and then trains the reward model using a noise-corrected surrogate loss. Theoretically, we prove that the resulting objective is unbiased with respect to the clean risk. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CleanRM outperforms state-of-the-art denoising baselines and yields more effective downstream RLHF alignment.

</details>


### [226] [All-in-One Memory: Process-Rewarded Reinforcement Learning for Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21147)
*Bingbing Wang,Jingjie Lin,Weiming Qiao,Jing Li,Ruifeng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 All-in-One Memory (AiO-Mem)，将检索、记忆写入与停止决策统一到共享证据状态与统一奖励空间，并用过程奖励模型与 PA-DAPO 优化记忆策略，在长上下文记忆基准上稳定提升回答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越依赖外部记忆来回答基于长时程历史（多轮会话、长文档）的查询，但现有记忆方法通常将检索优化与记忆构建分开处理，缺乏一个用于评估搜索、写入与停止决策的共享奖励空间。

Method: 1) 将检索、写入、停止等操作统一建模为共享证据状态上的状态转移，并在统一奖励空间中直接评估每个动作的语义质量；2) 将异构历史编译为可溯源的多视角事件，作为检索与记忆写入的共享基底；3) 用统一记忆策略 actor 在共享证据状态上展开完整交互轨迹；4) 训练独立的轨迹条件过程奖励模型，采用证据接地的监督微调与轨迹级 DPO；5) 将过程分数聚合为动作均衡的轨迹奖励，由 Process-Aligned DAPO (PA-DAPO) 优化记忆策略。

Result: 在多个长上下文记忆基准上，AiO-Mem 相较静态记忆系统与经强化学习训练的记忆智能体，均一致提升回答准确率。

Conclusion: 通过共享证据状态与统一奖励空间联合优化检索、写入与停止决策，并以过程级奖励对齐策略优化，是提升长时程记忆型 LLM 系统的有效范式。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly rely on external memory to answer queries grounded in long-horizon histories such as multi-session dialogues and lengthy documents. However, existing memory methods often optimize retrieval and memory construction separately, without a shared reward space for evaluating search, writing, and stopping decisions. To address this, we propose All-in-One Memory (AiO-Mem), which organizes these operations as transitions over a shared evidence state and directly evaluates the semantic quality of each action in a unified reward space. AiO-Mem compiles heterogeneous histories into source-traceable multi-view events, providing a shared substrate for retrieval and memory writing. A unified memory policy actor then rolls out complete interaction trajectories over this shared evidence state. A separate trajectory-conditioned process reward model is trained through evidence-grounded supervised fine-tuning and trajectory-level DPO to assign process scores to the collected trajectories. These scores are aggregated into an action-balanced trajectory reward and used by Process-Aligned DAPO (PA-DAPO) to optimize the memory policy. Across long-context memory benchmarks, AiO-Mem consistently improves answer accuracy over both static memory systems and RL-trained memory agents.

</details>


### [227] [GRADS: Graph-Regulated Agentic Data Synthesis with Controllable Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21167)
*Gaocheng Yang,Ruijie Tao,Kai Ye,Senhua Zhang,Zhengqin Zhu,Ziyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRADS 是一个图引导的智能体数据合成框架，通过显式难度控制生成多跳长程研究任务，可用于训练深度研究代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据流水线通过扩展种子任务来提升表面上的深度与广度，但新增证据与答案之间往往耦合微弱，导致意图难度与经验难度不匹配，无法可靠转化为必要推理。

Method: GRADS 以闭环方式运行种子选择、证据扩展、问答构建与验证，并维护一张记录推导答案所需证据与推理依赖的图；每一步都以相关子图为条件进行生成，保留不可或缺的推理链而不累积无关上下文，从而实现显式难度控制。

Result: 在安全、软件工程与科学文档三个领域，GRADS 持续生成经校验且难度分布由独立求解器测得可经验分离的任务；同等训练预算下，SFT 将解题终止率提升至95%以上，同时减少最多75%的工具调用与轮次，RL 进一步降低最多19.5%的工具使用；在域内留出基准上，所得4B模型在软件工程上媲美 Sonnet 4，在科学与安全领域超过其表现。

Conclusion: 以依赖图为核心的子图条件生成能够保证合成任务的必需推理并实现可控难度，从而显著提升深度研究代理的后训练效率与效果。

Abstract: Deep research agents are increasingly deployed to retrieve precise information from large corpora, yet multi-hop, long-horizon tasks remain challenging. Synthetic data generation has emerged as a scalable solution, with existing pipelines typically expanding seed tasks to increase their apparent depth and breadth. However, this expansion does not reliably translate into necessary reasoning: newly introduced evidence may be only weakly coupled to the answer, creating a mismatch between intended and empirical difficulty. To address this problem, we introduce GRADS, a graph-guided agentic data synthesis framework with explicit difficulty control. GRADS runs a closed loop of seed selection, evidence expansion, question--answer construction, and verification while maintaining a graph of the evidence and inference dependencies required to derive each answer. At each step, it conditions generation on a relevant subgraph, preserving indispensable inference without accumulating irrelevant context. Experiments across security, software-engineering, and scientific-document domains show that GRADS consistently generates verified tasks with empirically separated difficulty distributions, as measured by independent solvers. Moreover, under matched training budgets, post-training on this data delivers consistent gains at every level: SFT raises the solution-termination rate to over 95% while cutting tool calls and turns by up to 75%, RL trims tool use by up to a further 19.5%, and on our held-out, domain-specific benchmarks, the resulting 4B model matches Sonnet~4 on software engineering and surpasses it on the scientific and security domains.

</details>


### [228] [MemScope: Evolving Agent Memory Architectures through Localized Code Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21170)
*Miao Su,Yucan Guo,Zixuan Li,Long Bai,Zhongni Hou,Xiaolong Jin,Jiafeng Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MemScope 框架，利用原始执行轨迹、运行时内存状态与架构代码进行失败定位，实现有界、原地的记忆架构进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体记忆架构部署后通常固定，难以跨任务类型自适应；现有自进化方法依赖评估分数或压缩摘要等粗粒度反馈，在长时程多组件交互场景下无法精确定位失败，导致修改范围约束弱、常引发大范围重写。

Method: MemScope 使用工具型 Patch Agent，联合检查原始执行轨迹、运行时内存状态与当前架构代码，在提供者身份与内存兼容性约束下确定修复目标并执行有界、原地编辑。

Result: GAIA 上轨迹+内存检查变体达 86.67%（仅轨迹 70.00%、仅摘要 63.33%）；xBench 上 DeepSeek V3.2 由 49 提升至 55、Kimi K2.7 Code 由 50 提升至 53；ALFWorld 未见任务成功率由 50.75% 升至 71.64%；架构快照迁移的代码改动比中位数为 4.4%。

Conclusion: 细粒度执行证据可作为局部化、原地记忆架构进化的有效依据。

Abstract: Agent memory architectures define how LLM agents store and reuse experience across tasks, but they are typically fixed after deployment, limiting their adaptability across task types. Recent self-evolving approaches instead adapt these memory architectures using execution feedback, typically represented as evaluation scores or compressed summaries. However, such coarse-grained feedback is insufficient for precise failure localization in current agent systems, where long-horizon task execution involves multiple interacting components. Without a precise failure location, existing approaches lack a clear code-level target for modification, leaving the update scope weakly constrained and often leading to large-scale rewrites of an otherwise capable memory architecture. We propose MemScope, a framework for evidence-grounded, localized evolution of an existing memory architecture. Its tool-using Patch Agent jointly inspects raw execution traces, runtime memory state, and current architecture code to identify repair targets and apply bounded, in-place edits under provider-identity and memory-compatibility constraints. On GAIA, the trace-and-memory inspection variant achieves 86.67% accuracy, compared with 70.00% for trace-only and 63.33% for summary-only feedback. Under xBench tasks, the evolved memory architecture improves over its original version from 49 to 55 correct answers with DeepSeek V3.2 and from 50 to 53 with Kimi K2.7 Code. On disjoint ALFWorld unseen tasks, success rises from 50.75% to 71.64%. Across archived architecture-snapshot transitions, the median code-change ratio is 4.4%. These results support fine-grained execution evidence as a basis for localized, in-place memory-architecture evolution.

</details>


### [229] [AgentEval: Evidence-Bounded Assessment of Stateful Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21177)
*Ruiqi Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentEval系统，将"契约约束的评估记录"贯穿工具执行、状态与事件捕获、报告资格判定与案例维护，把"智能体做了什么"、"证据证明了什么"、"结论当前是否有支撑"三者分离，使评估的范围、证据支持与报告复用可被检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务完成本身不能判定有状态智能体是否履行了义务，而已记录的判断也不保证其支撑证据仍然有效。现有评估缺乏可执行、可追溯的评估记录，导致范围、证据基础与报告复用不可检验。

Method: AgentEval以契约绑定评估记录，流程包括：工具执行、状态与事件捕获、报告资格判定、案例维护；显式区分智能体行为、证据所确立的事实、结论的当前可用性。实验设置：32次工具世界管理（含2次中断保留）、对48个证据视图的受控干预、Scenario Studio连接受审案例与12份两轮报告（24个独立核验答案）、以及192次留出执行的公开基准研究。

Result: 32次管理中，独立检查在全部30个完整episode上一致，可区分"已满足/已违反/未决"义务；48个证据视图的受控干预下，仅哈希校验漏检的36个约束性故障被暴露，且不改变智能体行为；Scenario Studio产出12份两轮报告与24个独立核验答案；192次留出执行中识别出10个整体任务失败内已完成的返回子目标，并分离缺失证据的影响。

Conclusion: 可执行的评估记录使智能体评估工作流中的范围界定、证据支持与报告复用均可被审查，弥补了单看任务完成度与哈希式证据校验的不足。

Abstract: Task completion alone does not determine whether a stateful agent satisfied its obligations, and a recorded judgment does not guarantee that its supporting evidence remains valid. We present AgentEval, a system that carries a contract-bound assessment record through tool execution, state and event capture, report qualification, and case maintenance. It separates what the agent did, what the evidence establishes, and whether the conclusion is supported for present use. On 32 tool-world administrations, independent checks agree on all 30 complete episodes, distinguishing satisfied, violated, and unresolved obligations; two interrupted administrations are retained. Controlled interventions on 48 evidence views expose 36 binding faults missed by hash-only checks without changing agent behavior. Scenario Studio connects reviewed cases to 12 two-turn reports with 24 independently checked answers. A separate public-benchmark study on 192 held-out executions reveals ten completed return subgoals within broader task failures and isolates the effect of missing evidence. These studies show how an executable assessment record makes scope, evidential support, and report reuse inspectable across an agent evaluation workflow.

</details>


### [230] [Does Latent Chain-of-Thought Actually Reason? A Single, Unifying Taxonomy for Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21182)
*Jingjie Qiu,Sheldon Yu,Xunyi Jiang,Jiajin Liu,Zihan Huang,Zhendong Chu,Tong Yu,Julian McAuley,Junda Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统检验“潜在思维链(latent CoT)”是否真正参与推理：通过链删除、内容替换、探针与轨迹分析，在21个模型-任务配置上发现逻辑任务中潜在链常为“占位符”或提前已编码答案，而算术任务才真正依赖链内容；据此提出区分“链表征什么”与“模型如何使用链”的分类框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在思维链用连续隐藏状态替代显式推理步骤，但现有研究仅凭高准确率和可解码的中间答案就断言模型使用了潜在链，这种证据并不充分；论文希望厘清这些隐藏步骤实际执行了什么计算，以及模型是否真的依赖它们。

Method: 覆盖21个模型-任务配置（3种训练配方、3类推理任务、2种骨干模型），结合链删除（chain deletion）与内容替换（content replacement）两种因果干预，配合探针（probes）和状态轨迹分析，分别检验链是否影响答案、链编码了什么、状态如何演化。

Result: 在逻辑任务上，COCONUT模型往往在链开始前答案就已可读，状态早期即稳定，删除链几乎不改变预测；逐步监督能产生有序的步骤布局并使模型依赖链，但有序链仍可能只是占位符——删除损害性能而内容替换不影响答案；在算术任务上，所有测试配方均表现出对链内容的依赖。

Conclusion: 可解码的潜在状态不足以作为推理的证据。作者提出分类框架，将“链表征什么”（占位符、坍缩叠加、依赖步骤的因果角色等）与“模型如何使用链”区分开来；先前看似矛盾的结论实为不同任务、配方或测量下不同类型链的体现，只要明确研究所检验的属性，关于潜在推理的主张即可相互比较。

Abstract: Latent chain-of-thought replaces written reasoning steps with continuous hidden states, giving models additional steps to compute before answering. It remains unclear what computation these steps actually perform. High task accuracy and decodable intermediate answers do not establish that a model uses its latent chain to reach an answer. We study this question across twenty-one model-task configurations covering three training recipes, three reasoning tasks, and two backbones. We combine chain deletion and content replacement with probes and trajectory analysis to test whether the chain affects the answer, what it encodes, and how its states change. On logical tasks, COCONUT often carries an answer that is already readable before the chain begins. Its states settle early, and deleting the chain leaves predictions almost unchanged. Per-step supervision produces an ordered layout of reasoning steps and can make the model depend on the chain. Yet even an ordered chain can act as a placeholder, with deletion hurting performance while content replacement leaves answers unchanged. On arithmetic tasks, all tested recipes show dependence on chain content. These results show why readable latent states alone are insufficient evidence of reasoning. We introduce a taxonomy that separates what a chain represents from how the model uses it. Seen this way, earlier reports no longer compete: placeholder tokens, collapsed superposition and step-dependent causal roles are each consistent with a different kind of chain, each obtained on a different task, training recipe or measurement. Once a study says which property it tested, whether the model uses the chain, what the chain holds, or how its state moves, claims about latent reasoning can be compared.

</details>


### [231] [Mine, Sample, Render: Test-Time Diversity Alignment Of Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21185)
*Kostas Triaridis,Morris E. Sharp,Nebojsa Jojic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出推理时框架 MSR，把多样性从生成模型中"外置"为基于真实语料的显式采样流程：先从语料挖掘可解释的分类式 schema 与统计量，再按统计量采样属性配置，最后让生成模型仅作为条件渲染器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型能产出高质量单体样本，但即便被明确要求，也难以实现多样化采样，导致输出分布偏离真实语料分布。作者希望把"多样性"从模型内部行为中解耦出来，交由可解释、可统计控制的显式过程管理。

Method: 测试时（test-time）、模型无关的三段式流水线：(i) 从真实工件语料中挖掘可解释的分类式 schema，并给出各属性的采样统计量；(ii) 依据语料统计量采样属性配置；(iii) 生成模型仅作为该配置的条件渲染器（conditional renderer），不承担多样性职责。

Result: 在天气预报生成、用户模拟、网页设计、文本到图像生成以及智能体生成的 3D 资产五类任务上评估，MSR 引导的生成在输出质量可接受的前提下，提升了与语料分布的对齐度及生成输出的多样性。

Conclusion: 多样性可作为显式的、基于语料的统计采样问题，而非依赖生成模型的自发行为；该"挖掘—采样—渲染"范式具跨模态通用性，但从摘要看尚未给出与基线的定量增益细节及 schema 挖掘失败时的鲁棒性分析。

Abstract: Modern generative models produce high-quality individual outputs but struggle to sample diversely, even when explicitly instructed to do so. We introduce MINE-SAMPLE-RENDER (MSR), a test-time approach that externalizes the diversity into an explicit, corpus-derived sampling procedure. Our pipeline (i) mines an interpretable categorical schema from a corpus of real artifacts and supplies statistics for sampling; (ii) samples attribute configurations from the corpus-derived statistics; (iii) uses a generative model purely as a conditional renderer of a sampled configuration. Across weather report generation, user simulation, webpage design, text-to-image generation, and agent-generated 3D assets, we evaluate the diversity and output quality of MSR-guided generation and show that MSR improves alignment with corpus distributions and diversity of generated outputs.

</details>


### [232] [Condition-Contrastive Extrapolation for On-Policy Distillation of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21195)
*Wenxuan Luo,Chenxi Li,Haoyue Liu,Yongxin Guo,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CCE-OPD方法，通过"固定前缀条件对比+有界对数概率对比"重加权教师分布，使学生更少接受违反题目前提的无效续写，从而提升在策略蒸馏的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略蒸馏（OPD）中，学生用自己的前缀采样轨迹并由教师监督，但当条件于学生生成的前缀时，教师可能偏好违反题目前提的无效续写，导致OPD鼓励错误推理步骤。

Method: CCE-OPD包含两部分：(1) 固定前缀比较，即用同一冻结教师同时评估完整问题与遮蔽某一选定条件后的对照版本；(2) 有界对数概率对比，将教师分布重加权为辅助蒸馏目标，使教师监督对题目条件敏感。

Result: 在200个诊断推理状态上，条件对比将偏好无效续写的比例从27.0%降至16.0%；在六个数学基准上，将Qwen3-8B蒸馏到0.6B与1.7B学生时，avg@8分别平均提升1.60与1.95个百分点，并在四个迁移基准上取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 条件对比可有效缓解OPD中教师对无效续写的偏好，CCE-OPD在数学推理与迁移任务上均优于OPD。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves a student language model by training on its own generated trajectories with supervision from a stronger teacher. However, when conditioned on student-generated prefixes, the teacher can favor invalid continuations that violate the question's premises, causing OPD to encourage erroneous reasoning steps. As a remedy, we introduce Condition-Contrastive Extrapolated On-Policy Distillation (CCE-OPD), which makes teacher supervision sensitive to question conditions. CCE-OPD incorporates (1) a fixed-prefix comparison in which the same frozen teacher evaluates the complete question and a counterpart with a selected condition masked, and (2) a bounded log-probability contrast that reweights the teacher distribution into an auxiliary distillation target. On 200 diagnostic reasoning states, condition contrast reduces the rate of preferring invalid continuations from 27.0% to 16.0%. Extensive experiments across six mathematical benchmarks show that CCE-OPD improves avg@8 over OPD by an average of 1.60 and 1.95 percentage points when distilling Qwen3-8B into 0.6B and 1.7B students, respectively. It also achieves the strongest results on four transfer benchmarks.

</details>


### [233] [Who May Release an Agent's Promise? Standing over Commitments in Multi-Party Chat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21203)
*Lan Wei,Bingyu Shen,Jiaqi Tang,Qing Lu,Kuan Huang,Meng Xu,Miles Q. Li,Bin Hu,Boyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"立场"(standing)概念刻画承诺解除权归属,在受控聊天环境中将助理违反承诺的错误分解为"未授权释放"与"覆盖已释放承诺"两类,并在六个模型上发现不同规模模型呈现相反的失效模式,进而提出自我维护的承诺账本以缓解覆盖错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 助理型智能体在团队协作中会做出承诺,但现实中"谁有权取消承诺"缺乏明确规范:即便有层级关系,也只有债权人或被授权者才能释放承诺。现有研究未区分"错误地听从无立场者"与"错误地执行已解除的承诺"两种失效,也缺乏对规模、推理提示、显式规则等干预手段作用机制的解析。

Method: 构建受控聊天环境,系统变化"由谁提出取消"这一变量,并定义两类可分离的错误指标:未授权释放(服从无立场者)与覆盖(仍执行已被释放的承诺)。在六个模型上测量错误率;使用"神谕"(oracle)式提示分别告知规则、指明债权人、告知承诺是否仍然有效,以解离两类错误的成因;对比开启思维链的影响;并实现自我维护的承诺账本(commitment ledger)及配合一段释放政策文本的方案。

Result: 小模型倾向覆盖债权人意愿(Qwen3.5-9B 达 69%);大模型更易因同伴一句话丢弃承诺(Qwen3.8-27B 89%,gpt-6.1-sol 83%);几乎所有模型都会听从旁观者的"那就继续吧"。神谕实验显示:告知规则或指明债权人可抑制未授权释放,但对覆盖无效,且在四模型中的三个反而抬高覆盖;唯有"知道承诺是否仍被欠"能降低覆盖。开启思维链使小模型表现出类似规模扩大的行为变化。自我维护账本在五模型中的四个消除覆盖;与释放政策文本结合后可匹配甚至超过最佳神谕存储,将 gpt-6-luna 的严格准确率从 49%(仅政策)提升至 83%。

Conclusion: 承诺解除的"立场"问题需被显式建模,且未授权释放与覆盖是两种机制独立的失效,不能用同一干预统一解决;仅提供规则知识不足,关键在于维护承诺状态的动态真值,自我维护的承诺账本是一种轻量且有效的架构性方案。代码、提示、输出与标注均已公开。

Abstract: When an assistant serving a team makes a promise to Alice, who may cancel it? We call this standing: only the creditor (the person owed) or someone the creditor or operator policy authorizes may release a commitment, regardless of rank. In a controlled chat environment we vary who cancels and separate two errors: unauthorized release (obeying someone without standing) and override (executing a released promise). Across six models, smaller open ones override the creditor (Qwen3.5-9B: 69%); larger ones drop promises on a peer's direct word (Qwen3.8-27B: 89%; gpt-6.1-sol: 83%); nearly all act on a bystander's "go ahead after all". Oracles dissociate the errors: stating the rule or the creditor curbs unauthorized release but not override, which the latter even raises in three of four models; among them, only knowing whether the promise is still owed cuts override. Enabling thinking moves smaller models the way scale does. A self-kept commitment ledger stops override in four of five models; with a one-paragraph release policy it matches or beats the best oracle store, raising gpt-6-luna's strict accuracy from 49% (policy alone) to 83%. Code, prompts, outputs and labels are released.

</details>


### [234] [Entertain, then Cancel: How Thinking Makes Reasoning Models Read Some and Or Literally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21204)
*Zhizhuo Kou,Jian Yang,Pengfei Hao,Junyu Luo,Jingshu Peng,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究推理模型的"思考时长"如何影响日常语用推理（如"some"、"or"的会话隐涵），发现思考使模型按字面逻辑解读，并据此提出一种推理时补救方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型允许用户控制思考时长，但尚不清楚额外思考对"some students passed"这类日常语用推断（会话隐涵）有何影响，因此需要直接检验思考量与语义解读之间的关系。

Method: 固定模型权重，仅改变思考量并记录其选择的解读；另用一个工具在模型推理轨迹的不同位置截断并索要答案，以定位解读发生变化的节点。

Result: 在多数被测的开源推理模型及GPT-5中，思考会使模型取消"some"（非全部）和"or"（非两者皆）的隐涵，即按字面逻辑解读；而上下文驱动的推断（如间接回答）则有所改善。推理轨迹显示模型先考虑强化解读、随后又将其取消。基于早期解读作答的探索性推理时补救方法可在不损失字面准确率的情况下恢复这些隐涵。

Conclusion: 对推理模型的评估不应只看准确率，还应测量其如何解读语义；思考会系统性地改变语用解读方向。

Abstract: When a speaker says ``some students passed'', listeners infer that not all of them did. Reasoning models now let users decide how long a model thinks before it answers, but we do not know what extra thinking does to such everyday inferences. We study this directly: we keep a model's weights fixed, change only how much it thinks, and record which reading it chooses. A second tool cuts the model's own reasoning at successive points and asks for an answer, showing where the reading changes. Thinking makes models read logical words as logic: it cancels the implicatures of \emph{some} (\emph{not all}) and \emph{or} (\emph{not both}) in most tested open reasoning models and in GPT-5, while context-driven inferences such as indirect answers improve. The trace shows why: models first entertain the enriched reading and cancel it later. Answering from that early reading, an exploratory inference-time remedy, recovers these implicatures without losing literal accuracy. Evaluations of reasoning models should measure how they interpret, not only how accurate they are.

</details>


### [235] [Rubric Judgments Lack Uncertainty: Conformal Weighting for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21214)
*Licheng Pan,Haocheng Yang,Haoxuan Li,Yuzhou Chen,Yichen Sun,Yuan Lu,Xiaoxi Li,Hao Wang,Zhixuan Chu,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 URW 框架，用共形校准把评分细则（rubric）各项判定的不确定性量化为逐条权重，替代等权聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分细则的 LLM 评测将质量分解为多个准则并由 LLM 验证器给出二元判定，但不同准则上的判定确定性差异很大；现有方法对所有判定等权聚合成最终分数，把不确定判定不加区分地纳入奖励估计，从而给下游强化学习带来误导性训练信号。

Method: URW 为免训练框架：先借助一组固定的改述判定模板（paraphrased judgment templates）从验证器引出软不确定性信号，再通过分裂共形（split-conformal）p 值将其转换为每个准则的权重，且该权重具备可证明的覆盖率保证。

Result: 大量实验表明 URW 稳定提升成对偏好准确率，并在下游强化学习对齐中取得更强效果。

Conclusion: 按准则不确定性加权聚合优于等权聚合，能够为奖励估计与 RL 对齐提供更可靠的训练信号，且方法无需额外训练。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluation is widely used to assess large language model (LLM) outputs by decomposing quality into multiple criteria and assigning each a binary judgment from an LLM verifier. However, judgment certainty varies substantially across criteria. Existing methods uniformly aggregate these judgments into a final score, implicitly treating every criterion as equally certain and overlooking this heterogeneity. As a result, uncertain judgments remain incorporated into reward estimation without distinction, potentially yielding misleading training signals for downstream reinforcement learning (RL). To address this limitation, we propose Uncertainty-aware Rubric Weighting (URW), a training-free framework that quantifies criterion-level judgment uncertainty via conformal calibration. Specifically, URW elicits a soft uncertainty signal from a fixed pool of paraphrased judgment templates and converts it into a per-criterion weight using split-conformal $p$-values with provable coverage guarantees. Extensive experiments demonstrate that URW consistently improves pairwise preference accuracy and delivers stronger downstream RL alignment.

</details>


### [236] [AutoOnboarder: Autonomously Onboarding LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21218)
*Tanay Arora,Cong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AutoOnboarder：由"Onboarder"编码智能体监督下，自动协同优化基础模型、提示词与 harness 源码，使智能体在仅数十例样本下匹配/超越 Gemini 3.5 Flash 基线且成本最多降至 1/29，但性能波动大，指出现有 Onboarding 需更强环境支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体大语言模型被置入 harness（中介其观测与动作的软件）后推理能力下降，需一种机制来弥补这一落差。

Method: AutoOnboarder 将智能体置于一个智能"Onboarder"编码智能体的监督之下，模仿培训新员工的过程，迭代地定制其基础模型、提示词与 harness 源代码，以同时提升性能并降低成本；仅提供数十个示例，所有评测数据隔离在独立虚拟机上以防泄漏。在基因组测序事先授权表单提交任务上构建并测试。

Result: 自主 Onboard 后的智能体可匹配或超过 Gemini 3.5 Flash 基线，成本最多低 29 倍；但智能体仍不完美，且 Onboarding 过程的最终性能差异很大。

Conclusion: 未来 Onboarding 系统需要为 Onboarder 提供稳健环境，包括结构化思维接口、受控的互联网访问以及更宽的模型搜索空间。

Abstract: Agentic large language models have been documented to lose reasoning capability when placed inside a harness, the software that mediates their observations and actions. To reconcile these losses, we introduce AutoOnboarder, an autonomous system that adapts an agent to a task the way a trainer onboards a new employee. It places the agent under the supervision of an intelligent ``Onboarder'' coding agent, which iteratively tailors its foundation model, prompt, and harness source code to simultaneously improve performance and decrease cost. Like a real onboarding process, the Onboarder is only given tens of examples, and all evaluation data is isolated on a separate virtual machine to prevent leakage. We report the lessons learned from building and testing AutoOnboarder on genomic-sequencing prior authorization form submission. Autonomously onboarded agents are able to match or exceed the performance of a Gemini 3.5 Flash baseline at up to 29$\times$ lower costs. However, these agents remain imperfect, and the Onboarding process varies greatly in terms of final performance. We conclude that future Onboarding systems require a robust environment for the Onboarder, including a structured thought interface, (controlled) internet access, and a wide model search space.

</details>


### [237] [Auditing Escalation Reliability in Healthcare LLM Agents: A Perturbation-Controlled Benchmark of Workflow-State Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21270)
*Zihao Zheng,Junyi Yao,Baichuan Li,Jiayu Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向医疗工作流的"回合级"评估协议（400个合成情节、四类任务、5:1漏报与冗余升级代价），发现开放权重模型干净条件下决策准确率达0.97，但在缺失/冲突/滞后证据下漏掉32–97%的必要升级，且词面化置信度不可靠；一个透明的工作流感知控制器可提升5:1效用并把漏报降至0.12–0.24，但词汇压力测试显示其规则检测器在未见同义改写上召回从1.00跌至0.00，说明路由控制本身也需泛化检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可信生物医学AI不应只追求静态输入上的答案准确，而需在证据缺失、过期、冲突或滞后时做出可靠的动作选择（回答/追问/弃答/升级）。现有评估缺乏对"何时行动"的过程级刻画，且模型往往低估需要升级的情形。

Method: 构建400个合成医疗工作流情节：40个手工撰写的语义种子覆盖4个任务类别，配2种表层参数化与5个受控变体；在预先声明的5:1（漏报:冗余升级）代价下对ANSWER/ASK CLARIFICATION/ABSTAIN/ESCALATE四类动作打分；参考动作为单一作者的研究策略标签而非临床医生裁决的真值；评估5个开放权重配置（1.7B–9.7B）；分析词面化置信度的校准（ECE、失败AUROC）；提出并消融一个透明的工作流感知控制器，并做种子聚类统计、跨任务迁移与30概念词汇压力测试。

Result: 干净条件下决策准确率高达0.97，但经不确定性提示的模型漏掉32–97%的必要升级；9.7B配置在冲突情形下从0.97降至0.78，差距收窄但未消除。词面化置信度不稳定（ECE 0.10–0.54；失败AUROC 0.48–0.77），仅调优置信度的策略无实用增益。工作流感知控制器对所有模型均提升5:1效用，漏报降至0.12–0.24，特异度0.83–0.93。但30概念词汇压力测试使各规则检测器召回由触发短语上的1.00跌至未见同义改写上的0.00。

Conclusion: 结果支持对工作流状态进行显式评估，而非仅看静态答案准确率；同时表明看似有效的路由/控制机制必须自备泛化测试，控制器的效果声明仅限本基准。种子聚类统计、消融、跨任务迁移、原始输出与清单均已打包以备审计。

Abstract: Trustworthy biomedical AI requires reliable action selection when evidence is missing, stale, conflicting, or late, not only accurate answers on static inputs. We instantiate an episode-level evaluation protocol as 400 synthetic healthcare workflow episodes: 40 hand-authored semantic seeds across four task classes, two surface parameterizations, and five controlled variants, scored on ANSWER/ASK CLARIFICATION/ABSTAIN/ESCALATE under a pre-declared 5:1 missed-versus-unnecessary escalation cost. Reference actions are single-author study-policy labels, not clinician-adjudicated ground truth. Across five open-weight configurations (1.7B--9.7B), clean decision accuracy reaches 0.97, yet uncertainty-prompted models miss 32--97% of required escalations. The stronger 9.7B configuration narrows but does not remove the gap, falling from 0.97 clean accuracy to 0.78 under conflict. Verbalized confidence is inconsistent (ECE 0.10--0.54; failure AUROC 0.48--0.77), and a development-tuned confidence-only policy yields no utility gain. A transparent workflow-aware controller improves 5:1 utility for every model and reduces missed escalations to 0.12--0.24, at specificity 0.83--0.93. However, a 30-concept lexical stress test reduces each rule detector's recall from 1.00 on trigger phrases to 0.00 on unseen paraphrases, limiting the controller claim to this benchmark. Seed-clustered statistics, ablations, cross-task transfer, raw outputs, and manifests are packaged for audit. The results motivate explicit workflow-state evaluation while showing that apparently effective routing controls need their own generalization tests.

</details>


### [238] [Diviner: Agentic RCA with Human-Governed Adaptation for Large-Scale Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21281)
*Pradipta Ghosh,Gaurav Mitra,Shripad Nadgowda,Akshil Shah,Junyu Liu,Canturk Isci,Syamla Bandla,Xinyao Hu,Onkar Deshpande,Anik Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Diviner 是 Meta 部署的 agentic 根因分析（RCA）系统，以"编码—执行—评估—适配"四阶段可靠性生命周期组织，通过领域知识编码、上下文隔离的并行 worker 与可验证的证据链提升大规模分布式系统的故障定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式系统中的单一症状可横跨服务、数据、配置、计算与组织边界，响应者需拼凑不完整证据并协调掌握因果链不同片段的专家。通用 LLM agent 仅靠工具访问无法解决：易使用过时或时序错位的上下文、将查询失败误判为症状缺失、报告无依据的系统状态，并以超人类节奏压垮可观测性服务。

Method: 四阶段生命周期：Encode 结合经复核的历史工单语料与服务负责人经验，产出有作用域、版本化的 skills、workflows、typed tools 及 incident 知识图谱；Execute 由自适应 Lead 与上下文隔离的 worker 将相关知识应用于时点证据，并维护证据链接的假设；Evaluate 将保存的调查轨迹与响应者复核结果对比，度量 exact cause、useful direction 与 Groundedness。

Result: 提出以"精确根因、有用方向、证据接地性"三项指标构成的评估体系，用于量化调查轨迹质量；Adapt 阶段可基于反复出现的知识缺口与值班反馈生成聚焦的运维知识更新，并仅在回放、质量检查与 owner 审批后准入。

Conclusion: RCA 的可靠性不应仅依赖工具调用能力，而需将领域知识工程化、证据可追溯与人类审核闭环贯穿系统生命周期；该设计与 Meta 生产环境实践相结合，为 agentic 运维系统提供了可验证、可持续演进的范式。

Abstract: Root-cause analysis (RCA) in large-scale distributed systems is challenging because a single symptom can cross service, data, configuration, compute, and organizational boundaries. Responders must assemble incomplete evidence and coordinate experts who hold different parts of the causal path. Generic language-model agents do not solve this problem through tool access alone: they can use stale or mistimed context, interpret failed queries as absent symptoms, report unsupported system state, and overload observability services designed for human-paced use.
  We present Diviner (Domain-Informed Verifiable Incident Navigation through Evidence and Review), an agentic RCA system operating at Meta and organized around a four-stage reliability lifecycle. Encode combines a large corpus of reviewed incidents with service-owner expertise to produce scoped, versioned skills, workflows, typed tools, and an incident knowledge graph. Execute uses an adaptive Lead and context-isolated workers to apply relevant knowledge to point-in-time evidence and maintain evidence-linked hypotheses.
