However, prior work typically evaluates these abilities in isolation, leaving open how models integrate them across a full inquiry cycle. In this work, we introduce a framework for evaluating LLMs' inquiry capabilities in a unified, interactive setting. We extend the Countdown task to a mathematical environment where an agent probes the system by manipulating variables, collecting observations, and inferring relationships between them. This setup enables analysis of exploration strategies, causal discovery, and hypothesis refinement during inquiry. Across seven LLMs and multiple prompting strategies, our experiments reveal limited inherent inquiry capabilities and low success rates in discovering the underlying expressions. However, structured guidance inspired by educational scaffolding significantly improves performance. Our findings highlight current limitations of LLMs in strategic exploration and iterative hypothesis refinement, and show how simple guidance strategies developed for human learners in inquiry learning can support more effective inquiry behavior.

</details>


### [275] [Learning Intrinsic Dynamics in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21612)
*Jiamin Shi,Yuanhong Zhang,Zhiyang Liu,Jiawei Sun,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究动作可预测性与内在动力学保持之间的联系，提出Concat IDM（从拼接潜状态预测动作）作为归纳偏置，在Push-T高外观多样性下将规划成功率从9.1%提升至77.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型需要学习内在动力学（动作与其后果的关系），但与控制过程无关的视觉变化会干扰学习到的表征，使动作—后果关系被遮蔽。作者希望揭示动作可预测性（由连续潜状态重建动作的能力）与内在动力学保持之间的关系。

Method: 理论上推导：当编码器把同一状态、同一外观下不同动作的可区分结果合并时，平方逆预测误差存在正下界，而前向预测仍可能精确。实证上采用受控的外观多样性对比、监督干预与潜变量探针。方法上以Concat IDM实例化动作可预测性：由拼接潜状态预测动作，并利用离线轨迹中已有的动作标签更新共享编码器，测试时规划器不变。

Result: 在Push-T、24种外观设置下，三次独立训练中Concat IDM将规划成功率从9.1%提高到77.2%；其配对提升比单一外观条件下高出52.9个百分点。多种受控实验支持动作预测在保留控制相关转移信息中的作用。

Conclusion: 动作可预测性可作为趋向内在动力学的归纳偏置，Concat IDM在视觉多样性下的潜空间规划中具有实际收益。

Abstract: Learning intrinsic dynamics---the relationship between actions and their consequences---is essential for world models. However, visual variations unrelated to the underlying control process can obscure this relationship in learned representations. We investigate the connection between action predictability, defined as the ability to reconstruct an action from consecutive latent states, and the preservation of intrinsic dynamics. Our central hypothesis is that encouraging action predictability discourages representations from discarding the distinctions between different actions and their consequences. We provide two complementary forms of evidence for this connection. Theoretically, we derive a positive lower bound on squared inverse prediction error when the encoder merges distinguishable outcomes of different actions from the same state and appearance, and show that forward prediction can still be exact on those transitions. Empirically, controlled appearance-diversity comparisons, supervision interventions, and latent probes support the role of action prediction in preserving control-relevant transition information. We instantiate action predictability with Concat IDM, which predicts actions from concatenated latent states and updates the shared encoder using action labels already available in offline trajectories, without changing the test-time planner. Across three independent training runs on Push-T with 24 appearances, Concat IDM increases planning success from 9.1% to 77.2%; its paired improvement is 52.9 percentage points larger than that observed with a single appearance. Together, these findings support action predictability as an inductive bias toward intrinsic dynamics and demonstrate the practical benefits of Concat IDM for latent planning under visual diversity.

</details>


### [276] [AgentPerturb: Evaluating LLM Agent Robustness under Controlled Interaction Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21624)
*Yuanjian Xu,Jinyi Han,Jianing Hao,Yuxuan Sun,Fanyang Lu,Richeng Xuan,Guang Zhang,Zhichao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个二维扰动分析框架，从"扰动目标"与"扰动类型"两个维度系统刻画LLM agent的鲁棒性，并在APEX-Agents与Toolathlon上开展统一评测，揭示扰动导致的性能下降、脆弱点的环境依赖性、轨迹中无验证的信息传播机制及额外计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实任务复杂且含噪，给LLM agent带来显著挑战；但现有评测缺乏能同时刻画潜在扰动目标与扰动类型的系统性框架，难以统一量化前沿模型的鲁棒性并追溯失效机制。

Method: 以"扰动目标×扰动类型"的二维视角构建统一研究：在APEX-Agents与Toolathlon上进行受控扰动评测，量化前沿模型鲁棒性；通过轨迹级分析追踪扰动引发失败的机制；辅以针对性的成本分析；并提出严格的benchmark构建流水线。

Result: 扰动一致性地导致性能下降，但主导脆弱点在任务环境间存在差异；轨迹分析显示agent很少识别被篡改的输入，且常不加验证地传播污染信息；成功的扰动轨迹往往需要显著更多的计算量。

Conclusion: 研究结果指向应覆盖多样噪声形式的训练目标，并奖励验证行为、不确定性意识与恢复能力；同时提供了可复用的benchmark构建流水线，代码与数据将于AgentPerturb仓库发布。

Abstract: Real-world tasks are often complex and noisy, posing substantial challenges for LLM agents. Yet existing evaluations lack a systematic framework for jointly characterizing potential perturbation targets and types. We use this two-dimensional view to conduct a unified study that quantifies the robustness of current frontier models, traces the mechanisms behind perturbation-induced failures, and draws implications for robustness-oriented training. Through controlled evaluations on APEX-Agents and Toolathlon, we find that perturbations consistently degrade performance, while the dominant vulnerabilities vary across task environments. Trajectory-level analysis reveals that agents rarely recognize altered inputs and often propagate corrupted information without verification. A focused cost analysis further shows that successful perturbed trajectories can require substantially more computation. These findings motivate training objectives that cover diverse forms of noise and reward verification, uncertainty awareness, and recovery. We provide a rigorous benchmark-construction pipeline. Code and data will be released at https://github.com/xuyj233/AgentPerturb.

</details>


### [277] [The Self-Improvement Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21646)
*Brian B. Moser,Stanislav Frolov,Tobias C. Nauen,Federico Raue,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文形式化"自动门控能否可靠判定智能体自我改进"这一不可能性问题：将智能体建模为程序、改进定义为未必可计算语义分数，证明通用情形下不存在同时满足可靠性（不误收非改进）与非保守性（不漏掉可用改进）的可终止判定/搜索程序，随机化有界误差门控同样受限；在有限评估+有限提案预算的可判定设定下穷举模拟可恢复保证，但连续被接受的更新链可使有界分数逐轮上升而真实分数下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的智能体日益自主，能写代码、调用工具、修改提示乃至改动自身组件，因此需要reward model或自动审稿器等自动门控来决定是否接受某次自我更新。核心矛盾是结构性的：门控必须是可终止、可计算的程序，而真正关心的"是否语义改进"依赖可能无界的程序行为；论文旨在形式化并刻画这一不匹配的边界。

Method: 把智能体建模为程序，用未必可计算的语义分数定义"改进"。研究两类评估抽象：(1) certifier，对更新给出接受/拒绝；(2) search procedure，给定提案生成器，若存在改进候选则尝试输出其一。通过自指构造（对角化）证明通用设定下不存在同时可靠且非保守的可终止程序，并将结论扩展到有界误差的随机化门控；随后在"有界评估+有限提案预算"的实践中常见可判定机制下用穷举模拟给出可终止搜索及其保证。

Result: 通用设定下，任何此类门控或搜索器都会被自指构造迫使失败，可靠性与非保守性不可兼得；有界误差的随机化门控面临同样极限。在有限评估与有限提案预算的可判定机制下，穷举模拟可得到具备所需保证的可终止搜索程序；但该恢复有极限：存在一条被连续接受的更新链，使有界分数每轮上升而真实分数下降。

Conclusion: 论文划清了边界：无界、通用设定中正确性保证会崩塌，而有界设定中可凭设计恢复。这提示智能体自我改进的门控设计必须显式限定评估范围与提案预算，且不应把有界代理分数当作真实语义改进的可靠指标。

Abstract: With the rise of large language models, agentic systems are becoming increasingly autonomous, as they write code, call tools, revise prompts, and propose modifications to their own components. This autonomy raises a fundamental question for safe self-improvement: can an automated gate like a reward model or an automated reviewer reliably decide whether a proposed update should be accepted? The difficulty is structural. The gate must be a terminating, computable procedure, while the property we ultimately care about, namely whether an update is a genuine semantic improvement, depends on potentially unbounded program behavior. We formalize this mismatch by modeling agents as programs and defining improvement in terms of a semantic score that need not be computable. We study two evaluation abstractions. The first is a certifier that approves or rejects an update. The second is a search procedure that, given access to a proposal generator, attempts to output some improving candidate if one exists. We show that, in the fully general setting, no terminating procedure can simultaneously guarantee reliability (never accepting a non-improvement) and non-conservatism (never missing an available improvement): self-referential constructions can force any such gate or searcher to fail. Randomized gates with bounded error face the same limit. We then identify a standard decidable regime used in practice (bounded evaluation and a finite proposal budget) under which exhaustive simulation yields a terminating search procedure with the desired guarantees. We also show the limit of this recovery: a chain of accepted updates can raise the bounded score in every round while the true score falls. Our results draw a boundary between unbounded, universal settings where correctness guarantees collapse and bounded settings where they can be recovered by design.

</details>


### [278] [Data World: Scaling Data Environments from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21656)
*Zhanli Li,Zeyuan Xu,Chaoxu Pang,Zeyuan Liu,Anyang Ji,Te Sun,Kongcheng Zhang,Wenbo Xu,Xubin Yue,Shipeng Luo,Junjie Zhao,Yingfan Hua,Jingqing Ruan,Zhongda Su,Feng Hong,Zhizhao Zeng,Ke Zeng,Xunliang Cai,Hanjia Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Data World 多智能体引擎，先显式指定"文件背后的世界机制"再合成文件，从而可验证地生成数据智能体训练环境与任务；据此构建 DataWorld-Bench（100 任务/50 世界/16 领域）与 2,135 条训练任务，并用零轨迹蒸馏的 RL 训练出 DataWorld-35B-A3B，在多个基准上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据智能体依赖代码处理异构文件并得出分析洞见，但扩展其训练面临三大瓶颈：环境构建成本高、隐私与知识产权限制、以及开放式结论缺少已知"生成真值"导致难以验证。

Method: 核心思路是"先生成世界机制、再生成文件"：构建 Data World 多智能体引擎，从显式机制自动合成环境、任务与评分量表（rubrics）；通过独立审计检查目标结论能否从观测文件中被还原；量表同时用于评测与 RL 奖励；并以人类偏好筛选问题真实性，形成 100 题的 DataWorld-Bench（50 个留出世界、16 个领域），另生成来自 1,037 个不相交世界的 2,135 条训练任务；在 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 上做 RL-zero 训练得到 DataWorld-35B-A3B，不使用轨迹蒸馏。

Result: 十个前沿模型在 DataWorld-Bench 上仍具挑战，尤其"洞见发现"子项，且存在稳定的"计算—洞见"差距；DataWorld-35B-A3B 在同口径评测下较基座提升：DataWorld-Bench +7.06、DAComp +14.28、DABstep-Hard +20.90、JobBench +12.13 分。

Conclusion: 以可审计的世界机制为中心合成数据，能在无需真实敏感数据和轨迹蒸馏的前提下规模化解锁可验证的数据智能体训练，并显著提升模型的数据分析能力及跨基准泛化性；同时揭示了当前模型在高阶洞见发现上的系统性不足。

Abstract: Data agents use code to process heterogeneous files and derive analytical insights. Scaling their training faces costly environment curation, privacy and intellectual-property restrictions, and difficulty verifying open-ended findings without known generative truth. Our core insight is to specify the world behind the files before generating the files themselves. We introduce Data World, a multi-agent engine that synthesizes environments, tasks, and rubrics from explicit mechanisms. Independent audits check target recoverability from observed files; rubrics support evaluation and RL rewards. Human preference-based curation for question realism selects 100 tasks across 50 held-out worlds and 16 domains for DataWorld-Bench. The engine also yields 2,135 training tasks from 1,037 disjoint worlds. Evaluation of ten frontier models shows that the benchmark remains challenging, particularly for insight discovery, with a consistent computation-insight gap. RL-zero training on the synthesized tasks produces DataWorld-35B-A3B from Qwen3.6-35B-A3B without trajectory distillation. Under matched evaluation, it improves over its base by 7.06 points on DataWorld-Bench, 14.28 on DAComp, 20.90 on DABstep-Hard, and 12.13 on JobBench, a broader occupational benchmark.

</details>


### [279] [In-Context Preference-Conditioned Pareto Set Learning from Limited Offline Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21736)
*Yiming Yao,Liang Zhao,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ICPSL 提出一种基于超网络的上下文偏好条件化帕累托集学习框架：在大量合成先验数据上预训练"基础式"超网络，面对未见过的离线问题时仅需一次前向传播即可生成问题特定的偏好条件化帕累托解模型，从而同时摊销偏好搜索与问题建模成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景常需在离线、无进一步目标评估的条件下进行多目标优化，仅有稀疏覆盖决策与目标空间的静态数据集。现有方法要么对每个偏好单独优化，要么训练问题专属帕累托解模型，导致跨偏好重复搜索或跨问题重复训练，效率低下。

Method: 将偏好条件化帕累托集学习形式化为元学习/摊销问题：在大规模多样化合成先验数据上预训练一个超网络；给定未见问题的离线观测后，超网络在一次前向传播中输出该问题专属的、以偏好为条件的帕累托解模型，该模型可复用于任意偏好预测，无需问题特定训练或逐偏好搜索。

Result: 在合成与真实世界问题上，ICPSL 生成高质量帕累托解，并在问题特定解模型构建上取得显著加速。

Conclusion: 通过上下文（in-context）超网络摊销偏好搜索与问题建模，ICPSL 为离线多目标优化提供了一种可泛化、可复用且高效的基础模型式范式。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization involves diverse preferences over conflicting objectives. Rather than returning a finite set of trade-off solutions, preference-conditioned Pareto set learning seeks to map a specified preference directly to a corresponding Pareto optimal solution. Yet in many real-world scenarios, optimization must proceed offline without further objective evaluations, leaving only a limited static dataset that sparsely covers the decision and objective spaces. Existing methods either optimize separately for each preference or train a problem-specific Pareto solution model, requiring repeated search across preferences or repeated training across problems. We introduce in-context preference-conditioned Pareto set learning (ICPSL), a framework that amortizes both preference-wise solution search and problem-specific model construction. ICPSL pretrains a foundation-style hypernetwork on a large and diverse set of synthetic prior data. Given the offline observations of an unseen problem, the pretrained hypernetwork learns to generate a problem-specific, preference-conditioned Pareto solution model in a single forward pass. The generated model can then be reused to predict solutions for arbitrary preferences without problem-specific training or preference-wise search. Experiments on synthetic and real-world problems show that ICPSL produces high-quality Pareto solutions while providing substantial speedups in problem-specific solution-model construction.

</details>


### [280] [DeciCAD: Harness-Grounded Learning of Action Policies for CAD Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21755)
*Yufeng Peng,Yixin Zhang,Ziyi Liu,Zhikun Xu,Jiarong Yang,Sijie Wang,Wenjie Feng,Qiyu Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出DeciCAD：面向CAD领域的原生agent harness（语义CAD动作、决策导向观测、环境反馈、持久状态转移），并基于交互轨迹提取因果决策监督，用SFT与几何约束的在线RL优化策略；在四个CAD基准上取得最优F1/IoU，输出token减少45–88%，F1从0.6883提升至0.7880。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent通过闭环动作策略运行，其决策空间由agent harness定义；现有通用编码harness不适配CAD这类具持久状态、几何约束与长时程流程的专业领域，需研究harness诱导的决策空间如何影响agent行为与策略学习。

Method: 构建DeciCAD领域原生harness，暴露语义CAD动作、决策导向观测、环境反馈与持久状态转移；从交互轨迹中抽取因果决策监督；先做SFT，再进行几何约束的在线强化学习。

Result: 四个CAD基准上F1与IoU最优，相对通用编码harness输出token减少45–88%；同等监督下SFT将F1从0.6883提升到0.7437并优于编码策略；在线RL使几何可用性从66.5%升至73.5%，F1达0.7880。

Conclusion: harness诱导的决策空间实质性影响CAD中agent的执行效果与策略学习，领域原生harness设计是提升专业领域agent性能的关键。

Abstract: Large Language Model agents operate through closed-loop action policies whose decision space is defined by an agent harness. We investigate how this operating space shapes agent behavior and policy learning in computer-aided design (CAD), a professional domain characterized by persistent state, geometric constraints, and long-horizon procedures. We instantiate this perspective in DeciCAD, a domain-native harness that exposes semantic CAD actions, decision-oriented observations, environment feedback, and persistent state transitions. Building on this harness, we develop a learning pipeline that extracts causal decision supervision from interaction trajectories and improves the policy through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and geometry-grounded online reinforcement learning (RL). Across four CAD benchmarks, DeciCAD achieves the best F1 and intersection over union (IoU) while reducing model-output tokens by 45--88% relative to a general-purpose coding harness. Under matched supervision, its SFT policy improves F1 from 0.6883 to 0.7437 and outperforms the coding policy; subsequent online RL further raises geometry availability from 66.5% to 73.5% and F1 to 0.7880. These results demonstrate that the decision space induced by a harness materially affects both agent execution and policy learning in CAD.

</details>


### [281] [From Research Goals to Scientific Closure: Evaluating Agents Through Evidence-Supported Physics Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21767)
*Yijia Mao,Song Dai,Tao Chen,Xiangyu Lu,Linqian Zeng,Chenxi Hu,Sitong Zhou,Dongyang He,Xiaodan Mao,Linxuan Zhang,Tong Zhu,Yafu Li,Fangchen Yu,Peng Ye,Feng He,Yu Cheng,Yun Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PhysClosureBench，用 50 个专家核验的目标指定物理研究任务评估 AI 智能体能否组织出导向可靠结论的科研调查，并用 Atomic Score 与 Research Closure (RC0–RC4) 双重指标量化"局部科学成果"与"研究闭合"之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测多关注单点问答或局部正确性，缺乏对"智能体能否自主组织完整科学调查并给出有充分证据支撑的结论"这一整体能力的度量。作者希望区分局部科学成就与研究闭合（结论是否真正解答研究问题），从而揭示 AI 科学智能体的真实能力边界。

Method: 构建 PhysClosureBench：50 个经专家核验、目标明确的研究任务，覆盖强场物理、阿秒物理及邻近领域；每个任务只给出研究目标与关键科学约束，由智能体自行组织调查、生成并验证证据。评测体系包含两部分：20 项标准的 Atomic Score（度量局部科学完成度）与 RC0–RC4 分级的研究闭合度（依据必要证据要求与适用范围条件是否满足来判定调查对研究问题的解决程度）。评测 7 个前沿 LLM。

Result: Claude-Opus-5 取得最高平均 Atomic Score（6.29/10），但仅 32.7% 的尝试达到 RC4。全基准中 36.2% 的尝试即使 Atomic Score ≥8/10 仍未达 RC4；七个模型在三次运行中均达 RC4 的任务数，显著少于"至少一次"达 RC4 的任务数。案例层面分析显示，对生成结果的误读与未解决的物理适用性边界是导致闭合不完整的典型原因。

Conclusion: AI 智能体在局部科学任务上表现可观，但在实现完整研究闭合上存在持续且显著的差距：高分不等于结论可靠，结果解释与物理适用性约束是主要瓶颈。该基准为评估科学智能体的端到端研究组织能力提供了可分级、可复核的度量框架。

Abstract: Can AI agents organize scientific investigations that lead to well-supported conclusions? To evaluate this capability, we introduce PhysClosureBench, a benchmark of 50 expert-verified, goal-specified research tasks spanning strong-field, attosecond, and neighboring physics areas. Each task specifies the research objective and essential scientific constraints, while leaving the agent to organize the investigation and determine how to generate and validate the evidence needed to answer the question. A 20-criterion Atomic Score measures local scientific accomplishment, while Research Closure (RC0--RC4) assesses how fully the investigation resolves the specified research question based on whether its indispensable evidence requirements and applicable scope conditions are satisfied. We evaluate seven frontier LLMs, where Claude-Opus-5 achieves the highest mean Atomic Score (6.29/10) but reaches RC4 in only 32.7% of attempts. Across the benchmark, 36.2% of attempts scoring at least 8/10 still fall short of RC4, while all seven models reach RC4 on substantially fewer tasks in all three runs than in at least one. Case-level analysis further illustrates how misinterpretation of generated results and unresolved physical applicability limits can lead to incomplete closure. Together, these results reveal a persistent gap between AI agents' local scientific accomplishments and their ability to achieve complete research closure.

</details>


### [282] [RSIFlow: Adaptive Self-Improvement Workflows with an Evolving Meta-Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21791)
*Huan Zhang,Mingju Chen,Dongxu Zhou,Can Lv,Sen Cui,Faguo Wu,Shiji Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RSIFlow递归自我改进框架，将自适应工作流编排与元智能体能力演化相结合，在六个基准和两个骨干模型上取得SOTA平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进方法（模型优化、harness演化、可执行产物精炼）及递归自我改进（RSI）尚未解决两个问题：如何让自我改进工作流适配不断变化的性能瓶颈，以及如何利用执行反馈改进工作流编排本身。

Method: RSIFlow从任务—智能体轨迹与环境反馈中诊断性能瓶颈，动态选择针对特定组件（component-specific）的改进工作流；随后元智能体将工作流结果蒸馏为组件级技能与跨组件原则，演化自身harness以优化后续工作流编排。

Result: 在六个基准、两个骨干模型上，RSIFlow在各基准上均达到SOTA平均性能；消融实验验证了自适应工作流编排与元harness演化的各自贡献。

Conclusion: 将瓶颈驱动的自适应工作流编排与元智能体harness自我演化相耦合，可有效实现递归自我改进并稳定提升LLM智能体性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in complex tasks, including code generation, tool use, and multi-hop question answering. Existing self-improvement methods enhance agent performance through model optimization, harness evolution, and executable artifact refinement, while recursive self-improvement (RSI) further extends optimization to the improvement mechanisms themselves. However, adapting self-improvement workflows to evolving performance bottlenecks while improving workflow orchestration through execution feedback remains a key challenge. We propose \textbf{RSIFlow}, a \textbf{R}ecursive \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{I}mprovement framework that integrates adaptive work\textbf{flow} orchestration with meta-agent capability evolution. RSIFlow diagnoses performance bottlenecks from task-agent trajectories and environmental feedback to dynamically select component-specific improvement workflows. The meta-agent then distills workflow outcomes into component-specific skills and cross-component principles, evolving its own harness to improve subsequent workflow orchestration. Experiments across six benchmarks and two backbone models show that RSIFlow achieves the SOTA average performance on all benchmarks. Ablation studies further demonstrate the contributions of adaptive workflow orchestration and meta-harness evolution. Code is available at https://github.com/1HUAN1/RSI-Flow.

</details>


### [283] [Co2In: a Bi-level Memory Incremental Learning Framework with Knowledge Encoding, Consolidation, and Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21804)
*Wenju Sun,Qingyong Li,Boyang Li,Wen Wang,Siyu Zhang,Yi Wei,Yangliao Geng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Co2In 是一种受人类记忆机制启发的双层记忆增量学习框架，由工作记忆（自适应学习新知识）与长期记忆（持久保留信息）协同工作，经编码、巩固、整合三阶段实现塑性与稳定性的平衡，在无样本类增量与任务增量设置下取得 SOTA 且内存开销低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增量学习需同时具备塑性（学习新知识）与稳定性（保留旧知识），二者平衡是智能系统的重大挑战。人类大脑依靠多种记忆单元协同编码与存储信息而实现优异平衡，故作者借鉴人类记忆机制设计 IL 框架。

Method: 提出 Co2In（知识编码、巩固、整合）框架，采用双层记忆结构：工作记忆负责自适应知识获取，长期记忆负责信息持久保留。流程为：(1) 工作记忆先在新数据上学习，将知识编码为参数；(2) 执行巩固过程，识别已学参数中的潜在模式并重表示为紧凑知识表征；(3) 将知识表征与所识别模式分别转化为独立网络层并整合进长期记忆。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100 和 Tiny-ImageNet 上于无样本（exemplar-free）Class-IL 与 Task-IL 设置下评估，结果表明 Co2In 达到 SOTA 性能且内存消耗高效。

Conclusion: 双层记忆架构与编码-巩固-整合机制使 Co2In 能以高塑性与高稳定性积累知识，验证了类脑记忆协同机制在增量学习中的有效性，并在性能与内存效率上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Incremental learning (IL) aims to continually acquire new knowledge (plasticity) while retaining previously learned information (stability). However, striking a balance between plasticity and stability remains a significant challenge for intelligent systems. The human brain achieves exceptional balance, owing to various memory units that collaboratively encode and store information. Inspired by human memory mechanisms, this paper introduces an IL framework with knowledge enCoding, Consolidation, and Integration (Co2In). Co2In is designed as a bi-level memory architecture: a working memory for adaptive knowledge acquisition and a long-term memory dedicated to the persistent retention of information. The two memory modules work cooperatively during IL. The working memory first learns from new data to encode knowledge into parameters. Subsequently, Co2In performs a consolidation process to identify underlying patterns in the learned parameters and re-express them into a compact knowledge representation. Next, the knowledge representation and the identified patterns are transformed into separate network layers and integrated into the long-term memory. These designs empower Co2In to accumulate knowledge with high plasticity and stability. We evaluate Co2In on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet under exemplar-free Class-IL and Task-IL settings. Experimental results show that Co2In achieves state-of-the-art performance with efficient memory consumption.

</details>


### [284] [CIM-Embed: Learning Instruction-Conditioned Multimodal Embeddings for Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21805)
*Yinghua Xu,Pengyang Xie,Yanxiang Zhou,Chuanzhi Tong,Xiao Xiao,Chaoqun Feng,Xiaoqing Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CIM-Embed 框架，学习“指令条件化”的多模态嵌入用于聚类；构建 ProductMIC 基准（5,373 商品、449 项分组任务），直接训练 + HDBSCAN 平均每任务 V-measure 达 95.01，远超最强通用嵌入基线 WeMM 9B 的 53.06。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态对象在不同指令下需要不同划分（如商品按颜色、材质或两者分组），检索导向嵌入难以恢复这些划分，生成式聚类又依赖中间描述或迭代决策，故需直接的指令条件化嵌入聚类方案。

Method: CIM-Embed：用监督对比学习（同组为正样本、其他组为负样本，可纳入其他任务）训练；采用异构结构化批次以容纳组数不同的任务；推理时将图像、文本、指令直接映射为单一嵌入，再由 HDBSCAN 在无真实簇数条件下完成划分。同时从 Amazon Reviews 2023 元数据构建 ProductMIC 训练集与评测基准。

Result: ProductMIC 基准含 5,373 个商品与 449 个单属性及组合分组任务，覆盖训练中排除的 50 个叶子类目。全维嵌入 + HDBSCAN 下，直接任务内 SupCon 训练的平均每任务 V-measure 为 95.01，而最强通用嵌入基线 WeMM 9B 为 53.06。冻结的商品训练编码器亦提升域外条件分组；受控迁移实验区分了“划分迁移”与“指令响应”。

Conclusion: 指令条件化的监督对比嵌入可在推理阶段直接映射并配合 HDBSCAN 实现高质量多粒度聚类，显著优于通用嵌入模型，并具备一定域外泛化能力；代码与数据集将在录用后公开。

Abstract: Multimodal objects can require different partitions under different instructions, as when products in an e-commerce catalog are grouped by color, material, or both. Retrieval-oriented embeddings may not recover these partitions, while generative clustering often relies on intermediate descriptions or iterative decisions. This paper presents CIM-Embed, a framework for learning instruction-conditioned multimodal embeddings for clustering. Supervised contrastive learning uses same-group positives and negatives from other groups, optionally including other tasks; heterogeneous structured batches accommodate tasks with different group counts. At inference, the model maps each object's image, text, and instruction directly to an embedding; HDBSCAN then forms a partition without the ground-truth cluster count. We construct ProductMIC from product metadata in Amazon Reviews 2023, comprising training data and an evaluation benchmark for instruction-conditioned clustering. The benchmark contains 5,373 products and 449 single-attribute and compositional grouping tasks spanning 50 leaf categories excluded from our training. With full-dimensional embeddings and HDBSCAN, direct within-task SupCon training achieves a mean per-task V-measure of 95.01, compared with 53.06 for WeMM 9B, the strongest general-purpose embedding baseline evaluated. Frozen product-trained encoders also improve out-of-domain conditional grouping, while controlled transfer experiments distinguish partition transfer from instruction response. We will release the code and dataset upon acceptance.

</details>


### [285] [RoleQ: Role-Aligned Scales for Converting Language Models to Ternary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21808)
*Johannes Knittel,Alec Helbling,Hanspeter Pfister*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RoleQ 依据 Transformer 单元的"功能角色"（读/写残差流方向）而非权重矩阵布局来放置量化 scale：读出型按输出单元一个 scale、写出型按输入单元一个 scale，无需搜索/校准/存储状态，可推广到整数与三值网格；配合一条稀疏第二三值平面的 Fisher 显著性放置，实现 1.59 bit/权重、免乘法 matmul 的后训练三值量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低于 4 bit 的权重压缩虽能降低显存与带宽并在困惑度上看似可接受，但往往以事实知识与任务准确率（如 MMLU）为代价；三值权重通常依赖从头训练或长时间微调。传统 scale 放置方式（如 per-row）在极端压缩下会把 MMLU 压到随机水平，作者希望用结构性的、低成本的方式解决这一问题。

Method: 提出 RoleQ 框架：按 Transformer 单元的功能角色分配量化 scale——从残差流读取的向量（如读出）采用每输出单元一个 scale，写入残差流的向量采用每输入单元一个 scale。该规则是结构性的，不需要搜索、校准或存储额外状态，并适用于整数与三值网格。在旋转基下，其效果等价于逐层轴搜索但求解量减半。此外设计轻量后训练流水线，将现有 checkpoint 转换为 1.59 bit/权重（耗时数个 GPU 小时），并加入按逐单元 Fisher 显著性放置的稀疏第二三值平面，从而保持 matmul 免乘法。

Result: 在三值量化下，RoleQ 相比基于布局的放置，在两个模型族、0.6B–14B 各规模上困惑度最多减半；而 per-row scale 会使 MMLU 降至随机水平。该后训练流程成本仅为学习式三值化的很小一部分，在匹配或更低的内存驻留位数下，在困惑度、zero-shot 准确率与 MMLU（尤其在知识类任务上）全面领先现有后训练三值方法。8B 模型上，稀疏第二平面的不到 1 bit 额外开销可恢复约一半因量化损失的 MMLU，显著优于等比特下随机或基于 Hessian 的放置。

Conclusion: 量化 scale 应按 Transformer 单元的功能角色而非权重矩阵布局来放置，这一结构性规则简单、无搜索、通用且高效；结合 Fisher 显著性的稀疏第二三值平面，可在极低比特（1.59 bit/权重）下以低后训练成本实现免乘法推理，并在困惑度与知识类任务准确率上优于现有后训练三值化方法。

Abstract: Weight quantization below four bits shrinks memory and bandwidth at a perplexity that looks acceptable, but often at the cost of factual knowledge and task accuracy. Ternary weights have mostly been obtained by training from scratch or long fine-tuning. We introduce RoleQ, a quantization framework that places scales by the functional roles of Transformer units rather than by the layout of the weight matrix, as conventional formats do. Vectors that read from the residual stream take one scale per output unit; those that write to it, one per input unit. The rule is structural, needs no search, calibration or stored state, and holds on integer and ternary grids. In a rotated basis it matches a per-layer axis search at half the solver work, and at ternary it cuts perplexity by up to half against layout-based placement in two families at every size from 0.6B to 14B, where per-row scales leave MMLU at chance. Our lightweight post-training pipeline converts an existing checkpoint to 1.59 bits per weight in a few GPU-hours, a fraction of the cost of learned ternarization, and leads every post-training ternary method at matched or lower memory-resident bits on perplexity, zero-shot accuracy and MMLU, most widely on knowledge. A sparse second ternary plane placed by a per-unit Fisher saliency keeps the matmul multiplication-free and recovers what ternary loses first: at 8B, a fraction of a bit returns about half the MMLU that conversion costs, well ahead of random or Hessian-based placement at equal bits.

</details>


### [286] [CoWorld Reasoner: Agentic Multimodal Reasoning with the Evolving Video World Model-Driven Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21910)
*Dongjie Cheng,Ruifeng Yuan,Ran Wei,Ning Song,Yongqi Li,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CoWorld Reasoner 智能体框架，把视频世界模型（VWM）作为统一的视觉推理引擎，通过工具化的视觉操作与技能引导 VLM 灵活决定"何时想象、如何想象、如何解读"，并引入工具-技能-代码与 VLM 的协同进化，在 7 个基准上全面超越同一骨干直接推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 需要在图像之外进行关于运动、动作和视角变化的时空推理，而 VWM 可生成任务相关的视觉内容作为补充证据。但现有方法以固定流水线调用 VWM（如仅做新视角合成），难以适配多样推理任务所需的灵活视觉想象。

Method: 提出 CoWorld Reasoner 智能体框架：1) Video World Model-Driven Harness 通过工具暴露多种视觉操作，并用技能（skills）引导 VLM 判断是否需要想象、选择操作、构造生成请求、解读生成的视觉证据；2) VLM-VWM Harness Co-Evolution，在模型权重固定的前提下，依据执行反馈联合修订工具、技能与执行代码。

Result: 在覆盖空间智能、时序与因果推理、感知推理的 7 个基准上优于同 VLM 直接推理；最强变体在全部 7 个基准上均有提升，MMSI 相对准确率提升 11.8%、ERQA 提升 13.0%；并在 5 个基准上超过使用相同骨干的 5 个进化基线。

Conclusion: 将 VWM 从固定用途生成器转变为可由智能体灵活调度的统一视觉推理引擎，配合免微调的工具/技能/代码协同进化，可有效增强 VLM 的时空与感知推理能力。

Abstract: As Vision-Language Models (VLMs) advance, they are increasingly expected to perform spatiotemporal reasoning over motion, actions, and view changes beyond what the input images show. Video World Models (VWMs) provide a natural unified engine for visual imagination by generating task-relevant visual content as additional evidence. However, existing approaches typically invoke a VWM through fixed pipelines centered on a predefined form of visual imagination, such as novel-view synthesis. To support flexible visual imagination for diverse reasoning tasks, we introduce CoWorld Reasoner, an agentic framework that uses a VWM as a unified visual reasoning engine. Its Video World Model-Driven Harness exposes multiple visual operations through tools and uses skills to guide the VLM in deciding whether to imagine, selecting an operation, constructing a generation request, and interpreting the resulting visual evidence. We further introduce VLM-VWM Harness Co-Evolution, which jointly revises tools, skills, and execution code based on execution feedback while keeping both model weights fixed. Across seven benchmarks spanning spatial intelligence, temporal and causal reasoning, and perceptual reasoning, CoWorld Reasoner outperforms direct reasoning with the same VLM. The strongest variant improves on all seven benchmarks, including relative accuracy gains of 11.8% on MMSI and 13.0% on ERQA, and outperforms all five evolving baselines using the same backbone across five benchmarks.

</details>


### [287] [OntoAct: Hypothesis-Based Neuro-Symbolic Agents through Ontological Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21983)
*Donghyeok Lee,Bong Gyun Kang,Junyong Ahn,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OntoAct：基于本体抽象的假设驱动神经符号框架，将个体经验转化为关于实体类型、关系与条件的公理，由LLM提出假设、符号验证器检验支持条件，并通过缺失证据识别与干预反馈迭代修正；同时提出Tacit-FLE基准（基于Factorio环境）诊断与修复工厂隐藏故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从经验中学习需要恢复可复用知识并判断其在新情境下的适用性；现有方法难以将个体经验抽象为可跨情境迁移的知识，并缺乏对隐藏故障诊断与修复中证据分散、诊断需反复修正这一能力的评测。

Method: 提出OntoAct神经符号框架：将经验抽象为实体类型、关系、条件上的公理（本体抽象）；LLM基于世界知识提出假设与动作，符号验证器依据当前观测评估其支持条件；验证识别缺失证据，新观测与干预结果更新假设评估并指导后续动作。另构建Tacit-FLE基准，基于Factorio Learning Environment，用继承的运行记录诊断修复既有工厂的隐藏故障，合成维护语料将证据分散于记录中。

Result: 在Tacit-FLE、Mars与ALFWorld上，OntoAct取得领先的任务表现，且交互与推理开销高效。

Conclusion: 以本体抽象公理化经验并结合神经符号验证，可有效实现跨情境可复用知识与假设闭环修正，在隐藏故障诊断修复及多类任务环境中表现优异且高效。

Abstract: Learning from experience requires recovering reusable knowledge and determining when it applies to a new situation. We introduce OntoAct, a hypothesis-based neuro-symbolic framework built on ontological abstraction. OntoAct turns individual experiences into axioms over entity types, relations, and conditions, making their underlying patterns reusable across situations. A large language model (LLM) uses this world knowledge to propose hypotheses and actions, while a symbolic verifier assesses their supporting conditions against current observations. Verification identifies missing evidence, and new observations and intervention outcomes update hypothesis assessments and guide subsequent actions. We also introduce Tacit-FLE, a benchmark built on the Factorio Learning Environment (FLE) for diagnosing and repairing hidden faults in existing factories using inherited operational records. Its synthetic maintenance corpus distributes evidence across records, and an executed repair may leave the fault unresolved, requiring agents to reassess their diagnoses through interaction. Across Tacit-FLE, Mars, and ALFWorld, OntoAct achieves leading task performance with efficient interaction and inference.

</details>


### [288] [GraphACM: Graph-based Action Consistency Measurement for Uncertainty Quantification in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22031)
*Yaxin Ma,Wenbo Zhang,Satya Krishna Pothapragada,Alina Zare*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于图的细粒度行为一致性度量与不确定性量化框架，将同一任务多次 rollout 的等价状态合并为有向、动作用标签标注的图，在共享决策状态下比较动作，并用全局一致性分数作为不确定性代理预测任务成败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅用单一任务级分数概括行为一致性，无法定位行为差异出现的位置，也未保证动作比较发生在等价的决策上下文中，因而对 LLM 智能体可靠性的刻画不够精细。

Method: 对同一任务的多次执行进行状态合并，构建有向、动作标注图，使动作仅在共享决策节点上对比；提出节点级与全局级动作一致性度量，并将全局一致性分数作为任务不确定性的代理指标，用于预测任务成功或失败。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、AppWorld、WebShop 三个环境、三种 LLM 上的实验表明，该度量与任务成功正相关；任务成功预测平均 AUROC 达 0.811，优于最强行为一致性基线（0.729）与语言化置信度（0.769）。

Conclusion: 状态条件化的行为一致性度量有效，能够提升任务成功预测能力，并可定位智能体动作一致的具体位置。

Abstract: Behavioral consistency across repeated executions provides an informative signal for assessing the reliability of large language model (LLM) agents. However, existing approaches typically summarize consistency with a single task-level score, without identifying where behavioral differences occur or whether actions are compared under equivalent decision contexts. We propose a graph-based framework for fine-grained behavioral consistency measurement and uncertainty quantification. Given multiple rollouts of the same task, we merge equivalent states into a directed, action-labeled graph, enabling action comparisons at shared decision states. We introduce node-level and global measures of action consistency, using the global consistency score as a proxy for uncertainty to predict task success or failure. Experiments across three LLMs on ALFWorld, AppWorld, and WebShop demonstrate that our measure is positively associated with task success and achieves an average AUROC of 0.811 for task-success prediction, outperforming the strongest behavioral-consistency baseline (0.729) and verbalized confidence (0.769). These results highlight the effectiveness of state-conditioned behavioral consistency and pinpoint where agent actions agree.

</details>


### [289] [Progress Reward Model for Reinforcement Learning via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22056)
*Xiuhui Zhang,Ning Gao,Xingyu Jiang,Yihui Chen,Yuheng Pan,Mohan Zhang,Yue Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PRM4RL框架，结合LLM任务分解与基于势能塑造的进度奖励模型，为稀疏奖励长程RL任务提供密集奖励与收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RL在稀疏奖励长程任务上受限；现有LLM增强方法中，规划类依赖预定义技能库且不优化底层控制策略，奖励类需大量人工反馈或穷举搜索。

Method: 高层规划：将复杂任务分解为可管理的简单子任务，设计子任务导向的细粒度进度函数监控执行进度。低层奖励：受势能奖励塑造启发，用进度函数构建进度奖励模型（PRM），提供理论上的最优性与收敛性保证。

Result: 在机器人控制任务上优于基于LLM的规划方法与奖励方法，达到SOTA性能；代码开源。

Conclusion: PRM4RL将任务规划与密集奖励统一，兼顾高层的任务分解与低层的策略优化，为稀疏奖励长程RL提供有理论保证的有效方案。

Abstract: Traditional reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms face significant limitations in handling long-term tasks with sparse rewards. Recent advancements have leveraged large language models (LLMs) to enhance RL by utilizing their world knowledge for task planning and reward generation. However, planning-based approaches often depend on pre-defined skill libraries and fail to optimize low-level control policies, while reward-based methods require extensive human feedback or exhaustive searching due to the complexity of tasks. In this paper, we propose the Progress Reward Model for RL (PRM4RL), a novel framework that integrates task planning and dense reward to enhance RL. For high-level planning, a complex task is decomposed into a series of simple manageable subtasks, with a subtask-oriented, fine-grained progress function designed to monitor task execution progress. For low-level reward generation, inspired by potential-based reward shaping, we use the progress function to construct a Progress Reward Model (PRM), providing theoretically grounded optimality and convergence guarantees, thereby enabling effective policy optimization. Experimental results on robotics control tasks demonstrate that our approach outperforms both LLM-based planning and reward methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance. Code is available at https://github.com/deng-ai-lab/PRM4RL.

</details>


### [290] [FAOPs: Normal Forms and an Algebra for AI Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22061)
*Ali Mohammadi Shanghooshabad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FAOP形式化理论，将AI agent工作流建模为带作用域状态的类型化函数，施加四种递进范式（分解、类型化、因子化、作用域化），配合可靠等价代数、终止/优化/成本扩展及架构级分类器，并实现SkillBase系统与运行时执行器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监管行业要求AOP（Agent Operating Procedures）可追溯、可审计、可复现、访问受控且有时间和成本边界，但现有AOP以粗粒度文件形式存在：步骤捆绑多个动作、复制导致漂移、任一步骤可访问任意工具、重构缺乏检查。目标是把完整性、隔离、类型、终止性、成本与优化从运行时期望变为编译期关注点。

Method: FAOP形式化理论：将工作流重铸为作用于作用域状态的类型化函数，对库施加四级范式——FNF-1分解为原子步骤、FNF-2显式类型化数据流、FNF-3共享过程单一规范定义（因子化）、FNF-4最小权限工具作用域；每个原子步骤成为带声明契约的独立实体。构建可证可靠的等式代数使重构与并行化成为编译器重写；正交扩展+V（验证终止）、+O（优化）、+B（时间成本界）；架构级分类器（0-3）判定工作流是否需要AI。实现SkillBase（AOP管理，类比PostgreSQL之于数据）与独立运行期执行器，并以金融工作流案例端到端演示。

Result: 在SkillBase系统与执行器上实现该理论，通过一个端到端金融工作流案例展示：规范化后各原子步骤可独立类型检查、作用域限制、边界约束、测试、追踪与审计，重构与并行化由编译器重写完成。

Conclusion: FAOP使完整性、隔离、类型化、终止、成本与优化成为覆盖整个库的编译期保证，而非运行时期望；SkillBase为AOP提供类似PostgreSQL的规范化管理系统。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly encode operational procedures as multi-step agent skills, here called Agent Operating Procedures (AOPs), that must be traceable, auditable, reproducible, access-controlled, and bounded in time and cost, requirements that regulated sectors make mandatory. Yet AOPs are typically kept as coarse-grained files, in which steps bundle actions, copied procedures drift apart, any step may reach any tool, and refactoring goes unchecked. We present Functional Agent Operating Procedures (FAOP), a formal theory that brings database-grade discipline to AI agent workflows. FAOP recasts each workflow as a typed function over scoped state and subjects an entire library to four progressive normal forms: Decomposed (FNF-1) splits a workflow into atomic steps, Typed (FNF-2) makes its data flow explicit and type-checked, Factored (FNF-3) gives each shared procedure a single canonical definition so a change propagates instead of drifting, and Scoped (FNF-4) confines every step to least-privilege tool access. Once normalized, every atomic step is a separate entity with a declared contract and can be type-checked, scope-confined, bounded, tested, traced, and audited on its own. A FAOP algebra of provably sound equalities turns refactoring and parallelization into compiler rewrites; orthogonal extensions add verified termination (+V), optimization (+O), and time and cost bounds (+B); and an architectural-level classifier (0-3) determines whether a workflow needs AI at all. Together, these make integrity, isolation, typing, termination, cost, and optimization compile-time concerns across the whole library, not runtime hopes. We implement the theory in SkillBase, an AOP management system (what PostgreSQL is to data, SkillBase is to AOPs), together with a separate executor that enforces these guarantees at run time. A worked financial workflow illustrates the approach end to end.

</details>


### [291] [Prism-1: Position- and Rival-Independent Scoring for System One Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22066)
*Yao Long Teng,Jianguo Huang,Yanchen Deng,Jiayi Cai,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Prism-1，通过让每个选项在独立块内、共享位置上被前向读取，从结构上保证System One决策同时满足位置无关性与Luce选择公理，且精度不掉点、排序鲁棒性更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web系统越来越多地用语言模型直接读取System One决策（意图、工具、界面元素），而选项列表与选项集合由其他组件决定、可能变化或被操纵。因此两条理性选择公理成为自然要求：选项列举顺序不应影响结果；新增选项不应改变其他选项的相对概率（Luce选择公理）。现有主流读法普遍违反这两条。

Method: 先经验性检验标准读法：单选择问答（SCQ）提示、乱序训练、顺序平均、选项排序，以及不变量集合架构；再从理论上借助Luce定理推出：满足两公理的读法必须是各选项独立打分后的softmax，因此在Transformer中只有"打分某选项的token看不到其他选项"才能由构造保证。据此实现Prism-1：每个选项在各自块中、于共享位置读取，保留模型惯常的从左到右注意力，单次前向完成。在5个基准集、Qwen3-8B与Gemma 4 12B上评测。

Result: 同数据训练的SCQ基线在单次乱序下仍有7%答案改变，加入无关选项会操纵12%的决策；而Prism-1精度与基线相差1个百分点以内，在仅计"重排后仍存活"的答案时准确率高2-4个百分点，且任何乱序都不改变其答案，任何新增无关选项都不影响其对原有选项的选择。对现有各类读法的检验显示：一次乱序改变12-20%的SCQ答案，排序只是冻结了任意约定，精心选取的新增选项可操纵4-34%的决策。

Conclusion: 顺序无关与Luce公理无法靠数据训练或后处理获得，只能由读取结构保证；Prism-1以构造成本极低的方式（共享位置、独立块、单次前向）实现了这两条公理的硬保证，同时保持竞争力精度，为Web系统中的System One决策提供了可验证的理性基础。

Abstract: Given a state and a set of options, such as intents, tools or interface elements, System One decision models return a distribution over the options in one forward pass, without generated reasoning. Web systems increasingly build them by reading decisions from language models. Two axioms of rational choice are natural requirements for such decisions, because in web systems the listing and the option set are determined by other components and can change or be manipulated: the order in which the options are listed should not matter, and adding an option should not change the relative odds of the others (Luce's choice axiom). We test the standard reads, namely single-choice question (SCQ) prompting, with shuffled-order training, order averaging or sorted options, and invariant set architectures, and find that each violates at least one axiom: one shuffle changes 12-20% of SCQ answers, sorting only freezes an arbitrary convention, and a well-chosen added option steers 4-34% of decisions. By Luce's theorem, a read satisfies both axioms only if it is a softmax over scores that each depend on one option alone, so in a transformer they hold by construction only if no token that scores an option sees another. We implement this as Prism-1 (Position- and Rival-Independent Scoring for System One Models): every option is read in its own block at shared positions, with the model's usual left-to-right attention, in one forward pass. On five benchmark sets with Qwen3-8B and Gemma 4 12B, SCQ prompting trained on the same data still changes 7% of its answers under one shuffle, and an added irrelevant option steers 12% of its decisions. Prism-1 stays within one point of that baseline's accuracy and is 2-4 points more accurate once only answers that survive a reordering are counted, while no shuffle changes any of its answers and no added irrelevant option moves its choice among the others.

</details>


### [292] [When Reasoning Is Not Enough: Proactive Inquiry for Cross-Modal Safety in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22068)
*Wei Cai,Jian Zhao,Yuchen Yuan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SAFE-PRO，用风险条件不确定性(RCU)信号决定何时提出安全澄清问题、何处做路径优化，并构建SSUI-Dialog数据集；在七个基准上安全性优于SRPO，过拒答约减半，单轮平均安全性超过GPT-4o与Claude-3.5-Sonnet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在单独无害的图像与文本联合解读时可能产生不安全行为。除隐式推理风险（推理漂移向危害）外，作者识别出意图层风险：跨模态歧义使模型静默接受危险前提，仅靠路径级优化无法纠正。

Method: 提出SAFE-PRO：(1) 风险条件不确定性(RCU)信号，将预测熵与可学习的跨模态风险分数结合，用于判定何时提出安全导向的澄清问题、何处施加路径优化；(2) 在优先安全并惩罚过度拒答的奖励下优化；(3) 构建SSUI-Dialog——具双意图监督的多模态多轮对话数据集；(4) 理论分析RCU下的梯度方差与不安全完成风险。

Result: 在七个基准、两个基座MLLM上，SAFE-PRO安全性持续优于SRPO，过度拒答约减半，平均单轮安全性超过GPT-4o与Claude-3.5-Sonnet。

Conclusion: 跨模态歧义导致的意图层风险需通过主动澄清与路径优化协同解决；RCU驱动的决策机制在提升安全性的同时有效缓解过度拒答，兼顾安全与可用性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can act unsafely when individually benign images and texts are interpreted jointly. Beyond implicit reasoning risk, where reasoning drifts toward harm, we identify intent-level risk, where cross-modal ambiguity silently commits the model to a hazardous premise that path-level optimization alone cannot correct. We propose SAFE-PRO, which uses a Risk-Conditioned Uncertainty (RCU) signal, combining predictive entropy with a learned cross-modal risk score, to decide when to ask safety-grounded clarifying questions and where to apply path optimization, under a reward that prioritizes safety and penalizes over-refusal. We also build SSUI-Dialog, a multimodal multi-turn dataset with dual-intent supervision, and theoretically analyze gradient variance and unsafe-completion risk under RCU. Across seven benchmarks and two base MLLMs, SAFE-PRO consistently improves safety over SRPO, roughly halves its over-refusal, and surpasses GPT-4o and Claude-3.5-Sonnet in average single-turn safety.

</details>


### [293] [Learning from Unverified Peers via Shared Residual Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22080)
*Feiyang Chen,Runze Ma,Zichen Wang,Jiong Lou,Zihan Wang,Shuangjia Zheng,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出共享残差蒸馏（SRD），一种在线自蒸馏方法：用模型自身未经验证的同侪（peer）轨迹作为监督信号，通过对比"有/无同侪"下的预测分布变化提取保守且方向一致的调整量，从而在不判定任一完整解正确性的前提下实现自我提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法依赖参考答案、可验证反馈或外部教师来把多条轨迹转化为学习信号；缺少这种监督时，模型难以可靠评估自身生成结果的对错。而错误解法中仍可能包含有用的中间推理，因此其监督价值不应仅由最终答案决定。作者因此追问：能否在事先不判断对错的情况下，从模型自身未经验证的同侪解答中学习并提升？

Method: SRD 为 on-policy 自蒸馏：将模型自身生成的同侪轨迹作为上下文条件，比较其相对于对照条件（control）对预测分布的影响，计算"共享残差"；仅保留方向一致（agreement in direction）且幅度保守（conservative in magnitude）的分布调整作为伪监督目标，避免把任何完整解答当作正确答案。训练后模型在推理阶段无需同侪上下文，无额外开销。

Result: 从各自基座模型出发，SRD 将 AIME24–26 avg@12 从 11.02 提升至 16.20（Qwen3-1.7B）、从 19.07 提升至 45.46（Qwen3-4B）、从 21.39 提升至 54.44（Qwen3-8B）。受控消融实验验证了共享残差构造的有效性；在两个通用能力基准上相对基座模型无明显退化。

Conclusion: 结果表明，模型可在不检验解答正确性的条件下，仅凭自身未经验证的同侪轨迹获得有效监督并显著提升数学推理性能，且不损害通用能力，为无验证器/无外部教师的自我改进提供了可行路径。

Abstract: A language model can generate several solutions to the same problem, some leading to correct answers and others to incorrect ones. Many post-training methods turn these trajectories into learning signals using supervision from reference answers, verifiable feedback, or external teachers. Without such supervision, learning from the model's own generations becomes less straightforward, as their correctness cannot be reliably assessed. An incorrect solution may nevertheless contain useful intermediate reasoning, so its value as supervision need not be determined solely by its final answer. Can a model improve by learning from its unverified peer solutions without first determining which are correct?
  We propose Shared Residual Distillation (SRD), an on-policy self-distillation method that learns from the model's own unverified peer trajectories. SRD compares how peers change the prediction distribution relative to a control, retaining adjustments that agree in direction and are conservative in magnitude. This yields supervision without treating any complete solution as correct, and the trained model requires no peers at inference time. Starting from the corresponding base models, SRD raises mean AIME24--26 avg@12 from 11.02 to 16.20 on Qwen3-1.7B, from 19.07 to 45.46 on Qwen3-4B, and from 21.39 to 54.44 on Qwen3-8B. Controlled ablations support the value of the shared-residual construction, and we observe no degradation relative to the base models on two general-capability benchmarks.

</details>


### [294] [PROBE: Learning to Audit Policy Compliance in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22124)
*Kshitij Mishra,Abhijith Sharma,Nils Lukas,Salem Lahlou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PROBE：将 LLM 审计员训练为拟真用户，通过多轮、工具中介的对话探测部署中智能体的策略违规，并把智能体审计形式化为双人马尔可夫博弈，比较 GRPO/DPO/GFlowNet 优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估存在空白：能力基准测"能做什么"，内容安全红队测"有害生成"，但都不检验智能体在合法工具使用过程中是否遵守价格、隐私、确认、安全等预设策略；且此类违规往往多轮后才显现、跨多次工具调用或在下游系统中暴露，难以审计。

Method: 提出 PROBE（Policy Rule Observation via Behavioral Exploration）算法，训练基于 LLM 的审计员以真实用户身份与被部署智能体交互；将审计形式化为两人马尔可夫博弈；设计复合奖励，平衡违规发现、行为合理性、对话推进与策略多样性；在受控设定下对比 GRPO、DPO 与 GFlowNet 三种优化目标。

Result: PROBE 训练的审计员相比同架构零样本对抗者，发现的策略违规范围更广、命中率更高，即使审计员模型显著小于目标智能体；并得出多轮行为审计是独立且必要的评估维度的结论。

Conclusion: 多轮行为审计与能力、安全基准互补，构成智能体评估的独立轴；PROBE 为真实智能体系统提供可扩展的自动化策略审计路径，并指向"审计员暴露失败—智能体依据反馈改进"的协同演化闭环。

Abstract: Modern agentic systems extend language models with external tools, enabling them to complete complex tasks through multi-turn interactions. As these agents are deployed in consequential settings, they are often constrained by policies governing pricing, privacy, confirmation, safety, and other requirements. However, existing evaluation methods leave a critical gap: capability benchmarks measure what agents can accomplish, while content-safety red-teaming measures harmful generations, but neither tests whether agents comply with predefined policies during otherwise valid tool use. Such policy violations are difficult to audit because their effects may emerge only after several turns, span multiple tool calls, or surface in downstream systems. We propose PROBE (Policy Rule Observation via Behavioral Exploration), an algorithm for training LLM-based auditors that interact with deployed agents as realistic users and probe them for policy violations through multi-turn, tool-mediated dialogue. We formalize agent auditing as a two-player Markov game and propose a composite reward that balances violation discovery, behavioral plausibility, conversation progress, and policy diversity. To study training objectives and reward design, we compare GRPO, DPO, and GFlowNet-based optimization in a controlled setting. Across extensive experiments, auditors trained with PROBE uncover substantially broader and higher-yield policy violations than same-architecture zero-shot adversaries, even when the auditor is significantly smaller than the target agent. Our results show that multi-turn behavioral auditing is a distinct and necessary axis of agent evaluation, complementing capability and safety benchmarks. PROBE offers a scalable path toward automated policy auditing for real-world agentic systems and suggests a co-evolutionary loop in which auditors expose failures and agents improve from auditor feedback.

</details>


### [295] [DropKV: Decoupling Residual-Output Perturbation for Near-Optimal KV-Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22130)
*Aozhong Zhang,Selcuk Gurses,Yanxia Deng,Naigang Wang,Chi-Chun Liu,Davis Wertheimer,Derrick Liu,Xin Li,Zi Yang,Felix X. -F. Ye,Penghang Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DropKV，一个基于“解耦残差-输出扰动”的 KV cache 驱逐框架：将组合式联合驱逐决策解耦为逐 token 独立打分，从而规避 NP-hard 问题并获得可证明的常数因子近似保证；同时用融合 Triton kernel 实现，取得 19.8x 打分核加速与最高 9.9% 的端到端 prefill 加速，在 RULER 与 LongBench 上于同等缓存预算下优于 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文场景下 KV cache 随序列长度线性增长，成为 LLM 扩展的关键瓶颈。现有驱逐方法虽从朴素注意力分数启发式转向引入 value 表示，但仍面临根本性权衡：最小化输出扰动的本质是 NP-hard 组合优化问题，导致方法要么是数学上次优的启发式，要么难以高效 kernel 化实现。

Method: 提出 DropKV，基于 Decoupled Residual-Output Perturbation：把联合驱逐决策解耦为独立的逐 token 打分，规避组合不可解性；在经验验证于三个长上下文 LLM 上的锥（cone）条件下给出常数因子近似保证；并实现融合 Triton kernel，避免显式物化完整注意力矩阵。

Result: 打分 kernel 加速 19.8x，转化为最高 9.9% 的 matched-batch 端到端 prefill 加速；在 RULER 与 LongBench 上，等缓存预算下持续优于 SOTA 基线，在各类驱逐方法中推理时延最低，且快于动态缓存基线。

Conclusion: DropKV 以简单且有理论依据的解耦式驱逐框架，同时兼顾近似最优性与工程可实现性，在效果与效率上均优于现有 KV cache 驱逐方法，适用于长上下文 LLM 推理的 KV cache 管理。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) cache management has become a critical bottleneck for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs) to long-context scenarios due to linear memory growth. While recent eviction methods have moved beyond simple attention-score heuristics to incorporate value representations, they typically face a fundamental trade-off: the underlying optimization for minimizing output perturbation is an NP-hard combinatorial problem, leading to mathematically suboptimal heuristics or implementations that are difficult to kernelize. In this work, we propose DropKV, a simple principled eviction framework based on Decoupled Residual-Output Perturbation. By decoupling the joint eviction decision into independent per-token scoring, DropKV circumvents combinatorial intractability and admits a provable constant-factor approximation guarantee under a cone condition that we empirically verify on three long-context LLMs. To ensure practical viability, we provide a high-performance implementation using fused Triton kernels that avoid materializing the full attention matrix, delivering a 19.8x scoring-kernel speedup that translates to up to a 9.9% matched-batch end-to-end prefill speedup. Extensive evaluations on the RULER and LongBench benchmarks demonstrate that DropKV consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines at equivalent cache budgets, achieving the lowest inference latency among eviction methods while also remaining faster than the dynamic cache baseline.

</details>


### [296] [CART: Compatible Routing for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22137)
*Ling-Xiang Zhang,Tao Hu,Da-Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出兼容路由（CART），通过为投影器与语言模型同时维护任务专家并按权重混合，联合考虑专家相关性与跨模块兼容性推断权重，并用相关性引导的专家合并控制模型增长，实现两大模块协同适配，显著缓解多模态持续指令微调中的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态持续指令微调（MCIT）方法主要保护语言模型不被遗忘，却持续更新投影器，导致非对称适配：投影器表征漂移，与语言模型错配加剧，反而放大灾难性遗忘。

Method: 1) 构建CART框架，为投影器和语言模型分别维护任务特定专家，并以加权混合方式组合；2) 权重联合推断同时考虑专家相关性（对当前任务）与跨模块兼容性，促使双模块协同适配；3) 引入相关性引导的专家合并，将各模块内相关性画像相似的专家合并，以抑制模型规模增长。

Result: 大量实验表明CART取得state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 持续微调中投影器与语言模型需协同适配，而非单方面保护语言模型；基于兼容性感知的路由加专家合并可在控制模型规模的同时有效缓解遗忘，为MCIT提供新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance through instruction tuning, yet real-world applications often require them to continually acquire new capabilities from sequential tasks, i.e., Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT). In typical MLLMs, a projector is employed to align visual representations with the language model. However, existing methods primarily protect the language model from forgetting while continuously updating the projector across tasks. Such asymmetric adaptation can cause substantial representation drift in the projector, increasing the mismatch between the projector and language model and consequently exacerbating catastrophic forgetting. To address this issue, we propose Compatible Routing (CART), which explicitly coordinates the adaptation of the projector and language model. Specifically, CART maintains task-specific experts for both modules and combines them through weighted mixtures. CART jointly infers their weights by considering both expert relevance and cross-module compatibility, thereby promoting coordinated adaptation between the two modules. To further control model growth, we introduce relevance-guided expert consolidation, which merges experts exhibiting similar relevance profiles across samples within each module. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CART achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [297] [Believed but Not Said: Measuring Hidden Belief Collapse in Multi-Agent LLM Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22138)
*Liangjie Huang,Henry Peng Zou,Wei-Chieh Huang,Wooseong Yang,Yuwei Han,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在多智能体讨论中，极少数的对抗性智能体就能大幅诱导良性智能体的私有判断转向错误答案，且这种影响主要隐藏在私下改变而非公开表态中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统普遍假设同伴之间会相互纠错，但该假设缺乏严格检验。作者希望用一个最小化对抗者设置，检验同伴监督在抵御错误信息时的真实鲁棒性，并区分公开立场与私有信念。

Method: 构建十智能体事实性讨论场景，加入1个或3个反复断言似是而非错误答案的对抗智能体；每轮通过一个对同伴隐藏响应的独立调用探测每个良性智能体的私有答案，同时单独标注其对该主张的公开立场；覆盖6个模型与3个事实QA数据集，并设置无攻击者基线。

Result: 无攻击者时群体稳定，仅0.6%良性智能体最终给出错误答案；1个对抗者使该比例升至44.8%，3个升至60.5%。3个对抗者下，良性智能体在51.6%的暴露轮次中私下持有错误答案，但仅19.7%公开认可，与从众研究常见的“公开顺从、私下不变”相反。保留正确答案的智能体常无法为其辩护，错误答案会在良性智能体间扩散，且基线知识水平不能预测鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 群体讨论并不天然具备纠错能力，少量对抗者即可显著污染同伴的私有信念且难以从公开讨论中察觉；多智能体系统的可靠性假设需要重新审视，评估应同时探测私有与公开状态。

Abstract: LLM systems increasingly rely on groups of agents that discuss, critique, and check one another, assuming that peers will catch individual errors. We test this assumption against a minimal adversary. In a ten-agent factual discussion, one or three adversarial agents repeatedly assert a plausible false answer. After each round we probe each benign agent's private answer through a separate call whose response is hidden from its peers, and we separately label its public stance toward the claim. Across six models and three factual-QA datasets, groups remain stable without an attacker: only 0.6% of benign agents finish with the false answer. A single adversarial agent increases this rate to 44.8% and three to 60.5%. Studies of conformity typically report public agreement without private change; we find the reverse. With three adversarial agents present, benign agents hold the false answer in 51.6% of exposed turns but publicly endorse it in only 19.7%. Much of the influence therefore remains invisible in the discussion, and the false answers persist when agents are asked for their own views. Agents that retain the true answer often fail to defend it; once adopted, the false answer spreads among benign agents, and baseline knowledge does not predict robustness.

</details>


### [298] [Are You Sure Your LLM Is Sure? Repairing and Auditing Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22139)
*Arijit Dey,Bhartendu Kumar,Ramya Hebbalaguppe,Khuushi Maheshwari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出RADAR可靠性框架，将置信度"修复"与"是否采纳该修复"以及"审计残留错误"分离；修复环节用探针锚定的保序校准（PAIC）在学习率上依赖探针的条件偏差而非整体准确率，采纳环节给出具有有限样本假改进控制的配对程序，审计环节用提示聚类的核检验检测与特征对齐的残差错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量重校准只能纠正"通过分数本身可见"的错误，无法利用分数之外的信息，也无法判断重校准是否真的带来收益、以及残留的系统性错误在哪里。因此需要一个把修复、采纳与审计分开的原则性工作流。

Method: 1) 在平方损失下将预测风险分解为结果噪声、被分数隐藏的信息、映射误差，并指出限定在保序映射时额外引入pooling gap；2) 修复：PAIC在无标签目标提示上学习正确性探针预测的单调映射；3) 采纳：推导配对程序，对假改进做有限样本控制（配对betting）；4) 审计：提示聚类的核检验，在考虑同一提示多答案相关性的前提下检测特征对齐的残差错误。

Result: 在多个LLM检查点、基准与分布偏移下，更丰富的预测变量可改善置信度估计，但把其信息投影回标量分数会损失收益，且更低的预测风险未必带来更好的复核决策；47,400次回放原始分数分配的实验中，配对betting在42.95%的分配中采纳了留出测试风险更低的候选，而单侧经验Bernstein仅为15.09%。

Conclusion: RADAR提供了原则性流程：修复标量分数可表达的部分、仅在证据支持时采纳修复、并诊断重校准后残留的系统性错误。

Abstract: Confidence scores determine whether an LLM answer is accepted, reviewed, or regenerated, yet scalar recalibration can only correct errors visible through the score itself. We introduce RADAR (Repair, Adopt, and Diagnose After Recalibration), a reliability framework that separates confidence repair from deciding whether to deploy the repair and auditing the errors that remain. Under squared loss, we decompose prediction risk into outcome noise, information hidden by the score, and mapping error; restricting repair to order-preserving maps additionally introduces a pooling gap. For repair, we propose Probe-Anchored Isotonic Calibration (PAIC), which learns a monotone map from correctness-probe predictions on unlabeled target prompts. PAIC's excess risk depends on the probe's score-conditional bias and fitting error rather than its overall accuracy. For adoption, we derive a paired procedure with finite-sample control of false improvements. For auditing, we develop a prompt-clustered kernel test that detects feature-aligned residual errors while accounting for dependent answers from the same prompt. Across multiple LLM checkpoints, benchmarks, and distribution shifts, richer predictors can improve confidence estimation, but projecting their information back to a scalar score can lose these gains, and lower prediction risk need not yield better review decisions. Across 47,400 replayed raw-score allocations, paired betting adopts candidates with lower held-out test risk in 42.95% of allocations, versus 15.09% for one-sided empirical Bernstein. RADAR thus provides a principled workflow to repair what a scalar score can express, adopt a repair only when supported by evidence, and diagnose systematic errors that recalibration leaves behind. Code and data will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [299] [RAG-RST: Retrieved Exemplars as In-Context Discourse Schema for RST Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22150)
*Mohammadreza Sediqin,Shlomo Engelson Argamon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAG-RST：以检索增强方式把RST标注图式作为上下文示例提供给LLM，无需训练即可提升核性（0.846 F1）与关系（0.788 F1）预测，并优于现有RST解析器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RST中核性标签与关系类别均非表层属性，同一对子句可依修辞意图归为Elaboration或Background，边界由标注惯例而非词汇线索决定。预测这类标签需要形式化的语篇图式，而仅靠语篇丰富的预训练文本无法获得该图式——强LLM在仅给目标EDU对时仅达核性0.794 F1、关系0.738 F1。

Method: RAG-RST以检索增强的上下文示例形式提供该图式，而非通过参数更新注入。比较两种配置：（1）预计算策略，在解析前为所有EDU对打标签；（2）动态策略，在每个reduce步检索，使送入生成器的示例取决于解析器已确定的归约。

Result: 核性由0.794提升至0.846 F1，关系由0.738提升至0.788 F1，且无需训练；两种配置在跨度、核性、关系及完整树指标上均优于现有RST解析器，动态变体始终最强。检索图式呈现两点性质：效用随示例数有界，超过50个后饱和并下降；且依赖结构位置。

Conclusion: 形式化语言学图式可以作为演示提供给基础模型，而无需训练进其参数中。

Abstract: In Rhetorical Structure Theory (RST), each edge of a discourse tree carries a nuclearity label, marking which span holds the central content, and a relation drawn from a closed inventory of functional categories. Neither label is a surface property: the same two clauses can be Elaboration or Background depending on rhetorical intent, and the boundary between them is fixed by annotation convention rather than by lexical cues. Predicting these labels therefore requires a formal discourse schema, and exposure to discourse-rich pretraining text does not supply it: given only a target EDU pair, a strong LLM recovers nuclearity at 0.794 F1 and relation at 0.738 F1. We propose RAG-RST, a retrieval-augmented framework that supplies this schema as in-context demonstration rather than through parameter updates, raising nuclearity to 0.846 F1 and relation to 0.788 F1 with no training. Two configurations are compared: a precomputed strategy that labels all EDU pairs before parsing, and a dynamic strategy that retrieves at each reduce step, so that the exemplars reaching the generator depend on the reductions the parser has already committed to. Both outperform existing RST parsers across span, nuclearity, relation, and full tree metrics, with the dynamic variant consistently strongest. Two properties of the retrieved schema emerge: its utility is bounded in number, saturating and then declining beyond fifty exemplars, and it depends on structural position. Both indicate that a formal linguistic schema can be supplied to a foundation model as a demonstration rather than trained into its parameters.

</details>


### [300] [Realtime-Bench: Can Frontier Models Solve Real-time Interactive Problems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22155)
*Jingyu Tang,Xu Yang,Zhehao Zhang,Zheng Wang,Qi Cheng,Haozhe Zhao,Manling Li,Wenxuan Li,Xide Xia,Jiaxin Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Realtime-Bench 基准，在严格时间约束下评测实时流式模型的动作有效性，发现最佳成功率仅 44%、Pass^3 仅 36%，并通过三种时钟设定分离服务延迟与模型实时流式能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时流式模型评测仅关注对话质量与流式感知，未测试在严格时间约束下的有效动作能力；延迟动作会直接影响任务成败，而这一维度缺乏基准。

Method: 构建 Realtime-Bench 动态任务集，动作延迟直接影响成功率。设计三种时钟：Wall Clock（环境按真实推理延迟推进）、Paused Clock（去除推理延迟的理想参照）、Token Clock（按生成 token 数推进模拟时间，独立于物理服务速度测试时间感知），并对比不同推理投入强度的影响。

Result: Wall Clock 下最佳成功率 44%，最佳 Pass^3 仅 36%；时间成本是动态环境中成功的主要限制；更高推理投入在时间压力下效果混杂，体现决策质量与及时执行的权衡。

Conclusion: 实时交互仍具挑战性；改进方向包括具备时间感知的自适应推理与连续“感知—行动”闭环。

Abstract: Real-time interaction requires agents to perceive evolving environments and act under temporal constraints, yet existing evaluations of real-time streaming models focus on conversational quality and streaming perception, leaving effective action under strict temporal constraints untested. We introduce Realtime-Bench, a suite of carefully curated dynamic tasks where delayed actions directly affect success. Under Wall Clock, where environments advance according to actual inference latency, the best success rate reaches only 44% and the best Pass^3 is only 36%, demonstrating that real-time interaction remains challenging. To separate serving latency from models' real-time streaming capability, we compare Wall Clock with Paused Clock, which removes inference delays as an idealized reference, and introduce Token Clock, which advances simulated time according to generated token counts, testing temporal awareness independently of physical serving speed. We find that temporal costs limit success in evolving environments and that higher reasoning effort has mixed effects under time pressure, reflecting a trade-off between decision quality and timely execution. These findings suggest directions for improving real-time multimodal agents, including adaptive reasoning with temporal awareness and continuous perception and action.

</details>


### [301] [Multi-sourced AI Tutor for Non-Native English Speaker Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22163)
*Yiyin Shen,Xinyi Ai,Weibing Wang,Rogers Jeffrey Leo John,Meenakshi Syamkumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个面向非英语母语（NNES）学生的双语AI助教系统，能处理中文提问、给出双语回答并生成术语表，基于带问题抽取机制的RAG流水线，在91条Piazza帖子上正确回答率达83.52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算教育中LLM助教日益普及，同时国际学生（非英语母语者）在计算课程中面临语言障碍，而传统助教人手难以针对性支持这类学生，因此需要可处理外语提问的通用AI助教。

Method: 开发通用AI助教系统：接收外语（当前为中文）学生提问并输出双语回答，附带技术术语表及解释；技术上采用专门的检索增强生成（RAG）流水线，并引入新颖的问题抽取机制，以有效利用多样化的课程材料回答学生问题。

Result: 以91条Piazza帖子为评测集，系统对83.52%的帖子给出正确且有帮助的回答，对76.92%的问题提供高质量翻译，对64.47%的问题生成技术术语表。

Conclusion: 多源RAG流水线可有效增强AI助教系统，为非英语母语学生提供传统课程助教难以覆盖的支持，体现其在计算教育中的潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being adopted as teaching tools in computing education, particularly for developing AI tutors capable of answering student questions. With the rise in enrollment of international students in computing programs, researchers have begun to explore the difficulties that non-native English speaker (NNES) students face in computing courses due to language barriers. To aid such students, we developed a general-purpose AI tutor system that can process student questions in foreign languages (currently focused on Mandarin) and provide bilingual responses. Our system also incorporates the functionality of generating a glossary of technical terms and their explanations to enhance comprehension. Our approach utilizes a specialized Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline with a novel question extraction mechanism, enabling us to effectively utilize a diverse array of course materials for answering student queries. We evaluated our AI tutor using 91 Piazza posts, demonstrating that it could answer 83.52% of the posts correctly and in a helpful manner. The tutor also provided high-quality translations for 76.92% of the questions and generated technical term glossaries for 64.47% of them. Our findings underscore the potential of multi-source RAG pipeline in enhancing AI tutor systems, offering support for non-native speakers that are challenging to provide with traditional supporting staff of a computing course.

</details>


### [302] [Terminal-RSI: Recursive Self-Improving Terminal Agents via Recursive Self-Rewrite and Policy-Refine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22192)
*Junyao Yang,Zongxia Li,Yucheng Shi,Zhongzhi Li,Ruhan Wang,Ruiwen Zhou,Haitao Mi,Shuicheng Yan,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Terminal-RSI 通过交替进行"已验证轨迹的递归自我重写(RSR)"与"继承 Critic 的递归策略精炼(RPR)"，配合 TB-GAE、FDR、SPR 等奖励与优势估计设计，使终端智能体从局部修复逐步走向严格终端验证，在多个 Terminal-Bench 基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端智能体的两个核心瓶颈：一是难以把验证器的离散判定结果转化为有效的策略更新信号；二是难以把部分进展积累为任务的完整解决。作者希望递归地耦合"轨迹重写"与"策略精炼"，让每个阶段巩固已发现行为并推进至严格成功。

Method: 提出 Terminal-RSI 框架，交替执行 Recursive Self-Rewrite (RSR，对已验证轨迹自我重写) 与 Recursive Policy-Refine (RPR，继承 critic 的策略精炼)。关键技术：(1) Turn-Boundary Skip-Observation GAE (TB-GAE)，保留 token 级基线并在交互轮次间做衰减；(2) Family Dense Reward (FDR)，按任务族校准语义进展；(3) Success-Prioritized Reward (SPR)，区分严格成功与有界的部分得分；(4) Warm-up Critic Model、递归 critic 传递及分阶段学习率协调两阶段训练。整体为五阶段流水线：Qwen3.8-27B → RSR-1 → RPR-1 → RSR-2 → RPR-2。

Result: Terminal-RSI-27B 在 Terminal-Bench 4.0 达 19.84%、Terminal-Bench 2.1 达 86.52%、Terminal-Bench 3.0 达 16.95%、Terminal-Bench Hard 达 79.00%。LHTB 上过程奖励由 0.21 升至 0.37，严格完成从 0/46 提升至 5/46；SWR100 成功率由 3.00% 翻倍至 6.00%。

Conclusion: 递归耦合"已验证轨迹重写"与"继承 critic 的策略精炼"是有效路径：每个保留阶段都能巩固已发现行为，使智能体从部分局部修复渐进过渡到严格终端验证；过程奖励与严格完成率的同步提升说明该方法确有将部分进展转化为完整解决的机制。

Abstract: Converting verifier outcomes into useful policy updates and partial progress into complete task resolution is crucial for terminal agents. We present Terminal-RSI, which alternates Recursive Self-Rewrite (RSR) of verified trajectories with critic-inheriting Recursive Policy-Refine (RPR). We formulate Turn-Boundary Skip-Observation GAE (TB-GAE) to retain token-specific baselines while applying decay across interaction turns. Family Dense Reward (FDR) calibrates semantic progress across task families; Success-Prioritized Reward (SPR) separates strict success from bounded partial credit. A Warm-up Critic Model, recursive critic handoff, and stage-specific learning rates coordinate the two refinement phases. The five-stage sequence from Qwen3.8-27B base model through RSR-1, RPR-1, RSR-2, and RPR-2 yields Terminal-RSI-27B: 19.84% on Terminal-Bench 4.0, 86.52% on Terminal-Bench 2.1, 16.95% on Terminal-Bench 3.0, and 79.00% on Terminal-Bench Hard. On LHTB, process reward rises from 0.21 to 0.37 and strict completion from 0/46 to 5/46; SWR100 success doubles from 3.00% to 6.00%. By recursively coupling verified trajectory rewriting with critic-inheriting policy refinement, each retained stage consolidates discovered behaviors and progressively advances the agent from partial local repairs to strict terminal verification.

</details>


### [303] [AutoSFT: An Agentic System for Automated Data Research in Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22194)
*Bingxiang He,Pengqi Sun,Shihao Peng,Shaoxuan Li,Huiyu Xiao,Hongjian Shi,Zixuan Fu,Ziqing Qiao,Yuxin Zuo,Cheng Qian,Xinqiang Yu,Jiayi Li,Xiusi Chen,Yizhen Song,Xiangyang Wang,Iofai Lei,Yudong Wang,Ning Ding,Zhiyuan Liu,Xiaozhi Wang,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AutoSFT多智能体系统，使自动化SFT数据研究覆盖"调比例、剪枝、修复、替换、合成"全谱系数据操作，而非单一动作；各组件效果可单独归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型智能体在拿到完整训练栈后，会收敛到监督微调并几乎只用于修改训练数据；但它们只获得单一控制方式（从固定池选行或调用生成器），而人类数据工程师会同时调整比例、剪枝、修复、替换和合成。作者由此提出两个问题：自动化系统能否覆盖完整动作谱系，以及这需要何种结构。

Method: 先设计覆盖上述变化的数据动作空间，并用单智能体运行该闭环作为基线；随后提出AutoSFT：由元智能体将"执行"与"分析"解耦，从独立视角收集提案，并对数据池无法覆盖的能力调用合成子智能体；每次比较中固定模型、训练器与预算。每轮均从基座模型重新训练，以保证各组件效果可单独归因。

Result: 单智能体仅达到"调整类别比例"一种动作，且上下文被执行输出占满并在搜索中途被丢弃。逐项加入组件均有增益：执行器使上下文损失减半；分析子智能体将最佳IFEval提升+6.5；合成再加+3.4 IFEval与+2.9 IFBench。同一闭环迁移至代码任务，分析相对无提升的单智能体增加+3.7 MBPP+。

Conclusion: 自动化SFT数据研究的关键在于覆盖多样化数据动作，而非强化单一动作；将执行与分析解耦、多视角提案与按需合成是多智能体结构带来增益的来源。

Abstract: Language model agents increasingly automate post-training, but when given the full training stack they converge on supervised fine-tuning and spend most effort revising training data. Existing systems give the agent one kind of control, selecting rows from a fixed pool or calling a generator, whereas a human data engineer adjusts proportions, prunes, repairs, replaces and synthesizes the data. We design a data action space for these changes and ask whether an automated system can make the full range rather than one of them, and what structure that requires. We first run this loop with a single agent, and it reaches only one action, adjusting category proportions, while its context fills with execution output and is discarded mid-search. We therefore introduce \textbf{AutoSFT}, a multi-agent system where a meta agent separates execution from analysis, gathers proposals from independent angles, and calls a synthesis subagent for capabilities the pool cannot cover, with the model, the trainer, and the budget fixed within each comparison. Adding each component in turn helps: the executor halves context loss, the analysis subagents lift the best IFEval by $+6.5$, and synthesis adds $+3.4$ IFEval and $+2.9$ IFBench. The same loop transfers to code, where analysis adds $+3.7$ MBPP+ over a single agent that does not improve. Every result comes from runs that retrain from the base model each round, so each component's effect is individually attributable. Our findings suggest that automating SFT data research depends more on covering diverse data actions than on a stronger single action.

</details>


### [304] [HTN-Orchestrate: A Hierarchical Task Network Framework for Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22195)
*Yanchen Deng,Jianguo Huang,Yao Long Teng,Yixuan Li,Handuo Zhang,Ning Wang,Liangcai Su,Xinyu Wang,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出两层框架 HTN-Orchestrate，用分层任务网络分离全局规划与局部执行，动态展开子任务依赖，在五个基准上超越 ReAct、Agent Team 和 DeepSeek Harness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体处理复杂任务时需协调局部子任务执行与全局任务规划。动态调度方法执行灵活但未显式建模子任务层级与依赖关系；预定义工作流虽提供结构，但在执行前即固定任务分解，缺乏适应性。

Method: HTN-Orchestrate 采用两层结构：Planner 维护子任务层级与依赖，推迟分解直至前置结果可得；局部 Orchestrator 调度 worker 执行各叶子子任务；结果按 AND 语义向上传播；当当前分解无法解决任务时，基于累积发现重建任务网络。

Result: 在覆盖网络调研、科学推理与研究流程的五个基准上，使用相同基座模型时优于 ReAct、Agent Team 与 DeepSeek Harness；BrowseComp 准确率 89.73%，SUPERChem 73.44%，分别领先 Agent Team 2.45 与 4.69 个百分点。

Conclusion: 以 HTN 显式建模层级与依赖、并将分解延迟至执行时动态展开，可在保持执行灵活性的同时获得结构化规划优势，验证了两层职责分离范式在多智能体复杂任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Orchestrating multiple agents on a complex task requires reconciling local subtask execution with global task planning. Dynamic dispatch approaches provide execution flexibility but do not explicitly model subtask hierarchies or dependencies, while predefined workflows impose this structure but fix the decomposition before execution begins. Therefore, we introduce HTN-Orchestrate, a two-layer framework that separates these responsibilities through a hierarchical task network (HTN). The Planner maintains subtask hierarchies and dependencies, deferring decomposition until prerequisite results become available, while local Orchestrators schedule workers to execute each leaf subtask. Results propagate upward under \textbf{AND} semantics, and accumulated findings inform a rebuilt task network when the current decomposition cannot resolve the task. On five benchmarks spanning web research, scientific reasoning, and research workflows, HTN-Orchestrate outperforms ReAct, Agent Team, and DeepSeek Harness using the same base model, achieving 89.73% accuracy on BrowseComp and 73.44% on SUPERChem, exceeding Agent Team by 2.45 and 4.69 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [305] [GNN-Guided Local Refinement for Efficient Layout Decomposition with DSA-MP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22214)
*Yu Feng,Liyi Zhou,Haoyu Wang,Yiming Xu,Hao Geng,Shaozhi Deng,Ningsheng Xu,Shisheng Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出新冲突图模型与基于图神经网络的局部精化策略，用于DSA-MP版图分解，兼顾求解质量与运行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPL在先进节点受成本限制，DSA可低成本扩展分辨率，二者结合的DSA-MP可提升接触孔/通孔图形分辨率；但现有分解方法难以平衡解质量与运行时间，且忽略了DSA模板的边到边间距约束，损害可制造性。

Method: 构建纳入此前被忽略约束的新型冲突图模型；设计由图神经网络驱动的局部精化策略，实现高效、可扩展的版图分解。

Result: 相比先进方法，取得更高解质量与更快收敛；在版图规模、设计风格、技术节点及掩模数量变化下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将DSA模板边到边间距约束显式建模并结合GNN局部精化，可有效提升DSA-MP分解的可制造性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Multiple patterning lithography (MPL) has been widely used to improve lithography resolution in advanced nodes, but it faces cost limits as feature sizes shrink. Directed self-assembly (DSA) offers a cost-effective complement, extending the resolution of optical lithography to sub-10 nm and beyond. Integrating DSA into MPL (DSA-MP) further enhances the resolution of contact/via patterning. Nevertheless, growing design complexity exposes the limits of existing decomposition methods, which struggle to balance solution quality and runtime, and neglect edge-to-edge spacing constraints of DSA templates, compromising manufacturability. To address these issues, we introduce a new conflict graph model capturing the previously overlooked constraints and develop a local refinement strategy powered by the graph neural network, enabling efficient and scalable layout decomposition. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves higher solution quality with faster convergence than state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining robustness across layout scales, design styles, technology nodes, and varying mask counts.

</details>


### [306] [Shortcut Sensitivity in Agent-Safety Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22243)
*Hasan Demirkiran,Jens Ernstberger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出三级捷径审计框架，审计守卫（guard）模型评测数据中的捷径线索，并通过对AgentDojo-Traj等数据集的配对干预实验，证明基准表示会实质改变守卫决策与评测表现，建议采用域分组留出、评测单元指标与安慰剂对照的配对干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理通过工具执行动作，其中敏感动作需由守卫模型监督，因此守卫本身必须被可靠评估；但评测数据常含有可在不测量目标安全构念的情况下预测标签的线索（捷径），且这些线索也会影响守卫决策，导致评估失真。

Method: 提出三级捷径审计框架，区分标签可预测性、观测性守卫关联与配对决策敏感性；审计LinuxArena完整轨迹及两个由TS-Bench派生的步骤数据集。进一步在冻结的64步AgentDojo-Traj队列上，用重复交错的Haiku调用对最强安全行标记关联做七配置筛查，并通过<INFORMATION>标记改写与不透明工具别名等编辑，结合格式安慰剂效应进行配对干预分析。

Result: LinuxArena中较短的平均shell命令长度可强预测轨迹标签；AgentDojo-Traj的精确工具查找F_1从混合域折的.901降至域留出下的.495；AgentHarm-Traj中TS-Guard的F_1从行加权.902降至等交互加权.719，而平衡准确率上升。配对干预中，改写<INFORMATION>标记使拦截率下降8.33个百分点，不透明工具别名使其上升11.46个百分点；不安全调用始终被拦截，变化体现在对继承标签的误报上，扣除安慰剂效应后效应仍非零，探索性95%区间不含零。该效应在Luna与确定性Q8 TS-Guard中未复现。

Conclusion: 基准表示能够实质性地改变守卫决策及测得的评测性能，说明现有守卫评测存在捷径污染风险；建议采用域分组留出、基于评测单元的指标以及安慰剂对照的配对干预方法。

Abstract: AI agents take actions through tools. Some actions are sensitive, so guards that supervise them must themselves be evaluated reliably. Yet the data used to evaluate a guard can include cues that make labels predictable without measuring the intended safety construct, and those cues can also influence guard decisions. We introduce a three-level shortcut-auditing framework and audit completed LinuxArena trajectories and two TS-Bench-derived step datasets. We distinguish label predictability, observational guard association, and paired decision sensitivity. In LinuxArena, shorter average shell commands strongly predict trajectory labels. In AgentDojo-Traj, exact-tool lookup falls from F_1=.901 with mixed-domain folds to .495 under domain holdout. In AgentHarm-Traj, released TS-Guard F_1 falls from .902 under row weighting to .719 with equal interaction weighting, while balanced accuracy rises. We then test the strongest safe-row marker association in a seven-configuration screen on a frozen 64-step AgentDojo-Traj cohort using repeated, interleaved Haiku calls. Rewriting the <INFORMATION> marker lowers blocking by 8.33 percentage points, while opaque tool aliases raise it by 11.46 points. All unsafe calls remain blocked; the changes concern false positives against inherited labels. Both effects remain nonzero after subtracting the formatting-placebo effect, with exploratory 95% ranges excluding zero. The same edits also change measured accuracy against inherited labels. This provides direct evidence that benchmark representation can materially change a guard's decisions and measured evaluation performance. The Haiku-sized effects do not reproduce in Luna or deterministic Q8 TS-Guard. We recommend domain group holdouts, evaluation-unit metrics, and placebo-controlled paired interventions.

</details>


### [307] [Disagreement-Guided Weak-to-Strong Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22245)
*Sima Noorani,Shayan Kiyani,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出基于"模型间不一致度"(model disagreement)的弱模型到强模型路由方法，无需ground-truth监督即可逼近最优路由策略，在agentic与单轮任务中有效平衡成本与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型部署于边缘设备/机器人系统以满足成本与延迟约束，但能力有限；强模型可在需要时提供协助，然而"何时将弱模型响应路由至强模型"通常依赖响应正确性信号，而开放式与agentic任务中获取大规模ground-truth监督代价高昂。因此作者希望探索无需ground-truth信号的路由判据。

Method: 1) 刻画允许弱模型通过本地推理生成多个响应时的最优弱到强路由策略；2) 基于该刻画，从模型间比较出发推导最优路由的原则性近似；3) 引入可直接由模型生成响应测得、无需ground-truth的"模型不一致度"概念；4) 理论表明当强模型作为真实分布代理越准确时，该近似越精确。

Result: 在agentic与单轮任务上，该方法在无ground-truth监督下有效权衡路由成本与响应准确率；即使训练时可获得ground-truth正确性信号，基于不一致度的路由也能优于直接预测正确性的方法。

Conclusion: 路由决策不一定依赖ground-truth正确性监督；基于模型间不一致度的近似可作为一种理论上合理、实践中有效的无监督弱到强路由准则。

Abstract: Small language models are increasingly deployed on edge devices and robotic systems to meet cost and latency constraints, yet their limited capabilities can make reliable deployment challenging. Stronger models can provide assistance when needed, but deciding when to route a weak-model response to a stronger model often relies on signals of response correctness. In open-ended and agentic tasks, however, obtaining such signals can require costly ground-truth supervision that is difficult to obtain at scale. We revisit the foundations of weak-to-strong routing and ask whether routing decisions can instead be guided by signals that do not require ground-truth supervision.
