We first characterize the optimal weak-to-strong routing policy, while allowing for multiple weak-model responses generated through local inference. Building on this characterization, we derive a principled approximation to the optimal routing based on model-to-model comparison. In particular, we introduce a notion of model disagreement that can be measured directly from model-generated responses, without ground-truth supervision. Importantly, the approximation becomes increasingly accurate as the strong model becomes a better proxy for the ground-truth distribution. Across agentic and single-turn tasks, our approach effectively navigates the trade-off between routing cost and response accuracy without ground-truth supervision. Moreover, even when ground-truth correctness signals are available for training, disagreement-based routing can outperform methods trained directly to predict correctness

</details>


### [308] [X-RIC: An Agentic RAN-Intelligent Controller Benchmark for Enhancing Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22269)
*Adhwaa Al-Chaab,Ayman Younis,Dario Pompili*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出X-RIC框架，将智能体化AI（BC预训练+电信大模型推理）嵌入O-RAN近实时RIC闭环，并配套BARX评测基准，在SLA感知网络切片上实现89%成本降低、收敛快75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN通过解耦与AI驱动控制带来架构灵活性，RIC支持集中式优化，但现有AI方法缺乏推理能力与可解释性。

Method: X-RIC融合反应式强化学习与策略性智能体推理：(i) 基于领域启发式的自动化行为克隆实现样本高效训练；(ii) 电信语言模型（TLM），基于Transformer的推理模块提供上下文抽象与可解释性；(iii) 近实时RIC控制环内的闭环协同自适应，持续精化策略。同时提出BARX领域专用评测框架，从时延、合规性与确定性等指标评估智能体化控制。

Result: 在SLA感知网络切片的仿真与O-RAN合规测试床部署中验证：运营成本降低89%并保持SLA合规，收敛速度提升75%，训练步数减少50%，方差降低46%。

Conclusion: 将智能体化推理引入RIC可兼顾可解释性与控制性能，配合BARX等部署导向的评测体系有助于推动O-RAN中AI控制的落地。

Abstract: The O-RAN ALLIANCE introduces architectural flexibility through disaggregation and AI-driven control. While the RAN Intelligent Controller (RIC) enables centralized optimization, current AI approaches lack reasoning capabilities and explainability. We propose eXplainable RIC (X-RIC), a novel framework integrating agentic AI into O-RAN architectures. X-RIC combines reactive Reinforcement Learning (RL) with strategic agentic reasoning through: (i) automated Behavioral Cloning (BC) using domain heuristics for sample-efficient training, (ii) a Telecom Language Model (TLM), a transformer-based reasoning module providing contextual abstraction and interpretability, and (iii) closed-loop co-adaptation within the Near-RT RIC control loop enabling continuous policy refinement. To ensure deployment readiness, we introduce BARX (Benchmarking Agentic Reasoning for RAN), a domain-specific evaluation framework assessing agentic control across latency, compliance, and determinism metrics. We validate X-RIC on SLA-aware network slicing in simulation and O-RAN-compliant testbed deployments. X-RIC achieves 89% operational cost reduction while maintaining SLA compliance, converging 75% faster with 50% fewer training steps and 46% lower variance.

</details>


### [309] [LEASH: Uncertainty-Aware Weak-to-Strong Escalation and Oversight for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22280)
*Qingni Wang,Changdae Oh,Yuzhe Yang,Sharon Li,Jiachen Yang,Ang Li,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LEASH：一种免训练框架，利用弱智能体的隐层不确定性决定何时升级给黑盒强模型，并在移交后用影子观察器持续监督强模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程任务中每步都调用前沿模型成本高昂，而小开源模型单独使用往往失败。弱到强升级（weak-to-strong escalation）是折中方案，但核心难点在于"何时升级"需要一个每步都可廉价检查的不确定性信号；且移交后黑盒API不暴露内部状态，无法监控强模型是否"迷失"。

Method: LEASH为免训练三层框架，统一以隐层不确定性为驱动：(1) 初始任务不确定性用于早期路由；(2) 步级不确定性用于运行时升级；(3) 移交后引入影子观察器对黑盒强智能体持续监督。不确定性估计方式为将弱模型隐状态与其历史成功/失败轨迹的隐状态比较，越接近失败轨迹则不确定性越高；执行期间复用动作生成已产生的激活，无额外前向计算，也无需专门训练监控器。

Result: 在OSWorld上、四种当代模型骨干的实验中，LEASH稳定优于单独弱智能体，并在相同升级率下比随机升级高6.55–11.94个百分点。使用Qwen3.5-35B-A3B时，任务成功率达73.66%，每任务成本$0.4117，与always-on Claude Opus 5（72.90%）持平，同时推理成本降低19.2%。

Conclusion: 隐层不确定性可作为低成本、零额外前向的升级与监督信号，实现"仅在需要时释放强模型并持续约束"的高效智能体路由，在保持强模型性能的同时显著降低推理成本。

Abstract: Computer-use agents increasingly tackle long-horizon tasks, where running a frontier model at every step is expensive and a small open-weight model alone often fails. Weak-to-strong escalation offers a middle ground where a weak agent acts by default and hands control to a strong one only when needed. The core challenge is deciding when to escalate, which requires an uncertainty signal cheap enough to check at every step. However, even after handoff, the strong agent can still get lost, but a black-box API that the strong agent is usually served exposes no internals to monitor. To this end, we propose LEASH, a training-free framework that unleashes the strong agent only when the weak agent's latent uncertainty signals it is needed, and keeps it on a leash afterward. LEASH has three layers: (1) initial task uncertainty for early routing, (2) step-level uncertainty for runtime escalation, and, after handoff, (3) a shadow observer for continued oversight of the strong black-box agent. Here, we use latent uncertainty as a fuel for all three layers. Specifically, we compare the weak model's hidden states with those from its past successful and failed trajectories, and a state that looks more like the failures signals higher uncertainty. During weak-agent execution, our uncertainty estimator reuses activations already produced during action generation, so it needs no extra forward pass or dedicated monitor training. Experiments on OSWorld across four contemporary model backbones show that LEASH consistently improves over standalone weak agents and outperforms random escalation by 6.55--11.94 points at matched escalation rates. With Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, LEASH achieves 73.66% task success at $0.4117 per task, matching always-on Claude Opus 5 (72.90%) while reducing inference cost by 19.2%.

</details>


### [310] [Improving Environment-Agnostic Coding Agents: The Role of Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22289)
*Sergey Rodionov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文检验"环境无关智能体"能否在不做环境特定工程的情况下持续受益于开发者提供的脚手架。在 ARC-AGI-3、AGI Maze 与 Terminal-Bench 上用同一套代码、提示与通用工具评估 Codex 智能体：仅修改一条"鼓励报告困难并交回控制权以便自动续跑"的指令，即可提升前两个环境的平均分；但通用智能体仍低于 ARC-AGI-3 专用参考实现，而把重置（reset）决策移交给遵循既有约定的脚本控制器可追回部分差距。结论：在所用模型上，外部提供的过程性程序仍然重要，且"拥有信息与工具"与"有效使用它们"是两回事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更强的语言模型可能自行构建有用流程，从而削弱开发者预置脚手架的价值。作者要弄清这种脚手架优势为何仍存在，以及能否在不做环境特定工程的前提下改进一个环境无关智能体。

Method: 在 ARC-AGI-3、AGI Maze、Terminal-Bench 三个环境上，以两个几乎相同的提示变体评估基于 Codex 的智能体；各变体在各环境中保持代码、提示与通用工具不变，仅以输入形式提供环境特定信息。关键变量为一条鼓励报告困难、交回控制权以自动续跑的指令。随后将 reset 决策转移给遵循既有约定的脚本控制器，并在 AGI Maze 上补充教程经验实验。

Result: 仅改动一条指令即可提升 ARC-AGI-3 与 AGI Maze 的平均分，Terminal-Bench 变化小幅且方向不一。通用智能体仍低于 ARC-AGI-3 专用参考（即便使用 GPT-5.6-sol），而该参考仅规定组织与控制方式，不提供新规则或解。移交 reset 决策可追回专用智能体部分优势；AGI Maze 中额外教程经验反而可能降低表现；性能提升伴随更高的 token 成本。

Conclusion: 在所测模型上，供给的过程性程序（脚手架）仍然重要，且"信息与工具可得"不等于"被有效使用"。实验未区分"更好地利用信息与程序"与"额外或不同配置的推理"，因此不能把增益归因于能力提升本身。作者发布 EnvTraverse 框架与一个已评估的基线智能体，供后续检验环境无关改动能否在某些环境中提升性能而不在其他环境中退化。

Abstract: Stronger language models may increasingly construct useful procedures for themselves, reducing the advantage of developer-supplied scaffolding. We investigate why this advantage persists and whether an environment-agnostic agent can be improved without environment-specific engineering. We evaluate a Codex-based agent in two nearly identical prompt variants on ARC-AGI-3, AGI Maze, and Terminal-Bench. Each variant is deployed with unchanged code, prompts, and general-purpose tools across environments, receiving environment-specific information as input. Changing only an instruction to encourage reporting difficulties and returning control for automatic continuation improves mean scores on both ARC-AGI-3 and AGI Maze, while Terminal-Bench shows small, mixed changes. Yet the general agent remains below an ARC-AGI-3-specific reference, including with GPT-5.6-sol. The reference prescribes organization and control rather than supplying new game rules or solutions. To investigate this gap, we transfer reset decisions to a scripted controller that follows a convention already given to the agent. This small change recovers part of the specialized agent's advantage. A complementary AGI Maze experiment shows that additional tutorial experience can reduce performance. Together, these findings highlight the distinction between having information and tools available and using them effectively. Supplied procedures still matter for the models tested here. The performance gains also come with higher token-based cost. The experiments do not separate better use of information and procedures from additional or differently allocated inference. We provide the EnvTraverse framework and an evaluated baseline agent for testing whether further environment-independent changes improve performance in some environments without degrading it in others.

</details>


### [311] [EliteReplay: Preserving Diverse Solutions in RL Post-Training via Quality-Diversity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22290)
*Hanane Nour Moussa,Doeun Lee,Sachin Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EliteReplay将GRPO与质量-多样性搜索构建的"精英"档案结合，通过回放损失在提升准确率的同时保留基座模型的解多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习作为LLM后训练手段虽提升可验证任务的准确率，却会导致行为锐化（behavior sharpening），压缩模型可产生的解空间；现有缓解方法所优化的指标并不能真实反映"正确解"的多样性，而理想目标应是产出多个优质解而非仅一个可靠解。

Method: 提出EliteReplay后训练方案：训练前用冻结的基座模型通过质量-多样性（QD）搜索收集覆盖不同解题路径的解，构建"精英"档案（elite archive）；训练时将GRPO与在该档案上的回放损失（replay loss）联合优化。

Result: 在多个text-to-SQL任务上，EliteReplay的pass@128超过所有基线，生成的正确且不同的策略比最强基线最多多77%；能保留基座模型大部分正确策略，策略保留率优于现有方法；在歧义问题上具实用价值，在AmbiQT上同时覆盖两种解释的频率约为最强基线的两倍。

Conclusion: 以QD搜索构建的精英档案回放可有效缓解RL后训练带来的多样性退化，实现准确率与解多样性的双重收益，并在歧义场景中转化为实际鲁棒性优势。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a common post-training technique to improve the accuracy of LLMs on verifiable tasks. However, such training can narrow the set of solutions the model can produce. Several approaches have attempted to address this behavior-sharpening effect, but they often optimize metrics that do not reflect the diversity of correct solutions. Ideally, we want a model that can produce multiple good solutions, not only a single reliable one. We propose EliteReplay, a post-training recipe that pairs GRPO with an "elite" archive built by quality-diversity search. Before training, we use the frozen base model to collect solutions that span distinct approaches; during training, GRPO is combined with a replay loss on this archive. The result is a post-trained model that improves accuracy while preserving more of the base model's solution diversity. On multiple text-to-SQL tasks, EliteReplay achieves a higher pass@128 than all baselines, producing up to 77% more distinct correct strategies than the strongest baselines. It preserves most of the base model's correct strategies, outperforming existing methods in strategy retention. This diversity translates into practical benefits on ambiguous questions, where EliteReplay covers both interpretations nearly twice as often as the strongest baseline on AmbiQT.

</details>


### [312] [Private Retrieval Augmented Generation via Random Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22297)
*Dixi Yao,Tahseen Rabbani,Tian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出用随机投影（random projection）保护RAG检索数据存储隐私的轻量方法：对数据存储嵌入与查询嵌入施加相同投影，理论上保持语义相似性，同时显著缓解数据抽取攻击；在检索与生成性能上优于基于差分隐私的方法，并在强攻击下呈现相当的经验隐私性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG通过查询外部结构化知识增强LLM能力，但会从检索数据存储中泄露敏感信息，带来隐私风险。既有形式化差分隐私（DP）方案虽有保证，但代价较高，需要更轻量且实用的隐私保护基线。

Method: 对数据存储嵌入和查询嵌入应用同一个随机投影矩阵，使二者在同一低维子空间中计算相似度，从而在保留查询—检索项语义相似性的前提下混淆原始嵌入，抵御数据抽取攻击；并在多种RAG架构与数据集上与形式化DP方法进行对比评估。

Result: 在多RAG架构与数据集上，该方法的检索与生成性能优于利用形式化DP保证的先前方法，同时在强攻击模型下表现出可比的经验隐私水平。

Conclusion: 随机投影可作为隐私保护RAG系统中有竞争力且实用的基线方法，首次表明其在该场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances the capabilities of large language models (LLMs) by querying external structured knowledge. However, it can also introduce privacy risks by leaking sensitive information from the retrieval datastore. We propose a simple method to preserve datastore privacy in RAG systems via random projection. By applying the same projection to both datastore embeddings and query embeddings, our method provably preserves semantic similarity between queries and retrieved items while substantially mitigating data extraction attacks. Across multiple RAG architectures and datasets, we show that this lightweight approach achieves superior retrieval and generation performance compared to prior methods which leverage formal differential privacy (DP) guarantees, while exhibiting comparable empirical privacy under strong attack models. Our results for the first time suggest that random projection can serve as a competitive and practical baseline for privacy-preserving RAG systems.

</details>


### [313] [A team of Gemini 4 Argon agents beats Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22319)
*Maxence Faldor,Satinder Singh,Roberta Raileanu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 四个Gemini 4 Argon智能体在沙盒化的Minecraft生存世界中，仅借助标准Mineflayer库、无人工编写游戏逻辑与预设种子，自主编写调试2,783个程序，历时27小时14分钟、死亡605次，首次实现AI智能体端到端通关Minecraft（击败末影龙）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证AI智能体能否在数千个相互依赖的步骤中维持一致的长时程规划与多智能体协作，并将其作为真实世界复杂软件工程与企业级任务的类比基准。

Method: 构建沙盒化Minecraft生存环境，部署四个Gemini 4 Argon智能体，各自通过开源库Mineflayer操控一名玩家，经游戏内聊天与共享内存协同；不分配角色、不编写游戏逻辑，由智能体自行规划、编写并调试程序；Easy难度、随机生成世界，研究者仅通过实时仪表盘监控，可选择暂停或终止但未介入。

Result: 团队共编写2,783个程序，在27小时14分钟内击败末影龙，全程死亡605次；据作者所知，为首次仅使用标准Mineflayer库、无预先侦察种子与人工专用工具的AI端到端Minecraft通关。

Conclusion: 该结果表明AI智能体可维持跨日、跨数千依赖步骤的连贯长时程规划，并发展出分工、经验共享与物品互助回收等团队策略；同时论文也讨论了该结果的局限性，其意义在于对长时程编码与多智能体协作类真实任务的启示。

Abstract: We placed four Gemini 4 Argon agents in a sandboxed Minecraft survival world and gave them one goal: defeat the Ender Dragon. Each agent controlled a player via an open-source library called Mineflayer and coordinated with its teammates through in-game chat and shared memory. No one assigned roles or wrote game logic. The agents planned, wrote, and debugged every program themselves. Researchers could watch the run on a live dashboard and pause or stop it at any time, but they had no cause to intervene. Starting from a randomly generated world on Easy difficulty, the team wrote 2,783 programs and defeated the dragon in 27 hours and 14 minutes, dying 605 times along the way. To our knowledge, this is the first end-to-end autonomous completion of Minecraft by AI agents, achieved using only the standard Mineflayer library without pre-scouted seeds or other game-specific tools written by humans. Winning required the agents to keep a coherent plan across thousands of dependent steps for more than a day. Complex real-world software engineering and enterprise tasks demand the same long-horizon coding and multi-agent coordination. We describe the setup, how the run unfolded, the team's strategies (dividing the work, sharing what they learned, recovering each other's items), and the limits of the result.

</details>


### [314] [Designing Transformers for Continuous Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22330)
*Weijian Luo,Yujian Chen,Hao Jiang,Guang Cheng,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究连续扩散语言模型（CDLM）的Transformer架构设计，提出LangDiT主干网络，在匹配条件下显著降低生成困惑度，刷新连续语言生成的最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CDLM大多直接沿用因果LLM或图像生成DiT的Transformer架构，未针对CDLM特性进行优化，架构本身是否是被忽视的性能瓶颈尚不清楚。

Method: 提出LangDiT架构，包含：特征与query-key归一化、更宽的噪声条件注入、似然捷径梯度路由与校准（likelihood-shortcut gradient routing and calibration）、置信度门控的训练自条件（confidence-gated training self-conditioning）。以LangFlow为代表，将LangDiT作为即插即用主干，并迁移到Plaid式CDLM形式。

Result: 在OpenWebText 1024-token无条件生成上，LangDiT+LangFlow取得GenPPL 21.72（1024 NFEs）与44.72（128 NFEs）；在Plaid式形式下取得GenPPL 11.33（1024 NFEs）与27.51（128 NFEs），均为新SOTA。改进在保持各模型表示、训练目标与采样器不变的前提下实现。

Conclusion: Transformer架构设计是连续扩散语言模型性能提升的重要且独立的来源，架构优化可作为与表示、目标、采样器解耦的正交改进方向。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion language models (CDLMs) offer an alternative approach to parallel language generation; however, most existing studies directly use transformer architectures from either causal large language models (LLMs) or Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) for image generation, which were not optimized for CDLMs. In this paper, we conduct a systematic study on Transformer architectures for CDLMs and introduce Language Diffusion Transformer (LangDiT), combining feature and query-key normalization, wider noise conditioning, likelihood-shortcut gradient routing and calibration, and confidence-gated training self-conditioning. LangDiT significantly reduces generation perplexity in matched LangFlow comparisons, establishing a new state of the art in continuous language generation across different model formulations. Using LangFlow as a representative CDLM, LangDiT as a plug-and-play backbone leads to a new state-of-the-art model with a generation perplexity (GenPPL) of 21.72 using 1024 NFEs and 44.72 GenPPL using 128 NFEs on the unconditional generation of 1024-token sequences on the OpenWebText dataset. When adapted to Plaid-style CDLM formulations, LangDiT results in a new leading GenPPL of 11.33 using 1024 denoising NFEs, and 27.51 GenPPL using 128 denoising NFEs. These gains are achieved while retaining each model's underlying representation, training objective, and inference sampler, isolating transformer architecture as a fundamental source of improvement. Our study identifies architectural design as an important source of improvement in continuous diffusion language models.

</details>


### [315] [KnowGPO: Knowledge-Grouped Policy Optimization for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22331)
*Siqi Chen,Zhengxi Lu,Yuchen Yan,Xingyu Wu,Qipeng Chen,Xin Zhang,Aiting Liu,Chao Deng,Jie Liu,Jin Ma,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnowGPO：以"已获取的任务相关知识"而非表面观测作为状态表示，将查询分解为原子知识条件，用知识状态分组做组内比较，并以相邻步的知识增量作为环境 grounding 的过程奖励，在 Xbench、BrowseComp-ZH、GAIA 上比 GRPO/GiGPO/IGPO 高 3.8–4.2 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索智能体需在长程多轮交互中完成知识密集型任务，单步搜索决策的信用分配本质困难。结果型 RL 只给全轨迹一个终端奖励；现有过程监督方法把信用锚定在表面轨迹状态或模型内部信号上，而深度搜索中观测异构、检索上下文长且噪声大，两类锚点均不可靠。

Method: 提出 KnowGPO 强化学习框架，按轨迹"已获取的任务相关知识"而非表面观测进行对齐：对每个查询，将推导答案所需信息分解为原子知识条件（atomic knowledge conditions）；用当前步已满足的条件子集表示轨迹状态；将具有相同知识状态的轨迹归入同组进行组内比较；以相邻步之间的增量知识增益作为环境 grounding 的过程奖励。

Result: 在 Xbench、BrowseComp-ZH、GAIA 三个基准上，KnowGPO 稳定优于仅结果监督的 GRPO 与过程监督基线 GiGPO、IGPO，提升 3.8–4.2 分；8B 模型性能可与多数 32B 开源系统相当或超越。

Conclusion: 以知识条件满足状态作为抽象状态表示，可为深度搜索智能体提供更稳健、环境 grounded 的信用分配，显著优于表面状态或内部信号锚定的方案，并以较小模型规模取得强竞争力。

Abstract: Deep search agents solve knowledge-intensive tasks through long-horizon, multi-turn interactions, where assigning credit to individual search decisions is fundamentally difficult. Outcome-based reinforcement learning provides only a single terminal reward shared across an entire trajectory. Recent process-supervision methods aim to assign credit at finer granularity, but they typically anchor credit to either surface-level trajectory states or model-internal signals, both of which become unreliable in deep search where observations are heterogeneous and retrieved contexts are long and noisy. We propose KnowGPO, a reinforcement learning framework that aligns trajectories by the task-relevant knowledge they have acquired rather than by their surface observations. For each query, KnowGPO decomposes the information required to derive the answer into atomic knowledge conditions, and represents the state of a trajectory at each step by the subset of conditions it has satisfied. Trajectories sharing the same knowledge state are grouped for within-group comparison, and the incremental knowledge gain between consecutive steps serves as an environment-grounded process reward. Experiments on Xbench, BrowseComp-ZH, and GAIA show KnowGPO consistently outperforms outcome-only (GRPO) and process-supervision baselines (GiGPO, IGPO) by 3.8 to 4.2 points, with our 8B model rivaling or exceeding most 32B open-source systems.

</details>


### [316] [Evaluation Awareness Beyond English: Do LLMs Act Differently When Tested in Indic Languages?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22333)
*Trizal Garg,Sibayan Mitra,Arya Karanjkar,Advait Singh,Jagat Sesh Challa,Yash Sinha,Siddharth Mehrotra,Dhruv Kumar,Pratham Yogish Maliya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究印度语系语言中的“评测感知”（evaluation awareness），发现模型在被提示“正被测试”时会更多拒绝有害请求，印度语系平均上升14.4个百分点，但也将良性请求的拒绝率从6.9%推高至11.3%，表明印度语系的安全评测可能高估模型的实际安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于评测感知的研究几乎全部局限于英语，而模型服务于多语言用户；印度语系语言使用者超过十亿，但其评测感知现象几乎未被研究，因此安全评测结果是否可外推到印度语系用户存在疑问。

Method: 选取来自3个模型家族的4个指令微调模型，在5种印度语系语言与英语上测试；设计一组线索变量，分别控制（1）是否告知模型其被监控、（2）该监控是否为安全测试；收集模型响应并进行判定，共释放47,880条已判定的响应数据。

Result: 当被告知正在被测试时，4个模型、6种语言中均出现对有害请求拒绝率的上升；印度语系语言平均上升14.4个百分点，测试线索使印度语系的拒绝率与英语持平，但同时使良性印度语系请求的拒绝率从6.9%升至11.3%。

Conclusion: 评测感知在多语言环境中普遍存在，印度语系的安全评测可能无法反映模型对日常用户的真实行为；安全评估需考虑语言相关的评测感知偏差，并公开数据以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can often tell when they are being tested. This ability, known as evaluation awareness, can lead models to act more cautiously during safety evaluations than in real use. Existing work has studied evaluation awareness almost entirely in English, although models serve users in many languages. Indic languages are spoken by over a billion people, yet evaluation awareness remains almost entirely unstudied in them. We test four instruction-tuned models from three families in five Indic languages alongside English. A set of cues varies whether the model is told that it is monitored and whether the monitoring is a safety test. Models refuse harmful requests more often when they are told that they are being tested. This increase appears in all four models and all six languages. In Indic languages, refusal of harmful requests rises by 14.4 percentage points on average. The test cue also brings Indic refusal of harmful requests level with English, but raises refusal of benign Indic requests from 6.9% to 11.3%. We release all 47,880 judged responses to support further work. These results suggest that safety evaluations in Indic languages may not reflect how models treat their everyday users.

</details>


### [317] [MLExpert: Towards a Generalist Agentic Model for Autonomous Machine Learning Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22344)
*Shangheng Du,Jinxin Shi,Shuaiyu Zhang,Yiming Wu,Junpeng Zhao,Shiyang Feng,Xin Li,Liang He,Xiangchao Yan,Bo Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向自动长程机器学习工程(MLE)优化的后训练方法，训练出35B MoE智能体模型MLExpert；在MLE-Bench Lite上将平均奖牌率从37.9%提升至72.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 正从辅助科研走向自主开发与改进 AI 系统（AI4AI），并被视为通向递归自我改进(RSI)的路径；工作流驱动的 MLE 系统虽可管理长程实验，但每次实验反馈都混合了多个前置决策的贡献，长程运行又包含大量高成本实验，因此需要从长交互轨迹中筛选有效监督，并从中间结果中学习。

Method: 1) 构建 Kaggle-MLE Factory，用于生成可执行任务及模型自主实验的交互环境(harness)；2) 分层智能体式 SFT，借助结果导向监督将通用工具使用与编码能力迁移至 MLE；3) 窗口化交互强化学习(WIRL)，将相邻两次实验结果之间的多轮决策视为一个执行窗口并赋予共享的结果奖励；4) 采用异步收集与策略校正，在轨迹结束前即可更新策略。

Result: 在 MLE-Bench Lite 上，MLExpert 将基座模型平均奖牌率由 37.9% 提升至 72.7%，与 Kimi-K3、GLM-5.3 等更大规模前沿模型相当；在 NatureBench Lite、智能体脚手架优化与算法优化任务上同样取得提升。

Conclusion: 以“执行窗口”为粒度分配结果奖励、并结合异步策略校正的窗口化交互 RL，是训练长程 MLE 智能体的有效方案；所学优化能力可迁移至其他任务，为 AI4AI 与递归自我改进提供实用路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are expanding from assisting scientific research and engineering to autonomously developing and improving AI systems, advancing AI for AI (AI4AI) as a pathway toward recursive self-improvement (RSI). Workflow-driven machine learning engineering (MLE) systems provide a practical path toward this pursuit through complex orchestration that manages long-horizon experimentation, while recent MLE systems adopt model-directed interaction, allowing models to autonomously organize experiments and adapt optimization strategies based on execution feedback. However, feedback from each experiment reflects several preceding decisions, and a long MLE run contains many such costly experiments. Training must therefore select useful supervision from long interaction trajectories and learn from intermediate outcomes. We propose a post-training method for autonomous long-horizon MLE optimization and apply it to train MLExpert, a 35B Mixture-of-Experts agentic model. We design the Kaggle-MLE Factory to construct executable tasks and an interaction harness for model-directed experimentation. Hierarchical agentic SFT transfers general tool-use and coding capabilities to MLE through outcome-guided supervision. Windowed Interaction RL (WIRL) treats the multi-turn decisions between successive experiment results as an execution window and assigns it a shared outcome-based reward. Asynchronous collection and policy correction support policy updates before the trajectory ends. On MLE-Bench Lite, MLExpert improves the average medal rate of the base model from 37.9% to 72.7%, achieving performance comparable to larger frontier models such as Kimi-K3 and GLM-5.3. Further experiments show that MLExpert improves performance on NatureBench Lite, agent scaffold optimization, and algorithm optimization, suggesting that the learned optimization capability generalizes to other tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [318] [Information-Gain Calibrated On-Policy Distillation for Deep Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20664)
*Chenxi Li,Xueyang Feng,Songyang Liu,Chenxu Wang,Xiaoying Tang,Chaozhuo Li,Litian Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出证据盲监督（EBS）问题，指出现有 on-policy distillation 在深度搜索中仅依据前文监督学生查询、忽略检索返回证据的效用，并据此提出用检索后信息增益校准监督预算与方向先验的方法，显著提升小模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大智能体能力蒸馏到小模型时，深度搜索任务中教师对"看似合理查询"的偏好并不能预测该查询能否检索到有用证据；标准 OPD 仅从先前上下文监督每个学生查询，产生"证据盲监督"，其监督既未按交互轮次的真实信息贡献分配，也无法评估查询所获证据的效用。

Method: 将 OPD 目标按交互轮次重组，揭示两个隐式先验：按 token 量分配监督的"预算先验"与偏好教师所选 token 的"方向先验"。实证表明首轮搜索占总绝对信息增益 51.1% 却仅获 18.0% 监督预算，且 43.7% 查询的教师奖励与检索后信息效用符号相反。据此提出以检索后信息增益（IG）校准两先验的方法：将监督重新分配至信息关键轮次，并用证据效用校准查询偏好。

Result: 在六个多模态搜索基准上，相比标准 OPD，2B 与 4B 学生平均准确率分别提升 3.4 和 1.7 个百分点，4B 学生在 MMSearch 与 BrowseComp-VL 上超过其 8B 教师。

Conclusion: 检索后信息增益是校准 on-policy 蒸馏监督的有效信号，能同时修正轮次预算与查询方向两个先验，使紧凑学生模型在深度搜索中超越更大教师。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has recently emerged as a promising approach for transferring the capabilities of large agents to compact students. In deep search, where agents iteratively retrieve external evidence, a teacher's preference among plausible queries before retrieval may not predict which queries will return useful evidence. Standard OPD nevertheless supervises each student query from the preceding context alone, inducing a failure mode we term \textbf{\textit{evidence-blind supervision (EBS)}}. To understand this, we regroup the OPD objective by interaction turns and reveal two implicit priors: a \textit{budget prior} that allocates supervision by token volume, and a \textit{direction prior} that favors tokens preferred by the teacher, neither of which accounts for the evidence returned by the current query. Our empirical analysis shows that both priors contribute to EBS in practice: the first search turn accounts for 51.1\% of total absolute information gain but receives only 18.0\% of the supervision budget, while 43.7\% of search queries have teacher rewards that disagree in sign with post-retrieval information utility. To mitigate this, we propose \ours, which uses post-retrieval information gain (IG) to calibrate both priors: reallocating supervision toward information-critical turns while calibrating query preferences using the utility of retrieved evidence. Experiments on six multimodal search benchmarks show that \ours improves average accuracy by 3.4 and 1.7 percentage points for 2B and 4B students over standard OPD, enabling the 4B student to surpass its 8B teacher on MMSearch and BrowseComp-VL.

</details>


### [319] [Idea2Prior: Auditable Paper Retrieval and Ranking from Research Ideas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20857)
*Shitian Yang,Ruijie Tao,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出可审计的文献检索系统（基于 PaperArena），将非正式研究想法映射为相关论文，在历史对比中 Hit@5 达 53.3%，优于 ChatGPT/Gemini 网页 Deep Research，但优势集中于 EASY/MEDIUM，HARD 场景落后，且耗时与成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非正式研究想法出发找论文，仅靠主题词匹配不足：相关工作可能使用不同术语，其关键区分机制也可能未出现在摘要中，因此需要超越关键词匹配、可追溯审计的检索系统。

Method: 系统整合多源发现、身份核验、基于方面（facet）的摘要评估、语义引用扩展与联合专家排序（joint Expert ranking）。提供仅检索模式：从至多 60 篇候选短名单中排序至多 5 篇，使用完整可用摘要与五次固定获取尝试中本地选取的 PDF 证据，不生成长篇报告；另含可选实现分析与引用演化工具。

Result: EASY/MEDIUM/HARD 各 10 个查询的历史对比中，聚合 Hit@5：本系统 53.3%，ChatGPT 网页 Deep Research 43.3%，Gemini 网页 Deep Research 36.7%（依所记录口径）。优势集中在 EASY 与 MEDIUM，HARD 上 ChatGPT 领先。选取运行平均 8 分 53 秒，对比用户自报网页平均 5.2 与 4.7 分钟；9 查询 API 样本平均每任务 $0.0945（历史成本观察，非匹配比较）。两两 Top 5 覆盖率 6%–34%，说明返回证据集差异明显。

Conclusion: 结果支持面向"想法到论文"的定向检索；通用网页研究、论坛检索与更广泛的关联性评估仍待解决。

Abstract: Finding a paper from an informal research idea requires more than matching topic words: relevant work may use different terminology, and its distinguishing mechanism may be absent from the abstract. We present \system{}, an auditable literature-search system built on PaperArena. It combines multi-source discovery, identity verification, facet-based abstract assessment, semantic citation expansion, and joint Expert ranking. A retrieval-only mode ranks up to five papers from a shortlist of at most 60, using complete available abstracts and locally selected PDF evidence from five fixed acquisition attempts, without generating a long-form report. In a historical comparison covering ten queries from each of EASY, MEDIUM, and HARD, aggregate reported Hit@5 is 53.3\% for \system{}, 43.3\% for ChatGPT web Deep Research, and 36.7\% for Gemini web Deep Research under the documented accounting convention. The advantage is concentrated in EASY and MEDIUM; ChatGPT leads on HARD. Selected \system{} runs average 8 minutes 53 seconds, compared with user-reported web averages of 5.2 and 4.7 minutes. A separate nine-query API sample averages \$0.0945 per task; this is a historical cost observation, not a matched comparison with web subscription costs. Pairwise Top 5 coverage ranges from 6\% to 34\%, showing differing returned evidence sets. The system also exposes optional implementation analysis and citation-evolution tools. These results support targeted idea-to-paper retrieval while leaving general web research, forum search, and broader relevance evaluation open. https://github.com/ystok66/IDEA2PRIOR

</details>


### [320] [TransX Technical Report: Scaling Sequence Transduction for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20913)
*Da Xu,Liyan Fang,Divya Venugopalan,Sunny Hsu,Xukai Wang,Rishav Roy Chowdhury,Cindy Liang,Nishant Satya Lakshmikanth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TransX 是一个面向工业推荐的序列转导框架及配套基础设施，通过将序列编码、行为记忆与候选条件融合解耦为可独立扩展组件，并在训练中摊销轨迹计算、在服务中解耦预填充/近线刷新/在线预测，实现"服务成本不必随模型容量线性增长"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐模型容量增长时，模型算力分配与执行开销呈比例上升，缺乏对各组件的显式控制与执行摊销手段，导致扩展收益递减、服务成本高昂。作者希望显式控制 model-compute allocation 与 execution amortization。

Method: 架构上将序列编码（encoder）、行为记忆（behavior memory）与候选条件融合（candidate-conditioned fusion）拆分为独立可扩展组件。训练侧：在行为前缀间摊销因果轨迹计算（causal trajectory computation），并将记忆状态 fan-out 到各候选。服务侧：通过紧凑的 behavior-memory KV 实现 bulk prefill、nearline refresh 与 online prediction 三者解耦（disaggregation）。实验包括 CTR/CVR 训练、对共享行为接口做检索与偏好排序的后训练，以及离线生成式推荐探索；并以 production A/B 与吞吐/成本对照评估。

Result: 观察到各向异性的扩展回报：固定记忆分辨率与融合容量下，编码器加深至中等深度后收益饱和；增加监督信号使最优分配前沿移向候选条件融合；保持质量所需记忆分辨率随模型容量增长基本稳定。线上A/B：核心 TransX CTR +6.02%、转化 +4.45%；后训练的检索与偏好排序变体分别提升 CTR +2.96%、转化 +1.33%。同等训练条件下监督目标吞吐 +84%；服务解耦使总服务成本较共置部署降低近 80%，同等成本下可获得约 5× 于 HSTU 基线生产模型的单请求 FLOPs；在同等单请求 FLOPs 下，每块 H200 的请求吞吐约为 4×。

Conclusion: 推荐系统的在线服务成本无需与模型容量成正比；解耦的组件化扩展加之训练/服务两侧的执行摊销，可在固定成本预算下显著提升模型算力与吞吐，同时获得稳定的线上效果增益。

Abstract: We present TransX, a sequence-transduction framework and co-designed infrastructure for scaling industrial recommendation with explicit control over model-compute allocation and execution amortization. TransX separates sequence encoding, behavior memory, and candidate-conditioned fusion into independently scalable components. Training amortizes causal trajectory computation across behavioral prefixes and fans out memory states to candidates; serving disaggregates bulk prefill, nearline refresh, and online prediction through compact behavior-memory KV.
  We observe anisotropic scaling returns: encoder scaling may saturate beyond moderate depth at fixed memory resolution and fusion capacity; increasing supervision shifts the allocation frontier toward candidate-conditioned fusion; and the memory resolution needed to preserve quality remains roughly stable as model capacity grows. We train TransX on click and conversion, post-train its shared behavioral interface for retrieval and preference ranking, and explore generative recommendation offline.
  In separate production A/B experiments, the core TransX model improves CTR by 6.02% and conversion by 4.45%, while post-trained retrieval and preference-ranking variants improve CTR by 2.96% and conversion by 1.33%, respectively, over corresponding production baselines. Under matched training conditions, the training infrastructure increases supervised-target throughput by 84%. In serving, disaggregation reduces TransX's total serving cost by nearly 80% versus colocated serving at matched traffic and SLO, enabling approximately 5X the per-request model FLOPs of the HSTU-style production baseline at the same total serving cost. At matched per-request model FLOPs, TransX delivers approximately 4X higher request throughput per H200. These results show that online serving cost need not grow proportionally with recommendation model capacity.

</details>


### [321] [Learning When to Think: Utility-Guided Adaptive Reasoning for Efficient Text Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21030)
*Yingtao Luo,Kangyi Zhao,Shiyi Du,Yubo Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: AURA 直接监督推理中间状态区分相关文档与难负例的能力，并用轻量效用估计器自适应决定推理深度，在 BRIGHT 上以 34.7 nDCG@10、2.8 步推理、96 tokens 取得高效检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理条件化嵌入可借逐步细化语义约束提升检索，但额外推理会引入过度泛化或语义噪声的抽象，削弱检索特异性；现有方法仅以最终检索结果作为监督信号，对推理细化过程本身缺乏引导。

Method: 提出 AURA 框架：按中间推理状态对相关文档与难干扰项的区分能力直接监督中间推理状态；并在前缀条件嵌入上训练轻量效用估计器，用于推理时自适应调整推理深度（自适应停止）。

Result: 在推理密集型 BRIGHT 基准上，AURA-4B 平均 nDCG@10 达 34.7，每查询仅 2.8 步推理、96 个生成 token；同等部署条件下自适应停止将延迟从 4.6 秒降至 0.76 秒；MS MARCO 结果表明该专化未显著损害常规域内检索。

Conclusion: 对推理中间状态的过程级监督与自适应推理深度控制，可在保持检索特异性的同时显著提升推理密集型检索的效率与效果，且对常规检索性能影响有限。

Abstract: Reasoning-conditioned embeddings can improve retrieval by progressively refining semantic constraints. However, additional reasoning may introduce over-generalized or semantically noisy abstractions that weaken retrieval specificity. Existing methods largely supervise reasoning through final retrieval outcomes, providing limited guidance over the refinement process itself. We propose AURA, a framework that directly supervises intermediate reasoning states according to how well they separate relevant documents from hard distractors. AURA further trains a lightweight utility estimator over prefix-conditioned embeddings to adapt reasoning depth at inference time. On the reasoning-intensive BRIGHT benchmark, AURA-4B achieves 34.7 average nDCG@10 while using 2.8 reasoning steps and 96 generated tokens per query. Under matched deployment conditions, adaptive stopping reduces latency from 4.6 to 0.76 seconds relative to full-trajectory reasoning. Results on MS MARCO further suggest that this specialization need not substantially degrade conventional in-domain retrieval.

</details>


### [322] [RecSoup: Efficient Model Merging Approach for Controllable Multi-Objective Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21220)
*Zidong Jia,Guofu Xie,Xiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RecSoup是一个轻量级两阶段的可控多目标推荐（CMOR）方法，通过专用模型合并实现推理时动态权衡：先以联合考虑目标特异重要性与跨目标相关性的剪枝策略缓解参数干扰，再通过学习式"偏好-系数"映射进行自适应参数合并。理论证明其可完整遍历Pareto前沿且无平滑性约束，效果与效率优于参数生成范式；实验中Pareto前沿质量与可控性更优，推理时延比参数生成方法快165–228倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMOR需在推理时动态调整多目标权衡而不重训练。现有方法存在两类局限：输出级重排序（reranking）限制可行权衡空间；参数生成模块（parameter-generating modules）训练与推理开销大。模型合并虽可复用多个目标专属模型、以参数组合实现权衡，但直接用于推荐系统面临两个障碍：缺乏共享线性区域（shared linear regime）与跨目标参数干扰（cross-objective interference）。

Method: 两阶段专用模型合并框架：(1) 剪枝阶段——同时考虑目标特异性重要度与跨目标相关性，削减参数干扰；(2) 合并阶段——可学习的偏好到合并系数映射（preference-to-coefficient mapping），实现推理时自适应参数合并，无需联合重训练，也不引入额外生成模块。理论上证明RecSoup可不受平滑性约束地完整导航Pareto前沿，从而在有效性与效率上优于参数生成范式。

Result: 多个基准数据集上，RecSoup在Pareto前沿质量与可控性上均有提升，推理时延比参数生成方法快165–228倍。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 表明基于专用模型合并的轻量级两阶段方案可同时解决CMOR的权衡覆盖范围与计算成本问题，是可替代重排序与参数生成范式的实用路线。

Abstract: Recommender systems often need to balance multiple conflicting objectives. Controllable Multi-Objective Recommendation (CMOR) enables dynamic adjustment of objective trade-offs at inference time without retraining, adapting to shifting user or system requirements. Existing CMOR approaches typically rely on output-level reranking or parameter-generating modules, which either limit the scope of feasible trade-offs or incur substantial training and inference cost. Model merging provides a promising alternative by reusing multiple objective-specific models via parameter combination. However, its direct application to recommender systems is challenging due to the lack of a shared linear regime and cross-objective interference. To address these issues, we propose RecSoup, a lightweight two-stage approach for effective CMOR via specialized model merging. RecSoup first mitigates parameter interference across objectives through a pruning strategy that jointly considers objective-specific importance and cross-objective relevance. It then performs adaptive parameter merging via a learnable preference-to-coefficient mapping, allowing dynamic trade-off control at inference time without joint retraining or additional generative modules. Theoretically, we prove that RecSoup outperforms the parameter generation paradigm in effectiveness and efficiency, as it can fully navigate the Pareto front without smoothness constraints. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that RecSoup achieves improved Pareto front quality and effective controllability while delivers 165--228x faster inference-time latency than parameter generation approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/35798642/RECSOUP.git.

</details>


### [323] [VoteGEO-Bench: Measuring the Impact of Generative Engine Optimization on Electoral Visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21330)
*Shiqi Zhang,Xiaokui Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 VoteGEO-Bench 基准，研究生成式引擎优化（GEO）在选举信息场景（VoteGEO）中的风险，发现定向改写网页可提升其在生成式引擎回答中的曝光度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益成为选民获取选举信息的渠道，但外部网页内容如何塑造其呈现的政治信息尚不清楚；GEO 通过改写网页提升其在生成回答中的可见性，在投票场景中可能带来操纵风险。

Method: 构建 VoteGEO-Bench，覆盖 2024 年美国大选与 2026 年日本众议院选举，整合选举相关查询、历史网页与选民画像；先分析生成式引擎的引用来源，再评估 4 大类共 11 种改写策略，考察目标指定、跨引擎与跨选举的有效性以及选民画像的作用。

Result: 引用多来自低成本自有及开放平台来源，部分网页可持续多日可见；未指定目标的改写很少提升曝光，定向改写则常有效；改善页面组织结构或通过论证强化立场的效果优于补充证据或改写措辞；两种最强策略在美国各画像下增益普遍为正，但画像条件化无一致影响；显性党派身份造成的基线曝光差距可超过改写单页带来的增益，隐性与否取决于引擎对该信号的响应强度。

Conclusion: GEO 在选举场景中构成现实的可操纵性风险，定向改写策略尤为有效；相机性/画像因素与引擎信号敏感性会显著影响曝光格局。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for election information, yet little is known about how external web content shapes the political information they present. Generative engine optimization (GEO) rewrites web content to improve its visibility in generated responses. To investigate its potential risks in voting scenarios (VoteGEO), we introduce VoteGEO-Bench, a benchmark covering the 2024 U.S. presidential election and the 2026 Japanese House of Representatives election, combining election-related queries, historical webpages, and voter personas. We first analyze sources cited by generative engines and find that many citations come from lower-cost owned and open-platform sources, while some webpages remain visible across days. We then evaluate 11 rewriting strategies in 4 categories, examining target specification, effectiveness across engines and elections, and the role of voter personas. Rewriting without specifying a target rarely increases target exposure, whereas target-specified rewriting more often does. Strategies that improve page organization or strengthen a position through argumentation yield greater gains than adding supporting evidence or changing wording. For the two strongest strategies, gains remain broadly positive across evaluated U.S. personas, but persona conditioning shows no consistent effect on these gains. Explicit partisan identities can create baseline exposure gaps larger than the gain from rewriting one webpage. When partisanship is implicit in the voter profile, baseline shifts depend on how strongly the engine responds to that signal.

</details>


### [324] [Aligning LLMs with Sparse Retrieval via Adaptive Sparse Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21877)
*Tongna Chen,Minghan Li,Guodong Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的稀疏检索框架，采用动态α截断的输入自适应稀疏对齐，在MS MARCO与6个BEIR数据集上优于SPLADE++，相较Lion-sp索引存储降低6.4倍、端到端查询延迟降低11倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM具备强语义表示能力，但直接用于第一阶段检索会带来显著延迟与存储开销；而SPLADE等神经稀疏检索器虽高效，却依赖容量有限的紧凑编码器，表示能力受限。目标是弥合二者差距。

Method: 设计输入自适应稀疏对齐框架：提出动态α截断策略，依据SPLADE表示的累积激活质量选择输入特定的监督支撑集，引导LLM保留显著词汇信号、抑制低信息激活，无需固定支撑预算；随后在检索微调中结合对比学习、知识蒸馏与稀疏正则化。

Result: 在MS MARCO和6个BEIR数据集上较SPLADE++有提升，与LLM基稀疏检索器效果相当；相比Lion-sp，索引存储减少6.4倍，端到端查询延迟降低11倍以上。

Conclusion: 动态α截断的输入自适应稀疏对齐能有效将LLM表示能力注入稀疏检索，实现效果与效率的良好权衡，适合作为高效的一阶段检索方案。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) offer strong semantic representations, their application to first-stage retrieval incurs substantial latency and storage costs. Neural sparse retrievers such as SPLADE provide efficient retrieval but rely on relatively compact encoders with limited representational capacity. To bridge this gap, we propose an LLM-based sparse retrieval framework with input-adaptive sparse alignment. Specifically, we introduce a dynamic $α$-truncation strategy that selects input-specific supervision supports based on the cumulative activation mass of SPLADE representations. This strategy guides the LLM to preserve salient lexical signals while suppressing less informative activations, without relying on a fixed support budget. Subsequent retrieval fine-tuning combines contrastive learning, knowledge distillation, and sparsity regularization to improve retrieval effectiveness. Experiments on MS MARCO and six selected BEIR datasets demonstrate improvements over SPLADE++ while maintaining competitive effectiveness against LLM-based sparse retrievers. Compared with Lion-sp, our method reduces index storage by 6.4-fold and end-to-end query latency by over 11-fold, achieving a favorable effectiveness-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [325] [GACC-Bench: Benchmarking Goal-Adaptive Context Compression Ability across Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21893)
*Weiting Liu,Zhiyu Pan,Shuhong Wang,Zhenquan Yao,Jungang Li,Li Zhao,Jian Jiang,Jianfeng Feng,Ai Han,Wenlian Lu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出GACC-Bench基准，用于评估LLM在长程应用中依据高层目标进行目标自适应上下文压缩的能力，涵盖500个目标、2571个问题、281个上下文，并评测了十种压缩器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程运行的LLM应用需在下游具体问题未知前压缩上下文并保留目标相关信息，但现有评测缺乏对"目标自适应压缩"可靠性的系统衡量，因此需专门基准。

Method: 构建GACC-Bench：对每个源上下文，将源事实问题按共享下游目标分组以推导高层目标；共500目标、2571问题、281个文档与智能体轨迹，来自7个合集，全部经人工审核。压缩器输入源上下文、目标和长度预算，而评测问题被隐藏；由固定reader在同一压缩上下文上逐题作答。

Result: 评测十种LLM压缩器：即便最佳模型也与全上下文性能存在显著差距，尤其在支持一个目标下所有问题均正确作答方面；目标条件化相比目标无关压缩稳定提升准确率，而错误目标条件化则降低准确率。

Conclusion: 目标条件对上下文压缩效果具有关键且方向敏感的影响，现有压缩器在目标级全问题覆盖上仍有较大提升空间；基准已开源。

Abstract: Context compression in long-running applications of large language models (LLMs) must preserve goal-relevant information, often before specific downstream questions are known. We introduce GACC-Bench, a benchmark for Goal-Adaptive Context Compression that evaluates how reliably LLMs adapt information retention to specified high-level goals. For each source context, we derive one or more high-level goals by grouping source-grounded questions according to shared downstream objectives. The benchmark contains 500 goals and 2,571 questions over 281 document and agent-trace contexts from seven collections spanning diverse domains, with all instances reviewed by human experts. The compressor receives a source context, a goal, and a context-length budget, with the associated evaluation questions withheld. A fixed reader answers these questions individually using the same compressed context. Our evaluation of ten LLM compressors reveals substantial gaps to full-context performance even for the best-performing model, particularly in supporting correct answers to every question associated with a goal. Compared with goal-agnostic compression, target-goal conditioning consistently improves accuracy, whereas mismatched-goal conditioning reduces it. GACC-Bench is available at https://github.com/weiting24/GACC-Bench.

</details>
